# 301: Chapter 298: Koraidon's Experience; Can Paradox Pokemon Lay Eggs? (Double Chapter)

"Calm down, Professor!"

D pondered carefully and then thought of another possibility.

He looked up at Professor Sada on the opposite side and said, "Is it possible that they're not friends, but enemies who once fought?"

Upon hearing this, Professor Sada was slightly taken aback, and only after D's reminder did she manage to calm down a bit.

Due to the destructive and primal energy from ancient times, these Paradox Pokemon from the past are mostly aggressive and battle-hungry, with a strong sense of territory.

Through her and Turo's observations of the summoned Paradox Pokemon, they had never seen them coexist amicably.

Apart from hunting, most of the time was spent in territorial disputes or patrolling their territory.

Thinking about this, Professor Sada couldn't help but let out a slight sigh.

"I can't rule out that possibility, but my intuition tells me that Flutter Mane is different," she said. "In any case, I hope you and Koraidon can make a trip to the Area Zero Research Institute."

"I'll first communicate with Koraidon," D contemplated.

"Okay."

Without hanging up the call, D turned and went to find Koraidon in the pasture.

Koraidon was sunbathing, lying on the grass, the warm sunlight shining on it, making Koraidon feel warm all over.

The scent of the grass floated in the air, mixed with a hint of the sweet smell of Poffins.

D was at home baking today's Delicious Poffins for Dragonite and Dragonair.

The aroma of the Poffins drifted out through the cracks in the window, accompanied by a gentle, serene breeze that just reached where it lay.

This feeling made Koraidon feel peaceful inside, instinctively relaxing from inside out.

At this moment, Koraidon heard a familiar set of footsteps.

With its eyes half-open, half-closed, it turned to look at D, who had come to its side.

"Ah ghiss?"

D asked, "Did you have Pokemon friends in ancient times?"

Hearing this, Koraidon was momentarily stunned, then quickly realized, nodding its big head: "Ah ghiss!"

An image of a Pokemon levitating in mid-air appeared in its mind.

It was a Flutter Mane.

It met that fellow in that primitive and ancient era.

At that time, it was traveling around, searching for D and the pasture.

The path was not safe; the primitive world was full of wild aggressive Pokemon, including several powerful Koraidons.

Koraidon's mind was filled with thoughts of D. Because of this, it initially didn't recognize that Flutter Mane.

It wasn't until later that it recognized Flutter Mane and took it along on the search for D.

No one knows how much time passed, but after going through countless hardships, it felt as if something was guiding them in the mysterious realm.

After battling a powerful wild Koraidon, although Koraidon was injured, they finally reached their destination.

At that time, Koraidon remembered sensing a dangerous aura below and said farewell to Flutter Mane, telling it to stay there.

Flutter Mane agreed.

Koraidon ventured alone, wounded, down to the depths of the Giant Pit and finally arrived at an underground cavern filled with glowing minerals.

There, it encountered a group of shining turtle Pokemon.

The Pokemon seemed very curious about it, surrounding it and asking who it was, why it had come there.

Koraidon replied in distress, "I'm Cyclizar, looking for D."

The little turtles curiously asked, "Who is D?"

Koraidon answered sorrowfully, "He is someone very, very important to me. I miss him so much, but I can't find him."

Hearing this, the little turtles were touched by its emotions, and all lowered their heads, their spirits dampening.

Then a few of the little turtles looked at it and said they might be able to help.

Next, Koraidon saw this group of originally small turtle Pokemon light up with a brilliant glow.

In the glow, their appearances changed and became larger turtles.

The big turtles activated their power, and the next moment, Koraidon saw the whole world enveloped in dazzling crystals.

A terrifying wave of Tera Energy distorted spacetime, and at that moment, Koraidon felt something was calling to it from the other end of spacetime.

Before it could react, the radiant light engulfed it.

When Koraidon emerged from the light, it saw the excited faces of Professor Sada and Professor Turo.

They seemed to want to capture it with a Poke Ball, but at that time, Koraidon was desperate to see D, so it slapped the Poke Ball away with a paw.

Then, it charged out of the research institute, flew out of Area Zero, flew out of Paldea Giant Pit, and flew towards home in the direction it remembered.

When it finally returned to the pasture, it collapsed from severe injuries and fell unconscious.

Afterward, it was D and the Cyclizar, who was its past self, that dragged it into the room and prepared a cooked meat dish for it.

Initially, Koraidon was bewildered and confused by the events it had experienced, but when it saw its past self standing in front of it, everything suddenly became clear to it.

---

In the beginning, due to those experiences in ancient times, it couldn't get along well with Cyclizar.

Especially when it saw Cyclizar getting close to D, it would always feel jealous and angry.

At that time, Cyclizar probably sensed its emotions too, so it was always cautious around it.

It wasn't until later that Koraidon slowly adapted to everything and reluctantly accepted Cyclizar's presence.

Thinking about it now, this was something it had experienced before!

Having obtained the information he wanted and with Professor Sada waiting, D turned and left to give Professor Sada a reply.

In front of the video phone, D told Professor Sada the information he learned from Koraidon.

"I asked Koraidon if it had ever made friends before, and Koraidon said it indeed had friends."

Hearing this answer, a look of joy appeared on Professor Sada's face.

Flutter Mane...

D pondered for a moment and then agreed to Professor Sada's request: "We will depart for the Paldea Giant Pit tomorrow."

Since that Flutter Mane is Koraidon's friend, it means the two Pokemon can get along without fighting like other Paradox Pokemon.

Does this mean he can capture that Flutter Mane?

Professor Sada's eyes lit up slightly: "In that case, Turo and I will be waiting for you and Koraidon at the institute!"

With those words, the call ended.

D found the Indeedee Sibling and told them about his temporary departure.

The siblings immediately assured him that they would take good care of the house so he could leave with peace of mind.

D was incredibly confident in the management abilities of the Indeedee Sibling.

So early the next morning, he flew to the Paldea Giant Pit on Koraidon.

Koraidon was also a little excited, as it had been a long time since they last parted, and it wondered how that Flutter Mane was doing now.

At top speed, it took just over two hours to fly from the ranch to the top of the Paldea Giant Pit.

Looking at the small world below shrouded in vast, ethereal clouds, D couldn't help but feel a sense of wonder.

The last time he came, he witnessed those violent Paradox Pokemon, and the entirely different ecological habits from modern Pokemon left a deep impression on him.

Thinking about it, D suddenly lost the desire to explore.

As the saying goes, familiarity breeds comfort.

This time he didn't stop midway but instead flew straight down through the entire pit on Koraidon, arriving at the Area Zero Research Institute at the bottom layer.

Professor Sada was already prepared to receive them.

She looked at Koraidon with a beaming smile and greeted it: "Long time no see, Koraidon!"

"Ah gah sss~"

Standing beside Koraidon, D nodded slightly to Professor Sada.

Professor Sada nodded back and then turned to lead the way:

"Follow me! That Flutter Mane is now hiding in the institute ahead; it seems to be very shy. Even Turo and I couldn't lure it out, so it will be up to you and Koraidon."

"Shy?" D was taken aback because this was obviously an adjective that shouldn't appear with Paradox Pokemon.

Professor Sada understood his point, nodded, and said, "This Flutter Mane is special, the most similar to modern Pokemon among all Paradox Pokemon I've seen."

At this, Professor Sada suddenly seemed to remember something, slapped her forehead, and said, "Ah, I almost forgot about your Koraidon!"

D looked down at Koraidon walking beside him.

Professor Sada mumbled a hypothesis: "So, is there some unknown connection between the two?"

Is it Flutter Mane that was influenced by Koraidon and changed its temperament, or was it Koraidon affected by Flutter Mane and became calmer...

Hmm, that's a question.

As a Pokemon research doctor, I must learn to make bold assumptions and carefully verify them.

Professor Sada looked at Koraidon, and thinking of that Flutter Mane, a fantastic idea flashed across her mind.

She covered her mouth in surprise: "Could it be that in the distant past, Koraidon and Flutter Mane were from the same egg group?"

Hearing this, D was momentarily dumbfounded.

Egg group? Could it be...

He also looked at Koraidon in shock and murmured: "Could it be that Koraidon and Flutter Mane fell in love with each other?"

Just like how Tropius fell in love with Miss Joy's Meganium in the anime.

Professor Sada calmly analyzed: "Although we haven't found any examples of Paradox Pokemon breeding, we cannot rule out the possibility."

At this moment, understanding the implication in their conversation, Koraidon quickly shook its head: "Ah gah sss!"

That's not it! It and Flutter Mane are just good friends!

D saw Koraidon's meaning and asked: "Is that not the case?"

"Ah gah sss!" Koraidon nodded its big head affirmatively.

Seeing this, Professor Sada couldn't help but speculate again: "Then could it be that Flutter Mane fell in love with Koraidon, responding to the Time Machine's call and following its footsteps here?"

Koraidon: "..."

---

D: "..."

Noticing their expressions, Professor Sada quickly reacted and said apologetically to D:

"Sorry, apart from this possibility, I can't think of any other reasons."

D thought Professor Sada's imagination was really exaggerated, worthy of someone capable of creating the Time Machine.

However, as far as he knows, Paradox Pokemon don't seem to be able to lay eggs like regular Pokemon.

Moreover, Koraidon, the Legendary Pokemon, is genderless just like Kyogre and Groudon.

But then came the problem: if this is truly the case, then how did those Paradox Pokemon reproduce in ancient times?

If they don't reproduce, wouldn't their species become extinct after several hundred or thousand years?

Or did the Pokemon World also experience a catastrophic asteroid crash into Earth, causing ancient Pokemon like Koraidon, Scream Tail, and Roaring Moon to all be wiped out like the dinosaurs?

D couldn't figure it out. The answer to this question might only be found by traveling back in time to ancient days using the Time Machine to witness it firsthand.

At this moment, the two arrived in front of the gigantic research institute towering beside the Tera Pillar.

Professor Sada swiped a card, and the entrance to the institute automatically opened.

She led D and Koraidon inside and said to them:

"Flutter Mane is inside this building. However, it's very cautious and rarely appears in front of humans. We were only able to track it through surveillance devices."

"I see....." D nodded. This kind of situation is quite normal.

Because, in this era, Flutter Mane and these Paradox Pokemon are like wild men living in ancient times.

They have never been exposed to modern technology. Even if they want to hide, they simply can't escape the range of surveillance.

Professor Sada suggested: "How about letting Koraidon try calling it?"

Upon hearing this, D looked at Koraidon: "Give it a try?"

"Agh-ssi." Koraidon nodded its large head, then raised its foot and started to slowly walk down the corridor inside the institute.

It progressed while glancing around, trying to find traces of Flutter Mane.

D and Professor Sada slowed their pace and followed behind Koraidon, holding their breath and observing the surroundings.

The corridor was silent.

At this time, the vast institute had no one inside.

According to D's guess, it must be that Professor Sada was worried that the hidden Flutter Mane would suddenly launch an attack, so she relocated the assistants and researchers elsewhere in advance.

Time passed, and even though Koraidon had walked for a long time, Flutter Mane still didn't appear.

Seeing this, Professor Sada stopped, furrowed her brows, and speculated: "Could it be because of our presence here?"

D thought carefully and said: "There's a possibility."

Professor Sada pondered for a moment and came up with a good idea: "Why don't we go to the surveillance room and let Koraidon, a Pokemon, stay here? Maybe Flutter Mane will come out to see it."

"Sounds good." D patted Koraidon on the head and said softly, "It seems your friend doesn't want to see us. In that case, we'll leave first and wait for you outside. Go and have a talk with it."

"Agh-ssi!" Koraidon nodded in agreement.

So D and Professor Sada left the institute.

Halfway through, they happened to encounter Professor Turo emerging from the ecological zone.

Upon hearing about their progress, Professor Turo also displayed an interested gaze and joined them in the surveillance room to observe what was happening inside the institute.

On the monitor screen, Koraidon, transformed into its ride form, was slowly traversing the corridor.

Once Koraidon's position was confirmed, D began searching through the real-time footage taken by other surveillance cameras.

Suddenly, Professor Turo exclaimed: "That Pokemon appeared!"

D looked down and saw Flutter Mane quietly emerge from the shadows of the wall.

Then, it went to the end of Koraidon's corridor, silently watching it from across a hallway.

And at that moment, the Koraidon in the frame also sensed Flutter Mane's presence, abruptly turning its head.

In an instant, their eyes met.

---

---

---

---

# 302: Chapter 299: Koraidon, Flutter Mane, and Miraidon

In the vast and silent corridor, Flutter Mane silently watched Koraidon across from it.

Seeing Flutter Mane, Koraidon's face lit up with a joyful expression.

"Aga-si!"

Looking at Koraidon like this, Flutter Mane couldn't help but recall the Koraidon from deep in its memory, always covered in scars.

In the past, they had traversed many places together.

To find that important person, it followed this Koraidon, weaving through the tall, dense Ancient Tree Forests, leaping off bottomless cliffs, and passing through deep, dark mysterious caves.

At that time, the Koraidon in its memory never smiled; no matter the situation, it always wore a sad expression.

Koraidon had told it many things, such as how it came from a ranch, living with many Pokemon there.

Koraidon also said that it was very scared, afraid it would never find D again, afraid it would never be able to go home.

And now, seeing the happy expression and wide grin on Koraidon's face, Flutter Mane's lips couldn't help but curl up slightly, revealing a faint smile.

It was happy for Koraidon.

"Dream." (You found him.) Flutter Mane said.

"Aga-si!" Koraidon nodded its big head excitedly and said, "Aga-si!" (D is here too!)

Flutter Mane already knew because it had just seen that creature called a human beside Koraidon.

Mentioning this, Koraidon suddenly wanted to know how Flutter Mane came here.

Thinking about it, it asked, "Aga-si?"

Flutter Mane replied, "Dream." (I was waiting there, you didn't come back, so I went below and was brought to this place.)

Below? Koraidon was momentarily stunned, then suddenly realized it must have been sent over by the power of those little turtle Pokemon, just like it!

Thinking of past experiences, Koraidon invited: "Aga-si?" (Would you like to come back to the ranch with us?)

*

Meanwhile, in the monitoring room.

Watching the footage of the two Ancient Pokemon getting along, Professor Sada's face was full of interest:

"As expected, they know each other!"

Seeing this scene, Professor Turo beside her couldn't help but feel a bit envious.

Professor Sada's research on Ancient Pokemon had made new progress once again.

While his research on Futuristic Pokemon had hit a bottleneck.

Compared to the primitive and ferocious Ancient Barbarian Pokemon, Futuristic Pokemon, despite their calm demeanor and seemingly high intelligence, posed significant challenges in research due to their unpredictable behavior and thoughts.

Thinking of this, Professor Turo couldn't help but look at D beside him.

As a renowned Pokemon Doctor, he could easily see that D was very adept at getting along with Pokemon.

No matter what kind of Pokemon, he could eventually befriend them and have them obediently listen to him.

So the question arose, could he entrust some Futuristic Pokemon to D and have them form bonds?

Just like Koraidon.

He once tried to create a friendship between Arven and Miraidon, but for some unknown reason, Arven was strongly resistant to both Koraidon and Miraidon.

Professor Turo fell into thought.

If Miraidon were entrusted to D's care, could Miraidon become another Koraidon?

Thinking of this, Professor Turo couldn't help but invite: "D, are you interested in a Pokemon like Miraidon?"

Hearing this, D was slightly stunned.

"What do you mean?" he asked.

Professor Turo explained: "I want to entrust Miraidon No. 1 to your care, just like Koraidon, and see if you can also form such a profound bond with Miraidon."

D instantly understood what Professor Turo meant.

He was intending to give him a Miraidon!

D's eyes widened immediately: "Can I?"

"Of course." Professor Turo nodded with a smile.

Professor Sada, who was attentively observing Koraidon and Flutter Mane, also heard their conversation at this moment. Her lips curled slightly, and she smiled at her husband:

"You're sneakily taking advantage again!"

Professor Turo was quite helpless: "There's no other way. As long as Ancient Pokemon are given enough time and receive guidance and training to adapt, they can integrate into this era."

"But the Futuristic Pokemon, with their already impressive intelligence, can't be influenced by this method at all."

On one hand, there's the yet-to-awaken, ferocious wildman Pokemon that only know how to fight for territory and hunt for food.

On the other hand, there's the unknown origin, metallic-bodied Futuristic Pokemon that possess extraordinary intelligence and thinking.

After speaking, Professor Turo looked at D: "So, I'd like to ask you to reach out to Miraidon, try to interact with it, and see if you can form bonds with it like you did with Koraidon."

D gradually calmed down.

Honestly, Koraidon's case was quite special since back then, he helped Koraidon heal its wounds, which gained him quite a bit of favor.

He had never interacted with Miraidon before, and he feared the process would be very challenging.

Out of caution, D didn't claim he could definitely accomplish it, but instead said, "I can give it a try."

Professor Turo nodded in satisfaction. He glanced at the monitor screen and said:

"It seems those two still have a lot to talk about, so let's take this opportunity to go to the Eco Garden where Miraidon is."

Thus, Professor Sada stayed behind to continue observing Koraidon and Flutter Mane.

D followed Professor Turo to the Eco Garden where Miraidon resided.

With the automatic opening of multiple doors, the two arrived at a vast garden full of cliff valleys.

This Eco Garden was extremely spacious. As soon as they entered, D began searching for Miraidon's silhouette.

Finally, he spotted Miraidon at the base of a towering cliff.

That Miraidon was levitating in mid-air, gazing indifferently at the sky with lifeless eyes, unclear of what it was looking at.

Professor Turo watched this scene and softly said: "That's Miraidon, the legendary Iron Snake that turns the earth to ash with lightning."

D murmured, "It resembles Cyclizar's skeleton."

Professor Turo stated seriously: "But Miraidon's power and ruthlessness far surpass that of a Pokemon like Cyclizar."

"It's far more intelligent than I imagined, possessing the observational and insightful power to even see through personalities, yet its expression is too monotonous and mechanized. Even I can't interpret its emotions or facial changes."

Hearing Professor Turo's words, along with what he witnessed, D couldn't help but mentally compare Miraidon with Koraidon.

"Indeed, Koraidon's emotions are much clearer and easier to understand, and its appearance is more biological..."

---

---

---

---

# 303: Chapter 300: Cat and Dog

"Miraidon!"

Professor Turo called out.

Upon hearing the voice, the Miraidon that was originally floating above the mountain cliff abruptly turned its head and looked in this direction from afar.

Its face showed no expression, appearing cold and mechanical.

It floated there, merely turning its head without taking any action.

"It seems to have a 'stranger beware' vibe!" D said.

Professor Turo chuckled, "That's just how Miraidon is. Although it doesn't usually express its emotions like Koraidon, its emotional richness is actually no less than Koraidon's."

"Kind of like a cool, aloof cat?" D murmured.

It's the kind that's usually cold and unapproachable, but once close, occasionally snuggles up to you and acts cute.

While Koraidon is the typical enthusiastic golden retriever.

Professor Turo thought D was referring to Pokemon like Purugly or Glameow.

He hadn't thought of this before, but D's words suddenly made Professor Turo feel the description was so fitting.

"Exactly, Miraidon is like that. But compared to cat-like Pokemon, Miraidon is more mysterious, and its emotional changes are extremely subtle, making it impossible for me to decipher." He nodded.

This reminded D of his own Glaceon.

However, after spending a lot of time with Alcremie and Pawmo, Glaceon's nature gradually became more lively.

"Let's go! I'll take you closer for a look!" Professor Turo suggested.

Upon hearing this, D still felt a bit uneasy.

Because this Miraidon clearly wasn't the one from the game, its nature likely wouldn't be as approachable.

But Professor Turo had already walked some distance, and D could only hurry to keep up.

The two of them followed the mountain path to the cliff, Miraidon turned around, watching as their distance grew closer.

To these humans' presence, Miraidon seemed to have already adapted.

Its gaze first fell on Professor Turo, then its lifeless eyes shifted subtly, silently transferring its focus to the unfamiliar human beside Professor Turo.

Instantly, Miraidon's demeanor became dangerous.

Because it sensed a completely different, hazardous energy from this human!

Miraidon narrowed its eyes slightly, staying alert but still floating calmly, keeping its gaze fixed on the unfamiliar human, observing his movements.

"It's watching me... Professor." D also noticed Miraidon's gaze.

Professor Turo: "Don't worry, it's just a bit cautious because it's seeing you for the first time."

With that, Professor Turo looked up at Miraidon, greeting it:

"Hello, Miraidon, are you getting used to life here? This is my friend D, he's a very skilled trainer."

Miraidon did not respond.

Seeing this, D also greeted, cooperating with Professor Turo: "Hello, Miraidon! Nice to meet you!"

As if recognizing the unthreatening nature of these humans, Miraidon's tense demeanor gradually relaxed.

—Weak humans pose no threat to it and won't compete for territory.

After some observation, Miraidon concluded and slightly opened its mouth:

"Meow... Mew."

Upon hearing this soft and cute sound, D's eyes widened immediately.

Did he... did he hear it wrong?

Is this Miraidon's growl?

So cute!

Noticing D's expression, Professor Turo felt very pleased, "See? Compared to Koraidon from ancient times, Miraidon living in the future actually has more charm, right?"

D: "..."

This question... he couldn't answer!

Both Koraidon and Miraidon have their own adorable aspects.

"It's a pity!" Professor Turo sighed, "Sada always argues with me over this, I think future Pokemon have more charm but she prefers ancient wild Pokemon. Sometimes she even sends me to sleep in the study because of this!"

D was taken aback, was this something he should hear?

Professor Turo laughed, at present Miraidon seemed to have accepted D's presence.

Just as he intended to take the next step, allowing Miraidon to spend more time with D, a roar suddenly rang out from the entrance of this Eco Garden.

"Aga-Tzzz!"

D and Turo turned their heads and saw Professor Sada arriving with Koraidon and Flutter Mane.

Professor Sada looked helpless, "This kid heard D was missing and insisted on coming to find him, I simply couldn't stop it."

Koraidon stared intently at Miraidon, and in the next moment, its wings expanded abruptly and it stamped the ground hard, using the momentum to fly towards D.

Miraidon was also drawn to the Koraidon it had never seen before.

It turned around to stare at it, though its expression remained calm, the sudden emergence of electricity from its body indicated Miraidon's inner alertness.

It felt a powerful threat and had entered a state ready for battle at any moment!

"Calm down! Miraidon! It's not an enemy!" Professor Turo immediately tried to soothe Miraidon's emotions.

Koraidon landed in front of D, shielding him behind, its golden eyes fixed on Miraidon, issuing a warning growl: "Grr-Awoo!"

It was because of Miraidon that it fell into the Time Machine, forced to separate from D for so many years!

Towards such deceitful Pokemon, Koraidon felt no goodwill.

Was Koraidon going to fight Miraidon? D's heart tightened, he hurriedly shouted, "Koraidon!"

However, the next moment, Miraidon had already floated to a higher position.

It was choosing a favorable battle location!

Seeing this, Koraidon no longer hesitated, its instincts honed in ancient times erupted furiously, charging fiercely towards Miraidon.

In an instant, Miraidon released a terrifying burst of electricity, the sparks enveloped the entire Eco Garden.

The dense electricity surged towards Koraidon, but Koraidon's eyes locked on Miraidon, it almost rudely ignored Miraidon's electricity and directly reached Miraidon's side, punching it hard.

Struck heavily, Miraidon's electricity faltered momentarily, its body was knocked into the valley, creating a large crater on the ground.

However, Miraidon did not lose its fighting ability.

It flew out fiercely from the scattered dust, its originally blue eyes turned crimson, a powerful current surged from within.

Miraidon's gaze locked onto the Koraidon in front, changing its form while trying to pierce its opponent with dazzling electricity.

Facing such a terrifying attack, Koraidon seemed undeterred, it began charging towards Miraidon.

As they got close, Koraidon suddenly extended its claws, in Professor Sada and Professor Turo's shocked gaze, it ignored Miraidon's electricity, grabbed Miraidon, interrupting its attack!

Then, it punched Miraidon's head, knocking it roughly back.

In those short tens of seconds, too many things happened, neither D nor Professor Turo had time to stop them.

At this moment, looking at the downed Miraidon, Professor Sada couldn't help but widen her eyes, "How is this possible?!"

D was also shocked by this scene.

This Koraidon of his... is it really this strong?

Below, Miraidon seemed to realize it couldn't defeat this Koraidon.

It showed fear, curling its body, instinctively retreating and hiding as Koraidon approached step by step.

Observing this, D hurriedly shouted while running towards Koraidon, "Koraidon! Stop!"

Meanwhile, Professor Turo ran towards Miraidon, clutching a Poke Ball, promptly returning Miraidon inside.

---

---

---

---

# 304: Chapter 301: The Best Koraidon in the World

"Do you dislike that Miraidon?"

In the medical room, Koraidon lay on the ground, showing the scars on its body caused by Miraidon's lightning.

The on-duty doctor handed over the Super Potion for treating electrical burns. D reached out to take it, opened the cap, and sprayed it on Koraidon's wounds.

Koraidon drooped its head, looking dispirited after just being scolded by D.

"Ah—gah ss..."

Seeing Koraidon like this, D's heart softened, and he sighed helplessly:

"Alright then, since you don't like it, I'll reject Professor Turo's plan."

"Ah—gah ss?" Koraidon's eyes lit up instantly.

"Really." D nodded: "Were you just worried that Miraidon would harm me, so you stood in front of it?"

Koraidon honestly nodded its big head.

"I see..." D reached out to touch Koraidon's face, apologizing: "Sorry, I misunderstood you. I shouldn't have scolded you just now."

Koraidon nuzzled D's palm, its expression visibly brightening.

"Ah—gah ss!" It doesn't mind!

D felt touched, sitting on the floor hugging Koraidon's head, letting it lean against him.

Koraidon—this is his Koraidon, the best Koraidon in the world.

Things that Koraidon doesn't like, he won't do.

At this moment, D completely abandoned the idea of contacting Miraidon.

He looked at Flutter Mane watching silently not far away, and asked:

"Koraidon, is this your friend you met before?"

Koraidon turned to look at Flutter Mane, happily nodding its big head: "Ah—ga!"

D waved to Flutter Mane: "Hello, Flutter Mane, I'm D, nice to meet you for the first time!"

Flutter Mane knew this human's name, and at this moment, watching the happy Koraidon and the human, it couldn't help but smile.

Seeing this, D's eyes widened in disbelief.

Flutter Mane... is smiling?

Perhaps sensing his surprised gaze, Flutter Mane's smile vanished instantly, and it turned, gradually sinking into the shadows.

D asked Koraidon in shock: "Did it leave?"

Koraidon shook its head: "Ah—ga!"

No, Flutter Mane agreed to live together in the garden! So it will silently tag along!

"That's good." D breathed a sigh of relief: "Since we've successfully found Flutter Mane, let's leave later!"

With this, they can return to the garden before nightfall.

Koraidon happily agreed.

Meanwhile, in the neighboring research institute.

Professor Sada and Professor Turo were discussing the reason for Miraidon's loss of control.

"Even if it's a Pokemon from the future, it still has territorial instincts within its body, and they will fight with creatures that invade their territory. Could this instinct be the reason for Miraidon's loss of control?" Professor Sada speculated.

Koraidon suddenly barged in, putting Miraidon on high alert.

At such times, if Koraidon showed even a bit of hostility, Miraidon would instantly release lightning to drive away the opponent.

And Koraidon indeed did so.

It seems to have mistaken that Miraidon was harming D, which is why the two Pokemon fought each other.

Thinking of this, Professor Turo frowned: "You shouldn't have brought Koraidon over."

There is not only Miraidon but also Koraidon in the research institute, so to prevent conflict between the two Pokemon, they had intentionally kept them separated.

Being criticized like this, Professor Sada felt a bit agitated: "That Koraidon followed D's scent here by itself, and if I didn't open the door for it, it would have smashed the door on its own, potentially destroying the entire Eco Garden."

Professor Turo: "..."

He paused for a moment, then spoke: "I was careless."

Seeing his wife's anger yet undiminished, Professor Turo continued: "I'll go sleep in the study tonight."

Professor Sada suddenly felt somewhat exasperated.

She turned around, heading out while saying: "D should be almost done."

Professor Turo immediately followed.

As the two arrived at the medical room, D just happened to be coming out with Koraidon.

Seeing the two professors, D apologized for Koraidon's provocation: "Sorry, Professors."

"This was our oversight, you don't need to apologize." Professor Sada shook her head.

D then turned his gaze to Professor Turo: "Professor, let's forget about the contact with Miraidon!"

Professor Turo was slightly taken aback, then asked: "Did something happen?"

D shook his head: "Because Koraidon doesn't like it, having one Koraidon is enough for me."

Professor Turo felt regretful: "Alright, then I'll think about it myself later."

D nodded, then rode on Koraidon: "Well, it's getting late, we should return to the garden. We'll visit again if there's an opportunity."

"Alright." Professor Sada nodded slightly: "Goodbye D, goodbye, Koraidon!"

"Ah—gah ss!"

Koraidon responded, then suddenly unfolded its wings, carrying D, flying straight into the sky.

The surroundings rapidly fell away.

D tightly hugged Koraidon's neck while looking down, and sure enough saw Flutter Mane catching up at high speed from below.

Seeing this, D suddenly felt curious.

Would this pair of good friends hunt together in ancient times?

If they hunted together, according to modern Pokemon terminology, wouldn't they be engaging in Double Battles?

Koraidon's ability "Scarlet Pulse" could just trigger Flutter Mane's ability "Ancient Activation."

Considering the power Koraidon has shown, wouldn't they be invincible in ancient times?

Even other wild Koraidons wouldn't be a match for them.

Because Flutter Mane's Ghost and Fairy Type counter Koraidon's Fighting and Dragon Type.

Thinking about this, D couldn't help but wonder how these two Pokemon became friends.

Gradually, Koraidon passed through the layers of clouds, flying out of the Paldea Giant Pit.

Immediately, the vast Paldea land spread out before them like a painting.

Koraidon deliberately slowed down, waiting for Flutter Mane to catch up.

Flutter Mane flew while looking down at the scenery below in a daze.

This place... is completely different from where it used to live!

Here there are no tall ancient trees, deep vines, nor giant fern plants.

Instead, there's just an expansive open view, stretching endlessly.

D noticed Flutter Mane's expression and smiled at it:

"Welcome to our era, Flutter Mane."

# 305: Chapter 302: Heroic Expedition Team and Zapdos

As D rode Koraidon back to the ranch, the sky had already darkened.

The house was unlit, but the Pokemon Tavern was bright with lights.

D guessed Pawmot and the others felt bored, so they all went there to chat with other Pokemon.

After a day of running around and not yet having dinner, D didn't bother them and instead took Koraidon and Flutter Mane back to the living room.

But when he changed his shoes and was about to turn around to greet Flutter Mane, he found it had already disappeared.

D paused for a second, asked: "Did you see where Flutter Mane went?"

"Ah gaa hiss!?"

Hearing the question, Koraidon also turned its head to look around, clearly just realizing Flutter Mane was gone too.

D: "..."

This Pokemon is really something.

Forget it, I'll wait for it to come out by itself.

But just as D was about to go make dinner, he suddenly remembered Flutter Mane's identity.

Flutter Mane, a Paradox Pokemon from ancient times, is aggressive and warlike, knowing very little about the modern world...

So, could it have gone out hunting?

D was shocked, after all, he had once seen Roaring Moon, also a Paradox Pokemon, prey on other Pokemon!

This is serious, the Pokemon in the ranch are in danger!

D hurriedly ran out the door without bothering to change shoes.

But just then, Koraidon suddenly looked in the direction of the darkness.

"Ah gaa?"

D turned his head and saw Flutter Mane secretly watching the stable from the shadows.

There, Glastrier and Spectrier had their heads buried in the food trough, eating dinner.

Seeing this scene, D immediately breathed a sigh of relief.

Luckily... nothing went terribly wrong.

"Flutter Mane, come over for dinner!" D called.

Hearing the voice, Flutter Mane turned around and silently floated over.

D brought it into the living room, intending to get some food for it as a snack first.

Flutter Mane looked around, observing the situation here.

Koraidon cheerfully introduced: "Ah gaa hiss!"

D chuckled, then opened the refrigerator, taking out fresh steaks, milk, and Berries.

"Flutter Mane, come eat this!"

Flutter Mane turned to look at him, then floated over.

Its gaze moved between the Berries and steaks before opening its mouth, revealing sharp fangs, biting into the raw steak.

D: "???"

D was startled, vigorously shaking it, trying to pull out the inedible raw meat.

Seeing this, Flutter Mane was puzzled, wasn't it food meant for it?

But recalling what Koraidon said, it slightly relaxed its mouth.

The next second, D fell onto the floor with a thud, groaning in pain.

Flutter Mane watched this amusing scene, chuckling "hoo hoo" from its mouth.

D looked dazed, was Flutter Mane finding his fall amusing?

No wonder it's an ancient Misdreavus known for teasing!

Seeing Flutter Mane's happy expression, D couldn't help but laugh too.

He stood up, rubbing his behind, then told Flutter Mane: "You can't eat this, I'll cook it first before you can eat. For now, eat this."

Saying so, D handed the Berry to Flutter Mane.

Flutter Mane lowered its head to look at the Berry, then with its wing-like hand, picked up the Berry and tossed it into its mouth, chewing big bites.

"Definitely wild!"

Flutter Mane, over a meter tall, is much bigger than a normal Misdreavus.

D called over Koraidon and whispered to it: "Go introduce Flutter Mane to our ranch, tell it I'll provide food in the future, no need to hunt, and remind it not to harm the Pokemon in the ranch!"

"Ah gaa hiss!"

Koraidon happily agreed and took Flutter Mane aside to explain the situation.

Meanwhile, D officially started preparing dinner.

Heat the oil, wait for the temperature to rise before putting the steaks in to fry.

Soon, a special aroma filled the room.

Never having smelled such a scent, Flutter Mane was immediately drawn to it.

It and Koraidon followed the aroma over.

"Gululu!"

The two Pokemon's stomachs began to growl.

D smiled and placed two steaks on plates, then set them in front of Koraidon and Flutter Mane: "Eat up!"

Koraidon immediately buried its head into the plate.

While Flutter Mane stretched out its winged claw, grabbed the large steak and stuffed it into its mouth.

The steak was fried crisp and fragrant, full and juicy, biting into it, Flutter Mane immediately felt the rich flavorful juice burst in its mouth.

It had never eaten such delicious food before, its blood-red eyes instantly brightened.

"Misdreavus!"

D smiled, looking at the two ancient Pokemon enjoying their meal.

"Eat up! If you need more, I'll make more for you!"

*

Meanwhile, in the Kanto Region, north of Lavender Town, an abandoned power plant without humans.

After traversing mountains and waters, Mew and Teddiursa returned here once again.

Deep inside the power plant, a Bird-type Pokemon with yellow feathers was resting atop a large power machine.

It was a Bird-type Pokemon surrounded by black ring-shaped feathers around its eyes, with a long green sharp beak, wings and tail covered in numerous spiky yellow feathers.

Suddenly, the Bird-type Pokemon seemed to sense something and abruptly opened its eyes.

The next moment, it saw a Mew and Teddiursa floating in front of it.

Seeing these two Pokemon, Zapdos let out a clear birdsong: "Chirp?" (Why did you come here?)

Mew smiled softly, "Miu!" (We'd like to invite you to live with us in our hometown!)

Beside it, Teddiursa nodded repeatedly, its eyes dark and bright, revealing a happy glow.

"Yeaee!" (That's right! In our hometown! If you come with us, no humans will ever disturb you again!)

Zapdos didn't expect this, it had always liked a quiet life, which is why it came to this human-abandoned power plant.

This place still retained a trace of power, which it loves.

But this quiet life only lasted until not long ago.

One day, a human accidentally stumbled into this place, trying to capture it.

At that time, it was furious, unleashed lightning, driving the human away.

Zapdos thought that would end it, but not long after, more humans arrived trying to capture it.

Among them were some powerful Human Trainers, it was even injured during battles.

It was then it met this duo calling themselves the Heroic Expedition Team.

They helped it, bringing healing Berries.

Zapdos' thoughts wandered, its face showing surprise as it inquired uncertainly: "Chirp?" (Your hometown?)

Teddiursa nodded, and as it spoke, gestured with its little bear paws:

"Yeaee yeaee!" (Yes! There's a World Tree there, D says the World Tree will grow very large, bigger than mountains! If you live there, no one will bother you! So, we want to invite you to live with us on the World Tree!)

# 306: Chapter 303: Good Friends, Pawmot vs. Flutter Mane!

"Chirp?" (World Tree?)

What is that? Zapdos had never heard of it.

For a Pokémon like it, changing territories was just part of life.

So far, it had changed homes thirty-eight times, and the only place it felt satisfied with was this abandoned power plant.

Now, Mew and Teddiursa wanted to invite it to live in their hometown, leaving Zapdos quite surprised.

Teddiursa nodded: "Uma! Uma Uma!" (That's right! It's the World Tree! It's a really nice place! Calyrex likes it too!)

Zapdos was even more puzzled: "Chirp?" (Calyrex? Is it also a Pokémon? Is World Tree a tree? But how can a tree be as big as a mountain?)

Faced with Zapdos's line of questioning, Teddiursa was at a loss for where to start explaining.

Mew, however, picked up the thread: "Miu!" (Calyrex is a Pokémon that used to be a human king! The World Tree is of course a tree! I hadn't seen it before either, but it's there! You'll know once you see it!)

Hearing this, Zapdos opened its beak in surprise, and deep inside, a sense of curiosity began to take hold.

Moreover, this place was becoming less peaceful, with humans bothering it almost every other day.

Zapdos was tired of this kind of life, and Mew and Teddiursa's proposal was appealing to it.

But as far as it believed, powerful Pokémon often disliked intruders on their turf, just as it disliked humans disturbing it in the power plant.

Thinking of this, Zapdos couldn't help but ask: "Chirp?" (If I go, will Calyrex drive me away?)

Teddiursa and Mew hadn't expected this question, and they exchanged glances before both shaking their heads.

"Uma!" (Of course not! Calyrex is a very kind Pokémon!)

Upon hearing this, Zapdos fell into contemplation.

Gazing at the two hopeful Pokémon before it, Zapdos couldn't help but ask one final question.

"Chirp?" (Why did you come to invite me to live on that tree?)

Mew answered without hesitation: "Miu!" (Because then we can all play together forever! Never apart again!)

Teddiursa nodded its little head: "Uma!" (That's right! Zapdos, you're our good friend too! We want to always play with you, never to part again!)

Good friends...

These three words hit Zapdos deeply.

It stared blankly at Teddiursa and Mew, their eyes were so sincere.

Zapdos felt its face growing warm, blushing at a visible speed.

It turned its head, closed its eyes, and gave its response: "Chirp." (In that case, I'll agree to your request.)

Upon hearing this, Teddiursa and Mew's eyes immediately brightened.

"Miu!"

"Uma!"

Witnessing this, Zapdos felt a warmth in its heart.

Is this what having good friends feels like?

Zapdos agreed to their invitation, and next was to take it back to the ranch to see the World Tree.

Mew and Teddiursa thought carefully and then decided to return to the ranch with Zapdos to check if the World Tree had grown big enough for residents.

So, the two Pokémon climbed onto Zapdos's back.

Once they were seated, Zapdos flapped its wings, electricity surging as it bid farewell to the power plant where it had lived for so many years, carrying its two good friends towards the blue sky and their hometown.

With Zapdos's departure, the abandoned power plant returned to silence.

*

League Year 199, February 9th, Spring, the ranch is bustling with life.

Dragonites who had come from Dragonite Island picked up MooMoo Milk ordered by guests from D, then flew towards Porto Marinada Town one after another.

Gogoats gathered by the farm, with green Grass Energy gushing from their bodies, flowing into the ground below.

Instantly, the whole farm was submerged in a sea of green energy.

Just ten minutes earlier, Dragonite had used Rain Dance over the farm.

At this moment, every plant in the field absorbed enough water and nutrients, flourishing under the Gogoats' Grass Energy.

Miltanks moved to an open grassland, energetically rolling around, seemingly exercising their bodies in this way.

In the living room, Calyrex sat on the sofa, watching a Pokémon anime called "Blossom's Dream" on TV.

Sinistea and Polteageist floated beside it, watching the TV together.

Since encountering the TV, this newfound novelty, Calyrex had developed a strong interest in this box that could play animations, becoming engrossed in watching TV.

Interestingly, Calyrex didn't like watching human news programs or culinary shows, nor did it enjoy Pokémon documentaries, preferring to watch animations about humans and Pokémon living in harmony and becoming friends.

Calyrex watched intently, enthralled and unblinking, not even noticing when the tea on the coffee table went cold.

By the fireplace not far away, Pawmot, Alcremie, Glaceon, and Dolliv were surrounding Flutter Mane, who was snoring on a cushion.

---

Watching this newly arrived Pokémon at the ranch, Pawmot scratched its head in bewilderment, "Pamo..." (Why is it sleeping during the day...)

"Maaa." Alcremie shook its head, indicating it didn't know either.

Looking at this giant Pokémon, Alcremie was quite amazed, "Maaa!" (It's so big!)

It looked even bigger than the Naclstack!

Glaceon remained silent, vaguely sensing a sense of wildness from this Pokémon.

All of a sudden, Flutter Mane seemed to sense something while asleep, suddenly opened its eyes, and locked gaze with Pawmot in front of it.

"Pamo!"

Pawmot was so startled it fell onto the wooden floor with a thud.

Flutter Mane's eyes were blood red, looking quite frightening.

With Flutter Mane staring at it, Glaceon's fur stood on end in fear, and it quickly hid behind Pawmot with Alcremie and Dolliv, cautiously peeking out at Flutter Mane.

Flutter Mane's eyes were half-open, half-closed, looking as though it hadn't fully awakened. It yawningly glanced at this group of Pokémon it had never seen before.

The several Pokémon stared at each other, with no one speaking, and the atmosphere quickly descended into silence.

Suddenly, Flutter Mane opened its mouth wide, revealing sharp little fangs, "Awuu!"

"Maaa!"

"Beep!"

"Pamo!"

"Doll!"

The four Pokémon were taken aback, Glaceon's fur stood on end, Alcremie's cream flew up in fright, and Dolliv cowered, covering its eyes with its hands, trembling.

Pawmot swallowed hard, summoning up the courage to stand in front of Alcremie and the others, gripping its little hammer tightly, looking at the larger Flutter Mane.

It couldn't run away; it was Pawmot, the aspiring superhero, set on protecting everyone!

Being stared at by Pawmot like this, Flutter Mane suddenly showed interest, its wild and belligerent nature seemingly provoked, wanting to spar with Pawmot.

Just then, the door to the room was pushed open, and D entered with Koraidon and Cyclizar.

"...What are you guys up to?"

Seeing D, Alcremie and the others quickly hid behind him.

Flutter Mane, full of battle intent, looked at Pawmot and made challenging sounds.

Pawmot was slightly stunned; this Pokémon seemed to want to have a battle with it?

Pawmot didn't back down because it was no longer the weak Pawmi it used to be!

Thinking this, it gripped its little hammer tightly, then loudly agreed, "Pamo!"

Seeing this, Koraidon sighed helplessly, "Ahh-sigh..."

D asked uncertainly, "Do they want to battle?"

Koraidon nodded its large head, "Ahh-sigh."

"I see..." D originally thought Flutter Mane had lost control and was going to harm Alcremie and the others.

But as it turns out, Flutter Mane seemed more interested in Pawmot, wanting to spar.

"What a scare." D breathed a sigh of relief, calling out to Pawmot and Flutter Mane, "If it's sparring, let's take it outside! I'll be your referee!"

It would be a good opportunity to see Flutter Mane's strength.

Thus, D led a group of Pokémon to the field prepared for the Grass Trial beside the Gogoat pen.

D first had Koraidon explain the rules of battle in this era to Flutter Mane, stating that victory was achieved by rendering the opponent unable to battle, not by defeating them.

After listening, Flutter Mane nodded, acknowledging with a low "Mun."

"Alright then, get ready!" D stood at the referee's position, and once the two Pokémon were in place, he blew the whistle around his neck.

The whistle sounded, and the battle officially began.

Pawmot took the initiative, leaping swiftly into the air, swinging its Mjolnir towards Flutter Mane.

Flutter Mane's eyes gleamed with excitement. Rather than evading Pawmot's attack, it shouted in anticipation, charging to meet it.

Its body emitted a radiant, gem-like light.

"Is it using Power Gem?" D watched with surprise.

He had initially thought Flutter Mane would counter with Phantom Force, but was surprised to see it make no attempt to evade, choosing instead to confront Pawmot head-on.

Furthermore, this Flutter Mane seemed completely ignorant of Type Matchups, using the Rock-type Power Gem against Electric + Fighting Type Pawmot.

"No wonder it's an ancient Pokémon from the Primitive World!" D mused silently, noting its direct and aggressive battle style.

As he pondered, Pawmot and Flutter Mane collided mid-air.

Pawmot's hammer landed with a crash on Flutter Mane's head. The dense Electric Type Energy clashed with the light emitted by Flutter Mane, immediately triggering a massive explosion.

The two Pokémon were sent flying by the blast.

Flutter Mane winced in pain, but its eyes shone with even more excitement, filled with unwavering battle intent.

It let out a roar, waving the wing claws atop its head like a gigantic ape pounding its chest.

As the wing claws swung, a Shadow Ball formed spontaneously. Flutter Mane gripped the Shadow Ball with its wing claw, then hurled it toward Pawmot.

---

Pawmot swiftly flew upwards to dodge and simultaneously gathered electricity onto the hammer in its hands, then threw it towards Flutter Mane.

This time, Flutter Mane didn't stubbornly rush up; it mimicked the evasive flying.

The electrically charged hammer grazed past underneath it.

However, just as Flutter Mane thought it had dodged the attack, Pawmot grinned.

"Pamo..."

The next second, it suddenly extended its padded paw, and the thrown Mjolnir returned quickly as if attracted.

"That's..." Seeing this move, D's eyes lit up, when did Pawmot become so proficient with the hammer?

As a spectator, he could see clearly, but Flutter Mane hadn't noticed what was happening behind it.

Right after, it was struck heavily by the hammer from behind, simultaneously a strong surge of electricity emanated from Pawmot.

The currents covered its entire body, rushing fiercely towards Flutter Mane.

Under the frontal and rear assault of Spark + Mjolnir, Flutter Mane was instantly knocked down, losing its ability to battle.

Pawmot extended its padded paw, and the hammer that fell on the ground automatically flew back into its hand.

With the hammer gripped tightly in its hand, Pawmot looked down at Flutter Mane with fighting spirit, the bright red cape fluttered behind it in the wind, making it look majestically impressive.

Pawmot looked exceptionally handsome like this.

The Alcremie and Glaceon in the audience also had their attention completely drawn to Pawmot.

"Maa-maa!" Amazing!

D helplessly stepped forward, feeding Life Sheep Milk to Flutter Mane.

Compared to Pawmot, Flutter Mane's small stature is still too fragile.

If he remembered correctly, Flutter Mane's Special Attack, Special Defense, and Speed species strength were as high as 135, but its HP and physical defense were only 55.

Moreover, this Flutter Mane, coming from a faraway ancient time, treated itself like a macho warrior, in melee battles it couldn't possibly be a match for Pawmot.

Accompanied by the sheep milk entering its mouth, Flutter Mane gradually regained consciousness.

It slowly opened its eyes, the first thing it saw was D, then the electric Pokemon floating beside D.

"Feeling better?" D asked,

Beside him, Pawmot also looked at it with bright eyes: "Pamo?" (Are you alright?)

"Moth..."

Flutter Mane slightly opened its mouth, staring blankly at Pawmot.

This Pokemon is really powerful...

At this moment, Koraidon stepped forward, helplessly saying: "Aga-sii!" (I've told you before, you didn't believe, the Pokemon trained by D are all very strong!)

Flutter Mane's pupils dilated, it excitedly looked at D then turned to Koraidon: "Moth?" (Can I get stronger too?)

Koraidon nodded its big head, affirming: "Aga-sii." (As long as you listen to D.)

If it remembered correctly, this Flutter Mane had lost to many Pokemon in the past, even got chased out of territories by savage Pokemon, wandering around.

Under such circumstances, Flutter Mane couldn't possibly resist the temptation to become stronger.

Sure enough, the next second Koraidon saw Flutter Mane wide-eyed looking at D, its eyes filled with excitement:

"Moth!" (I'll listen, get stronger!)
