# 307: Chapter 304: Grassy Terrain Reinforced Again! The Invincible Regeneration Flow of Gogoat! (5k)

Seeing Flutter Mane's gaze directed at himself, D was slightly taken aback.

What did Koraidon say to it? Why is Flutter Mane looking at him with such excitement?

D turned his head to glance at Koraidon.

Koraidon innocently tilted its head: "Ah gah hiss~"

D raised his eyebrow, thinking Flutter Mane probably heard from Koraidon about how Pawmot became so strong because of him.

So, Flutter Mane wants to become stronger too and is looking at him like that.

"Do you want to become stronger?" D asked.

As it turned out, his assumption was correct.

This Flutter Mane from ancient times miraculously understood his meaning, nodding its head excitedly.

"Alright!" D took a deep breath, helping this Flutter Mane become stronger wasn't a difficult task.

For example, teaching it Type Matchups, changing its battle style from a body-hardening Berserker to a swift and explosive Mage lurking in the shadows.

From D's observation, it's clear this Flutter Mane can use Phantom Force.

In the recent battle, it could have used Phantom Force to hide itself and then attack from the darkness.

But instead of doing that, Flutter Mane used Power Gem to hit Pawmot, which was resisted by Pawmot.

Seeing D's agreement, Flutter Mane's eyes immediately lit up, happily flitting around in mid-air, flapping its wing claws.

D then took out an empty Poke Ball and said to it, "However, there's something else we need to do first."

Flutter Mane stopped and looked down, gazing over with a puzzled expression.

Next, it saw that red-and-white ball.

Yesterday, Koraidon told it that humans here use red-and-white balls to capture Pokemon, then live together and help them grow stronger.

Thinking about this, Flutter Mane instantly understood.

"Dream!"

Excitedly, it floated over and snatched the red-and-white ball from D with its wing claws.

Under D's surprised gaze, Flutter Mane fiddled with the Poke Ball using its wing claws, poking and prodding, accidentally pressing the button on the Poke Ball.

In the next moment, a beam of red light shot from the ball, enveloping Flutter Mane's body, and it was stored inside the ball.

The Poke Ball fell onto the grass, starting to shake vigorously.

Once, twice, thrice.

By the fourth shake, the movement slightly eased, seemingly as Flutter Mane gradually adapted to the space inside the Poke Ball.

With the red light on the ball fading, the Poke Ball entirely calmed down.

D: "..."

To be honest, he had never seen a Pokemon so eager to jump into a Poke Ball before.

But he supposed he successfully captured Flutter Mane?

As a Ghost Type Pokemon, Flutter Mane preferred to roam at night; the daytime was for it to regain strength and recuperate.

Thus, after the battle with Pawmot, Flutter Mane stayed obediently inside the Poke Ball, sleeping and did not come out.

"Alright, everyone go have fun!"

D waved his hand, and the gathered Pokemon scattered and ran off.

Then he began taking out tools to repair the fences in the pasture.

Over more than a year, some wooden fences were damaged by wind and rain, looking quite unsightly.

No need to ask Keri for such a small task; he could handle it alone.

The day quietly passed amidst his hammering.

After finishing dinner, D, having been busy all day, went directly to the bedroom and fell asleep on the bed.

At midnight, when the lights in the pasture were all extinguished and every Pokemon had entered dreamland, the Poke Ball placed by D on the bedside suddenly moved.

Having slept for an entire day, Flutter Mane energetically emerged from the Poke Ball.

With its blood-red eyes, it glanced around, looking at the familiar dark environment and thought: It's nighttime, hunting time.

Yet suddenly, Flutter Mane realized something.

Koraidon mentioned that here, there's no need to go out hunting; D would feed them.

But then it wondered, how did this human get those food items?

Could it be he goes out hunting as well?

But he's just a frail human; can he catch good prey?

Flutter Mane hovered in the dark, feeling conflicted.

A moment later, it floated by the bedside, looking at the sleeping D.

"Dream… Dream!" Flutter Mane tapped D's face with its wing claws, wanting to drag him out to hunt, wondering if it could help.

Before meeting Koraidon, Flutter Mane didn't have this kind of consciousness.

It was only after meeting Koraidon that it developed a sense of mutual support.

Previously, Koraidon often cooperated with it on hunting trips, as such times usually yielded more food.

Feeling something patting his face, D, bleary-eyed, opened his eyes.

The next second, he saw a pair of red eyes lurking in the darkness, staring at him.

"Oh my!"

D was startled and hurriedly switched on the bedside lamp, finally seeing the owner of those red eyes and he let out a long sigh of relief.

"It's you... scared me to death!"

Koraidon and Pawmot were roused by the noise, rubbing their sleepy eyes and glanced towards him.

---

"Bamomo?"

"Aga-sii?"

Flutter Mane's stomach made a rumbling noise, it looked down and then wanted to drag D out for a hunt together.

D was puzzled by Flutter Mane's actions, but upon hearing its stomach rumbling, immediately understood: "Hungry?"

"Moe?"

D flipped out of bed, led Flutter Mane to the living room, and took out a sandwich and a Berry from the fridge, along with a bottle of Berry Milk, placing them in front of Flutter Mane.

Since Flutter Mane was sleeping during dinner, D hadn't woken it up, but instead left food for it to eat once it woke up.

Flutter Mane looked at the food on the table that it had never seen before, first sniffing it, then its eyes lit up as it picked up the sandwich with its wing claw and began devouring it.

D watched with a smile: "Go ahead and eat!"

After finally waiting for Flutter Mane to finish eating, D thought about going back to his room for more sleep.

However, Flutter Mane remained full of energy.

Seeing that D seemed not to plan to go out hunting, it flew around the room on its own, curiously observing everything around.

During this time, it accidentally knocked things over, creating noises that woke up several Pokemons.

Koraidon: "Aga-sii..."

D: "..."

No way! Tomorrow morning he must start staying up with this Flutter Mane, to get its schedule aligned with theirs!

So when it came to early next morning, facing a drowsy Flutter Mane wanting to return to the Poke Ball to sleep, D with dark circles proposed a plan to help it become stronger.

Due to the lack of language gap between Paradox Pokemon and modern humans, Flutter Mane quickly understood what he meant.

It instantly lost all sleepiness, looking over with anticipation.

D: "To become strong, first you need to change your combat approach."

Combat approach? What is a combat approach? Flutter Mane didn't understand.

D continued: "However, before formally starting, I need to know the skills you currently master, come on! Show me!"

Koraidon added beside: "Aga-sii!" (The moves you use in battle!)

Flutter Mane understood at once, then began demonstrating.

D watched from the side, jotting down on the Pokemon Observation Notes about Flutter Mane, recording all its mastered moves.

[Name: Flutter Mane (Misdreavus' Paradox Species)

Type: Ghost, Fairy

Temper: Not as ferocious as imagined, but still retains wildness of ancient Pokemon species, is completely ignorant of modern things, eats raw meat like a beast, slightly combative (?)]

Hobby: Yet to be discovered

Favorite food: Grilled steak, Berry, Sandwich

Mastered moves: Confuse Ray, Astonish, Psybeam, Mean Look, Helping Hand, Parting Shot, Wish, Dazzling Gleam, Power Gem, Shadow Ball, Phantom Force, Mystical Fire, Psyshock]

Not until Flutter Mane finished its demonstration did D stop recording.

"Huh? This Flutter Mane has quite a lot of moves?" Seeing the content on the notes, D was a bit surprised.

Moreover, the moves Flutter Mane currently mastered have a wide coverage.

Ghost Type, Psychic Type, Fairy Type, Fire Type, and Rock Type.

Besides these, there's Confuse Ray with confusion effect, Wish that can restore health, Parting Shot that can greatly lower the opponent's attack and special attack, and the Helping Hand that can strengthen a teammate.

Picturing Flutter Mane skillfully using Helping Hand on Koraidon just now, D suddenly became interested.

Could it be that these two good friends were actually engaging in double battles in ancient times?

He turned to Koraidon and asked: "Did you guys used to hunt together?"

"Aga-sii!" Koraidon nodded its big head.

"Did Flutter Mane also use Helping Hand on you?" D was greatly astonished.

Koraidon nodded again.

"Wow!" D let out an exclamation.

If so, given Koraidon's power, plus Flutter Mane's type coverage, wouldn't these two Pokemons have been invincible in ancient times?

If... just if, Flutter Mane and Koraidon teamed up for ancient double battle trials in the pasture, would anyone pass?

He'd heard Geeta mention before, in the sister school of Paldea Academy established by the Unova League on the sea, Blueberry Academy, which specializes in double battles.

Where a Trainer commands two Pokemons simultaneously, engaging in 2v2 battles.

But in that scenario, no one would probably pass.

"Thinking too much is useless, now the most important thing is to teach Flutter Mane about Type Matchups and how to utilize its advantages in battles."

After a brief ponder, D spoke to Flutter Mane in plain and straightforward language:

"From my observation, your advantages lie in speed and special attack, relatively speaking, your stamina is somewhat lacking, so you mustn't engage in direct close combat with the opponent."

"Moe?" Flutter Mane listened with its mouth open, looking confused.

D: "…It means you mustn't, like yesterday, use your body to tackle Pawmot, in battles, under any circumstance, maintain distance from your opponent."

Flutter Mane understood now.

Seeing this, D continued: "So, learn to use your advantage, try using Phantom Force."

---

Upon hearing these words, Flutter Mane's surroundings were shrouded in shadows, and in the next moment, its body merged into the shadows, disappearing from sight.

D surveyed the surroundings, unable to spot Flutter Mane, but he knew it was still nearby, just invisible to him.

This is Phantom Force, disappearing into the shadows, then launching an attack at the opportune moment.

Sometimes, this move isn't just for offense; for a Pokemon like Flutter Mane, the functionality of Phantom Force far exceeds its power.

"See, like this, we can't see you. In battle, you need to learn to use this, appearing and disappearing like a true ghost, striking at the critical moment to launch a deadly attack on your opponent."

For example, choosing moves with advantages based on the opponent's type, such as Mystical Fire, Power Gem, Psyshock, or Shadow Ball, to launch an attack.

"Alright, come out!" D said.

Flutter Mane's form gradually materialized.

Even though it was expected, witnessing this scene firsthand, D still found it quite wondrous.

No wonder it's a Ghost-type Pokemon!

To adjust Flutter Mane's routine to match that of the others in the ranch, D specifically called over three tough and resilient Gogoats to serve as sparring partners for Flutter Mane.

After understanding D's intention, the three Gogoats unleashed all their energy, with a surge of dense Grass Energy sweeping out like a tide.

"Baa!"

In the blink of an eye, Grassy Terrain covered half of the ranch.

The fur on the Gogoats also began to emit a white glow, a special effect triggered by the Grassy Terrain activating their Grass Pelt trait.

Observing this scene, D compared the Grassy Terrain after consuming the Tree of Beginning Leaf with the previous Grassy Terrain.

"The effects seem more exaggerated." The grass formed by the Grass Energy looked incredibly real.

On the ground, a green energy glow emanated, and D clearly saw a continuous influx of green energy into the Gogoats.

At this moment, Flutter Mane used Phantom Force to attack one of the Gogoats.

A wound appeared on its body.

However, in the next second, the green glow from the Grassy Terrain surged into that Gogoat's body.

In the blink of an eye, the wound vanished.

Seeing this, D's eyes widened as he exclaimed, "Instant recovery? Such an exaggerated healing ability!"

It's well known that Grassy Terrain can restore stamina to Pokemon on the ground, but under normal circumstances, the amount restored is minimal.

But now, the healing effect of the Grassy Terrain was significantly enhanced.

Is it because the Gogoats have been eating the Tree of Beginning Leaf? Or is it influenced by the Leaf Cake?

D was shocked, yet somewhat regretful at the same time.

Because the recovery effect of Grassy Terrain also extends to the opponent's Pokemon.

This means, unless the Gogoats can one-hit KO the opponent's Pokemon, the battle would turn into a scene where you say, "Stop, you can't defeat them like this!"

Gradually, Flutter Mane and the Gogoats battled for three hundred rounds, with the Gogoats unscathed, while Flutter Mane was left panting with exhaustion.

In the end, it simply lay down on the ground and fell asleep immediately.

D: "..."

Helplessly, he could only return Flutter Mane to the Poke Ball.

The Gogoats then turned around, their eyes sparkling as they looked at him.

"Baa~"

D approached and patted each Gogoat's head, smiling as he said, "Such good kids, you've worked hard!"

The Gogoats enjoyed his touch, letting out happy growls: "Baa~"

What fabulous little darlings!

So well-behaved and reliable, Gogoats are just adorable!

D couldn't help but smile.

At that moment, he recalled how the Gogoats earlier released a massive amount of Grass Type Energy, covering the back mountain, giving him a bold idea.

Clearly, the Gogoats can control this Grass Type Energy.

So here's the question: Can they control the energy forming the Grassy Terrain to only affect their side, to prevent the opponent from recovering?

D felt this wasn't an impossible task, just that it required high demands on the Pokemon themselves.

For instance, the Pokemon's affinity and control over energy.

If they can reach a certain level of familiarity, reaching a peak state, then they could fully command these energies!

Thoughts swirled in D's mind as he continued to pat the Gogoat's head.

At that moment, the Gogoat he was rubbing suddenly let out a joyous growl: "Baa~"

D was slightly taken aback.

In the next second, he saw a surge of massive Grass Type Energy emerging from the Gogoat's body.

This energy instantly formed a vast area of Grassy Terrain.

Witnessing this, D was momentarily dazed and then looked at the Gogoat in bewilderment.

The Gogoat raised its head, meeting his gaze with clear, water-like eyes.

Its expression seemed to affirm something to him.

D looked at the Gogoat, then suddenly noticed his hand touching the Gogoat's horn.

At that moment, an unbelievable idea popped into his mind.

——Could it be that the Gogoat perceived everything he just thought of?

It's common knowledge that Gogoats can use their horns to sense human emotions.

Because of this, they don't need commands, running in perfect harmony with their rider, assisting humans for 5,000 years.

And now...

So, could it be?

D murmured, "What I just thought in my mind, can you accomplish it?"

The Gogoat nodded happily, looking quite pleased: "Baa~!"

---

---

---

---

# 308: Chapter 305: Special Potato Stew, Leon Is Riding Here (4k)

"Hiss!"

D instantly drew in a sharp breath.

To be able to control Grassy Terrain to affect only you and not your opponent, what does this mean?

It means that if the opponent's strength is not up to par, the damage dealt may not even match the amount restored to Gogoat and the others by Grassy Terrain!

Truly, the blows dealt are not as much as the recovery!

What shocked D even more was that without a word, his three Gogoat had achieved such mastery over Grass Type Energy?

Was it because of the feedback from the World Tree, greatly enhancing their affinity with Grass Type Energy, or was it the enhancement brought about by the previous Leaf Cake?

Or perhaps, was it a mutation caused by combining the two with the Grassy Seed?

Whatever the case, it was truly astonishing.

This Gogoat was terrifying!

And it wasn't just one.

D glanced at the other two Gogoat and asked, "Can you do it too?"

"Meh~!"

The two Gogoat gave a definite answer.

"Amazing!" D exclaimed.

With things as they were progressing, could anyone actually pass the Grass Trial?

With the infinite healing from Grassy Terrain, the Super Vinewhip thick as a python that could attack or create obstacles to interfere with the opponent, not to mention the ultimate move like Grass Sacred Sword.

In addition, combining Synthesis with Growth to enhance attack and special attack, D didn't know how to lose under the League's battle rules.

The only situation he could lose would be if the opponent possessed Pokemon of the same level that had a type advantage over Gogoat.

Those new trainers just entering Paldea Academy could never pass.

So, what if an Elite or Champion-level Trainer came to challenge?

Unfortunately, there weren't any Elite or Champion-level trainers visiting the farm yet.

Geeta just barely tasted the trial, and as for Rika, she had no intention to challenge it and hurried back to the League Headquarters to work overtime.

Of course, perhaps this was because the workload given by the Paldea League was too busy.

Flutter Mane, exhausted, fell asleep, and D returned its Poke Ball to the room.

For Ghost Type Pokemon like this, being active at night and dormant during the day was their lifestyle, which would be hard to change in the short term.

However, this was only Flutter Mane's second day at the farm. They still had plenty of time to gradually adjust to each other.

In the room, Calyrex was happily watching TV.

In front of it were a glass of Berry Juice and some sweet treats, looking very leisurely and at ease.

Seeing this, D was momentarily speechless.

It seemed that Calyrex was becoming a laid-back Pokemon under his care.

But on second thought, Calyrex must have been quite lonely during its days wandering the Crown Tundra, right?

D was very pleased that Calyrex could find new hobbies on the farm, so it wouldn't feel bored or lonely and could amuse itself by watching TV.

Caring for the lonely elderly is everyone's responsibility!

Thinking of this, D smiled slightly, then headed to the fields to check on the crops' growth.

Indeed, the effect of the Leaf Cake in shortening the crop lifecycle was not a joke.

In just a short half-month, some crops were already mature.

Seeing the potato stems and leaves beginning to yellow and the potatoes poking their heads out of the soft soil, D knew this plot could be harvested.

He immediately brought over a small cart to start harvesting these potatoes.

Seeing this, the Pawmot with its red cape flew over wanting to help.

It landed beside a potato, clutching the stem with its small paws, attempting to pull it out.

However, its strength was too much, and instead of succeeding, it snapped the exposed stem.

"Bamow?"

Pawmot looked puzzled.

"That's not how you pull it out," D chuckled as he stepped forward, using a hoe to dig around the soil of the potato plot and then dig out the roots.

Under D's operation, a string of connected, intact potatoes was dug out from the soil.

After shaking off the excess dirt and roots from the potatoes, only a string of potatoes of varying sizes remained.

Witnessing this process, a small light bulb lit up over Pawmot's head.

"Bamow!" It understood!

Just as Pawmot was about to use its paws to dig out the potatoes, D stopped it.

"I can handle this; you go play with Glaceon and the others!"

As he spoke, D looked up at the distant grassland, seeing Alcremie once again riding the Naclstack for fun.

Listening to the exclamations coming from the direction, Glaceon and Dolliv had also been hoisted onto the Naclstack's back.

"Go on!" D patted Pawmot's little head with his clean hand.

He thought about how cleaning Pawmot's fluffy pink fur from dirt would be too troublesome.

"Bamow~"

Pawmot gleefully ran off.

Watching the Pokemon playing on the grassland, D couldn't help but wear a smile.

Seeing everyone so happy living here, his heart was filled with motivation.

This is what life is all about!

Though it lacks the thrilling adventures and those touching moments of Pokemon evolving to protect their Trainer like in Ash's experiences.

---

Ordinary and plain is the truth, at this moment, D completely agreed with Larry's point of view.

However, just as he squatted down to dig out the next potato, he suddenly noticed something different about this potato.

In an instant, D determined that this potato had mutated!

[Good Potato: A special ingredient, a crop mutated after being nurtured by Gogoat's Grass Energy. Dishes made from it will have the "Golden Right Hand" effect.]

"Golden Right Hand?" D muttered as he stared at the bunch of potatoes in his hand, "What is this?"

After a brief moment of thought, D had a plan in mind.

Decided! He would use these potatoes to make a "Potato Stew" for lunch today!

Once decided, D finished harvesting the potatoes from the field, and as time went by, it was already noon.

He washed his hands, took the bunch of potatoes to the kitchen, and grabbed some meat he had just bought from Porto Marinada Town yesterday from the fridge.

He chopped the meat and potatoes into chunks, then added various spices and put them into a large pot to stew.

As time ticked by, the bubbling sound inside the pot grew, and D lifted the lid, releasing a rich aroma of meat.

The thick broth was bubbling continuously, with the soft and flavorful potato chunks rolling inside. D used a spoon to serve a small bowl of soup, a piece of potato, and a piece of meat.

After drinking, he felt the soup was simply a delicacy of the human world, perfectly blending the rich fragrance of potatoes with the aroma of meat.

The slow-cooked, tender potatoes and meat melted in the mouth, needing no vigorous chewing, making people want more after one bite.

Simply perfect! His culinary skills had improved again over the years!

The aroma of the Potato Stew drifted out of the window into the pasture, and it wasn't long before a few meat-loving Pokemon, who were playing outside, were drawn in by the scent.

"Graaargh!"

"Grraawl?"

"Howl oww?"

Koraidon, Cyclizar, Dragonite, working Dragonites, and several Dragonairs all flew over, gulping down their saliva while eagerly watching D.

Seeing this, D couldn't help but laugh and said, "Just wait a moment!"

He knew it, which is why he made a big pot.

There were a lot of Pokemon in the pasture, and the pots at his home were specially customized giant stew pots that could meet all the Pokemon's needs at once.

Paired with Poffins, sandwiches, and biscuits, he ensured every Pokemon could eat to its heart's content.

The Indeedee Sibling brought over the Pokemon bowls specially used for feeding each Pokemon.

D filled each bowl with soup and then placed them on the table for the Pokemon to drink.

After finishing everything, he finally had time to check the effect of the Potato Stew.

[Potato Stew (S): A delicious dish made by stewing freshly harvested Good Potato and meat on high heat. Drinking it fills one with warmth, making it a popular dish in cold regions.

Cooking Effect: Golden Right Hand, "Good Pokemon, bad guys got chased away!", after consuming, possesses the Golden Right Hand ability. When you place your right hand on the forehead of a frenzied and angry Pokemon and say this phrase, it can calm their emotions.

Note: Only humans can activate this ability.

Rating: A delectable Potato Stew, the best choice for cold winter days, highly recommended.]

D: "???"

Looking at the effect of this bowl of Potato Stew, D was a bit dumbfounded.

What about Nangong Wenya?

This ability is so awkward, right?

Not only do you have to put your hand on a Pokemon's forehead, but also make an adult say such embarrassing words...

For a moment, D didn't know what to say.

However, from a certain perspective, this ability paired with Translation Nuts Cake can even compare to the Triumphant Light granted to humans by Arceus.

In some situations, its utility even surpasses the Triumphant Light.

But one thing it can't compare with is that this ability requires physical contact with the target Pokemon.

D couldn't help but think about the scene in the movie "Arceus and the Jewel of Life" where the angry Arceus wanted to punish humans.

Would this Golden Right Hand work on Arceus?

D didn't know, but in that situation, he probably couldn't even get close to Arceus.

If it doesn't work on Arceus, how about Giratina?

D thought of his good neighbor.

So he gulped down the bowl of Potato Stew along with the meat and soup and then took some Poffins made for the Dragonites to visit that friendly neighbor.

When he reached the central lake of the pasture, a purple vortex slowly appeared there.

D walked in and arrived at the Reversed World where Giratina resided.

As expected, just as he anticipated, Giratina was enjoying sun and the fragrance of flowers in that flower field.

When D reached Flower Island, Giratina opened a slit of its eyes and gave him a glance.

D opened the picnic basket, spread the picnic cloth on the "flower cake" that had been flattened by the bad dragon, and then poured the Poffins on it.

Giratina snorted two breaths through its nose and began to eat the offerings in front of it without ceremony.

---

D was observing Giratina, wanting to test the ability of his Golden Right Hand on it.

He contemplated how he could naturally place his hand on Giratina's forehead.

Perhaps a trip and fall? Then pretend to "accidentally" put his hand on its forehead?

No, that would be too stupid.

At this moment, Giratina noticed his gaze and gruffly asked, "Human, what are you doing?"

D couldn't think of a better plan, so he honestly revealed his ability:

"... To be frank, I've just learned a new move that can calm irrational Pokemon, so I wanted to ask you for a favor."

As an ancient creature existing for a long time in this world, Giratina's temper wasn't great, but it was a clever dragon.

It instantly understood the meaning behind D's words; it initially thought of getting angry about being used as an experiment and teaching him a lesson.

But after thinking it over, the reason it could sleep peacefully in this wonderful place was all thanks to this human.

Even though it knew its temper wasn't the best, it wasn't a ungrateful dragon that repays kindness with evil. It despised such people and Pokemon greatly. If it encountered such a situation, it would definitely tear them apart!

Thinking about this, Giratina snorted and said, "Then I'll generously let you try it!"

"Thank you!"

D's eyes lit up, then with Giratina's cooperation, he placed his right hand on its forehead.

"Giratina, be good. The bad guys have been driven away!" He said with a slightly embarrassed face.

Giratina stared at him with its blood-red eyes, without reacting for a while.

"Huh?" Seeing this, D was a bit puzzled.

Giratina didn't seem to become foolish... Or is it because it isn't angry at the moment?

If that's the case, then the effect of the stewed potato and meat seems to only work on irrational Pokemon?

And it wouldn't affect emotionally stable, non-angry Pokemon.

Unfortunately, this means the "Golden Right Hand" doesn't work on Paradox Pokemon, and he can't forcibly enlighten them.

Because the aggressive nature of Paradox Pokemon is influenced by the environment; their brains are simple, focused only on hunting and territorial control, not on other complex matters. This state isn't equivalent to irrationality, nor is it an angry state.

In this case, using this ability won't make them docile, as it isn't mind control and can't implant the modern Pokemon's survival methods directly into their brains.

But this is still better than nothing. Next time if he encounters wild Pokemon fighting, he can use this ability to calm them down.

D withdrew his hand and said to the expressionless Giratina in front of him, "Thanks a lot!"

Giratina: "..."

Though there was no expression on its face, its heart was in turmoil.

This human... This human actually said such things!

Make it behave and say the bad guys have been driven away!

Giratina had a very clear self-awareness — the biggest bad guy here was probably itself, right?

Giratina widened its eyes and internally muttered: what a strange human...

*

Meanwhile, at the Paldea League Headquarters.

Leon, riding on Charizard, slowly descended in front of the huge building.

"This time, I shouldn't have gotten lost and ended up in the wrong place, right?" Leon thought.

He looked up at the sign on the building, which clearly read — Paldea League Headquarters.

Leon let out a sigh of relief.

Thankfully... after getting lost for half a month, he finally arrived at this place successfully.

Learning of the arrival of the Galar League Champion, Paldea's Chief Champion Geeta immediately came to greet him.

Looking at the young purple-haired man opposite, Geeta politely smiled and said, "Welcome to the Paldea Region, Mr. Leon. What brings you to the Paldea Region this time?"

Leon nodded, his mind recalled the Mysterious Trainer from the Paldea Region he met in a commercial tournament last year.

He hadn't encountered a trainer with such prowess for a long time, but unfortunately, the trainer was not interested in the competition and dropped out midway.

To spar with him, he embarked for the Paldea Region immediately after the New Year ended.

A trainer with Champion Level strength must be famous in the Paldea League.

Therefore, Leon nodded and said, "That's right! I came here to find a trainer to continue an unfinished match!"

Hearing this, Geeta couldn't help but be surprised and intrigued: "Oh? What kind of trainer makes Mr. Leon come all the way to the Paldea Region?"

Leon: "He is a trainer with Champion Level strength. I wonder if he is working for the Paldea League."

The Paldea League has a trainer so strong it attracts the attention of a neighboring region's champion? Geeta was quite surprised but still politely asked:

"What is his name? If he works for the Paldea League, maybe I can help."

Leon grinned: "D! His name is D, and he has a Pokemon named Calyrex!"

# 309: Chapter 306: Flutter Mane's Survival Instincts, Leon's Arrival

"D?"

Upon hearing this name, Geeta thought she might have misheard. Was it the D she knew?

Seeing Geeta's expression, Leon knew she indeed recognized this person. "That's right, it's D!"

To avoid confusion, Leon added, "Besides Calyrex, he also has a very powerful red Pokemon by his side. Its name seems to be Koraidon? Both of them should be Pokemon unique to the Paldea Region, right?"

Geeta: "…"

Koraidon is indeed a Pokemon from the Paldea Region, but Calyrex is not.

She had previously heard D say that Calyrex used to live in the Galar Region, where people treated it as a king, but it was later forgotten over time.

And now, judging by Leon's expression, he seemingly had no idea that Calyrex is a Legendary Pokemon from the Galar Region?

Thinking about this, Geeta was momentarily at a loss for words.

After a brief moment of thought, she gradually sorted out her thoughts

—Last year, D went to the Galar Region and found the forgotten Calyrex. He captured it and then used Calyrex to participate in a competition.

Coincidentally, Leon was also there, but for some reason, D withdrew from the competition halfway through, leaving Leon, intrigued by the strong trainer, longing for a match with him.

So, he requested a visit through the Paldea League and came to the Paldea Region.

It's just…

"D is not a trainer of the Paldea League," Geeta frankly admitted. "He also doesn't work for the Paldea League."

Leon was stunned, "What?"

Geeta looked at the cheerful and straightforward young man in front of her and explained to him:

"D is currently running a ranch in a small countryside town in the Paldea Region. If you want to find him, I can tell you his exact location."

Hearing this, Leon seemed a bit dumbfounded.

Ranch, rancher?

Geeta was familiar with this feeling; she smiled gently and said, "I was also quite surprised when I first heard about this!"

"But later I learned that D used to travel to various regions and only returned to Paldea last spring. He then inherited his late grandfather's ranch and settled there with a group of Pokemon."

"No wonder!" Leon finally understood, making sense of it all.

Retired champion back to rural employment? Just like his mentor Mustard running a dojo on an island.

Living a new life, leisurely running a ranch with a group of Pokemon, sounds like a brilliant choice!

Given that, he decided to visit him there!

Seeing Leon's expression, Geeta guessed what was on his mind.

"Porto Marinada Town, D's ranch is in Porto Marinada Town. When you get there, just ask the locals, and they will guide you. After all, the D Ranch is quite famous there!" Geeta said.

"Great! Thanks a lot!" Leon laughed heartily, then stood up and walked toward the door.

Geeta saw him off to the door.

Leon took out a Poke Ball, and in a flash of white light, a robust, keen-eyed Charizard appeared in front of the League Headquarters.

Leon took out a map and meticulously checked the specific location of Porto Marinada Town on it.

Standing behind him, Geeta couldn't help but remind him, "… Mr. Leon, it seems your map is upside down."

"Ah? Oh!" Leon quickly turned the map right side up. After confirming the direction to Porto Marinada Town, he climbed onto Charizard's back, indicating the direction to it.

"Roar!"

Charizard let out a deep growl, then flapped its mighty wings and soared into the sky.

Geeta stood in the corridor watching Leon gradually disappear into the distance, suddenly understanding why Leon had arrived so late.

*

As time passed unknowingly, it was now nighttime. After a nap, Flutter Mane had fully regained its strength.

D was once again woken up from his sleep by it.

"Flutter Mane?"

He glanced at the clock. It was already 11 PM.

Koraidon and the others were still in a deep sleep, occasionally smacking their lips in satisfaction, dreaming of something pleasant.

With its bright red eyes, Flutter Mane gazed at the human in the dark, thinking: Shouldn't we go hunting tonight? Otherwise, all the food would be eaten up.

"Dream?"

D resignedly got out of bed, went to the kitchen, and grabbed some food from the refrigerator to feed Flutter Mane.

However, even after eating, Flutter Mane did not abandon its plan to go out.

It floated around the room, looking anxious.

D was somewhat bewildered and turned to look outside, asking, "Do you want to go out?"

Flutter Mane nodded, as its instincts drove it to hunt, lest they run out of food and go hungry.

Hunger would lead to weakness, and if other Pokemon took advantage of this moment, they could be in a precarious situation.

This was why Flutter Mane was so restless.

Seeing this, D fell into deep thought.

Perplexed by Flutter Mane's behavior, he deliberated for a while, then decided to go out and see what Flutter Mane wanted to do.

D opened the door and followed Flutter Mane outside.

---

The midnight ranch was silent, except for an occasional Bug Buzz from the grass, there was no other sound.

Moonlight poured down, illuminating the night so brightly, it wasn't as clear as daytime, but one could barely make out the surrounding things.

In the middle of the night, Glastrier had already fallen asleep, while Spectrier hadn't yet drifted off.

Three Gogoats lay in the shed, resting their heads on the soft grass and peacefully dreaming.

The Combees burrowed into the hive, dreaming sweet dreams in their sweetly scented wooden house.

Butterfree alighted on a branch of the World Tree, standing there, deeply asleep.

D followed behind Flutter Mane, watching as Flutter Mane looked around, then began to levitate outside the ranch.

He helplessly raised his feet to follow, one trainer and one Pokemon turned into a dense forest.

Flutter Mane levitated in the shadows, then glanced back baffled at D who walked boldly on the main road.

——How does this human catch so many prey? With this reckless running outside, the prey would all be scared away.

D finally couldn't help but ask: "What are you doing?"

At this moment, the grass rustled ahead.

Flutter Mane's attention was instantly captured, it turned its head, ready to start capturing prey.

Seeing Flutter Mane's action, D instantly understood its thoughts.

It was engaging in a hunting activity!

This was Flutter Mane's survival instinct! The ecological nature bred from its ancient species!

"No!"

Seeing Flutter Mane about to attack the Nymble emerging from the grass, D hurriedly stopped it.

The Nymble, startled by the sound, quickly used its super jumping ability to leap ten meters high, fleeing in panic.

The hunting action was prevented, Flutter Mane wasn't angry but looked over in puzzlement.

Seeing this, D asked: "Were you catching prey just now?"

"Dream." Flutter Mane nodded.

D shook his head and said: "No, you don't need to catch prey anymore, I told you before, I will raise you, which means I will provide food for you, and you will never need to hunt by yourself again."

Flutter Mane still felt puzzled.

D didn't explain further, but took Flutter Mane back to the ranch, to the living room, and opened the storeroom and refrigerator doors, showing it the food inside, piled like a mountain.

Looking at the heap of food, Flutter Mane widened its eyes in shock.

"Dream…!"

This human's hunting ability is amazing!

It looked at D in admiration.

D: "...It seems you still don't understand the meaning of Trainer and Pokemon."

Just then, a few Pokemon were awakened by their voices and came out.

D looked at them, explaining to Flutter Mane: "For Pokemon, a Trainer will be its most important companion, friend, even family throughout its life."

"After living with a Trainer, Pokemon no longer need to go out to get food because Trainers will prepare all kinds of delicacies to feed them, nurture them."

In the darkness, D looked into Flutter Mane's eyes and said to it: "You have become my Pokemon, so I will take care of you like that."

Flutter Mane listened, somewhat understanding, through Koraidon's introduction, it gained some understanding of this entirely different world these past two days.

Like this world has humans, and humans live with Pokemon.

But it still didn't understand, how can humans acquire prey without the ability to fight or hunt?

D roughly guessed Flutter Mane's meaning.

So he took it to outside the Berry Garden and farm: "Food comes from these places, as for meat, I will go to town and buy it with money."

D felt Flutter Mane might not understand these things, so he explained carefully and patiently:

"To be precise, it's an exchange with others, like if someone only has meat but needs berries, I can use these berries from the ground to exchange for meat, now do you understand?"

Flutter Mane only found these things very complicated, listening until it felt dizzy, at the end it understood nothing.

D: "..."

He took a deep breath and then said to Flutter Mane:

"Nothing else matters, you don't need to understand, everything is up to me."

"All you need to remember is that I am your Trainer, from now on I will provide prey for you."

"What you need to do is just sleep well and play happily with Koraidon! Understand?"

"Dream…"

Flutter Mane stared blankly at the human in front of it, still not understanding what a Trainer is.

Is a Trainer just a human who hunts for Pokemon? Then what are they for?

Flutter Mane couldn't understand, in its perception, humans were really strange creatures.

D noticed Flutter Mane's confusion, pondering for a moment before speaking to it under the moonlight:

"Because human Trainers like Pokemon and want to live with them, that's why they do these things."

Like? What is like?

D continued: "I don't know if you know what like is; for us humans, liking is being willing to do anything for the other party."

Upon hearing this, Flutter Mane's mind suddenly recalled the "ghost" that vanished to protect Koraidon when it first met Koraidon.

---

Does that ghost... also like Koraidon?

So, is it protecting Koraidon?

Flutter Mane began to ponder for the first time in its life.

At this moment, it felt like it understood a bit about what Trainers and Pokemon meant.

D was getting sleepy.

He yawned and waved his hand, "Alright, everyone go back to sleep!"

The Cyclizar group returned one after another, but when D turned to look at Flutter Mane, he saw it showed no intention of going back to sleep.

"..."

Forget it, let it be for now! I'll buy some coffee later to see if I can make some refreshing drink, and by then help Flutter Mane adjust to the same routine as everyone else.

To prevent any accidents, D spoke to Flutter Mane before leaving:

"Don't harm other Pokemon. If you're hungry, you can open the fridge and pantry to get food yourself. If you still don't understand, then use your eyes to see, okay?"

Saying that, for the first time, he reached out and patted Flutter Mane's head.

Flutter Mane stared at him blankly but didn't resist.

D thought about the Golden Right Hand ability he had acquired earlier in the day and activated it smoothly.

However, Flutter Mane did not react at all.

Seeing this, D thought to himself, "...Sure enough, this ability has no effect on Paradox Pokemon!"

He withdrew his hand, then bid farewell to Flutter Mane and returned to his room.

Flutter Mane bathed in the moonlight, silently watching the figure gradually fade into the distance, murmuring, "Dream..."

*

The next day, life on the ranch returned to its usual peace.

Today's weather was very good, it was a bright and sunny day, and after breakfast, D devoted himself to ranch work.

Meanwhile, in Porto Marinada Town, Leon and Charizard, guided by a fleet of Squawkabilly taxis, finally arrived at this seaside countryside town.

They encountered this Squawkabilly taxi fleet yesterday afternoon.

Upon meeting halfway, the enthusiastic driver greeted him, and upon learning Leon was unfamiliar with the area and also heading to Porto Marinada Town, immediately said Leon could join them.

Leon, aware of his poor sense of direction, to avoid getting lost again and to fight the Trainer as soon as possible, obediently stayed at the back of the fleet.

With the driver's guidance, he finally didn't get lost this time and successfully reached Porto Marinada Town.

Charizard breathed a sigh of relief.

After landing, Leon bid farewell to the driver, returned Charizard to the Poke Ball, and then found an uncle on the road to politely ask:

"Hello, uncle, could you please tell me which way to D Ranch?"

"D Ranch?" The uncle's face lit up with excitement upon hearing the name, "Are you here for the legend too?"

Leon was puzzled, "What legend?"

"Of course, the legend of The King of Bountiful Harvests!" The uncle joyfully began introducing the local legend of Porto Marinada Town to this outsider.

"It's said that D has a close relationship with the Legendary Pokemon Calyrex! The people in Porto Marinada Town worship Calyrex, all thanks to D!"

"Calyrex?" Leon was even more confused; he recalled the Pokemon with the big head he'd seen in competitions. Did it have some sort of legend?

Why do people here seem very respectful towards it?

"Oh come on! You don't know even this? Calyrex is quite famous in the Paldea Region recently!" said the uncle.

Leon: "...To be honest, I'm from Galar, and I just arrived in Paldea not long ago."

"No wonder!" The uncle looked suddenly enlightened, "Since you don't know, let me tell you!"

"Legend has it that a Legendary Pokemon named Calyrex lives on a certain land, a Pokemon that brings bountiful harvests to the land, and people of old affectionately called it The King of Bountiful Harvests."

"However, after some time, people gradually forgot the King, causing it to lose its loyal steed, wandering alone in the cold snowfield."

"One day, D discovered records of it in ancient books and firmly believed in the existence of The King of Bountiful Harvests. His faith was felt by the King, and since then, his ranch has been blessed by the King."

"Later, D told the townspeople about Calyrex, and everyone began to trust in Calyrex."

"Perhaps Calyrex felt it all, and descended upon the Paldea Region, performing a miracle in a forest recently burned to ashes by a fire, restoring it to its original state instantly."

"Because of this event, the Paldea League built a shrine for it on the nearby mountain, and it's said that now Calyrex roams the mountain, appearing before those with a sincere heart."

"Is there such a thing?" Leon widened his eyes in disbelief, that Legendary Pokemon named Calyrex is amazing!

The uncle seemed a bit surprised: "You didn't know? I thought you were going to D Ranch to learn more about Calyrex's legend!"

Leon: "...I came here for something else."

"Alright." The uncle nodded then told him the location of D Ranch.

Leon thanked him, but just as he was about to turn away, the uncle suddenly called out to him.

Leon looked back.

The uncle pointed to a handicraft shop, and when Leon glanced in that direction, he saw rows of Pokemon statues on the shop's shelves.

That Pokemon was unmistakably the same one he once saw beside D during the competition!

The uncle showed a hearty smile, "Buy a little wood carving; The King of Bountiful Harvests will bless you!"

---

---

---

---

# 310: Chapter 307: Leon Challenges the Grass Trial

Thirty minutes later, on the country road heading to D Ranch.

Leon walked along, fiddling with the small object in his hand.

It was a delicate and small Pokemon wood carving, atop this Pokemon's head was a huge, crown-like dark green bud.

The tip of the bud bore beautiful patterns, with several antennae-like elements supporting the bud extending to the smaller head below, appearing as a giant crown.

Beneath the crown was a petite body, intricately carved with eyes, nose, mouth, and further down, with slender, exquisite limbs.

The craftsmanship of the wood carving was incredibly meticulous, particularly the eyes, which exuded a sense of majesty, resembling a true king.

"Calyrex…" Leon murmured to himself.

He put away the small wood carving, deciding to gift it to Hop when he returned to the Galar Region in a few days.

At this moment, a conspicuous sign appeared at the end of the road.

The sign prominently displayed the name D Ranch.

Seeing this sight, Leon breathed a sigh of relief, "Here at last!"

This trip was indeed challenging! Being lost before had made him almost forget what it felt like to find a place straight away.

Next, he would challenge the owner here and continue the unfinished match!

Suddenly, a shadow was cast from above, Leon looked up and saw several orange Pokemon flying overhead, descending into the nearby ranch.

Seeing this scene, Leon was slightly dumbfounded.

Those Pokemon… are Dragonite?

So many Dragonite! Even for a Champion, witnessing such a scene still filled him with shock.

Surely as he thought, the ranch's owner must be like his mentor Mustard, a retired Champion-level Trainer?

Leon quickened his pace, arriving at the entrance of the ranch, standing by the doorway peering inside.

One glance, and Leon could see the ranch was very well kept.

The field, where various crops were growing, had one of those Dragonite land by it and use a Rain Dance Move on the field.

Soon after, a gentle rain began to fall from the sky, nourishing the crops and moistening the land.

Beside the field, was endless green grass, seemingly trimmed intentionally, appearing neat and tidy without any messiness.

Many Pokemon frolicking on the grass, the scene remarkably harmonious and warm.

Looking at those Pokemon, Leon's mind filled with their names, "Miltank, Alcremie, Glaceon, Cyclizar..."

As for the few other Pokemon not found in the Galar Region, he couldn't recognize them.

"So lively!" Leon thought to himself, took a deep breath, and shouted brightly into the ranch:

"Hey! Is anyone there?"

Upon hearing the call, the Pokemon chasing each other on the grass stopped and turned their heads to look over.

Soon after, one pink Pokemon with a red cape and holding a small hammer, anxiously turned and flew away.

Leon was a bit surprised.

But before long, he saw a familiar figure following that Pokemon out of the house.

When D realized the visitor to the ranch was the one mentioned by Pawmot, he stood frozen in place.

That was… Leon?

Could he be seeing wrongly? Why would Leon come here?

Seeing him come out, Leon grinned and greeted first: "Hello!"

After a brief shock, D recovered his composure: "Hello, Mr. Leon!"

"You know me?" Leon was quite surprised by the fact the other party could call out his name instantly.

D: "..."

Oh no, he nearly forgot he had never interacted with Leon before!

D's mind raced quickly, then smiled saying: "I saw the commercial you endorsed while in the Galar Region earlier, but, Mr. Leon, is there anything specific that brings you here?"

He remembered he hadn't disclosed the ranch location while in the Galar Region before, did he?

Could it be…

D's mind flashed, suddenly recalling the commercial match he participated in the Galar Region, where Leon was present and watched his game.

With Geeta telling him Leon expressed a desire to visit Paldea Region before, D suddenly had an incredible thought.

—Could Leon be wanting to battle with him?

It's widely known that since becoming Champion, Leon hadn't tasted defeat until meeting Hop, and even Raihan never defeated him.

It's precisely due to this that Leon is dubbed as the strongest Champion.

Thus, the commercial match's final winner, without surprises, would still be Leon.

His withdrawal from the match piqued Leon's interest, leading Leon to travel far to the Paldea Region…

Thinking of this possibility, D was astounded, it couldn't be, could it?

Yet he saw Leon enthusiastically step forward, asking: "Remember the Rising Star Match held in Wyndon City?"

D: "..." Surely.

Out of hospitality, he turned and invited Leon inside for a chat: "Let's talk in the living room!"

---

"Excuse me!" Leon said.

The two walked into the house, and the Pokémon who had been playing in the ranch came up curiously, hiding behind the sofa and watching the visitor who had come from afar.

Leon looked at the Pokémon on the other end of the sofa who was watching TV, thinking, "Calyrex is indeed D's Pokémon!"

"Indeedee!" Seeing guests arriving, the younger sister Indeedee brought two cups of tea and placed them in front of D and Leon.

Leon took a sip before getting to the main point: "I was at the Nova Tournament too, I watched your match, that Koraidon is truly an impressive Pokémon!"

D glanced at Koraidon beside him with a smile and nodded, saying, "Koraidon is indeed very reliable."

Koraidon was very pleased: "Aga-sii~"

Leon said seriously, "After you withdrew midway, I defeated everyone else and won the championship, but I didn't get to battle you, which I find quite regrettable."

As a Champion-level Trainer, Leon could clearly sense the powerful aura emanating from both Koraidon and Calyrex.

This aura was not inferior to his mentor Mustard, and as their Trainer, D must also be a formidable presence.

Having never tasted defeat for many years, Leon was eager to battle stronger Trainers to hone his skills.

"So, I've come to continue the unfinished match with you!"

D: "..."

Does Leon have a bit of an obsession?

At this moment, he wanted to say he wasn't a Trainer, just a Rancher, but he feared Leon wouldn't find that believable.

Besides, when it comes to battles, he had never commanded Pokémon in real life, and compared to Leon's command experience, he was far behind.

Moreover, the Pokémon in the ranch varied in strength, and it wasn't guaranteed he could gather six Champion-level ones to battle Leon.

D remained silent, and Leon felt a bit nervous inside.

Could it be that the other party was unwilling to have a Pokémon battle with him?

It wasn't that D didn't want to; he was quite curious to see how far Gogoat had grown.

After all, with the World Tree and goat's milk, Pokémon could recover instantly from any injury.

In that case, let Gogoat spar with this strongest Champion's Pokémon!

With this thought, D made up his mind.

He nodded to Leon and said, "Of course, but I can't gather six Pokémon, so let's have a 3v3 battle!"

Three Gogoat vs. Leon.

3v3?

Leon, who thought the other would refuse, was relieved: "Alright! Let's start now!"

"Follow me!"

D got up and led the way. They walked out of the house to an open grassy field, where D had specifically built a battle arena for the Grass Trial.

Seeing D preparing for a battle, all the Pokémon on the ranch curiously gathered around the battle field to watch.

D called over three Gogoat, looked at Leon with a Poke Ball in hand, and said:

"To be honest, besides being a Rancher, I'm also a chef who specializes in culinary research. The dishes I create have some magical powers—like Berries that can detoxify Pokémon."

Upon hearing this, Leon looked puzzled: "What do you mean?"

D smiled slightly: "Not long ago, I set up several Trials on the ranch, and Trainers who pass these Trials receive rewards in the form of those magical dishes."

"For instance, fruit wine that increases Poison Type resistance and reduces Poison Type damage, fruit wine that enhances Paralysis Resistance, and gourmet dishes that have a chance to help Pokémon learn new Moves."

Hearing this, Leon's eyes widened.

Trials? Cuisine?

He was more intrigued by the cuisine than the Trials.

Is there really such a thing in this world? No! To be accurate, can cuisine really have such miraculous effects?

Seeing Leon's astonished expression, D continued: "And what's about to start next is the most famous Grass Trial on the ranch—a battle against my three Gogoat, and upon defeating us, I will gift you one of these fruit wines as a reward."

He usually never personally directs Gogoat battles, letting Gogoat battle on their own.

But today's opponent is Leon, so they are giving it their all.

Although his command skills are not up to Leon's, based on past experiences in games, he can provide some guidance to Gogoat during the battle.

"Grass Trial, huh?" Hearing this, Leon became interested, looking up at the three robustly bred Gogoat behind D: "This should indeed be exciting!"

Here, he was not a Champion but a challenger; he was going to challenge this Grass Trial to make himself stronger!

"There is no Dynamax in the Paldea Region, I wonder if you can handle it."

As D spoke, one of the Gogoat stepped forward and entered the battle arena, eyes full of fighting spirit staring at the human opposite.

Leon tossed the Poke Ball in his hand: "That's fine, without Dynamax, let's have an authentic Pokémon battle! Here we go, Haxorus!"

With a flash of white light, a Dragon-type Pokémon covered in tough armor with huge jaw-axes appeared opposite the Gogoat.

Seeing the two ready, the younger sister Indeedee took a step forward and blew the whistle around her neck.

"Whistle—

The crisp and melodious sound signified the official start of the match.

The next second, a large amount of Grass Energy surged from Gogoat's body, forming a dense Grassy Terrain almost instantaneously.

The green glow overflowed the entire battlefield, and the vast energy formed small grasses glowing with a faint green light, gently swaying in the energy ripples.

"Grassy Terrain?" Leon was slightly amazed, the speed of execution was fast! Almost instantaneous!

But compared to the Grass Type Gogoat, his Haxorus had a better move set advantage!

"Poison Jab!"

With a command, Haxorus charged towards Gogoat, a layer of purple glow appeared on its claws, filled with Poison Type energy.

Faced with this attack, Gogoat showed no sign of panic.

It stood calmly on the spot, and the next moment, two massive, serpentine Vine Whips shot out from its body, carrying a sharp sound of breaking through the air, heading towards the charging Haxorus.

The Vine Whip swept through, Haxorus' eyes remained calm, and with a sidestep, it dodged the attack.

However, in the next second, another Vine Whip swiftly whistled its way over, this time Haxorus couldn't dodge and was hit by the Vine Whip.

The immense impact instantly sent Haxorus flying,

"So strong!" Leon's face was shocked, those were Vine Whips? He had never seen a Pokémon use Vine Whips like that!

But now was not the time to think about it, that obviously powerful Vine Whip hit Haxorus, and Haxorus seemed stunned by the strike and couldn't stand up immediately.

"Haxorus, hang in there!" Leon quickly reminded.

Meanwhile, D, who had been calmly observing the situation, finally gave an order: "Use your best move!"

"Meeh!"

Upon hearing that, a flash of determination shone in Gogoat's eyes. It crouched slightly, eyes locked onto Haxorus, who was attempting to rise.

Its body turned into a white light, rushing towards Haxorus.

"Quick, dodge it!" Leon keenly sensed the danger.

However, Gogoat had already locked onto Haxorus, and during the charge, a terrifying surge of Grass Type Energy emerged from its mouth.

Then, the Grass Type Energy turned into a massive Grass Sacred Sword, held in Gogoat's mouth.

Seeing this, Leon's pupils contracted.

What move is this?!

The Aerial Ace + Grass Sacred Sword combo was successful. Gogoat held the leaf blade in its mouth, slicing straight past Haxorus, and then in a stylish move, stopped with its back to Haxorus, without looking back.

Haxorus, who had just risen, stood motionless in place.

In the next second, it fell to its knees on the grass with a thud.

---

---

---

---

# 311: Chapter 308: Gogoat vs. Champion!

Haxorus... has fallen?

Lost the ability to fight just because it missed Gogoat?

Leon thought of the gigantic, Onix-thick Vine Whip and the never-seen-or-heard Leaf Blade, unable to hide his astonishment.

That's definitely not something a normal Gogoat could accomplish!

After a brief shock, Leon quickly adjusted his emotions.

To be honest, it's been a long time since he encountered such a powerful rival.

In the Galar Region, ever since he left the martial arts Gym and entered the championship to become the Champion, he had never lost a single match.

Even the current strongest Gym Leader in Galar, Raihan, could not defeat him.

And now...

Leon felt the trainer's blood within him boiling.

He took out a Poke Ball, retrieved Haxorus, and then grabbed the next Poke Ball, throwing it forcefully.

"The battle has just begun! Dragapult, let the opponent feel our passion!"

A flash of white light, and a Pokemon that stands about three meters tall, with a triangular head extending to the sides, four holes on the horns like jet launchers, appeared on the battlefield.

Seeing this familiar Pokemon, D squinted his eyes: "Dragapult, huh? I thought you'd send out Charizard."

Leon grinned: "The best should always be saved for last!"

Indeed, D nodded slightly; whether in games or anime, people tend to reserve their ace Pokemon for the end.

"Then, let's begin!" he said.

"Shoo—"

Sister Indeedee blew the whistle again.

The battle officially commenced!

"Lock on with Dragon Arrow followed by Phantom Force!" Leon commanded first.

"Dree—py!"

Dragapult responded with a Growl, its small, narrow eyes locked onto Gogoat below.

Only "whoosh whoosh", two sounds were heard, as the Dreepys residing in its skull were launched like missiles.

They were extremely fast; D could only see two purple shadows crossing the air, hitting Gogoat, leaving two scars on it.

At the same time, Dragapult's figure vanished into the shadows, disappearing from the battlefield.

D remained unchanged, silently observing the battlefield, thinking futilely.

It's not a fatal injury; Gogoat will recover soon.

Sure enough, as he had anticipated, a vast flow of Grass Energy transformed into a river, flowing toward Gogoat's wounds.

The two damages began to heal at a visible rate.

Seeing this scene, Leon was visibly shocked.

"How could it recover so fast?" Is it Grassy Terrain?

Dragapult still hadn't revealed itself, hiding in the shadows, seeking an opportunity to attack.

Gogoat's eyes were resolute, calmly surveying, wary of the opponent's sudden attack.

Seeing this, D directly ordered, "Vine Whip!"

"Baa!"

With two whish sounds, Gogoat extended the two Vine Whips from its body, spinning like an electric fan, rapidly sweeping every area of the battlefield.

One second, two seconds, three seconds...

When D counted to the seventh second in his mind, the Vine Whip finally hit Dragapult in the shadows, knocking it out.

Dragapult let out a painful cry, and as it steadied itself in mid-air, another Vine Whip was coiling toward it.

"Fly up!" Leon shouted.

Dragapult took a deep breath, its jet-like body speeding straight upwards.

The two Vine Whips tightly pursued it.

D calmly watched the scene.

Leon, however, quickly considered his strategy internally.

"Can't advance slowly; must use a fast attack!"

The recovery effect of the Grassy Terrain was too exaggerated... and just then, Leon recalled that in the last battle, the Grassy Terrain didn't seem to affect his Haxorus?

"No, it's not time to think about this!"

In mid-air, the Vine Whip reached its maximum height and stopped, and the chased Dragapult finally breathed a sigh of relief.

Taking this opportunity, Leon resumed his thought process.

"Grassy Terrain requires a certain time to heal Gogoat's injuries." Leon recalled the previous scene, calculating the recovery time in his mind: "Three seconds."

"If using a fast attack, Gogoat must be incapacitated within three seconds."

Three seconds... That's enough time!

Leon looked up to the sky and shouted loudly, "Dragon Arrow!"

"Dree—py!"

Dragapult's body began to dive down, with a swoosh, two sounds, as two Dreepys were launched again.

Seeing this, D raised an eyebrow, judging by Leon's appearance, he seems to have thought of a strategy?

"Gogoat, Razor Leaf!" D ordered.

"Baa!"

Gogoat looked up to the sky, with immense Grass Energy surging from its body, transforming into green and harmless leaves, rushing towards the sky like a green snowstorm.

---

The "Dragon Arrow" sliced through the Razor Leaf, breaking through its defense, and continued to dive down, crashing towards Gogoat.

The remaining Razor Leaves simultaneously aimed at Dragapult diving from above.

Leon's eyes were tightly following Dragapult's figure; this is the right distance!

He seized the moment and shouted, "Flamethrower!"

"Dreee-gon!"

The duo of Leon and Dragapult had been partners for years, having developed excellent coordination.

As soon as Leon gave the command, Dragapult immediately reacted.

It opened its mouth wide, gathering blazing flames within, which then turned into a vast sea of fire, engulfing the Razor Leaves.

Instantly, a downpour of fire started from the sky.

Dragapult accelerated its diving speed, bursting through the fiery rain, with scorching flames surging forth to strike Gogoat together with the previously launched Dragon Arrow.

The flames instantly engulfed Gogoat.

Gogoat gritted its teeth in pain, enduring, unable to do anything else.

Leon watched the scene anxiously, counting seconds in his mind.

"Three seconds."

"Two seconds."

"Now! Rapid Retreat!"

"Dreee-gon!"

The Flamethrower stopped, and Dragapult was suddenly shrouded in a layer of pale green glow, swiftly crashing into Gogoat.

"One second!"

After a series of type advantages, Gogoat finally lost its ability to fight and collapsed on the ground.

Dragapult, exhausted and panting, transformed into a white light and returned safely to Leon after using Rapid Retreat to attack.

However, in the next moment, a more powerful surge of Grass Energy flowed into the fallen Gogoat.

The Gogoat, which had fallen unconscious a moment ago from losing its ability to fight, slowly opened its eyes.

Witnessing this scene, both Leon and Dragapult widened their eyes, nearly dropping their jaws in shock.

What on earth is this?!

D, seeing this, was also a bit surprised. He glanced at the World Tree not far away, noticing the shimmering green light emanating from its leaves, immediately understanding the situation.

He called back the Gogoat and said to Leon, "It's your win."

Truly worthy of a Champion! To come up with a strategy in such a short time!

D felt genuine admiration, but he had also learned a lot from the battle, and such an occurrence wouldn't happen again!

"What is going on here?" Leon was still in shock.

D could only helplessly explain, "Gogoat's Grassy Terrain is special; it lasts a long time, allowing Gogoat to harness the Grass Energy of the area to quickly restore its strength."

Leon understood instantly, with a surprised flash in his eyes: "This Gogoat's affinity and control over energy is remarkable!"

"Perhaps it's favored by nature!" D said with a smile, "We can talk more after the battle ends, but for now, let's continue!"

Leon nodded, choosing not to switch Pokemon, keeping Dragapult in play.

Meanwhile, D switched to a second Gogoat.

With a whistle blown by his sister Indeedee, the battle officially resumed.

"Take to the sky!" Leon wanted to use the previous tactic again.

Dragapult immediately soared high into the sky.

"This time, I won't let you succeed!" D looked skyward, "Gogoat, Energy Ball!"

"Maaa!"

With the command given, Gogoat immediately locked its gaze on the opponent above, conjuring a massive green Energy Ball in front of it, launching it towards Dragapult.

The Energy Ball flew at incredible speed, closely pursuing Dragapult.

"Already thought of a counter-strategy?" Leon felt extremely excited, glad he came!

Still, they had more than just this strength!

"Dragapult, dodge!" Leon commanded; the Energy Ball traveled in a straight line without any tracking function, so as long as Dragapult altered its path, it could easily evade.

Everything proceeded as Leon predicted, and the Energy Ball did not hit.

Seeing this, D persisted, "Once more!"

Gogoat launched another Energy Ball.

"Again!" D directed.

So, a third Energy Ball followed closely, chasing after Dragapult.

Then came the fourth, fifth, sixth...

Watching the Energy Balls spread across the sky like a cascade, Leon was stunned and then widened his eyes.

Consecutive Energy Balls? This Gogoat's control over Grass Energy is equally impressive!

In midair, Dragapult, engaged in an intense chase with the Energy Balls, gradually slowed down due to exhaustion.

Leon realized the crisis, immediately asserting, "Phantom Force!" Regardless, interrupting the opponent's offensive rhythm is priority!

Observing the situation, D decisively changed tactics, "Razor Leaf!"

"Maaa!"

Gogoat unleashed its power, sending a flurry of green leaves flying in all directions.

"Be vigilant and prepare to use Solar Beam!" D warned, noticing the Razor Leaves scattering across the battlefield, while a Solar Beam was primed for launch below. If Dragapult were exposed, it would face a dire predicament.

What to do?

Leon quickly thought of a strategy.

Within the battlefield, Gogoat remained poised, absorbing sunlight while vigilantly observing its surroundings.

At this moment, in the northeastern corner of the battlefield, something unusual happened with the Razor Leaves.

They fell to the ground as if they'd hit something.

"Now!" D shouted.

Gogoat spotted the location and, in the next second, unleashed a powerful Solar Beam, striking the concealed Dragapult.

In an instant, Dragapult plummeted to the grass below.

"Danger! Get up and evade!" Leon yelled.

Gogoat crouched slightly, focusing its energy, conjuring a giant Grass Blade, which struck the still-down Dragapult with unstoppable force.

In this moment, much like before with Haxorus, Dragapult's body crashed down, falling into unconsciousness.

"This is incredible! The bond between you and your Pokemon is amazing!" Leon recalled Dragapult to its Poke Ball, expressing his admiration. "Now let me show you the bond I share with Charizard! Go, Charizard!"

A white light flashed, and a robust Charizard, fiery red as blazing flames, landed dramatically on the battlefield.

Its gaze was sharp and fierce, its posture agile, with the flame on its tail burning intensely.

All these signs showcased that Charizard was in its prime, its strength formidable.

Leon was serious.

He had sent out his ace Pokemon.

D recalled the moveset of Leon's Charizard from the games.

Moves like Fire Blast, Air Blade, Heat Wave, Hurricane, Flamethrower...

Most were Fire and Flying Type moves, and as a Grass Type, Gogoat was dominated by Charizard!

Furthermore, Charizard had a quadruple resistance to Grass, rendering Grass Type Moves mere scratches, not to mention Leon's Charizard had the Blaze ability, increasing the power of Fire Type Moves as its health decreased.

Undoubtedly, faced with Charizard, he and Gogoat were at a significant disadvantage.

D sighed helplessly.

In the face of such a formidable opponent, Gogoat did not falter, its gaze remaining determined as it looked at Charizard across the field.

Seeing this, D took a deep breath, regaining his resolve.

If Gogoat hadn't given up, then he had no reason to retreat.

Seeing both ready,

His sister Indeedee, who had waited for some time, blew her whistle once more.

"Phew—"

---

---

---

---

# 312: Chapter 309: Leon: So All I Defeated Were Dairy Goats?

The third battle officially begins!

The grassy field remains unchanged, and Charizard spreads its wings, hovering in mid-air.

This time, D took the initiative to attack first.

"Razor Leaf!"

Gogoat's power activated, and countless leaves flew like blades towards Charizard.

Charizard flapped its wings, rapidly gathering Fire Type Energy from its body, and crimson sparks formed a Heat Wave in the airflow.

The two moves collided in mid-air. The Champion-level strength of Charizard made its Fire Type Energy incredibly strong, and in a blink of an eye, the Razor Leaf was consumed by the Heat Wave, ignited by flames, and fluttered down to the ground like butterflies.

"Indeed! Charizard's type advantage is truly unbeatable! Unless Gogoat learns Rock Slide, a move with four times the effectiveness could inflict heavy damage on Charizard," D muttered.

Unfortunately, Gogoat hasn't learned Rock Slide yet.

If that's the case, let's switch to a move of another type!

D steadied his mind, and at this moment, Leon also issued a command, as both voices sounded almost simultaneously.

"Gogoat, Double-Edge!"

"Charizard, Flamethrower!"

As soon as the words fell, the two Pokemon on the field moved simultaneously.

Gogoat locked its gaze on Charizard and charged straight towards it.

Charizard opened its mouth wide, flames surged within, finally turning into a blazing fire that swept towards Gogoat.

It breathed fire while flying, causing Gogoat's charge to miss while Charizard remained unharmed.

The Flamethrower inflicted significant damage on Gogoat.

Immediately, countless rays of green light emerged on the grassy terrain below, streaming towards Gogoat's body to heal its injuries.

Seeing this, Leon quickly said, "Don't give it a chance to recover, Air Blade!"

D: "Use Vine Whip to block!"

Gogoat immediately deployed two thick vine whips to block.

Charizard flapped its wings, and the milky-white airflow formed a wind blade that severed the thick vine whips in front of it, successfully hitting Gogoat.

Gogoat was knocked to the ground, losing the ability to battle.

D: "..."

It seems that today the first person to pass the Grass Trial will be born.

The final battle, the third Gogoat vs Charizard.

Leon seemed to have found a solution to dealing with Gogoat. This time, he directly let Charizard use Defog, eliminating the Grassy Terrain released by Gogoat.

D attempted to attack with a sure-hit combo move of Aerial Ace + Grass Sacred Sword, but unfortunately, not only did it fail to render Charizard unable to battle, it activated its Blaze Attribute.

Then, one Flare Blitz ended the battle.

Looking at the fallen Gogoat, D had a new judgment about their strength.

— Barely able to reach Champion Level, but facing an opponent with the same strength that has a type advantage, even Grassy Terrain cannot exert much effect.

Just like Leon's Charizard.

However, it's already quite good, at least the Gogoat defeated Leon's Haxorus and Dragapult.

They didn't lose in terms of strength, but because of type matchups.

If there's one Rock Slide move, perhaps today's result could be different.

D began to ponder how he could help Gogoat comprehend the move Rock Slide.

D's mind was filled with thoughts, and then he looked up at Leon across the field: "Congratulations, you successfully passed the Grass Trial."

Leon grinned broadly, his smile very candid: "Battling with your Pokemon, whether in mood or moves, was very thrilling! I had a super fun time!"

"I was happy too." D admired internally, truly worthy of being the strongest Champion, and it seemed today was also his first official Pokemon battle.

Facing such an excellent Trainer, Leon felt very satisfied, though regrettably failed to battle Koraidon and Calyrex.

"Looks like there's only next time." Leon thought.

At this moment, Indeedee, who had been waiting by the side for a long time, came forward with several cups of Life Sheep Milk.

D reached out to take them, feeding them to Gogoat, while patting their heads: "You've worked hard today!"

After losing the battle, Gogoat's face looked a bit gloomy.

Seeing this, D comforted them: "It's okay, it's just a match. Keep trying in the future, and I'll do my best to help you too."

For example, learning Rock Slide to target Leon's Charizard, under four times effectiveness, maybe one move could defeat Charizard.

Gogoat's mood improved somewhat at this.

"Alright, go play!" D patted their heads.

Gogoat finally hopped happily away.

Then D looked at Leon and said, "Release all your Pokemon!"

Leon was a bit surprised but still released the weakened Haxorus and Dragapult from their Poke Balls.

D stepped forward to feed them Life Sheep Milk.

The next second, a magical scene unfolded.

After drinking the milk, their injuries immediately began to heal rapidly, visible to the naked eye, and their spirits returned to their original condition.

Witnessing this, Leon displayed shock, looking incredulously at the completely restored two Pokemon and the remaining two bottles of white liquid resembling milk:

"What is that stuff? It has such exaggerated healing effects!"

"It's sheep milk," D replied.

"Sheep milk?"

D nodded, smiling as he explained: "It's the milk produced by the Gogoat, they usually work as dairy goats in the ranch, and the milk they produce is sold to the town and other places."

Upon hearing this, Leon's first reaction wasn't shock, but a slight existential doubt.

Leon: ???

Did he just spend all that effort to defeat three milk-producing goats?

Then how strong would the other Pokemon here be?

Leon reflexively turned his head to look at Koraidon.

At this moment, D stood up and said to Leon: "Alright, since you've already passed the Grass Trial, although today's trial content was different from usual, as the first Trainer to pass the trial, you can choose a magical fruit wine to feed to your Pokemon."

Leon instinctively asked: "Different? What is the normal Grass Trial like?"

"Under normal circumstances, the Grass Trial consists of three rounds, the first round, battling a single Gogoat, the second round, battling two Gogoat simultaneously, and the third round battling three."
