D was determined to help Gogoat learn Rock Slide to target Leon's Charizard, so he turned his head to Leon:

"Though this time you won, next time might not be the same!"

Leon was slightly startled, then understood D's meaning.

He chuckled and said: "Next time, bring your hundred percent strength to battle with me!"

D: "?"

Leon explained: "I want to battle your main Pokemon."

Saying this, he looked suggestively at Koraidon and Dragonite nearby.

D couldn't help but laugh: "Sure!" He found Leon quite interesting.

Traveling from afar to the Paldea Region for a battle with him.

Upon seeing D agree, Leon was very pleased, he began to ponder:

"I wonder how far the Galar Region is from the Paldea Region?"

Hearing this, D turned his head to look over.

Leon awkwardly explained: "Because I'm directionally challenged, I might not be able to smoothly get here from Galar, if I get lost, you must guide me!"

---

---

---

---

# 313: Chapter 310: Geeta's Plan, the Arrival of Zapdos!

D asked Leon to wait for a while in the living room while he went to the wine cellar, where he retrieved several glasses of brewed Berry Wine and arranged them in a line in front of Leon.

Leon's attention was immediately drawn to these wines with their mixed fruit aromas.

"Are these the Berry Wines you brewed with magical effects?" he inquired.

D nodded and introduced them one by one, "The bottle on the left is Pecha Berry Wine, drinking it will enhance a Pokemon's resistance to Poison Type."

"The one in the middle is Cheri Berry Wine, similar to Pecha Berry Wine, it boosts paralysis resistance for a Pokemon, reducing the likelihood of being paralyzed by opponent's moves like Thunder Wave or Stun Spore."

"The one on the right is Aspear Berry Wine, which warms a Pokemon's body, making it less susceptible to being frozen."

Hearing about these, Leon couldn't help but widen his eyes, disbelief written all over his face.

"Do these Berry Wines really have such effects?!"

In the Galar Region, some stores also sell Berry Wine, so for Leon, these wines weren't uncommon.

What surprised him were the effects these wines could offer.

It's known that some strict trainers push their Pokemon through grueling training to enhance resistance and endurances to different types.

Like making Fire Type Pokemon, which are afraid of water, swim in rivers, or having Poison-fearing Pokemon endure poisoning, achieving training goals that way.

And now, according to D, this power enhancement could be achieved without such training?

Leon couldn't help but look up.

D smiled, nodding, "Indeed, as the first Trainer to complete the Grass Trial, you deserve these rewards."

Grass Trial... reminded Leon of the original trial rules D mentioned earlier: "Does what I did count as passing?"

A single Gogoat could be countered by Charizard, two Gogoats were barely a victory, but facing three at once wouldn't be so easy.

D realized his implication and sighed, "I didn't consider this thoroughly at first, so I plan to modify the challenge rules later."

The stages remain unchanged, but challengers only need to defeat one to win a reward and gain qualification for the second stage, challenging two Gogoats simultaneously.

If the second stage is won as well, their strength is recognized, and more rare dishes will be presented as a reward.

And so on.

"I see!" Leon realized, smiling brightly, "Thank you very much!"

D gave the three Berry Wines away, "From now on, they are yours, and you can decide which Pokemon to give them to."

Without hesitation, Leon fed these Berry Wines to Pokemon like Dragapult, Charizard, and Haxorus.

Seeing the lively Pokemon, all spirited and without a trace of weakness, Leon once again felt astonished by the effects of the Life Sheep Milk.

"Is this Life Sheep Milk really produced by Gogoats?"

"Yes." D nodded slightly, "In the Paldea Region, it has a lovely name, called Life Sheep Milk."

"This Life Sheep Milk has the effect of instantly helping Pokemon recover strength, even curing hidden injuries that a Pokemon Center cannot heal."

"To think that such a Life Sheep Milk exists in this world!" Leon was astonished beyond measure.

Speaking of this, D suddenly remembered something.

He casually asked, "Does Mr. Leon know the Champion Alder from the Unova Region?"

"Alder?" Leon slightly froze.

D explained, "I heard from the chief that Mr. Alder's Volcarona has been overstrained and has become weak from battle, I was thinking maybe this Life Sheep Milk could help."

Hearing this, Leon immediately understood D's intent.

After a brief daze, he grinned and said, "Although I am not very familiar with Alder, I will contact the Unova League when I return to the Galar Region and have Alder come to see you!"

D really is a kind Trainer! Leon thought, not only powerful but also willing to help others, such talent... it would be great to have him in the Galar Region.

"I would appreciate it." D expressed his gratitude.

"No trouble at all." Leon beamed, eyes bright, seeming expectant, and asked, "If someday my Pokemon suffers an incurable injury, you will help me too, right?"

D: "…"

"You're always welcome at the ranch," he replied.

"I will!"

Next, D and Leon talked about other matters.

Like Calyrex and the shrine in the mountains.

When Calyrex was mentioned, Leon pulled a small wooden sculpture from his pocket and said to D:

"This was a souvenir recommended by an uncle when I first arrived in the town. To be honest, I was surprised to see it. I didn't expect Calyrex to be so famous in the Paldea Region!"

Upon hearing this, D glanced at the sculpture, momentarily unsure of what to say.

"Because not long ago, Calyrex performed a miracle on the mountainside, and as a token of gratitude, the Paldea League built a shrine for it on the mountainside."

---

"Amazing!" Leon deeply admired this great Pokemon.

He carefully put away the wooden carving and planned to buy a few more when he left, to give them to Hop, Sonia, Chairman Rose, and his mentor's family.

Consider it a special souvenir from the Paldea Region!

The two of them started talking again about the various trials at the ranch.

Leon seemed quite interested in these trials.

So, D explained the various trials at the ranch to him in detail.

"As of now, this ranch has four trials in total: Gogoat's Grass Trial, Dragonite's Storm Trial, Pawmot's Thunder Trial, and Alcremie's Metronome Trial."

"Metronome Trial?" The first two were understandable, but the third one sounded a bit strange.

D nodded, "Yes, the Metronome Trial. This trial is limited to Pokemon that can use Metronome, and they can only use the move Metronome."

"Compared to the other trials, this one is a test of luck, and you can earn rewards by passing it."

"A test of luck, huh? Sounds interesting! Too bad I don't have a Pokemon that can use Metronome with me." Leon felt a bit regretful.

The ranch was full of intriguing things, and he wanted to try almost everything.

Luckily, his Pokemon had all regained their energy.

Thinking about this, Leon was eager to challenge the Storm Trial and Thunder Trial.

But just then, a beeping sound suddenly came from his pocket.

D's attention was drawn to it, and then he saw Leon take out a fiery red, pager-like device from his pocket.

Leon pressed a button, and a young voice came out:

"Big brother! Where have you gone? The Galar League is looking everywhere for you! They even came to the house!"

Leon felt a bit embarrassed, he cleared his throat and said, "I'll be back soon, and I'll contact Chairman Rose later, oh yes Hop, I brought you an awesome gift this time!"

"What kind of gift? Big brother is the best!" The young voice replied with obvious excitement.

Leon grinned, "It's a secret!"

"Huh?" The other side sounded disappointed.

"Alright, you'll know when I get back, that's it for now, I'm hanging up!"

After saying this, Leon hung up the phone.

"Your brother?" D asked in surprise.

Leon nodded, "Yeah! That boy is quite reckless, I bet he's stressing out during my time away from Galar."

Of course, it's Hop! D thought.

"Alright, I need to head back, I've spent too much time away, there's still a lot to do in Galar!"

Leon stood up, glanced at Koraidon and Calyrex not far away, feeling a bit of regret, and then turned to D.

"It's settled then, next time I come over, show me everything you've got for a battle, I've been looking forward to it!"

D: "..."

Just wait! He'll soon have Naclstack teach Gogoat how to perform Rock Slide, to help Gogoat regain confidence from Charizard.

Leon left, and before departing, bought a few bottles of goat milk from him, saying he would take them back to the Galar Region as a treasure.

Watching Leon's silhouette ride away on Charizard, D silently wished in his heart for him not to get lost again, and to return to the Galar Region smoothly.

Unknowingly, it was already noon.

D was preparing to head back to make lunch for everyone when he heard the sound of the video phone as soon as he entered the door.

D went up to answer.

Geeta's face appeared on the screen.

"Leon has already arrived at the ranch, right?"

D: "He's already left, said the Galar side is looking for him."

Geeta smiled slightly, her tone curious, "So, did he battle with you? Who won?"

"Leon won." D said helplessly, "He challenged the Grass Trial at the ranch, and finally defeated my two Gogoats with Charizard, which counters them."

Geeta also had a Gogoat and deeply understood how this Pokemon feared Fire+Flying Pokemon like Charizard.

"But you've done very well; managing to defeat two of the Galar Champion's Pokemon." Geeta offered a smile of praise, "You truly have the talent I've recognized!"

D: "..."

Listening to Geeta's tone, it felt as if he had already become a Paldea League employee?

Geeta began thinking about D Ranch's Grass Trial.

She had tried it before, and it indeed helped challengers hone their skills and grow.

Perhaps she should send the Gym Leaders from the Paldea Region over for special training?

As she pondered, Geeta found this method feasible, and she proposed the idea to D.

Finally, she asked him, "What do you think?"

D: "... Those Gym Leaders are probably quite busy, right? I saw Larry juggling several tasks at once, and he's exhausted."

"They're indeed very busy usually." Geeta coughed lightly and said, "But every year, Gym Leaders can take a vacation and don't have to stay at the gym waiting for challengers."

D: "..."

Heartless Paldea League! Even squeezing the vacation time from Gym Leaders!

"I can't give a suggestion on this matter," D said.

In his heart, he was actually quite eager to meet the Gym Leaders from the Paldea Region.

Mainly because the students from Paldea Academy had varying levels of strength, including many novice trainers, who sometimes couldn't help Pawmot and Dragonite gain experience.

And if Gym Leaders came by...

Just great for using them as Gym Leader Level opponents to harvest experience, to train Pawmot and Dragonite in battle techniques, helping them grow stronger.

Thinking of this, D continued, "However, I always welcome challengers here."

Upon hearing this, Geeta smiled, "So it's settled then? Yes, just consider it a special task assigned to them."

The two of them reached some kind of agreement, and Geeta happily hung up.

Perhaps the Paldea League could use this opportunity to elevate the strength of the Gym Leaders overall!

Then let trainers traveling from other regions feel that the Paldea Region has the most talent and strength!

As for D and his ranch... Let's just treat it as the hidden boss of the Paldea Region!

Geeta would be happily inviting trainers from other regions to go there to challenge.

D didn't know what Geeta was thinking, but he could vaguely guess some of it.

But he was just a small rural farmer and couldn't change the chief champion's ideas.

After hanging up, D planned to check on the Gogoats, have a chat with them, and give them encouragement.

He stepped out of the house, and as he walked onto the grass outside, he suddenly felt a shadow looming over him.

Accompanied by a gradually nearing "Zap" call, D had just looked up when a strong gust of wind blew before his eyes.

Soon after, a sharp-eyed, proud-looking bird-type Pokemon with sharp feathers all over its body landed in front of him.

That's... Zapdos?!

D widened his eyes in disbelief.

Teddiursa and Mew were riding on the back of this Zapdos.

As soon as it landed, both Mew and Teddiursa jumped down.

Teddiursa leaped up, throwing itself into D's arms.

This must be the Zapdos that the Heroic Expedition Team befriended at the power plant, D thought.

His eyes moved from Zapdos to Teddiursa and Mew, surprised, "How did you return so quickly?"

"Zap Zap!"

After a brief hug, Teddiursa jumped down from D.

Alongside Mew, Teddiursa came to Zapdos's side, joyfully introducing to D:

"miu～"

"Zap Zap!" (We invited our good friend to come live at the ranch with us!)

---

---

---

---

# 314: Chapter 311: Zapdos's Plan (Double Episode)

Although they hadn't eaten the Translation Nuts Cake, based on D's understanding of Teddiursa, this little Bear-type Pokemon must be introducing the good friends it made outside.

Good friends...

Such as the nearly two-meter-tall Zapdos behind it, the legendary Thunder God, said to build its nest in the depths of dark thunderclouds and control thunder and lightning at will.

Although this might not be the Thunder God, its rarity far surpasses those ordinary Pokemon by the roadside!

And such a Pokemon was actually invited over by Mew and Teddiursa! D was shocked inside.

He initially thought it would take Teddiursa and Mew a long time to invite the Pokemon friends they made, but didn't expect them to take action so soon!

But now there's another problem, the World Tree hasn't fully grown yet!

Seeing D standing there in a daze, Teddiursa's little face showed a puzzled expression: "Hmm?"

D snapped out of it, looked at this Zapdos, and greeted it:

"Hello, Zapdos, welcome to the ranch, I am Teddiursa's trainer, D."

Zapdos stared sharply at the human in front of it.

Just like the humans it had seen in the power plant, a head and four legs.

However, unlike those humans who immediately wanted to battle it.

This human seemed in no rush to trap it in a Poke Ball.

In fact, on the way here, Mew and Teddiursa had introduced the ranch to it in detail.

The content included the Pokemon living in the ranch, the delicious foods everyone liked to eat, and playing games together in the ranch.

Besides that, they talked the most about this human named D.

Teddiursa said D would make various Poffins and sweets for them and bring back toys and gifts from town during festivals.

What's more, D would kindly prepare beds, arrange nests, and wouldn't always capture Pokemon in Poke Balls. He was the best human in the world.

At first, Zapdos was skeptical, thinking, how could such a person exist in the world?

Don't all humans want to capture it and then send it to battle other humans?

However, now, Zapdos somewhat believed it.

Because it found that many Pokemon indeed lived here.

They weren't trapped in Poke Balls, nor were they fighting others, but happily chasing and playing on the grassland or flying amid the flowers.

Seeing its arrival, those Pokemon also cast cheerful glances over and approached, curiously watching.

D felt a bit embarrassed being stared at by Zapdos.

He coughed lightly, turned his head to Mew and Teddiursa, and helplessly said to them, "The World Tree can't accommodate Pokemon yet!"

Today is February 11th, 199 in League calendar, and till now, Calyrex has only helped grow the World Tree three times.

Although the current World Tree has grown much compared to the beginning, it's barely more than three meters tall, close to four meters, and can't accommodate Pokemon yet.

"Miu?" Mew tilted its head, somewhat puzzled.

Thus, D took everyone to the World Tree.

The green canopy covered the sky, blocking out the sunlight overhead, casting dappled shadows on the grass.

A gentle breeze brushed by, and the leaves on the tree swayed lightly. Under the golden warm sunlight, each leaf emitted a shimmering green glow, vibrant with life force.

Seeing the World Tree, Mew and Teddiursa immediately happily introduced to Zapdos: "Hmm!" (This is the World Tree!)

Gazing at this "little tree", Zapdos showed a look of astonishment on its face.

In this state, can it really live on it?

That trunk, not even as thick as its leg, would surely break if it stepped on it, right?

Zapdos was full of doubt.

Seeing this, Mew appeared a bit embarrassed: "Miu!" (Although it can't be lived in now, the World Tree will grow one day!)

Teddiursa: "Hmm!" (That's right! D knows how long it will take to grow!)

Saying that, the two Pokemon turned their heads simultaneously, looking at D.

Right?

D: "..."

He calculated the time in his heart and found that today just happened to be Calyrex's Tree Nurturing Day.

Therefore, D planned to let Zapdos witness the growth of the World Tree firsthand.

In the afternoon, after lunch, when everything was ready.

All the Pokemon on the ranch gathered by the World Tree, surrounding it in the middle.

Calyrex floated in mid-air, faced with the World Tree before it, energy surging from its body, and it raised its arms to activate its power.

In the next second, an unprecedentedly strong energy surged out like a tide, all infusing into the trunk of the World Tree.

A blinding light instantly enveloped the entire World Tree.

Within this light, the trunk of the World Tree immediately began to grow taller at a speed visible to the naked eye.

"Boom boom boom!"

At this moment, the ground seemed to tremble slightly, and a strange humming sound came from all directions.

D's eyes were fixed on the World Tree, observing its changes.

[World Tree (infant stage): The sprout that has absorbed a large amount of the Power of Grass, because of Gogoat, has undergone a mutation during growth, with abundant life force brewing within, eventually breaking ground, it is said to support a world, nurturing all things with Natural Energy, it still needs 91,220 years to mature.]

---

[Year 90530.]

[Year 89650.]

[Year 89220.]

[Year 87600.]

Finally, the number stopped at 87600 years.

When the light enveloping the World Tree completely disappeared, an even sturdier, more lush, and vibrant tree appeared before D and the many Pokémon.

The World Tree now stands at an estimated height of seven meters, nearly reaching eight meters, and the trunk has grown substantially thicker.

What's even more delightful is that the World Tree has directly transformed from a sprout to a young stage, undoubtedly speeding up its growth pace.

"Indeed, the Leaf Cake's effects have worked again!" D thought, astonished that it reduced the growth duration by 3620 years, doubling the original time!

"No, it may not be entirely the effect of the Leaf Cake." D pondered.

Because he remembers Calyrex previously mentioning that the vast people of Paldea provided him with an immense amount of faith, transforming into his strength and elevating his power beyond its peak to a new height!

Plus, the establishment of Calyrex Shrine continuously attracts visitors and Trainers, making more people aware of Calyrex's existence, thus providing him with more power.

Undoubtedly, he should truly thank Geeta and the Paldea League for this matter.

At this rate, the World Tree will reach its ultimate form in less than four years!

This is truly delightful news!

D looked excitedly towards Calyrex.

Calyrex nodded slightly at him, using Telepathy to say, "Indeed, my power can accomplish more for the World Tree; it is truly my privilege!"

Koraidon and other Pokémon were also feeling very joyful.

The most shocked among them was Zapdos.

It lifted its head, staring unblinkingly at the World Tree in front.

As a Pokémon, it could sense the unique difference of this tree from others.

Now, the richness of Natural Energy on this tree astounded it immensely!

In that moment, Zapdos no longer doubted; it firmly believed this tree would become a new home for countless Pokémon in the future.

Beside them, Mew and Teddiursa turned towards Zapdos happily after celebrating.

"How is it?" (Didn't we tell the truth?)

"Miu~" (Stay and live with us~)

Upon hearing this, Zapdos reluctantly pulled away its gaze from the World Tree, turning to look at Teddiursa and Mew.

For these two Pokémon that wanted to live with it and had traveled long distances to find and invite it here, Zapdos felt incredibly moved.

It nodded: "Chirp!" (I will rely on your guidance in the days to come!)

"Clap!"

Seeing Zapdos agree to live with them, Mew and Teddiursa jumped up in joy and high-fived each other.

Then, they started whispering to Zapdos over there.

Witnessing this scene, D whispered to Calyrex next to him: "Did it agree to stay?"

Calyrex gently nodded, showing a kind smile on his face: "I think it has already decided to stay even before coming here."

D was slightly taken aback, realizing that otherwise, Zapdos wouldn't have followed Teddiursa and Mew to the ranch.

Since Zapdos plans to stay, next is arranging a temporary place for it.

It will reside there until the World Tree fully matures.

From Mew and Teddiursa, D learned earlier that this Zapdos likes quiet places and doesn't like being disturbed.

That's why it chose to live in an abandoned power plant.

Should he build a power supply room in the ranch?

D doesn't intend to use Zapdos to generate electricity because the ranch's electricity consumption isn't exaggerated; both Pawmot and Dragonite can easily supply it.

The idea of building a power supply room arose because he believed Zapdos might appreciate such an electrifying dwelling.

Moreover, the ranch should have a formal power supply room.

With these thoughts, D walked forward, intending to discuss with them.

However, before he could speak, Mew and Teddiursa informed him that Zapdos currently doesn't plan on staying directly in the ranch.

D was slightly puzzled by this.

What's going on?

Mew: "Miu!"

Calyrex stepped forward to help translate: "They say Zapdos thinks the tree hasn't matured yet and plans to travel with them, returning once the tree has fully grown."

A rare hint of gentleness appeared in Zapdos's sharp gaze, and then it called out to Calyrex, indicating his statement was correct.

Just as Mew and Teddiursa regard it as a good friend, it's also willing to consider them as good friends and live together from now on.

Therefore, upon learning that Teddiursa and Mew would continue traveling to help Pokémon in need, Zapdos made a plan in its heart.

Once, it had also been aided by the Heroic Expedition Team.

And now, it wishes to join these kind-hearted friends and travel together to help Pokémon in need, while also protecting them.

D understood this finally, and after a moment of surprise, he suddenly realized, is Zapdos going to join the Heroic Expedition Team?

---

"Oh... When are you planning to leave?" D asked.

Mew and Teddiursa hesitated for a moment, then said, "Miu!"

Calyrex: "Tomorrow."

D nodded with a smile: "Then have a good rest at the ranch tonight!"

"Yay～"

"Miu～"

D: "I'll prepare some snacks for you. You can play here for a while!"

Saying this, D turned and went back to the room, taking honey and other ingredients from the fridge, which were Teddiursa's favorites, and began making snacks for them.

It has to be said, the way Mew and Teddiursa handle things is really fast! They invited friends just like that.

And Zapdos is just the first one...

D remembered when Teddiursa and Mew told him about the Pokemon they met during their travels over the New Year. He had told them they could invite good friends to live at the ranch together.

Teddiursa and Mew looked like they couldn't wait to invite all the Pokemon friends they'd met outside to live at the ranch.

Given Mew and Teddiursa's personalities, probably no wild Pokemon would refuse to befriend them.

Therefore, D suspected that from time to time, more "friends" would be brought back to the ranch.

Like the Farfetch'd, Corphish, or Krabby they mentioned before.

Thinking of this, D suddenly found it quite amusing.

In this view, the Heroic Expedition Team formed by Mew and Teddiursa could be seen as a Pokemon lottery machine in some sense.

From Legendary Pokemon like Zapdos to roadside Corphish and Krabby, they could all be drawn.

These small-sized Pokemon are fine. Zapdos can help bring them back to the ranch.

But when it comes to large Pokemon like Aggron, Nidoking, Rhyhorn, or Snorlax, there's no way to bring them back.

Because Zapdos simply can't carry them.

It seemed that he needed to think of a good method for the Heroic Expedition Team!

As D kneaded the dough, he pondered in his mind.

Soon, he came up with a good idea.

——Why couldn't he buy some Poke Balls for Teddiursa and Mew?

The red and white balls on the market come with a shrinking function and can be reduced to the size of a marble when not in use.

Even Mew and Teddiursa can easily carry them.

If any Pokemon wanted to return to the ranch with them, it could enter the Poke Ball by itself.

Then, through hiring wild flying Pokemon or having Zapdos directly deliver, the Poke Ball with friends can be sent to the ranch.

And with Mew and Teddiursa's intelligence, they could easily learn how to use the Poke Balls.

The only concern was the preservation of Poke Balls.

In the past, with only Mew and Teddiursa, D had to worry about it.

But now with Zapdos, he could hang a Dragonite-style big satchel on Zapdos and put the Poke Balls and some emergency supplies inside.

Items like Life Sheep Milk to heal or Pecha Berries for antidotes.

When Zapdos delivers "friends" back to the ranch, it can also replenish consumed emergency supplies.

But if that's the case, Zapdos, the legendary Pokemon, would become the transport team for the Heroic Expedition Team...

"I wonder if Zapdos would be unhappy." D pondered.

To be on the safe side, he planned to have a good discussion with the three members of the Heroic Expedition Team later.

---

---

---

---

# 315: Chapter 312: A More and More Professional Expedition Team!

"Ding!"

Accompanied by a crisp sound, the oven stopped working.

D, wearing oven mitts, took out the freshly baked honey bread and Poffins, placed them in a picnic basket, and carried it outside to find the three members of the Heroic Expedition Team.

The three of them were busy murmuring about something next to the ice cellar of Glaceon.

As D approached, he immediately saw the secret base that had been punched and damaged by Koraidon earlier.

Evidently, Mew had discovered that its secret base was damaged, but wasn't sure who ruined it, which made it saddened.

Seeing this, D awkwardly explained,

"There was a toy inside that kept making noises. Glaceon thought this place was haunted, so they asked Koraidon and me to come and check it out."

"The sound was coming from the mountain inside, and we didn't know what it was. Sorry, Mew, but don't be sad, there are lots of places on the ranch where you can build a secret base!"

Upon hearing this, Mew finally understood what had happened.

It wasn't too upset, just a little puzzled and troubled by not knowing what had happened.

Besides, this secret base was only temporary. Once the World Tree grows, it plans to move the base onto the tree!

Now that everything was clear, Mew didn't care too much and felt a bit embarrassed when D apologized.

"Miu~"

After everything was explained, the aroma wafting from the picnic basket quickly attracted the attention of the Pokemon.

D smiled and said to them, "Let's talk on the grass!"

"Yima!"

"Miu!"

Members of the Heroic Expedition Team followed along. D spread the picnic cloth on the grass and took out food and drinks from the basket.

Seeing the scene, Alcremie, Pawmot, and Koraidon also gathered around.

Thus, D, with a group of Pokemon, started a picnic on the grass.

Teddiursa and Mew happily began to eat.

Zapdos lowered its head to look at the food in front of it, then imitated the others by poking a fragrant Poffin with its beak.

D was pleased to see them eating happily.

Nothing is more delightful than having Pokemon enjoy the treats he made.

"When are you planning to set off tomorrow?" D began discussing the serious matters.

Teddiursa thought carefully for a while before responding, "Yima!"

Observing its expression, D asked, "Same as last time, in the morning?"

Teddiursa nodded affirmatively.

D continued, "So do you plan to invite the Pokemon friends you make to come live on the ranch with everyone?"

This time, before Teddiursa could speak, Mew eagerly nodded, "Miu!" Of course!

With this, there would be no worry about not seeing each other again after parting!

"I see..."

D expressed his thoughts and concerns to Mew and Teddiursa,

"For flying Pokemon like Zapdos, it's fine since they can fly to the ranch themselves. But what if you make friends with large Pokemon like Snorlax or Aggron while you're out? How would you invite them to the ranch?"

Upon hearing this, Mew and Teddiursa immediately put down their Poffins.

They pondered for a moment, realizing this concern after D mentioned it.

"Yima..."

Exactly, what if they made friends with overly large Pokemon?

During their travels, they had indeed encountered such Pokemon.

For instance, the massive and iron-hard Rhyhorn.

Or the gigantic Onix made entirely of rock.

Neither would be able to cross the vast ocean to reach the ranch.

And it would be impossible for flying Pokemon to carry them across.

Due to their size, they would undoubtedly crush the flying Pokemon.

Thinking of this, Mew and Teddiursa became quite troubled, unable to enjoy the delicious snacks, appearing rather distressed.

Beside them, Zapdos also stopped and turned to its two friends, joining them in thought.

"Chirp!" it offered to help.

But Mew glanced at Zapdos's back, comparing the sizes of Onix and Zapdos in its mind, then shook its head, "Miu..."

No, Onix would crush Zapdos.

Seeing this, Zapdos was left without a solution, too.

The Pokemon pondered for quite a while but couldn't come up with a good idea, ultimately casting hopeful looks towards D.

"Yima!" Teddiursa gazed expectantly.

D is so capable, surely he would have a way to solve this problem?

D, sitting cross-legged on the grass, patted Teddiursa's little head and said, "Indeed, I do have a way."

"Miu?" Mew's eyes lit up.

Zapdos also looked over with interest.

"Do you remember how humans can carry so many Pokemon with them?" D asked.

Upon hearing this, Teddiursa, having been in a Poke Ball before, immediately recalled the answer.

---

"Right!"

"Exactly." D nodded his head: "The good idea I mentioned is using the Poke Ball."

"As long as we let the Pokemon enter the Poke Ball, no matter how big they are, they can easily be sent back to the ranch. We won't need Zapdos anymore; even a single Fearow can easily accomplish it."

Upon hearing this good idea, Mew's eyes lit up instantly.

With this method, they won't have any worries anymore!

Mew felt extremely happy.

Looking at the Pokemon in front of him, D smiled and asked: "Are you sure you want to use this method?"

To him, it didn't make much difference, though as a human, he also uses Poke Balls to catch Pokemon.

But usually, D never confines Pokemon inside the Poke Balls, and they aren't locked either. Pokemon can enter and leave as they please.

He even set up a Poke Ball stand in the corner of his living room, where Dragonite and Pawmot's Poke Balls are all placed.

Last winter, when it was cold, Dragonite would return to its Poke Ball to sleep every night and then come out when the sun rose.

He's just unsure if some wild Pokemon would accept being captured in Poke Balls.

In response to his question, Mew and Teddiursa nodded enthusiastically.

They also believe that D will treat the friends they invite well!

After all, D is a member of the Heroic Expedition Team, and he's the most important part of the logistics unit!

D: "In that case, you don't need to worry; I'll handle what's left."

After saying that, he said goodbye to the Pokemon and rode Koraidon to Porto Marinada Town.

He bought a red satchel from the department store similar to Dragonite's.

This satchel, just like the space backpack trainers carry, employs space compression technology. It can hold a lot of things, making it ideal for people and Pokemon traveling outside.

After leaving the department store, D went to a friendly store and bought fifty Poke Balls and some emergency medicines.

After completing all these tasks, he returned to the ranch and then found the Heroic Expedition Team to introduce each:

"This is a large-capacity space satchel; you can store many items inside it, including gadgets and Berries you find outside."

"These are emergency medicines for treating Pokemon; this bottle is Antidote, just like Pecha Berry, and can cure poison status in Pokemon."

The reason he didn't choose to give them Pecha Berries is that Berries spoil over time, making them prone to rot.

By comparison, Antidotes and Paralysis Heals are more suitable.

Once Mew and Teddiursa remembered the effects of each medicine, D packed them into the satchel.

Next, he packed fifty bottles of Life Sheep Milk from home: "This milk is from Gogoat. No matter how severe the injury, it can heal quickly; if you encounter injured Pokemon in the wild, use it on them!"

"Right!" Teddiursa nodded repeatedly; they've remembered.

"Great." D packed the milk and took out some snacks and food: "These are for your journey; eat them if you get hungry. They'll also help you recover your stamina."

"Lastly, these are the Poke Balls." D piled the miniature, marble-sized Poke Balls in front of the Pokemon: "Do you know how to use them?"

Mew and Teddiursa shook their heads.

"See the button on top?" D picked up a red and white ball to demonstrate: "Press it, and the Poke Ball will expand."

As he spoke, he pressed the button, and the tiny Poke Ball instantly grew to the size of a Berry.

"Press it again, and the Poke Ball will automatically open. If you want to capture a Pokemon, aim this button at their heads. Once sensed, the Poke Ball will automatically capture them."

"The shrink button is here. Even after capturing Pokemon, the Poke Ball can be shrunk for easy portability."

Hearing this, Mew and Teddiursa curiously looked at the Poke Ball in front of them.

Next, Mew floated forward, stretched out its small hand to touch the button, under D's slightly surprised gaze.

Soon, a red light enveloped Mew, and it was pulled inside.

Soon after, the Poke Ball stopped shaking.

D: "???"

The next second, the Poke Ball automatically opened, and Mew's tiny figure emerged, happily exclaiming: "miu!"

It learned how to use the Poke Ball!

Seeing this, D was momentarily at a loss for words.

Could it be that hearing others explain is inferior to firsthand experience?

D skipped over this topic and packed all the other Poke Balls, except for Mew's, into the satchel, which he then hung around Zapdos' neck.

Zapdos lowered its head to help D slip the satchel onto it.

It felt delighted and was willing to perform this task to help its two friends.

"Alright!" D clapped his hands: "You can also store your camera inside and take it out when you need it!"

Upon hearing this, Teddiursa removed the camera from its neck, jumped onto Zapdos, and stuffed the camera into the satchel.

---

"Hooray!"

D nodded with satisfaction, "Looks like you've learned how to use the backpack, so you can now set off on your travels with confidence!"

Hearing this, Mew and Teddiursa gave each other a high five, then turned to Zapdos, who had just joined the expedition team, and gave it a high five as well.

With a "pop," the Heroic Expedition Team was officially formed!

D stood to the side with Koraidon, watching this scene, and couldn't help but think to himself, "Things are really starting to get official now."

This expedition team reminded him of the exploration team from the Pokémon Mystery Dungeon: Explorers of Time series spinoff.

The expedition teams there were also entirely composed of Pokemon.

Their main goals, besides exploring mystery dungeons, were also to accept requests to help Pokémon in trouble within the dungeons.

Not only that, but there was also a very professional Explorer Team Guild there.

It also featured various facilities, like the Spinda Café,

and the Croagunk Item Swap Shop.

Apart from that, the expedition team even had its own motto.

For example, work hard, never run away, and smile all the way.

"Maybe I can replicate some of these things in this world," D thought.

Once the World Tree grows, he could massively develop various facilities on it, like a Pokemon Tavern, a Pokemon Restaurant, and the headquarters guild of the expedition team.

Zapdos was the first member to join the expedition team, but D had a premonition that it definitely wouldn't be the last.

Speaking of which, there are quite a few Pokémon in this world very suitable for expedition team work!

Like the highly intelligent Slowking, the exceedingly clever Oranguru, the sweet doctor Blissey, the banana producer Tropius…

And if there's one most suited for expedition team work, it has to be Demon God Hoopa.

Because its abilities perfectly match the expedition team's requirements.

Hoopa's aura effect is comparable to Doraemon's Anywhere Door, it can really go wherever it wants.

In the movie, it even used this ability to summon a bunch of legendary Pokémon from other regions for a grand battle.

"I just don't know what Hoopa is like in this world!" D murmured.

But discussing this is premature; everything will have to wait until the World Tree is fully grown to elaborate.

It seems that Calyrex's missionary work still needs to continue!

A silent night passed.

Early the next morning, the Heroic Expedition Team set off on their journey once more.

D, along with the Pokémon from the ranch, saw them off.

This time, Mew didn't transform into Fearow to fly with Teddiursa.

The two little ones simply sat on Zapdos, who carried them on their travels.

A red backpack full of supplies hung around Zapdos' neck.

It didn't look the least bit resentful, in fact, it seemed quite happy, as if it was eagerly anticipating the journey ahead.

Seeing this scene, D momentarily didn't know what to say.

But on second thought, Zapdos likely never had an experience like this before, right?

Traveling with the expedition team was an entirely new experience for it, which is why it was so excited.

As the sun rose, Zapdos' silhouette grew more distant.

D watched them leave, wishing in his heart, "Go forth! Use the Life Sheep Milk to help more Pokémon in need!"

At the same time, he began to look forward to what kind of Pokémon Teddiursa and Mew would invite to the ranch next time.

---

---

---

---

# 316: Chapter 313: Coffee and Flutter Mane

League Year 199, February 19th.

Seven days have passed since the Heroic Expedition Team departed, and with their departure, life on the ranch has once again returned to its former calm.

Under the dedicated guidance of Koraidon and D, Flutter Mane is gradually learning to adapt to this era.

The only thing that troubles D is that Flutter Mane's schedule still hasn't adjusted.

It sleeps all day and roams at night, leisurely floating in the air above the ranch, waving its wing claws, doing who knows what.

Due to past experiences, D always worries that Flutter Mane might go hunting other Pokemon.

Day thoughts become night dreams.

That night, he had a terrible dream.

In the dream, the figure of Flutter Mane quietly overlapped with the image of Roaring Moon, cruelly and brutally preying on a small, pitiful Wattrel.

D woke up in fright, distrustful, he turned over, got out of bed, and went to the ranch to check under the moonlight.

Only upon seeing Flutter Mane "enjoying the moon" on the rooftop did he finally breathe a sigh of relief.

Flutter Mane, sitting on the chimney, heard the rustling sounds from below. It turned its head and saw D draped in a coat, standing on the grass looking up at it.

"Dream?"

Flutter Mane looked a bit confused, thinking this human had something to ask it, so it floated down and came before D.

D felt he was overthinking, he reached out to pat Flutter Mane's head and asked, "How does life feel here?"

"Dream..."

Flutter Mane carefully recalled the life during this period.

There are no dangerous predators here that would ambush it, no need to worry about its territory being invaded by other Pokemon while resting, and no fear of going hungry or finding no food.

Moreover, everything here is gentle.

The night is gentle, the moon is gentle, and even the wind is gentle.

It very much likes the moon of this world and flies leisurely under the moonlight every quiet night.

The only thing that Flutter Mane finds a bit unaccustomed to is that it's too... too quiet here.

Flutter Mane didn't know how to express it, having not fought other Pokemon for so long, it felt uncomfortable, especially with its itchy teeth, always wanting to bite something fiercely.

But fortunately, it could endure this feeling itself.

D had been observing Flutter Mane's expressions, keenly noticing its small teeth-gnashing motions, thinking perhaps Flutter Mane's teeth were itching?

Considering the fierce nature of ancient Pokemon, D pondered for a moment and then asked, "Do you feel a bit uncomfortable all over because you haven't gone hunting?"

Hearing this, Flutter Mane was amazed, its blood-red eyes full of surprise, as if asking how did you know?

D chuckled, then looked at Flutter Mane's sharp little fangs, continuing to ask, "Do you feel like your teeth are itching, and you really want to bite something?"

Flutter Mane was even more astonished.

Seeing this, D understood in his heart.

This was something he had overlooked.

If there was something for Flutter Mane to chew on, would it alleviate its symptoms?

But what should be used? A chew toy?

Just then, D suddenly remembered that there was a thick bone that Teddiursa had picked up from outside at home.

Such bones are very hard and would commonly be used by Marowak and Cubone as weapons in the wild.

If this were given to Flutter Mane, could it ease its symptoms?

So, he immediately turned and went back to the living room, retrieved that big leg bone from the storage room, cleaned it, and handed it to Flutter Mane.

Flutter Mane took it with its wing claws, looked down at the big leg bone before it, and its wild instincts suddenly kicked in, instinctively starting to gnaw at the bone, polishing its teeth with the bone once used by a Marowak as a weapon.

As it gnawed and gnawed, it glanced at D.

Noticing Flutter Mane's little glance, D smiled and gently patted its head: "Behave now!"

Flutter Mane stopped its bone-gnawing action, looked up blankly.

Under the bright moonlight, the human's face appeared so gentle.

It could feel the sincere emotions emanating from the other, filled with expectation, tenderness, blessing...

Moments from its life here started flashing in its mind.

The way this human prepared food for it, taught it not to eat raw meat, taught it how to fight, and the worried face looking at it under the moonlight...

An unfamiliar feeling surged in Flutter Mane's heart once again.

It finally understood why Koraidon liked this human so much.

It felt that it also had started to like D, just a little bit.

*

To adjust Flutter Mane's schedule, the next morning, after finishing work on the ranch, D went to town to buy some coffee bean seeds.

Once back at the ranch, he went to the Glass Greenhouse, found an empty flower pot, dug a trowel of soil, and planted the coffee beans in.

Then, D brought the flower pot back to the living room, hoping to ask Calyrex for help in maturing them.

Calyrex was engrossed in watching TV, upon hearing D's words, it didn't even turn its head, just waved its hand slightly, and a brilliant light burst from the flower pot.

When the light faded, a coffee tree about one and a half meters tall, with its branches laden with coffee berries, appeared in D's arms.

---

D let out a cry of surprise, quickly maintaining his strange posture as he dashed out with the flower pot, and then placed the coffee tree in a corner of the Glass Greenhouse.

D glanced at these coffee fruits.

[Coffee Fruit: The fruit inside can be used to make coffee.]

Next step is to extract the coffee beans from these fruits, and then engage in fermentation, shelling, washing, drying, roasting, and finally grinding them into powder to make coffee!

This whole process took D nearly a week, and only after everything was completed did he start on the last step—brewing.

Reaching out to take the cup handed over by his sister Indeedee, he added coffee powder and hot water to the cup and began brewing.

"I wonder if the coffee I made will have the effect of refreshing the mind…" D murmured to himself while waiting.

Soon, a cup of coffee was brewed.

D immediately checked the effects of this coffee with impatience.

[Coffee (B-): Brewed with freshly ground coffee powder.

Beverage Effect: Sleeplessness lv2, after drinking, the consumer will not be able to fall asleep, duration: 6 hours.

Review: A magical drink that allows people to focus on something; if you feel sleepy, drink a cup! It can keep you fully focused at work.]

It's useful for Flutter Mane!

D felt delighted inside; now he finally had a way to help Flutter Mane adjust its schedule!

Thinking this, D happily turned his head to look at Flutter Mane, who was sleeping soundly on a cushion.

He walked over with the coffee, woke Flutter Mane up, and asked, "Flutter Mane, do you want to play with everyone?"

Flutter Mane was still groggy from sleep, and upon seeing D, it relaxed and let out a yawn.

Play with everyone? Although it wanted to, by the time it woke up, everyone was already asleep!

Flutter Mane zoned out, not understanding D's point, and nodded confusedly.

"Then let's have a drink of this!" D supported Flutter Mane, feeding it the cup of coffee.

Feeling the bitterness in its mouth, Flutter Mane immediately widened its eyes, dispelling its sleepiness, and began retching on the side.

D: "…"

Koraidon, witnessing the scene: "…"

Only after expelling all the bitter taste from its mouth did Flutter Mane breathe a sigh of relief, levitating in mid-air, panting heavily.

At this moment, Flutter Mane finally awoke completely, looking around in bewilderment, unsure of what had happened.

D asked, "Do you still want to sleep?"

Flutter Mane carefully sensed its state and realized it didn't feel a bit sleepy!

Seeing this, D knew the answer.

Seems like the coffee's effect is quite significant!

"Alright, now you can go play with everyone! Koraidon, take Flutter Mane out to play!"

Playing with other Pokemon will surely help Flutter Mane adapt to this era more quickly, getting used to life on the ranch.

Always wandering alone at night without interacting with other Pokemon, it would only grow lonelier.

Receiving the instruction, Koraidon happily took the sleepless Flutter Mane outside to play.

Meanwhile, D began cleaning up the spot where Flutter Mane had vomited the coffee.

"Maybe I should add some milk and sugar to it," D thought, "this way, it shouldn't be so bitter."

After cleaning the floor, he went outside, watching Flutter Mane levitate in mid-air as it observed Alcremie and the others playing below, D nodded in satisfaction.

The first step for Flutter Mane to adapt to modern times had finally been taken!

The coffee lasted only six hours; to help Flutter Mane adjust its schedule, D stayed by its side all day without going anywhere.

The six hours passed, and he immediately brought over the next cup of coffee with milk and sugar.

Flutter Mane tried it again and still vomited it out.

It was evident that it really disliked that taste.

D could only helplessly plead: "Please, Flutter Mane, just endure this for one day; once today is over, it'll be better!"

Flutter Mane watched him for a while, and only when D added a hefty spoonful of sugar did it reluctantly drink it.

After a chaotic day, the day finally ended.

At seven in the evening, after dinner, the coffee's effect finally dissipated, and the energetic Flutter Mane immediately fell into a deep sleep.

D breathed a sigh of relief; this way, there shouldn't be any problem, right?

In the deep of night, D woke up to check and found Flutter Mane was still asleep, which allowed him to breathe a long sigh of relief inside, turning back to the room to sleep peacefully.

Flutter Mane slept for a long time; when it opened its eyes again, it saw D sitting not far away, looking at it.

Seeing Flutter Mane wake up, D greeted it with a smile, "Good morning, Flutter Mane!"

Flutter Mane looked around, appearing somewhat puzzled but didn't say much.

Just then, Glaceon chased Alcremie past its eyes.

The reason was that while Glaceon was sleeping, Alcremie used Decorate on it, spreading cream to make its face look like a calico cat.

---

After spending a day together yesterday, Flutter Mane has gradually begun to accept these gentle, innocent, and lively Pokemon.

It watched Glaceon, who was angrily chasing Alcremie, and couldn't help but smile, seemingly finding the scene amusing.

Glaceon noticed the smiling Flutter Mane and thought it was being laughed at, turning even redder in the face, and immediately got angry, turning its back on Alcremie.

Seeing that Glaceon was upset, Alcremie quickly ran over to apologize, then smeared cream all over its face, turning it into a big cat, thus earning Glaceon's forgiveness.

Flutter Mane's gaze remained fixed on this scene, and the smile on its face grew clearer.

D had been observing Flutter Mane all along.

From this perspective, Flutter Mane isn't much different from Pawmo and the others now!

The plan to guide Flutter Mane was a great success, and D finally felt relieved.

Friendly emotions are contagious, not to mention that Alcremie and Pawmo naturally have very cheerful and lively personalities, enjoying making friends.

Given more time, Flutter Mane should be able to gradually integrate into the life of the pasture with the help of these Pokemon!

Thinking of this, D naturally thought of the two professors at the Area Zero Research Institute.

He wondered what methods they used to help the Paradox Pokemon adapt to this era.

In the game, the two AI professors commanded their captured Paradox Pokemon to battle against players.

And being able to get such fierce Paradox Pokemon to obey them clearly shows that the two professors succeeded too.

But unfortunately, by then, it was all too late.

Too many Paradox Pokemon had come into this era, with some breaking through the defensive line of the Paldea Giant Pit and entering the Paldea Region.

No matter what methods Professor Sada and Professor Turo used to tame the Paradox Pokemon, they couldn't possibly have the same effect on so many Paradox Pokemon all at once.

Forget it! There's no point in thinking about it! Even if he thought, he couldn't stop it.

D put aside these chaotic thoughts and then said to the group of Pokemon in front of him:

"It's time to eat. After we eat, I'm going into town."

"Bamor?" Pawmo immediately asked.

D understood what it meant and replied, "We're running low on food at home, so I'll go buy more meat for you!"

There's no need to buy eggs, milk, or vegetables, as they're grown in the pasture and can be picked and eaten directly from the fields.

After breakfast, D left the household chores to the Indeedee Sibling to manage, then rode the Cyclizar to Porto Marinada Town.

Koraidon, who was more familiar with Flutter Mane, was left at home to play with the Pokemon.

Koraidon: "Aga-sigh..."

Lying on the grass, Koraidon stared idly at the Pokemon playing nearby, not joining in.

Because it was already a big Pokemon and couldn't roll around freely on the grass and mud like the young ones.

Once D arrived at Porto Marinada Town, he headed straight to the Porto Marinada Market to stock up on groceries.

Being a coastal town, Porto Marinada not only sells meat but also seafood.

Things like shrimp, big crabs, squid, oysters, clams, and mussels.

Seeing these things, D suddenly got a craving for seafood and ended up buying several thousand League Coins worth of seafood to make a seafood feast at home.

Remembering some videos of sea foraging he'd watched in his previous life, D suddenly became interested.

Perhaps in a few days, he could visit the seaside to see if he could find any crabs, clams, mussels, or sea snails.

After finishing his shopping, D planned to head straight home.

Riding the Cyclizar through the streets of Porto Marinada, D suddenly noticed a newly opened antique shop in the distance.

At first, he gave it no thought and walked past its entrance.

But not long after, D remembered the Gimmighoul hiding in his home tavern.

So far, he hadn't had much interaction with it.

D had a fondness for Gimmighoul as a Pokemon.

The Gholdengo it evolves into is exceedingly rare, not only collectible but also serving as a lucky charm in the tavern.

But to evolve Gimmighoul into Gholdengo, it requires 999 ancient Paldea Gold Coins.

With this thought, D wondered if this antique shop might have coins that the elusive Gimmighoul would like?

If so, he could give the coins to Gimmighoul to strengthen their bond...

---

# 317: Chapter 314: Kitakami Agricultural Exchange Conference

D had never seen this antique shop before, so it must have just recently opened.

The shop door was a heavy carved wooden door, and as it opened, an ancient, rustic aura greeted him.

At first glance, the store was very spacious, with the bar facing the door, and rows of shelves lined against the wall, displaying various old antiques.

A middle-aged man, wearing round glasses, slicked-back hair, and dressed rather meticulously, was standing behind the bar, using a feather duster to clean the dust beside him.

On the bar, a Litleo lay lazily, snoring away.

Upon hearing the door open, the man immediately put down his feather duster, his eyes shining with a light: "Is there a customer?"

Litleo also raised its head to look over.

"Hello, boss!" D greeted them with a smile.

"What do you want to buy?" The man put down the feather duster, walked forward, and asked, "Are you looking to buy antique paintings for your collection, or perhaps antique tea sets that help Poltchageist and Sinistea evolve? I have everything here!"

Hearing this, D was confused. He knew Sinistea, but what was this Poltchageist Pokemon?

"Poltchageist?" D couldn't help but ask.

The boss looked surprised: "You've never heard of this Pokemon?"

D nodded honestly: "I only know Sinistea."

"I see!" The boss didn't mind and introduced with a smile, "Poltchageist and Sinistea are both Pokemon that inhabit tea sets, and they need specific tea sets to evolve."

"However, it's understandable, as the Paldea Region doesn't have Poltchageist. These Pokemons live far away in Kitakami!"

Kitakami... D instantly understood that although he hadn't heard of Poltchageist's name, based on the three words Kitakami, he could deduce that it's one of the new Pokemon introduced in the Scarlet and Violet DLC.

Speaking of Poltchageist, it seemed the boss had a lot to say.

He let out a sigh, walked to a shelf, pointed to an aged tea bowl on it, and said:

"Look, this is the tea set needed for Poltchageist's evolution!"

"It's said to be the regret of Tea Masters who couldn't reach the pinnacle of the tea ceremony before they died, attached to matcha, becoming this Pokemon."

"In Kitakami, there's a terrifying rumor about Poltchageist [if you waste food, Poltchageist will sprinkle matcha on you, then drain the essence from your body, turning you into a shriveled corpse]!"

"How about it, scary isn't it?" The boss asked.

D: "..."

This made him increasingly curious about Kitakami.

In D's perception, it was an agricultural and tourism-rich idyllic town, producing red apples and rice abundantly.

What a remarkable place! How could there be something this terrifying?

"Feels like it's been deliberately exaggerated like Sinistea and Polteageist," D thought.

Nonetheless, he wasn't there for this matter today.

Although this antique shop just opened, it had a wide variety of antiques.

Medieval knight armor, ancient manuscripts full of ancient scripts, old pottery used before...

When D's gaze lingered on them, the boss would actively introduce them:

"That's armor worn by knights from the Paldea Kingdom era, very well preserved, has been around for five hundred years, and still isn't rusted."

"These are Pokemon observation notes left by ancient scholars from the Paldea Kingdom era. Although the content might be commonplace for modern people, these notes were quite precious and important in those ancient times."

"This? This is also a Paldea Kingdom era item, and it's said that the King once drank water from it."

"..."

D stared silently at the pottery that looked like a chamber pot.

Just then, on the other end of the shelf, he suddenly saw an ancient treasure chest about thirty centimeters high, looking aged.

D was immediately attracted to it.

The boss followed him to the treasure chest: "This treasure chest, like that pottery, also comes from the Paldea Kingdom era."

"Over two thousand years ago, during the reign of the Paldea Kingdom, explorers organized a grand treasure hunt, and this treasure chest was something that some explorers got during their expedition. The treasure inside has been taken, leaving only this empty shell."

"How much is it?" D asked with interest.

If the treasure chest were given to Gimmighoul, it could help it transform from its Roaming Form to its Chest Form, and all the gold coins collected could be stored inside.

The boss said: "I won't hide from you, all the treasure inside this chest has been taken, it's just an empty shell now. If you want it, you can have it for twenty thousand League coins!"

Twenty thousand League coins... which isn't very expensive to him; he could earn it back with a bottle of goat's milk.

But such items don't have an official League price and depend on the vendor's preferences.

D didn't know whether he was getting a good deal or not, so he decided to try bargaining.

Thus, D began to use his limited bargaining experience: "Ten thousand!"

The boss: "..."

"That's too exaggerated, even store-bought decorations aren't this cheap!" The boss glanced at the corner of his mouth: "Moreover, this item has existed for two thousand years and is still a very collectible antique."

---

D: "Fifteen thousand? Besides this, I can also have a long-term collaboration with you."

"Oh?" The shop owner was instantly interested, "What kind of long-term collaboration?"

D had long noticed the two Paldea ancient gold coins placed on the shelf and pointed at them, saying, "As many of these ancient coins as you have, I'll take them all."

Upon hearing this, the shop owner turned around and saw the two ancient coins on the shelf.

These ancient coins also originated from the Paldea Kingdom period, minted by the kingdom, and were once circulating currency.

Some modern collectors specifically collect these historically significant coins for their collections.

But because these ancient coins are mostly scattered around, they aren't very expensive on the market, generally priced at around fifty thousand League currency per coin.

Compared to other antique tea sets, which easily go for two hundred thousand, they're quite cheap.

"You want that?" the shop owner asked.

"To be honest, not long ago, I encountered a Gimmighoul at home. Seeing it likes these coins a lot, I thought of giving it coins to see if I could establish a good relationship with it." D said honestly.

"Gimmighoul?" The shop owner was full of surprise.

The shop owner was puzzled by this young man's appearance as someone who didn't seem to love antiques because it was clear from his eyes.

Those precious antiques were just looked at like roadside cabbages, merely glanced at before he shifted his gaze.

Upon hearing this, he finally understood the reason!

"You want to help Gimmighoul collect coins to help it evolve into Gholdengo?" The shop owner was very shocked.

"Eh?" D was equally surprised, "You know about this Pokemon, Gholdengo?"

"Anyone in this business knows about Gholdengo, right?" the shop owner explained, "Because Gimmighoul originated two thousand years ago during the kingdom era, their existence is intricately linked to these antique treasures."

"Because Gimmighoul was born from explorers' greed for treasure!"

"I see..." D murmured, nodding.

The shop owner continued, "Originally, nobody knew Gimmighoul could evolve until two hundred years ago when it's said a collector became friends with a coin-collecting Gimmighoul."

"That antique collector spent a fortune gathering a large pile of ancient coins from around the world for his beloved Gimmighoul."

"Then, that Gimmighoul merged with these coins and evolved into a Pokemon called Gholdengo!"

"Since then, some people who liked Gholdengo began collecting coins, but Gimmighoul is so timid, they run away upon seeing humans, making it difficult for most to capture them."

"Moreover, these coins are hard to collect, so no second Gholdengo has been born in the Paldea Region to this day."

"Is it really that exaggerated?" Upon hearing this, D was astonished.

He only knew Gholdengo was rare, but not that it was this rare.

Only one has appeared in the past two hundred years...

"Of course!" The shop owner nodded, "Not to hide from you, some antique shops sell ancient coins, mostly priced at around fifty thousand League currency."

"One coin is fifty thousand, a thousand coins would be fifty million League currency! Most people couldn't afford that!"

"Moreover, the ancient coins currently on the market are mostly found by Trainers from ancient ruins and tombs."

"Those ancient ruins are extremely dangerous, although they're filled with precious metals, the tomb and ruins' owners set up deadly traps and powerful Pokemon guards inside to prevent grave robbers after their death."

"Once intruders are detected, those Pokemon will chase after ill-intentioned people without rest to protect their former master's tomb."

D entertaining the thought of grave-robbing: "..."

Forget it, let's change the approach; fighting is too unfriendly.

"I'll take these two ancient coins and this treasure chest," D said, "If you collect any more ancient gold coins in the future, feel free to contact me. I can pay you an additional fee."

"No problem at all!" The shop owner agreed, "But I have one condition."

D calmly thought and then nodded, "Tell me."

The shop owner showed yearning, "If one day you truly raise a Gholdengo, you must bring it for me to see with my own eyes!"

D grinned, "Alright."

He left the farm's phone number for the shop owner, paid, then carried the treasure chest and two coins back to the farm.

D had just gotten off the Cyclizar with seafood purchased in town, when several Dragonite and Dragonair from Dragonite Island gathered around, curiously looking at the bag containing seafood.

"Raaaw?"

D opened the bag to let them have a look, "I bought this from town..."

He intended to say he'd let the Dragonite taste it at noon, but from the look in their eyes, it's clear this wasn't their first time seeing such things.

For Dragonite who live on the sea, these little fish, shrimp, and shells should also be part of their diet.

The distant Dragonite also ran over, joining the working Dragonite around the bag of seafood.

The Dragonair's eyes were sparkling, they looked at the seafood, then at D, showing a sudden expression on their faces.

---

"Woo!" D, do you also like to eat these things?

If so, they can catch more on their way back to Dragonite Island tonight, and bring them back tomorrow morning as a gift for D!

D didn't know what the Dragonairs and Dragonites had in mind. He handed this bag of seafood to his sister, Indeedee, intending to steam it for lunch.

As for himself, he carried the treasure chest and two coins to the tavern.

The tavern was empty during the day. A ray of sunlight streamed through the window, clearly illuminating the dust floating in the air.

It seemed that his brother, Indeedee, had just finished sweeping.

D looked around and didn't see any trace of Gimmighoul, but he was sure that it was still there, just hiding.

He didn't do anything special, just put the treasure chest and two ancient gold coins in the shadow under the bar, then said to the surroundings:

"Gimmighoul, I found a treasure chest and two coins outside today, so I brought them back as a gift for you. Remember to check if you like them later."

After he finished speaking, the air remained silent, without any response.

D didn't mind and left immediately after speaking.

As time ticked by and the sound of footsteps faded away, Gimmighoul slowly peeked its head out from the darkness.

It cautiously surveyed the surroundings, then turned its gaze to the large treasure chest and the two coins beside it.

Gimmighoul didn't rush over immediately; it stared blankly at these things and then jumped onto the bar, looking toward the entrance.

Gimmighoul was very puzzled.

Why was that human being so nice to it? Giving it such precious gifts?

It hadn't done anything at all...

*

After leaving the tavern, D began to handle the seafood at home.

Once he had cleaned all the shells and conchs, it was midday.

D was about to steam them when he suddenly heard Mayor Thomas' voice outside.

"Is D home? Good afternoon, Cyclizar! Ah, Koraidon! You too!"

Upon hearing this, D was a bit confused.

Huh? Why would Mayor Thomas come to the ranch at this time?

He put down his work and went outside: "Mayor Thomas, is there something you need?"

Mayor Thomas nodded with a smile: "The town recently received an invitation."

"An invitation?" D was even more puzzled and quickly invited Mayor Thomas to sit inside.

After they sat on the sofa, Mayor Thomas explained the purpose of his visit.

"It's like this: last year, Porto Marinada Town had a great harvest and provided a lot of fresh vegetables and crops to other parts of the Paldea Region. Because of this, Porto Marinada Town has become more renowned in agricultural planting."

"Not long ago, the town received an invitation from Kitakami. They want to hold an agricultural exchange meeting with us and have invited an experienced farmer from our town to go to Kitakami to share planting techniques with the farmers there."

"After a town-wide discussion, we've decided to send you to attend the exchange meeting, so I came today to ask if you have time."

D understood. He had just heard from the antique store owner about the frightening rumors of Kitakami earlier and was thinking about visiting when he had the time.

Didn't expect the opportunity to come now?

Though he's not very experienced in farming, relying on Diglett for tilling, Gogoat for crop growth, and Dragonite for watering...

But that's okay, he can go there to preach about Calyrex!

With this in mind, D immediately asked, "When?"

Mayor Thomas cheerfully replied, "In three days. However, Kitakami is some distance away, and you'll have to take a plane to get there."

---

---

---

---

# 318: Chapter 315: Competing Dragonites

D previously didn't know the precise location of Kitakami.

Before he crossed over to this world, the Violet DLC hadn't been released yet, so he instinctively thought Kitakami was in the shadowy northeast part of the Paldea Region.

But now it seems that's not the case.

There lies an endless mountain range never shown in the anime, and heading north through those mountains takes you to the Kalos Region.

As of now, according to Mayor Thomas, Kitakami seems to be a very remote village in the south of the Sinnoh Region, northeast of the Kanto Region.

Mayor Thomas: "There's no airport in Porto Marinada Town, so you'll have to fly from the neighboring Cascarrafa City. Don't worry, all travel expenses will be covered by the town."

D nodded, simultaneously pondering.

Today is February 26, League Year 199, and three days later on the 29th, he will depart from Porto Marinada Town to go to Kitakami for a week-long agricultural exchange.

It's a perfect time to handle some things piling up at the ranch during these three days.

"Okay, I've got it."

Mayor Thomas smiled and nodded, then took out a card and handed it over.

"This represents the exchange member identification badge. When you get there, someone will meet you at the station. Present this badge, and the people in Kitakami will arrange everything for you."

D reached out to take it, then thanked Mayor Thomas.

It was late, and after everything was explained, Mayor Thomas got up to leave.

"I have to head back, just remember to set off later. Don't worry about the ranch, the town will help look after it."

"Alright."

D escorted Mayor Thomas to the door, watched his figure disappear down the road, and then turned back to the living room.

"AiYi?" His sister, Indeedee, pointed to the seafood in the bowl, asking if he wanted to continue preparing the ingredients.

D glanced at it, and then remembered the matter at hand: "No need, I've already washed them clean, just steam them."

With that said, he stepped forward, taking over Indeedee's position, and poured clams, sea snails, oysters, little shrimp, crabs, and other seafood into the steamer.

In the meantime, D crushed some garlic, made a simple garlic sauce, and prepared a few bowls of dipping sauces.

Porto Marinada Town is by the sea, so the seafood sold in the market is freshly caught in the morning, making them very lively.

Soon, the unique fragrance of seafood accompanied by steam wafted from the pot.

Seeing that it's about done, D lifted the lid, and poured the pile of seafood into a large iron basin.

D looked down, and in the next second, information about the seafood emerged from his mind.

[Seafood Stew (C+): A marine dish made from various kinds of seafood, possessing a unique ocean flavor.

Effect: Small Fish and Seafood lv1, moderately increases Water Type Pokemon's friendliness, only effective for Water Type Pokemon.

Rating: A simple seafood feast, a very common dish in life!]

"Small Fish and Seafood? Increases Water Type Pokemon friendliness?" D was quite surprised.

It seems his ranch doesn't really have any Water Type Pokemon...

Oh, right, he almost forgot those silly Psyduck!

D immediately prepared to separate some shells and shrimp to send to those Psyduck later.

Dragonites really love seafood.

As Pokemon living on an island, most of their food sources are in the vast ocean.

Fish, squid, shrimp, and shellfish from the sea are all on their menu.

Every morning, they wake up promptly and catch some fish from the sea to fill their stomachs.

After they are full, the Dragonites fly to the ranch to work and savor those delicious Poffins.

At noon, they don't go back to Dragonite Island, so D prepares nutritious sandwiches for them to fill their stomachs.

They then stay in the ranch until the evening, flying back to Dragonite Island after the sun sets.

Therefore, this seafood is not considered unusual for them.

But through D's cooking, the taste of this seafood is noticeably better compared to before!

D clearly saw Dragonite No. 2's eyes light up after eating a squid dipped in sauce.

Soon, Dragonite No. 3, Dragonite No. 4, and the faces of those five Dragonairs all showed expressions of enjoying the food.

Compared to the Dragonites, Koraidon and Cyclizar aren't too fond of this seafood.

They prefer big, juicy roasted meat over these salty-smelling weird foods.

One bite and the juice explodes kind.

So, the entire basin of seafood was eaten clean by the Dragonites.

After eating, they still looked like they wanted more.

D: "..."

*

In the evening, after playing all day at the ranch, the Dragonites and Dragonairs carried the Poffin Box and happily returned to Dragonite Island.

They sat in front of the central lake, cheerfully eating all the Poffins in the box, then a few Dragonites began discussing serious matters.

Dragonite No. 2: "Roar!" (D, like us, also likes to eat fish!)

Dragonair No. 1: "Woo~" (Tomorrow morning, I want to catch lots and lots of fish for D!)

---

Dragonite Four: "Roar!" (I want to catch it too!)

Dragonite Three drooled with greed: "Roar..." (Today's little fishes were really delicious! I want more...)

So, early the next morning before dawn, several Dragonites and Dragonairs plunged into the vast blue ocean under the puzzled eyes of their companions.

Every Dragonite is an expert swimmer, moving through the water like underwater torpedoes, swiftly traversing the ocean.

Soon, Dragonite Two emerged from the sea, clutching a large lobster with its claws.

The lobster, sensing danger, frantically flapped its tail, trying to escape.

Dragonite Two knocked it unconscious with a swipe and happily placed it in the Poffin Box, ready to take it to the ranch.

Then, there was a splash on the sea surface.

Dragonite Two turned around and saw Dragonite Four emerge from the water, holding a small, light-blue Pokemon dolphin with a white belly.

It tightly hugged the dolphin with its big pudgy claws and quickly turned to its companions, asking, "Roar?" (Will D like this?)

Before anyone could answer, the captured Finizen smacked Dragonite Four's face with its tail, broke free, and angrily used Water Gun against it.

Dragonite Four got sprayed in the face, watching helplessly as the Finizen escaped.

"Roar..."

Forget it, this Pokemon's personality is too bad, not obedient at all; D definitely won't like it.

Dragonite Four gave up on Finizen, dove back into the sea, and soon emerged holding an oversized tuna, knocked out by electricity.

It let out a joyful growl: "Roar!"

Yesterday's tiny fish and shrimp were too small; they didn't satisfy their appetite. This big fish will surely fill their bellies!

Seeing Dragonite Four catch such a big fish, Dragonite Two immediately felt the lobster in its claws was less appealing.

It casually tossed the lobster away, dove back into the ocean, and soon re-emerged with a large salmon, looking content and satisfied.

By then, other Dragonites and Dragonairs had caught their own prey.

There were large lobsters, big squids, and a pile of large crabs.

Carrying these big fish and shrimp, the Dragonites drooled as they flew eagerly toward the ranch.

*

7:25 am, D was milking three Gogoats.

He bottled the fresh goat milk and asked Koraidon and Cyclizar to store it in the milk cellar.

Meanwhile, Dragonite was next to the farm, using Rain Dance as always to help D water the fields.

Just then, the sky reverberated with a series of "Roar, roar" calls.

Hearing that sound, D knew without looking that the working Dragonites were arriving for their shift.

As usual, he raised his head to greet them.

But when D saw what was in front of him, he froze on the spot.

What is that?!

Each Dragonite and Dragonair carried something.

Two Dragonites held two huge fish with their big claws, while another Dragonite carried two large lobsters.

Even the surrounding Dragonairs had squids and crabs in their tails and mouths, along with two bunches of lush green seaweed.

"Roar~"

Seeing D, the Dragonites showed joyful expressions.

They flew to D and presented the gifts they caught.

Witnessing the Dragonites' actions, D was completely baffled: "For me?"

"Roar!" The Dragonairs nodded repeatedly, looking extremely expectant.

The surprise came too suddenly; D was dumbfounded for quite a while.

He lowered his head to look at the seafood carelessly strewn on the grass by the Dragonites. Information about these ingredients also surfaced in his mind.

The leftmost Dragonite brought a fish about one meter long, spindle-shaped, with a silvery-blue back and black spots above the lateral line.

It was [Salmon].
