The second Dragonite brought a fish over two meters long, with a sleek, streamlined body and blue dorsal fins.

It was [Bluefin Tuna].

The third Dragonite brought two large lobsters, seemingly still alive, slowly waving their claws.

The remaining Dragonairs offered squid, sardines, blue crabs, seaweed, and small shrimp.

Other Pokemon in the ranch were also drawn to the commotion happening there.

Pawmot and Alcremie all gathered around, shock on their faces upon seeing the tuna, which was many times bigger than them.

"Pawmo?!"

With big eyes looking at him, Dragonites and Dragonairs surrounded D, moving him greatly.

He stepped forward to touch each of their heads, smiling as he said, "Thank you all, I really love these gifts."

"Roar~" The working Dragonites felt very happy.

Dragonairs also purred as they encircled D.

Nearby, a Dragonite, having just finished watering, widened its eyes in shock upon seeing this.

It felt a bit jealous and angry.

Because these unruly Dragonites had secretly given gifts to D without inviting it!

Darn! They were surely trying to compete with it for D!

Using gift-giving to catch D's attention, so that D would like them...

---

Seeing D's touched expression as he petted those pesky Dragonites, it was clear that D was already starting to like them!

The more the Dragonite thought about it, the more panicked it felt. It even pictured D happily sitting at the dinner table with those Dragonites while it was forgotten in a dark corner, crying alone.

Darn it! This must not happen!

"Awoooo!"

The Dragonite roared, wanting to dash over immediately, grab D, and tell those Dragonites that D was its Trainer.

But then it realized that those Dragonites had given gifts to D, while it had done nothing...

No way!

The jealous Dragonite looked at D, who had turned around upon hearing its growl, and then flew away in a huff.

It was going to catch an even bigger fish to bring back to the ranch for D, completely outshining those Dragonites!

Seeing the Dragonite's actions, D immediately realized that his affectionate dragon was jealous.

He quickly called out, "Dragonite!"

Dragonite didn't turn back, its speed was fast, and in the blink of an eye, it disappeared into the horizon.

D panicked, immediately looking to Koraidon, hoping to ask it to take him to bring back the sulking Dragonite.

Koraidon looked speechless, squatting on the ground with a yawn, its expression very calm, clearly not worried about Dragonite at all.

D: "?"

"You think Dragonite will be okay?" D asked, very worried.

"Ah gah hiss..."

Koraidon simply closed its eyes, beginning to feign sleep.

D: "..."

Could it be that Koraidon was also jealous like Dragonite?

For a moment, D didn't know what to do.

For the first time in his life, he felt the trouble of having too many Pokemon.

Instead, those Dragonites at work quickly regained their composure after an initial shock.

They adjusted their little red satchels, took MooMoo Milk from their sister Indeedee, and happily flew off to Porto Marinada Town to start their daily work.

D, however, looked towards the direction Dragonite had disappeared, feeling very worried.

Pawmots stayed by D's side, looking up with him in that direction.

"Pawmo..."

D now had no mind to deal with the fish that the Dragonites had given.

He tossed all the fish into the ranch's lake for now to store them.

After finishing all this, D was about to leave, but then he noticed a purple vortex appearing out of nowhere on the lake's surface.

In the next second, all those fish vanished without a trace.

D: "..."

It was like a house leaking in the rain at night; Giratina must have taken these fish as offerings from him.

D felt an instant headache.

At the same time, over the distant ocean.

Having used Agility several times to reach its speed limit, Dragonite was swiftly traversing the ocean.

Soon, it overtook a flying airplane, causing a series of surprised exclamations from the passengers in the cabin.

Ignoring the airplane, Dragonite flew on for a while and then dove into the ocean, trying to find a bigger fish inside.

However, it swam for a long time without seeing a single one.

Dragonite was unwilling to give up. It had to catch a bigger fish to outshine those Dragonites!

So, Dragonite left the ocean and returned to high altitude, preparing to go further afield.

It flew over the ocean, over the Galar Region, over the Unova Region, over the Alola Region, looped around the Sinnoh Region, and finally arrived at the Hoenn Region.

Looking at the unfamiliar sea below, Dragonite plunged straight in.

---

---

---

---

# 319: Chapter 316: Dragonite and the Incarnation of the Ocean

In this unfamiliar sea, unfamiliar Pokémon thrive.

The Clamperl, shaped like a pearl, its body encased in a hard shell,

the slender Gorebyss, with fine pink scales covering its body, swimming in the water like cherry blossoms drifting down from treetops,

and the heart-shaped Luvdisc...

Dragonite grew up in the water from a young age and is very familiar with the ocean.

It quickly shuttles through the blue sea, its gaze sweeping over the unfamiliar fish-type Pokémon.

Simultaneously, it evaluates these fish-type Pokémon:

This one won't do, this one is too small, this one looks too fierce, this one won't work either...

Having roamed around, Dragonite finds none larger than that tuna.

Its heart is very reluctant, should it give up just like that?

No, absolutely not!

Thinking of D stroking the Dragonite's heads, Dragonite is instantly engulfed in blazing determination.

It twists its body and finds an older Huntail in the ruins of a sunken ship on the seafloor, asking if there are any large fish nearby.

"Awoo?"

Hearing the sound, the elderly Huntail, residing in the old cabin, peeks its head out of a porthole.

Its eyesight is excellent, thus even in this relatively dark sea, it can clearly see the stranger Pokémon.

Huntail has lived here for over sixty years and has never seen such a Pokémon before.

"Goo?" (What did you say?)

Dragonite repeats, "Awoo!"

"Goo..." Huntail, hearing it clearly this time, yeah, a big fish? It's heard of some.

Thus, it answers, "Goo goo." (I've seen a very large Sharpedo nearby before, don't know if it's the Pokémon you're looking for.)

Sharpedo? Dragonite has never heard of this name before and its eyes light up with curiosity, quickly asking, "Awoo?" (How big is it?)

Huntail stretches its neck, moving closer to inspect Dragonite carefully before responding, "Goo!" (About as big as you!)

Dragonite glances at its own chest, and the joy that had just risen in its heart instantly dissipates.

What? It's still not bigger!

No, what it is searching for is an even larger fish!

At this point, Huntail doesn't know what to say.

Its old fish face reveals a troubled look, "Goo." (If that's the case, then I don't know.)

"Awoo..." Dragonite is very disappointed, turning around to leave, planning to explore other places.

Just as it's about to ascend, however, Huntail suddenly seems to remember something and calls out to it.

"Goo!" (Oh right! I remembered!)

Dragonite immediately stops, turning around to look.

Appearing ancient, Huntail begins telling Dragonite a story:

"Goo, goo goo." (When I was young, I heard other Pokémon living here say that in an underwater cavern so dark you couldn't see your hand, there resides a very powerful fish-type Pokémon.)

"Goo." (Once, a young Luvdisc wandered into its resting place due to losing its way, and upon seeing this Pokémon, the poor Luvdisc was scared stiff.)

"Goo." (It said it's the biggest fish-type Pokémon it ever saw.)

"Awoo?!"

Upon hearing this, Dragonite's eyes light up once more.

Really?

Huntail nods, telling Dragonite, "Goo." (Legend has it that place is in the underwater canyon ahead, but it's too terrifying there. Normally, no Pokémon goes down there; it's said those who do are eaten by Dark Pokémon.)

Dragonite can't hear the rest, as it's already heading straight toward that direction.

Huntail watches its receding figure, eyes wide in thought: what a daring Pokémon!

Dragonite pays no mind, and swims ten kilometers ahead in one go, quickly arriving at the canyon the old Huntail mentioned.

Looking down at the bottomless abyss, Dragonite dives in.

It keeps swimming, until even the last thread of light is consumed by darkness.

With the resolve "to catch the biggest fish for D," it fearlessly plunges into the darkness.

It gathers the Electric Type energy in its body, using Spark to light up the surrounding environment.

Soon, Dragonite arrives at the end of this trench, yet still hasn't seen the huge Pokémon Huntail mentioned.

Dragonite is puzzled.

It stops, turning its head to look around.

Then suddenly, not far in the canyon, it sees a huge opening.

Seeing this scene, Dragonite's eyes instantly light up.

That must be the home of the big fish-type Pokémon!

Thus, it charges straight in, advancing through the cave, and after traversing the interconnected tunnels, faint rays of light suddenly appear above.

Dragonite swims toward that spot.

The next second, it surfaces, arriving at a vast underwater cavern.

Dragonite looks around, and when it sees the surroundings clearly, the entire dragon is momentarily stunned.

Inside the cavern, there's a blue lake.

Beams of light, originating from unknown sources, shine through the top of the cavern, reflecting the shimmering blue lake, dreamlike and illusory.

---

On the lake's surface, a large, whale-like mysterious Pokémon was quietly sleeping.

Its body was a deep blue, like the vast ocean, exuding a majestic and boundless aura.

It had two huge pectoral fins, each fin tipped with four white square structures, its body covered with mysterious and ancient red patterns.

"Argh..." (That's a big fish...)

Dragonite murmured to itself.

This Fish-type Pokémon's size far exceeded that of the tuna there and was clearly favored by D!

Dragonite's heart was restless.

Yet at that moment, the Pokémon, which had been sleeping with closed eyes, suddenly opened them as if sensing something.

An ancient oceanic aura surged around it.

Golden, intimidating pupils locked onto Dragonite, the intruder.

Sensing that aura, Dragonite's face changed slightly, its body froze, instantly realizing this was a powerful Pokémon!

In the next moment, an ancient, heavy voice echoed in its heart:

"Intruder, what is your purpose here?"

*

Paldea Region, Porto Marinada Town, D Ranch.

D worriedly looked in the direction where Dragonite flew off.

Two days had passed.

It's been two days since Dragonite left, and it still hasn't returned...

The Pokedex says Dragonite can circumnavigate the Earth in just 16 hours, but 48 hours have passed.

This time was enough for Dragonite to circle the globe three times.

Yet Dragonite still hadn't returned.

From D's understanding of Dragonite, his Dragonite clearly wasn't the type to leave home out of spite and not return.

And now...

D couldn't help but think: could it be delayed by something?

Or encountered some danger? Like Pokémon Hunters, Team Rocket, or Team Galactic, some evil organizations.

D was restless and lost interest in his upcoming Kitakami journey.

In this situation, even if he wanted to look for Dragonite, he couldn't.

Because the world is so vast, he had no idea where Dragonite went or where it is now.

D was worried, unable to eat or sleep peacefully, beginning to reflect if he did something wrong.

He went to town specifically to find Miss Joy for advice on what to do if a Pokémon leaves home upset.

Porto Marinada Town's Miss Joy couldn't help but ask: "Did a Pokémon leave home from the ranch?"

D nodded: "... It's Dragonite."

He elaborated to Miss Joy about the day Dragonite at the ranch gave him several fish while working, and he couldn't resist petting their heads because they were so adorable.

As a result, Dragonite saw this scene and immediately used the move Extreme Speed, leaving home at the speed of light.

"I see..." Miss Joy looked enlightened: "If I guess correctly, Dragonite probably got jealous seeing you pet other Dragonite and that's why it left home."

Seeing D's worried expression, Miss Joy explained softly:

"Pokémon, like humans, can throw tantrums. Often, when Trainers treat a specific Pokémon excellently, it leads to jealousy among other Pokémon in the team, causing them to leave home."

D: "..."

He knew, but what could he do?

The world has so many cute Pokémon; he wants to pet and hug every single one.

"In these situations, Trainers need to pay more attention to the Pokémon's feelings and try to evenly distribute your affection among all your Pokémon," Miss Joy said.

Therefore, major Leagues encourage Trainers to travel with six Pokémon, as more than that, a Trainer simply can't care for them properly.

This leads to Pokémon throwing tantrums, discord in the team, affecting the Trainer's overall strength.

Listening to Miss Joy's advice, D for a moment didn't know how to react.

It felt like Trainers were like philandering individuals...

But he genuinely just loved Pokémon! Not like some philanderer stringing Pokémon along! Playing with their emotions...

The kindly working Dragonite would bring him surprise gifts, always appearing so gentle and obedient; who could resist!

D appeared conflicted.

Miss Joy couldn't help but console: "Don't worry, Dragonite is a very gentle Pokémon; it will surely return to you, Mr. D."

She had seen D Ranch's Dragonite, a very cute Pokémon, with a gentle and obedient temperament, often carrying a little red bag flying around town, delivering goods to people.

Quite a few town residents liked it!

"Sigh." D sighed quietly.

Miss Joy continued: "Trainers have encountered similar situations before."

"There was a girl who met a swimming-proficient Piplup on her way home, and after that, they started living together."

"Later, that girl met several other Pokémon and cared for them just like she cared for Piplup, driving Piplup to jealousy, leading to it leaving home angrily."

"It swam across the sea from the Sinnoh Region to the Galar Region, and thanks to the assistance of kind Trainers, Piplup eventually returned to the girl's side, even setting a Guinness World Record, winning an inter-regional swimming championship!"

---

The afternoon at the Pokemon Center wasn't crowded with Trainers, and Miss Joy was leisurely chatting with D.

The TV in the Pokemon Center lobby was looping yesterday's news.

D sighed again, thanked Miss Joy, and prepared to return to the ranch to continue waiting for Dragonite to come home.

But just as he was about to turn and leave, a news report on the TV suddenly caught his attention.

"Purple Orchid TV Station is reporting, yesterday at 4 PM near the waters of Sootopolis City, an unprecedented giant tsunami occurred."

"It is reported that it was caused by an unknown Pokemon. Now, let's interview the eyewitnesses from that time."

Hearing this, D stopped and turned to look.

The date of the news broadcast was yesterday.

Yesterday... he was too worried about Dragonite to watch much TV.

The female reporter on TV was handing the microphone to a well-built, blonde woman wearing a swimsuit.

"It was terrifying! Yesterday, I was surfing in the ocean with my Goldeen, and that huge wave suddenly appeared! I was so scared. Upon looking closely, I saw a super-sized blue Pokemon fighting with a Dragonite!"

Then, the camera shifted, and the microphone was handed to the second eyewitness.

It was a young man wearing a swimsuit and holding a surfboard.

"It's true! I saw it too! That gigantic mysterious Pokemon was battling with that Dragonite! I thought a Trainer was capturing Pokemon, but it turns out it was a wild Dragonite! So strange, since Dragonite doesn't typically reside in the Hoenn Region."

The third person interviewed was a beach tourist wearing a floral shirt, a hat, and a camera hanging from his neck.

He contributed the rare photographs he risked his life to capture yesterday.

In the photo, an ancient-looking blue Pokemon was riding the huge wave, charging towards a Dragonite.

"Look, this is what it looked like! Unfortunately, I was too far away at the time, so I couldn't find a better shooting angle!"

"The battle ended quickly, I saw that Dragonite was defeated by the blue Pokemon, fell into the sea, then the giant wave disappeared, the rain stopped, and the blue Pokemon vanished!"

D: "???"

Staring at the familiar figure in the photograph on the TV screen, D was completely dumbfounded.

Beside him, Miss Joy also widened her eyes in disbelief, pointing at the screen and murmuring:

"Is that... Dragonite?"

D fell silent.

Because he recognized at a glance that the Dragonite fighting Kyogre was his own Dragonite.

So... what exactly happened? How did Dragonite end up confronting Kyogre?

But then, D remembered what the tourist had just said about Dragonite being knocked down and falling into the sea.

D's heart tightened.

Those interviewed may not recognize Kyogre, but he certainly did!

Kyogre, embodiment of the sea, creator of the primal ocean.

Even with the boost from the Storm Steamed Bun, the current Dragonite wouldn't stand a chance against Kyogre!

Imagining the scene of Dragonite being severely injured and falling into the sea at the brink of death, D felt awful.

No, he must take Koraidon and Calyrex to the Hoenn Region!

If necessary, he must also ask his good neighbor to come out of retirement!

---

---

---

---

# 320: Chapter 317: I Don't Want the Big Fish, I Just Want You

However, this trip to the Hoenn Region, D ultimately could not go.

Because just as he hurriedly arrived at the ranch, Dragonite returned covered in wounds.

Seeing this scene, D was stunned for a moment, then immediately rushed forward to check Dragonite's injuries.

Dragonite's scales were damaged, and there were red marks on its belly from intense impacts, looking very battered and spiritless.

"Why did you go so far away?" D, feeling a bit distressed, quickly called his brother Indeedee to fetch a bottle of Life Sheep Milk for Dragonite to drink.

After drinking the milk, Dragonite finally recovered, but remained dispirited.

It drooped its head and glanced at D, feeling very saddened in its heart.

It failed to catch a fish bigger than a tuna to give to D and lost to that big Fish-type Pokemon, only to return sheepishly to the ranch.

"Blargh..."

Dragonite felt like it had lost face before D.

"Forget it, I'm just glad you're back regardless." D sighed and reached out to pat Dragonite's head, apologizing, "Sorry, I neglected your feelings, I won't do that again."

Upon hearing this, a spark of light flickered in Dragonite's forlorn eyes, but it quickly drooped its head sadly once more.

D led it into the living room to Calyrex's side, asking Him to help with translating.

"Tell me, why did you suddenly run away from home?" D asked sternly.

Running away from home? Dragonite was baffled; it hadn't run away!

"Blargh, blargh!"

So next, with Calyrex's translation, D finally understood the whole story.

As it turns out, Dragonite didn't run away but intended to venture into the ocean to catch a bigger fish for him.

But when it wandered around the sea near Paldea, it didn't encounter a fish bigger than itself.

So it flew elsewhere, and after a long search and inquiring with local Pokemon, it finally found a large Fish-type Pokemon.

D: "..."

This peculiar spirit of competition and desire for victory.

Upon hearing this, D immediately grasped how Dragonite got involved with Kyogre.

Dragonite: "Blargh, blargh..." (That Pokemon dwells deep, deep underwater...)

It recounted the scene to D in detail.

At that time, it had barged into the Pokemon's abode, awakening the sleeping figure.

In the mysterious and quiet underwater cavern, that Pokemon gazed at it with golden eyes, its voice as profound as the sea:

"Intruder, what brings you here?"

Dragonite was stunned by the majestic aura emitted by the opponent, and stammeringly explained its purpose:

"Blargh..." (I'm searching for the biggest fish in the world to give to D...)

The biggest fish? Absurd! It was the embodiment of the sea!

Kyogre was a bit angry, and as for the mentioned D... he sounded like a human name.

A weak Dragonite intended to send it to a human?

The Sea God was infuriated, and the surrounding sea waters seemed to boil, stirring up monstrous waves instantly.

Dragonite realized the danger, its scales bristling, quickly summoning its energy to prepare a defense.

Strands of white airflow surged around it, and suddenly a gust tore through the dense cavern.

Just when Dragonite thought the opponent was about to lose control and attack, the mysterious Pokemon suddenly paused.

Kyogre gazed at the Dragonite confusedly because it sensed a sea-origin vibe emanating from Dragonite.

This vibe made Kyogre gradually calm down.

It wasn't out of fear; it just suddenly recalled its arch-nemesis.

Currently, the earth and ocean barely maintained balance.

If its power were slightly diminished, its nemesis would likely seize the opportunity to struggle for natural energy in the world, expanding its territory.

It didn't wish to see Groudon standing on Mt. Pyre setting off fireworks.

Hence, the calming Kyogre returned to the lake, addressing the overreaching Dragonite:

"Defeat me, and you shall earn my approval."

Then, Dragonite challenged Kyogre.

Kyogre didn't use its own energy and moves; it merely harnessed the Water Power hidden in the surrounding sea to effortlessly defeat Dragonite.

The defeated Dragonite fell into the ocean, while Kyogre returned to the underwater cavern, resuming its sleep, awaiting the opportune moment.

The struggle between earth and ocean will never cease, whether decades, centuries, or much further into the future.

Someday, the conflict between Kyogre and Groudon will reignite.

D: "..."

It seems he misunderstood Kyogre; it wasn't Kyogre who attacked Dragonite, rather Dragonite provoked Kyogre.

It's just unclear why the enraged Kyogre didn't intentionally harm Dragonite, instead offering a challenge for acknowledgment.

D pondered over the relationships of those Legendary Pokemon in the Hoenn Region, quickly finding the answer.

— Because of Groudon.

D sighed and told Dragonite, "We'll let it go this time, but never attempt such dangerous things again, understand?"

Dragonite was very saddened: "Blargh..."

---

It just wanted to catch a big fish for D, like those working Dragonites.

D understood Dragonite's intention and sighed in his heart. He reached out and patted Dragonite's head, saying to it:

"I don't want the biggest fish in the world."

Dragonite raised its head and stared blankly at him.

D then stepped forward and hugged Dragonite proactively: "Don't do anything dangerous. I don't care about big fish or the gifts you bring me. I only care about your safety, understood?"

Dragonite's eyes lit up completely, and it reached out its large paw, emotionally hugging D back: "Roar!"

D smiled, patting Dragonite on the back and said to it:

"We don't need to take risks, as long as the fish is enough to eat. Let's live our lives together, safely and securely like now."

Dragonite nodded happily.

Only then did D let go of Dragonite.

"Alright! I'm glad you're back! Mrs. Dante and the others were very worried about you these past two days. I'll give you some cookies and Poffins to bring over and let them know you're safe!"

"Roar~"

Dragonite readily agreed, stayed a while longer with D at the ranch, then picked up the gifts and flew to Porto Marinada Town.

The working Dragonites and Dragonairs at the ranch looked at Dragonite's happy, almost-flying appearance, with confused and puzzled expressions.

"Roar?" Did it find Poffins on the way? A Dragonite asked.

To them, finding Poffins on the road was indeed something to be happy about.

The other Dragonites shook their heads, indicating they didn't know either.

Not far away, Koraidon, basking in the sun on the grass, glanced at Dragonite, lazily yawned, closed its eyes, and continued to enjoy the beautiful afternoon.

*

League Year 199, February 29.

Early in the morning, D bid farewell to a group of Pokemon on the ranch and rode Koraidon to Cascarrafa City.

Because this business trip was for an agricultural exchange, there probably wouldn't be much time for sightseeing. Moreover, Kitakami is a very remote rural town, so D only brought Koraidon, Pawmo, Cyclizar, Flutter Mane, and Dragonite with him.

The rest of the Pokemon stayed behind at the ranch to maintain its normal operations.

At nine in the morning, D arrived at Cascarrafa Airport.

Since Koraidon couldn't be placed in a Poke Ball, D had bought a plane ticket for Koraidon as well.

But due to Koraidon's large size, it didn't fit in the airplane seats, so D had to, upon the suggestion of the flight attendants, place it in the luggage compartment.

Seeing Koraidon's aggrieved eyes, D patted its head: "Be good, we'll be there soon!"

"Gah-sis..." Koraidon replied pitifully.

D felt bad but had no choice. He didn't know the exact location of Kitakami, so he couldn't directly ride a Pokemon there. The best way for such cross-regional travel was by airplane and ships.

Settling back into the cabin, the plane took off normally.

D closed his eyes for a nap.

Partway through, he awoke, drank some water, visited the restroom, and then sat down to read a book.

After an unknown amount of time, the plane's broadcast finally announced its imminent arrival at the destination.

D put away his book. Once the plane landed, he quickly went to the luggage compartment to find Koraidon, who was huddled grievance-stricken in a corner, and gave it a big kiss on the forehead.

"Poor little guy, you've been through a lot. We've arrived at our destination, time to go!"

"Gah-sis?!" Koraidon widened its eyes in disbelief, then happily responded to D with a "Lick" attack.

The passengers behind originally thought from the conversation that it must have been a small, cute Pokemon like Eevee or Pikachu.

Upon closer inspection, they realized it was a huge creature with a fierce-looking face, making them silently wonder, "How is this a little guy?"

D didn't notice the other people's expressions. He led Koraidon off the plane, and the first thing he did was find the local Pokemon Center to use the video phone to contact the ranch and let everyone know he was safe.

After finishing all this, D took out a map, following the planned route to Kitakami.

Since Kitakami's location was far from big cities and in a secluded rural area, one could only reach there by taking a bus at a bus station.

But with Koraidon, even without taking the bus, it was possible.

So, after planning the route, D rode onto Koraidon's back, took to the skies, and headed towards Kitakami.

On the way, D kept reading the Kitakami travel guide he bought at the airport.

It said on it that Kitakami wasn't the name of a town but the name of a large region.

There was only one small town there, called Mossui Town.

Interestingly, the guide mentioned that many legends were also passed down in Mossui Town.

The one that attracted D the most was the legend of the "Ghost of Kitakami."

The content described it as follows:

"Legend has it that a long, long time ago, there was a very frightening ghost in Kitakami, residing in the mountain behind the village, usually scaring those entering the mountain.

One day, the ghost became furious, suddenly coming down the mountain, causing panic among the villagers; at that time, Okidogi, Munkidori, and Fezandipiti happened to be in the village.

---

The three Pokémon risked their lives, finally driving the ghost back into the mountains. The villagers affectionately called these brave creatures the Loyal Three and gave them a proper burial, building statues of them at the site.

"Interesting." D was intrigued; Pokémon killing other Pokémon?

If there were content like this in the games, it would be the first time he'd seen such a straightforward depiction of Pokémon death.

However, from this legend, it seems the ghost didn't actually die, it was just driven into the mountains, while the three Loyal Three perished.

Three against one and they were wiped out... How powerful must that ghost have been?

At this moment, D looked up to see what lay ahead. Upon seeing the distant scene, he knew in his heart: Kitakami had arrived.

Looking down from the sky, Kitakami features towering mountains, with the foothills serving as a living space for people. Nestled here is a quaint, Japanese-style rural town.

The town is surrounded by farmland, apple orchards, and other serene landscapes that differ from the Paldea Region.

"Is this Kitakami?" D's eyes sparkled as he admired the idyllic countryside.

He had to admit, this place was quite nice!

He looked down at the road below, then patted Koraidon's back, signaling it to descend.

Because Mayor Thomas had said there would be someone at the station to pick him up and take him to Mossui Town.

When Koraidon landed at the roadside bus stop, D indeed saw an elderly woman with white hair.

He stepped forward and showed his ID badge, which made the old woman express a surprised look.

"W-where did you come from?"

D smiled slightly and pointed to Koraidon behind him, saying, "My Pokémon flew me here."

"You really startled me; I didn't see a bus, and now I know why! Hello, D, welcome to Kitakami. I am Yukiko from the community center. Come on, I'll take you into town!" said the elderly woman named Yukiko.

"Thank you very much." D politely thanked her, and then followed Yukiko along the concrete road, with Koraidon behind him.

Kitakami has a twelve-hour time difference with the Paldea Region, and they had spent considerable time on the road; it was almost dark by the time they got there.

D looked around, gazing at Pokémon in the grass that were hard to see in the Paldea Region.

Spinarak, Poochyena, Sewaddle...

Gradually, as Mossui Town drew nearer, neat patches of rice fields began to appear along the road.

Even more surprisingly, D found Corphish and Wooper in these rice fields!

Yukiko kindly explained, "Wooper and Corphish help make the soil of the fields more fertile, which aids the growth of the rice. A long time ago, the town had these two Pokémon helping out in the rice paddies."

D was filled with questions.

Wouldn't Corphish damage the rice in the fields?

If he remembered correctly, Wooper's skin mucus was said to be poisonous, right?

After passing through the rice field area, they officially entered Mossui Town.

This rural town wasn't as bustling as Porto Marinada Town; there were few people here, specifically, few young people.

It was evening time; many elderly folks had just finished dinner and were leisurely strolling around town.

The town was paved with complete concrete roads, appearing very clean and tidy—typical of a Japanese style.

Like Porto Marinada Town, Pokémon were visible everywhere here.

Swinubs following Trainers, Hoothoot resting on rooftops, and several Vulpix lounging on eaves, all fluffy and furry.

D was immediately captivated by those Vulpix.

Wow! There are actually Vulpix here!

The fluffy warm Vulpix, with white bellies, were so adorable!

Koraidon, seeing D's expression, showed a resigned look: "Ah gah hiss..."

It's starting again.

---

---

---

---

# 321: Chapter 318: Infernal Pass, Slugma, and Ghosts

"This is the community center, and you'll be resting here tonight!" Yukiko led D to a two-story building and introduced it to him, "Tomorrow, the village head will take you to tour Kitakami's rice fields and apple garden."

"Alright." After a day of traveling, D indeed felt a bit tired.

Yukiko pushed open the door of the community center, and D walked in. After showing his ID badge to the front desk staff, he heard the person say:

"Welcome to Kitakami, your room is on the second floor. For meals, you can head to the restaurant on the right; the village head has already prepared a welcome dinner for you."

D nodded and then went upstairs to place his luggage in the room. Afterward, he returned to the lobby and, led by Yukiko, arrived at the community center's restaurant.

A bald elderly man, wearing a kimono, with glasses and a white beard, was already waiting there.

Upon seeing D arrive, he promptly came forward with a bright smile and extended his hand:

"Oh my, you must be Mr. D, the one from the Paldea Region here for agricultural exchange, right? I'm the head of Kitakami and also the mayor of this town. I'm so glad you could come here!"

"Hello, Mr. Village Head." D extended his hand, and after a brief self-introduction, the group sat down at the dining table.

Kitakami had prepared apple pie, candied apples, sticky rice cakes, mushroom soup, sushi, and other dishes for dinner.

Though not particularly lavish, these foods were full of local flavor.

At the table, the village head introduced the itinerary for the agricultural exchange to him:

"Tomorrow morning, I'll accompany you to visit Kitakami's rice fields. By the way, there aren't any rice fields in the Paldea Region, are there?"

D thought for a moment, shook his head, and said, "The principal crops grown in the Paldea Region are wheat and corn. As for rice, you don't see much of it; at least not near Porto Marinada Town, and I'm not sure about other areas."

"I see!" the village head smiled and nodded, "The food production in Porto Marinada Town was spectacular last year, it even made it to the World Agricultural News!"

D smiled slightly, "Last year, Porto Marinada Town had favorable weather and climate throughout the year, perfectly ideal for crop growth. Moreover, the locals there revere the great King of Bountiful Harvests."

"The King of Bountiful Harvests?" The village head was very surprised, with a curious expression on his face.

D nodded, "It's said to be a Legendary Pokemon that can make the soil fertile and ensure a bountiful harvest. Farmers who worship it are said to enjoy favorable weather and abundant crops all year round."

"For this reason, people affectionately call it the King of Bountiful Harvests and have built a shrine for it, holding yearly festivals to express their gratitude."

"Is there really such a thing…" The village head was quite taken aback.

D nodded slightly, "Perhaps Calyrex saw the hardworking people toiling earnestly on the land, was moved by their perseverance, and thus bestowed abundance upon them."

"Calyrex?" The village head was momentarily confused.

"That's the title of the King of Bountiful Harvests," D explained.

"Calyrex… the Legend of the King of Bountiful Harvests…" the village head muttered to himself, etching the name into his memory, "That said, we also have a Legend of Pokemon here in Kitakami!"

A Legendary Pokemon too? D thought of the things he had read in the travel guide earlier and raised an eyebrow before asking, "A ghost legend?"

"Huh? You know about it?" The village head was genuinely surprised.

D: "I skimmed through Kitakami's travel guide on the way here."

The village head realized, "I see!"

Happily, he explained, "Indeed, Kitakami has erected statues for the three Loyal Three Pokemon who sacrificed themselves to protect the town, drawing many Trainers and tourists, thanks to this legend!"

"Because of them, this remote small town, which originally survived on farming, has kept pace with the times, growing increasingly prosperous."

D nodded thoughtfully.

Developing tourism by leveraging the fame of Legendary Pokemon?

"The Loyal Three Statue is located at Loyalty Plaza in the northwest part of the town. After tomorrow's busy schedule, you can go take a stroll there," the village head said.

D indeed intended to do that, "Sure."

"Then it's settled," the village head smiled kindly, "Tonight, rest well at the community center! If you can't sleep, feel free to wander around the town."

After dinner, the village head left the community center and went home.

The well-fed D also returned to his room and began to sleep.

Early the next day, D got up bright and early. After having breakfast, he met with the village head and others and headed to the rice fields outside town to observe the farmers planting seedlings and cultivating rice paddies.

A few Wooper and Corphish, working in the field, stood on the ridge observing the situation here.

D watched for a while, then with Koraidon, he joined in, experiencing the local seedling planting work.

"How is it? Are you getting used to it?" the village head inquired.

D nodded; this work was relatively easy for him, "Unfortunately, Porto Marinada Town isn't suitable for planting rice."

The farms and ranches in Porto Marinada Town mainly focus on livestock farming, with the crops planted throughout the year primarily being wheat and corn.

---

If we want to switch to growing rice, we'll need to specially create rice paddies, which is too troublesome.

"That's right!" The village chief, familiar with the situation in Porto Marinada Town, agreed: "But every place has crops suited to it, and rice is our specialty in Kitakami, right?"

"Oh, by the way, we also have a local delicacy in Kitakami made from this rice, it's called sticky cake. You should try it sometime!"

*

D tried this Kitakami specialty at noon.

He was planning to return to the community hall for lunch, but just as he left the rice field, a warm-hearted lady invited him to her home, offering to let him taste her homemade sticky cake at lunch.

D was puzzled, wondering if everyone here was this hospitable.

But soon, he realized why.

Because the field he planted today with Koraidon belonged to this lady!

"Come in and eat here!" The lady welcomed D into the house and then busied herself in the kitchen.

Seeing this, D didn't refuse, thinking that the people of Kitakami were quite simple and kind.

He went into the courtyard and released all his Pokémon.

The lady, who lived alone for years since her husband passed away and her son settled in another region, rarely had her family come back except during holidays.

So aside from the lady, no one else lived in the house.

D couldn't help but feel a bit emotional, realizing that even in the Pokémon World, there were left-behind children and elderly.

The courtyard was meticulously cleaned, with a Magikarp Fountain situated in a corner.

Dragonite and Cyclizar were very curious about the Magikarp statue, and both went close to observe it.

Flutter Mane lurked silently in the shadows under the eaves, quietly watching the surroundings.

Koraidon and D stood on the pavement, observing the Pokémon around them.

Pawmo looked around in wonder.

Suddenly, D heard an excited growl from Pawmo.

He looked up and saw Pawmo had flown to the window, excitedly gazing at something inside.

The lady, who was cooking, saw Pawmo and opened the window with a smile: "Hungry, aren't you? Lunch will take a little longer, why not have some of the cookies I made for now!"

"Pawmo... Pawmo!" Pawmo extended its paw to take a cookie, happily growling twice, then turned around and flew to D, signaling for him to take a look.

"What's going on?" D, although surprised, stood up and went to check with Pawmo.

Soon, he understood Pawmo's intention.

Because in the kitchen, there was a Slugma!

The Slugma was hiding under the stove, using its body heat to help the lady cook!

"Pawmo!" Pawmo was overjoyed, as it had long wanted to make friends with a Slugma!

The lady noticed Pawmo's curiosity and, looking at the Slugma and then back at Pawmo, said with a smile:

"So you saw the Slugma?"

D was also delighted: "Ma'am, can you catch Slugma here in Kitakami?"

"Yes!" The lady replied with a smile, just as the pot was gurgling, indicating the food was ready.

She placed the food and sticky cake into dishes, saying, "You can find Slugma on Ghost Mountain behind the town, they live in a place called Infernal Pass."

"Infernal Pass?" D moved forward to help the lady set the table.

The lady nodded: "Yes, it's where the ground releases terrifying volcanic gases, making the surrounding temperature extremely hot, like hell, hence the name Infernal Pass."

"Although it sounds scary, Infernal Pass has been a famous training ground since ancient times. Many Trainers and travelers intentionally go there to train."

"I see..." D nodded, thinking that in such an environment, there must be many Fire Type Pokémon.

He decided, in the afternoon, he would go take a look!

"Lunch is ready! Come and eat!" The lady called out to the outside.

Three minutes later, Koraidon and the other Pokémon rushed over, gathering around the table to enjoy lunch.

The Slugma also came out, and the lady handed it a Berry, which it happily gobbled up.

"I met this Slugma on Ghost Mountain, it had fallen from Infernal Pass and got injured on the mountain path. I fed it a Berry, and since then, it started living with me."

"That's wonderful," D said softly.

"By the way!" The lady suddenly remembered something, her expression turning serious: "If you're going to Ghost Mountain, don't wander around there carelessly!"

D was slightly taken aback: "What do you mean?"

The lady explained: "It's said that the ghost driven away by the Loyal Three Pokémon is still lurking there, and if you encounter it, it will take your life!"

Ghost...

If he's not mistaken, that ghost should also be a Pokémon, right?

Perhaps it's some kind of Ghost Type Legendary Pokémon?

Upon hearing this, D hesitated slightly.

He looked at the Pokémon beside him, thinking that with them, even if the ghost was a Legendary Pokémon, they would be safe.

---

The lady muttered, "At dusk outside the village, shadows approach, be sure to stay alert.

Quickly put on a mask, do not reveal your face.

Whether the shadow is a person or a ghost, nodding with a covered face will let you pass.

If you didn't bring a mask, just hope that shadow is human.

If the shadow is human, fortune is yours; never forget the mask again.

If the shadow is a ghost, doom awaits; being seen would lead to tragedy.

Once your soul is taken by a ghost, you'll never return to the village again."

"This is an old village legend, they say someone once met a ghost on Ghost Mountain and never returned," the lady said seriously.

D nodded thoughtfully.

He realized that the villagers seemed to fear that ghost greatly.

Was it because the ghost had killed three Loyal Three Pokemon?

After lunch, D bid farewell to the lady.

With no plans for the afternoon, he could wander around the town as he pleased.

So, D immediately rode Koraidon to Ghost Mountain, intending to visit the place called Infernal Pass to see if he could find a Slugma.

Having watched the animated short "The Slugma-Powered Home" before, Pawmot was very fond of the "puff puff" sounds made by Slugma.

Upon hearing D mention that Slugma could be found there, Pawmot didn't even want to go back into its Poke Ball.

Instead, it decided to stay outside, accompany D into the mountain to find Slugma, make friends with it, and invite it to play at the ranch.

The range of Ghost Mountain was vast and lacked a specific map, so D didn't know the exact location of Infernal Pass.

To make the search easier, he directly rode Koraidon high into the sky to overlook the entire Ghost Mountain from above.

Pawmot also started flying, curiously looking around in all directions.

Before coming, the lady had told him that Infernal Pass was halfway up the western side of Ghost Mountain.

However, after searching for a long time, D couldn't pinpoint its exact location.

Frustrated, he could only let Koraidon randomly land on Ghost Mountain, planning to deduce the location of Infernal Pass based on the local Pokemon species and surrounding temperature.

Looking around, this area was mostly inhabited by Bronzor and Bonsly.

Bonsly, being very timid, turned around and fled in panic upon seeing them.

On the other hand, Bronzor was much bolder, floating mid-air with a tinkling sound, completely unfazed by the sudden appearance of Koraidon and Pawmot.

"No Fire Type Pokemon, and the temperature here is no different from the base of the mountain..." D mused briefly, then seeing a waterfall above, said to Koraidon, "Let's check above the waterfall."

"Gah-thss!"

Koraidon responded, exerting its strong legs and landing stably atop the cliff.

There was no sign of wild Pokemon here.

Amid the empty and quiet surroundings, D felt a bit puzzled.

At this moment, Pawmot, floating in mid-air, seemed to have noticed something, uttering a puzzled growl, "Pawmot?"

"What is it?" D turned his head, following Pawmot's gaze, spotting a green figure peering from around the mountain corner.

Realizing it had been seen, the figure turned and fled, quickly disappearing into the cliff.

D was slightly taken aback, was that... a villager living nearby?

---

---

---

---

# 322: Chapter 319: The Sinistcha

Previously, it was learned from the townspeople that the "ghost" that killed the Loyal Three is still on the mountain.

Usually, the townspeople rarely go up the mountain, mostly staying around the foot of it.

But considering now, it seems not everyone is that timid!

Spotting the mysterious figure, D had no other thoughts initially.

He withdrew his gaze, turned to the other side, and was ready to continue searching for the Slugma's habitat in the Infernal Pass, but unexpectedly, Koraidon directly chased after it.

D was stunned for a second: "Koraidon, where are you going?"

Pawmot was equally puzzled: "Pawmo?"

"Arghhh!"

Koraidon glanced back, momentarily unsure how to explain.

It had to turn around, transform from its mount form to its battle form, and scooped D onto its shoulder, carrying him and leaping across the protruding rocks.

D: "???"

Pawmot, though bewildered, quickly followed.

D only felt the scene in front of him changing rapidly, as Koraidon made quick twists and turns, with the surroundings changing rapidly.

In this intense rush akin to bungee jumping, D felt he almost went to see his ancestors.

When Koraidon finally placed him down, D was already seeing stars, almost about to throw up.

Koraidon and Pawmot looked worried.

Deep breaths, deep breaths!

D tried to regain his composure, without blaming Koraidon, instead asking curiously: "Did you find something?"

Koraidon nodded its big head repeatedly, turning to look ahead.

At this moment, D realized Koraidon had brought him to a very tranquil cliff.

Ahead was the enormous mountain range, with a magnificent waterfall cascading from the peak, surrounded by some greenery, connected to the outside by a particularly narrow mountain path.

"This is..."

Following Koraidon's gaze, D soon spotted a dark cave on that desolate cliff.

Koraidon lifted its foot and walked toward the cave.

Realizing there might be something inside, D cautiously moved forward with Pawmot alongside Koraidon.

They soon reached the cave entrance.

The cave was very narrow, with Koraidon having to lower its head to get inside.

After passing through a tunnel over three meters long, the cave's interior suddenly became spacious.

Seeing the scene inside, D was immediately stunned.

The cave wasn't large, containing several stones of varying sizes, polished to smoothness.

The largest stone looked like a bed, with a square stone piled at its side, and another smaller stone alongside, seemingly serving as a table and chair for dining.

And the green figure they had seen earlier was now huddled in the cave's corner, shivering with fear.

It's scared!

In an instant, D recognized the opposite party's emotion.

He carefully examined this unknown creature.

It wore a green cloak, its face resembling a little tangerine, with eyes that seemed to shimmer like stars.

And next to it lay a green mask.

No, this appearance definitely wasn't human!

Could it be a Pokémon?

D had never seen such a Pokémon before.

"Arghhh..."

"Pawmo..."

Koraidon and Pawmot also sensed the Pokémon's emotions, and they appeared somewhat at a loss.

Pawmot was quickest to react, recalling Alcremie who loved making friends everywhere, a small light bulb lit up on its head.

"Pawmo!" Pawmot flew forward, somehow pulled out a Berry, spread its paws, and placed it in front of the Pokémon.

It was D's first time seeing such a timid Pokémon, not knowing what to do, he tried to soothe it: "Hello? Don't be afraid, we won't hurt you."

Upon hearing this, the Pokémon cautiously glanced at them.

Then, as if seeing something more frightening, it bolted out the entrance, forgetting the mask altogether.

Pawmot's Berry was knocked over, rolled to the ground, and spun around a couple of times.

D, dumbfounded, followed outside, but the Pokémon was very fast and soon ran far away.

D didn't chase it because he realized the creature was quite timid and the terrain here was complex, one could easily fall.

Chasing it would only make the Pokémon more frantic, leading to a disastrous fall off the cliff.

Once the Pokémon was completely out of sight, Pawmot returned to the cave, regretfully picking up its cherished Berry from the ground.

Meanwhile, D examined the cave's surroundings.

Bed, table, chair...

It seemed someone used to live in seclusion here.

Or was it something the Pokémon made on its own?

"Its body is green, appearing to be a Grass Type Pokémon." D glanced at the mask on the bed and indeed saw parts resembling grass leaves.

Though unfamiliar, he could roughly guess this Pokémon might be one of the new additions from the Kitakami DLC.

"Pawmo..." Beside him, Pawmot sorrowfully wiped off the dust on the Berry.

With a glance, D told it: "Don't be sad, it might have just been scared by us."

"Pawmo!" Pawmot perked up again.

D felt his own pocket, then took off his backpack, pulling out some berries and placing them on that stone table.

When the pile of berries formed a small hill, he stopped.

"This place seems to be its home, we'll leave the berries here; when it gets hungry, it'll come back to eat."

Pawmot looked at the table, then at the Berry in its paw, happily adding it to the "Berry Hill".

"Pawmo~"

"Alright!" D took a deep breath, not forgetting his goal: "We should go and befriend Slugma now."

Upon hearing this, Pawmot eagerly hovered beside D: "Pawmo Pawmo!"

D smiled slightly, securing his backpack: "Let's go!"

He led the two Pokémon outside and continued the journey.

Koraidon reverted to its mount form, glanced back at the cave, and then at D, showing a puzzled expression.

Only after D and Pawmot were far did it hastily follow along.

Once they had departed, who knows how long later, a green figure cautiously returned along the narrow path.

It ran back to the cave, nervously inspecting the cave's interior with its star-like eyes.

Upon seeing the mask was still there, the Pokémon finally exhaled in relief, then moved forward to pick up the mask, hugging it dearly.

But just then, it suddenly noticed something different in the cave.

Staring at the small mountain of berries stacked on the stone table, the Pokémon showed a dazed expression.

---

---

---

---

# 323: Chapter 320: Making Friends with Slugma

After passing through a narrow mountain trail, traces of other Pokémon appeared ahead.

They were a group of robust Pokémon, resembling rocks in appearance, with a pair of muscular arms.

"So many Geodudes!"

D looked around, seeing Geodudes floating along this mountain path, wobbling back and forth leisurely.

Some Geodudes had half of their bodies buried underground, blending seamlessly with the surroundings, only revealing a pair of eyes and the top of their heads to observe him and Koraidon.

The number of Geodudes on this mountain path was quite large; as many as he could see, there were at least fifty or sixty.

Koraidon and Pawmot also saw those Geodudes floating in mid-air.

Pawmot was astonished by these Pokémon that looked like rocks yet could float above the ground, and quickly pointed to a floating Geodude, asking D how it was done.

D followed the direction and roughly guessed what Pawmot meant.

But this question was outside the realm of his knowledge.

Theoretically, Geodude doesn't have the Levitate ability, yet it could float off the ground like Gastly, which was truly amazing.

After a brief thought, D speculated, "Probably has something to do with gravity?"

"Pawmo..."

Pawmot didn't understand what gravity was and looked puzzled.

At this moment, D suddenly noticed Koraidon was about to step on a Geodude hiding underground, quickly grabbing it and carefully making a detour.

"Agh?" Koraidon was initially puzzled, but upon looking down, it immediately understood.

D: "You can't step on them."

The Pokédex records that the temperament of Geodudes is nasty, stubborn, and aggressive; if stepped on, they would be furious.

Although Koraidon is powerful, there were so many Geodudes here that if they rioted, it would be troublesome.

With the mindset that less trouble is better, D led the two Pokémon around the Geodudes.

About thirty minutes later, they finally climbed to the halfway point of the mountain.

At this time, D also detected a faint sulfur smell in the air.

The temperature around them had unknowingly risen considerably.

Everything indicated: Infernal Pass, reached!

"Here it is!"

D instantly perked up, and Pawmot was also very excited.

As they delved deeper, the surrounding temperature grew higher, and occasionally milky white steam spewed from the mountain path.

In this scorching environment, D and Pawmot were drenched in sweat, while Koraidon fared much better by comparison.

It's so hot... No wonder Trainers use this place as a training ground.

This environment is indeed perfect for honing willpower and temperament.

Many Fire Type Pokémon resided nearby.

Not far away, a few Houndour cautiously observed the situation, some of them letting out a series of frightening howls to communicate information to their nearby companions.

D took one glance and immediately understood.

This place seemed to be the territory of those Houndour!

Just then, the sound of a battle suddenly came from ahead.

The alert Houndour swiftly turned and raced towards the source of the sound.

It was the path to the deeper part of Infernal Pass, and D continued forward.

After passing through a rugged, rocky road, the scene ahead unfolded before them.

A young boy was commanding an Infernape in a battle against a wild Houndoom.

The Infernape was incredibly strong, easily defeating the Houndoom in just a few rounds.

Watching the Houndoom fall, the Trainer took out a Poke Ball and captured the Houndoom.

The gathered Houndour dispersed and vanished quickly.

The boy seemed indifferent, storing away the Houndoom's Poke Ball as he intended to leave.

But just at that moment, their eyes met.

The boy instantly noticed the mysterious Pokémon not far away, a crimson, robust creature.

Alongside it was a Pokémon covered in pink fur, with a red cape tied around its back, holding a small silver hammer.

The boy's eyes gleamed with interest, and his Infernape sensed the Trainer's excitement, its flames burning even more intensely.

D waved his hand and greeted, "Hi, hello?"

The boy was full of fighting spirit: "You're a Trainer here for training, right? Since we've met, why not have a Pokémon battle here!"

D: "..." He knew it!

The moment Trainers' eyes meet, a battle must start!

But it was too hot here, and he didn't want to stay longer; he just wanted to find a Slugma and head back to town to eat apples.

"No need, I'm not here for training." D declined, "I'm just here looking for a Pokémon."

With that, he turned to leave, but the boy ran after him.

"Looking for a Pokémon? What kind of Pokémon? Maybe I know it!"

D glanced at him.

The boy explained, "I've been training here for a month and am familiar with the Pokémon inhabiting the area! I can help you find it if you'll have a Pokémon battle with me!"

---

D: "..."

These Trainers are just way too fixated on Pokemon battles, aren't they?

But to quickly find Slugma and leave this scorching place, D stopped and asked, "Do you know where any Slugma are?"

Boy: "Slugma? Of course, I know!"

That's great, D straightforwardly said, "Then let's have a battle now!"

"Eh?!" The topic switched too fast, and the boy couldn't react.

But soon, he came to his senses, his eyes full of fighting spirit, "I'm looking forward to it! Infernape, let's go!"

The Infernape nodded coolly and then stepped forward.

On D's side, he sent out Koraidon.

In wild Pokemon battles, there aren't many restrictions. Once the Trainers and Pokemon are ready, the battle officially begins.

The boy looked at Koraidon and said, "Although I've never seen that Pokemon before, we absolutely won't lose! Infernape, Flame Wheel!"

The command was given, and the flames on Infernape soared high with a whoosh.

Fire engulfed its body, and Infernape rushed straight at Koraidon.

D wiped the sweat off his forehead, looked at the scene, and directly said, "Collision Course!"

"Ah gass!"

Koraidon nodded slightly, its crimson body leapt up, scarlet energy flowed over it, and an ancient aura spread rapidly.

Its form changed quickly, carrying the momentum of shattering the ground, heading for Infernape.

The two Pokemon directly collided, terrifying energy continually impacted, followed by a violent explosion.

Infernape was blasted away on the spot, falling to the ground and losing the ability to battle.

Boy: "???"

So... so strong!

The boy was utterly shocked, unable to regain his senses for a moment.

What in the world is that Pokemon? How did it take out his most prized Infernape in one move?

He once participated in the Lily of the Valley Conference and reached the top eight!

But now, he was defeated in one move by the opponent...

The boy's brain was racing, and then he thought of a shocking idea.

Could this person be a Champion-level Trainer?

Seeing the boy looking bewildered, seemingly still not reacting.

D awkwardly coughed, "It's over."

The boy was thoroughly beaten. He stepped forward and fed Infernape a potion to restore its stamina, then returned it to its Poke Ball to rest.

"A bet is a bet, I'll take you to the Slugma's habitat now!" the boy said.

"Thanks." D expressed his gratitude, now he could save quite a bit of time.

"You're not from Kitakami, are you?" the boy curiously asked, "Oh right, my name's Helian, what's yours?"

"D." D replied, "I'm from the Paldea Region."

"Pal... Paldea Region?" Helian was utterly shocked; that's on the other side of the planet! Coming from so far away...

"Yes." D nodded slightly, "I'm here on business."

No wonder, Helian realized, no wonder he didn't recognize these two Pokemon, they were from the Paldea Region!

He took out the Pokedex and scanned Koraidon and Pawmot.

The result said "Not found, belongs to newly discovered Pokemon species."

Seeing his curiosity about Koraidon and Pawmot, D explained, "This one is Koraidon, this one is Pawmot, both are Paldea Region Pokemon. You're from Sinnoh, right?"

Helian's eyes widened, "How did you figure that out?"

D: "...because Infernape is one of the Starter Pokemon given to new Trainers by the Sinnoh League."

Helian smiled embarrassedly, "I almost forgot, but speaking of, your strength is really impressive, should be at Champion Level, right?"

"I don't know either." D shook his head, unsure about this topic, as he hadn't participated in the Champion assessment.

Besides, most of his Pokemon were grown strong by eating food, making them hard to evaluate.

Helian was momentarily stunned: "What do you mean?"

D honestly said, "I've never participated in a Champion assessment because my main job is as a Rancher. I'm here in Kitakami to conduct agricultural exchange with the local farmers."

Helian: "???"

He opened his mouth, even more shocked.

He knew about Ranchers and farmers, but this Rancher in front of him seemed a bit different from his image of Ranchers.

"With your strength, it feels like there's a good chance you could directly become Champion if you participate in the Lily of the Valley Conference held in the Sinnoh Region." Helian said.

"Lily of the Valley Conference?" D raised an eyebrow, thinking of the Legendary Beast guy from the anime upon hearing those four words.

"That's right! It's the grandest conference in the Sinnoh Region!" Helian introduced, "Only Trainers who have collected eight badges can compete!"

"If you win the conference championship, not only can you gain fame and a large cash prize, but you'll also have the chance to battle against the regional Champion, Cynthia!"

Cynthia... D's mind flashed with the image of a blonde, cool beauty, and he chuckled, saying:

"Everyone has different ambitions! Rather than becoming a Champion, I prefer living with the Pokemon I love."

---

"Got it." Helian nodded.

At this moment, the temperature around them kept rising.

Only wearing a short sleeve shirt, Helian felt so hot that he took off his clothes, his face flushed: "It's too hot... I can hardly stand it!"

D's clothes were already drenched in sweat. He exhaled a hot breath, silently chanting to himself to stay calm and cool.

Helian looked around, seemingly checking something.

Then, he ran to a hillside and, after seeing the sight in front of him, he quickly turned back and shouted: "D, come here quickly! The Slugma are here!"

D rushed over.

On the sun-bleached slope lay a large group of bright red Slugma.

The piles of rubble around them constantly spewed hot volcanic gas upward, and the Slugma crawled around slowly, appearing lively.

Finally found them!

D's eyes lit up.

And just then, he suddenly noticed a strange phenomenon on one of the Slugma.

That Slugma was currently emitting a crystalline-like colorful radiance, with a red candlestick atop its head.

Is that... Terastalization?!

D was bewildered.

This isn't right, is it? Kitakami and Paldea Region are so far apart, how come there's Terastalization here too?

Helian also noticed the scene.

He was equally puzzled: "I don't know what that is either. That Slugma isn't the first Pokemon to turn like this. Over this past month, I've encountered several strange-looking Pokemon here, and they return to normal after being defeated."

"That's Terastalization." D explained.

"Terastalization?" Helian was startled.

"It's a mysterious phenomenon occurring in the Paldea Region. Tera Energy envelops a Pokemon, temporarily changing its type, making its moves more powerful, just like..."

D was about to say like Mega Evolution, but around this time, Mega Evolution probably hasn't emerged yet, so he changed it midway to:

"Just like Dynamax in the Galar Region."

"What's Dynamax?" Helian was even more confused.

D: "..."

He couldn't help but ask: "Don't you pay attention to news from other regions?"

Helian embarrassedly scratched his head: "Because those places are too far from us... and I'm usually traveling outside, with no chance to watch the news."

"Alright then." D thought of Helian's previous use of a Pokedex to scan Koraidon.

His Pokedex should just be the Sinnoh Region's regional Dex, not a National Dex, so it wouldn't contain data on Pokemon from other regions.

Plus, with the communication limitations of this era, the internet wasn't yet advanced, leaving many Trainers with no means to learn about situations in other regions.

Thinking of this, D said: "If you get a chance, you can go to the Galar Region and Paldea Region to see for yourself."

Helian: "I will!"

D looked at the group of Slugma and said to Pawmot:

"Pawmot, it's up to you!"

"Pawmo!"

Already unable to hold back at the sight of so many Slugma, Pawmot flew over with a Berry, a bit bashfully introducing itself to the Slugma.

"Pawmo Pawmo!" (Hello, I'm Pawmot, I want to be friends with you!)

The Slugma raised their heads to look at Pawmot, blinking one after another.

Very soon, the Slugma uninterested in Pawmot turned their gaze away, continuing to crawl slowly on the ground.

Pawmot felt quite dejected.

"Pawmo..."

But just then, a smaller Slugma leisurely crawled in front of it.

Pawmot's attention was instantly drawn. He turned to look at this Slugma.

The Slugma used its yellow eyes to look at the Berry in Pawmot's hand.

Pawmot looked down at the Berry, then back at the Slugma, happily extending its paw pad to offer the Berry: "Pawmo!"

The Slugma elongated its head, using its mouth to take the Berry from Pawmot's hand, then happily started eating it.

Pawmot happily stretched out a paw: "Pawmo?" (Will you be my good friend?)

Finishing the Berry, the Slugma looked up at this unfamiliar Pokemon, blinking its eyes in slight confusion.

"Goo?"

Pawmot explained: "Pawmo!" (A good friend is a good friend! I'll bring you lots of Berries, and we'll play together every day. If any Pokemon bullies you, I'll knock all of them down!)

The Slugma had never heard of such a thing. It imagined along with Pawmot's words, and then its whole body started emitting white smoke.

In an instant, the sound "boom boom boom" echoed across the entire cliffside.

Pawmot froze slightly, having seen "The Slugma-Powered Home," it instantly understood the Slugma's meaning.

This means the Slugma is very happy right now!

Pawmot's eyes sparkled with anticipation and asked: "Pawmo?"

The Slugma looked at it, then made the "boom boom boom" sound again.

Seeing this, Pawmot turned back to glance at D.

D handed over a Poke Ball.

Invited by Pawmot, the Slugma happily moved into the Poke Ball, planning to follow Pawmot home.

"Pawmo!"

Pawmot was extremely happy to have made a new friend, hugging the Poke Ball with the Slugma inside, and flew back to hand it over to D.

Having witnessed the whole process, Helian was utterly shocked, gaping in disbelief.

Nani?!

This this this...

Can you capture Pokemon like this?

No, no way! This Pawmot actually captured a Slugma!

But will the Slugma captured by Pawmot listen to D's command?

---

---

---

---

# 324: Chapter 321: Is It a Ghost?!

D put away the Poke Ball containing Slugma, preparing to interact with it after descending the mountain.

But when he turned his head, he immediately saw Helian's shocked expression.

D: "?"

Helian couldn't help but ask, "Do all trainers in the Paldea Region capture Pokémon like this? But if so, will the captured Pokémon listen to your commands?"

"...Not exactly." D thought carefully, then explained, "I don't need to command Pokémon to battle. The reason I captured Slugma is because I like this Pokémon and want to take it home with me to live together and get to know it slowly."

A collector-type Trainer? Helian probably understood as he had seen such Trainers before.

They are not very good at battling with Pokémon, and their purpose for traveling is just to capture cute Pokémon they've never seen before and send them home to live with them.

However, that method of capturing is still too...

Helian, for a moment, didn't know what language to use to describe that feeling.

Having Pokémon capture other Pokémon, such a thing was unheard of.

Seeing his expression, D chuckled and said, "If you have the chance, you can try this method too."

"Most Pokémon have simple and honest natures. If you give them some food or let them feel your love, there's a good chance they'll choose you as a partner."

"Of course, this method is only suitable for small and gentle-natured Pokémon like Slugma and Eevee. If it were Garchomp or Tyranitar, it might not work. In those cases, you have to prove your strength to them."

Helian listened attentively, feeling as if something had clicked in his mind.

"Got it!" Helian said.

D smiled slightly, turned around, and prepared to leave.

After a month of training in Infernal Pass, Helian also planned to head to Mossui Town to gather some supplies, so the two of them rushed down the mountain together.

Once they left the bounds of Infernal Pass, the surrounding temperature immediately returned to normal.

D took a deep breath and pulled a bottle of water from his backpack to replenish his fluids.

Helian also put on his clothes.

As they passed the place where the timid Pokémon was discovered earlier, D felt curious and asked Helian about it.

"By the way, Helian, have you seen any Pokémon unique to Kitakami around here?"

"Do you mean Sinistea and its evolved form, Sinistcha?"

Sinistcha? That name sounds like an evolution of Sinistea...

D pondered for a moment, then shook his head and said, "Besides these two?"

"There don't seem to be any," Helian recalled, "but there are many Pokémon here that you don't find in the Sinnoh Region. I've seen Mimikyu, Cramorant, Carbink… huh? Did you see some strange Pokémon?"

D nodded: "In the area up ahead, I saw a Pokémon I'd never seen before."

Hearing this, Helian looked in the direction he was pointing, and when he saw the scene ahead, his face showed a shocked expression.

"What? You went in there?"

D was puzzled, "What do you mean?"

Helian: "That's Oni's Maw, which is very famous in Kitakami! The terrain there is very treacherous, named for the protruding stones that look like a demon's teeth. One misstep and you could fall into a cliff."

"For this reason, the locals have listed it as an extremely dangerous area and have forbidden others from going in."

D was stunned: "Is it really that serious?"

"Besides that reason, there's another very important one. It's said that the ghost that killed the Loyal Three is hiding somewhere in Oni's Maw. Once intruders encounter it, their lives will be taken away by it!"

Helian trembled: "A ghost wearing a mask or something like that—it's terrifying! I fear these rural folk tales the most!"

D: "..."

A ghost with a mask...

Images he had seen earlier flashed through D's mind.

That green Pokémon seemed to have a mask in its hand too.

A sudden, unbelievable guess popped into D's mind—could it be the legendary ghost?!

But that's impossible. That Pokémon was obviously very timid. It wasn't capable of taking someone's life; it ran away in fright the moment it saw him.

Or could the ghost be someone else entirely?

Just then, a cold wind howled from the direction of Oni's Maw, reverberating through the valley like the wailing of wronged ghosts.

Helian's hair stood on end, feeling a chill down his spine, and he quickly said, "Let's get out of here quickly!"

D was at a loss for words but still took the two Pokémon down the mountain, back to Mossui Town.

Unknowingly, it was already four o'clock in the evening.

D bid farewell to Helian and then returned to the community center.

Tomorrow, he needed to visit Apple Hill with the village head to tour the Apple Garden, so after dinner, D returned to his room.

He released Slugma from the Poke Ball and, along with Pawmo, looked at the Slugma, feeding it Berries and snacks.

To this unfamiliar human, Slugma didn't seem scared at all. On the contrary, it appeared to be in a cheerful mood, slowly munching on the Berries.

---

If it weren't for the fact that Slugma doesn't have the Gluttony characteristic, D would suspect that this Slugma's characteristic is Gluttony.

It's undeniable; Slugma really is a naturally born mobile heater.

As soon as it's released, the room's temperature begins to rise, becoming cozy and warm.

"Interesting." D observed the interaction between Pawmo and Slugma, contemplating silently.

With Slugma around, fireplaces won't need to use firewood in the future.

"Yes, besides the fireplace, I need to build a special Slugma heating pipeline throughout the house, connecting all the rooms."

Just like in that anime, Slugma can freely crawl through the pipes, going wherever it pleases in daily life.

Meanwhile, the warmth it generates while moving can continuously heat the entire house.

Thinking about it, the Fire Type Slugma is truly convenient; it's simply the best partner for cold winter days!

D sat on a chair watching Pawmo and Slugma while deep in thought.

Soon, Slugma filled its belly.

Faced with Pawmo's friendly gestures, wanting to befriend it, Slugma was very happy.

Its joy was so great that several plumes of white smoke instantly sprayed out from its red-hot body.

"Boom boom boom!"

Suddenly—coughing from the smoke filled the room, and D quickly opened the window to ventilate, then turned back to look at the silly Slugma.

Why does he suddenly feel this Slugma isn't very smart?

Seeming to realize it caused trouble for everyone, the simple-minded Slugma was a bit flustered.

Fortunately, nobody was bothered; instead, they showed friendly smiles to it.

Pawmo comforted it: "Pamo pamo!"

Slugma blinked its eyes, then began to feel happy again.

Immediately after, there were more booming sounds in the room.

D: "..."

Indeed not very smart...

But, perhaps it's precisely this simple-mindedness, even seeming a bit foolish, that makes it willing to become friends with Pawmo?

Just like those children who have just started kindergarten.

*

Early the next morning, D followed the village chief to the local apple orchard.

Looking at the apple trees covering the hills, D thought: No wonder Kitakami is famous for producing apples!

"Here we are; this is Apple Hill!" the village chief introduced with a smile: "This hill has a wide view, without any terrain obstructions, allowing sunlight to fully shine on every apple, making them grow big and round."

D looked up, and sure enough, saw the apples on the trees were all plump, bright red, and shiny, looking very tempting at first glance.

The village chief stepped forward and picked a few, handing them to him: "Try them! They're a specialty of Kitakami!"

D reached out to take them and looked down; the information of the apple appeared in his mind.

[Kitakami Apple: A high-quality apple, a specialty of Kitakami, famous worldwide.]

D glanced at it and handed one of the big, round apples to Koraidon, tasting it together.

Taking a bite, the sweet apple juice burst forth in his mouth instantly.

Really good, D nodded approvingly.

"Nice, isn't it?" the village chief asked, his face filled with pride and joy when talking about his apples.

D responded affirmatively, looking out at the vibrant apple orchard, feeling: This is truly fertile land!

Like the rice field before, various Pokemon also live in the apple orchard.

There are Poochyena guarding the apple trees, Sentret running all around the orchard, slender Bellsprout, and Starly perched on branches.

It's clear Kitakami has fully integrated Pokemon into agricultural development here.

Following D's gaze, the village chief also saw those Pokemon, proudly proclaiming: "These are the Pokemon helping in the orchard!"

"Poochyena can guard the orchard to prevent wild Pokemon from coming to destroy the apple trees, and Sentret can patrol the entire hill, notifying Poochyena of the situation."

"Starly can provide pest control services for the apple trees, allowing them to grow smoothly, and Bellsprout can not only utilize Grass Type Energy to cultivate the apple trees but also use Vine Whip to help us pick apples."

"Does Paldea also involve Pokemon in the agricultural industry?" the village chief asked.

D hasn't visited other farms much, but he knows a bit, like Elif's Cherry Garden, where Pokemon do help.

But those Pokemon are merely the wild ones kindly taken in by Elif, not hired officially en masse to care for the trees and fields like in Kitakami.

"Maybe I can suggest to Mayor Thomas to share this method with everyone later," D thought while replying: "Yes."

"On my ranch, Dragonite uses Rain Dance to help water the fields, Dugtrio helps plow the land, Gogoat uses Grass Energy to help cultivate crops, and the Psyduck do help me pick the fruits."

"Exactly, that's it!" the village chief nodded repeatedly: "Pokemon truly are magical creatures! It's so good to have them around! Human life greatly improves thanks to them!"

---

D nodded with a smile, "Yeah!"

The group began to wander around Apple Hill.

During this time, D noticed vans parked along the road.

Some farmers and Pokemon were loading freshly picked apples into cardboard boxes and then moving them onto the trucks.
