The village chief, watching this scene, chuckled and said, "These apples will be transported all over the world—Kanto, Sinnoh, Paldea, Unova, Kalos—so everyone can taste Kitakami's apples."

"That's amazing!" D marveled.

To think, in a time when the internet wasn't as developed and all commercial publicity relied on newspapers, Kitakami apples managed to sell worldwide. It really showed their worth.

At this moment, the village chief suddenly spoke up: "There's plenty of interesting things in the town, it's just a shame you came at the wrong time. If you came in the summer, you could have joined the town's Mask Festival!"

Hearing this, D was taken aback: "The Mask Festival?"

The village chief explained, "That's right! It's an annual grand festival held specifically to celebrate the valor of the three Loyal Three. It attracts not just the townsfolk, but many Trainers from other regions too!"

"Everyone wears masks and heads to the Shrine in the center of Kitakami to enjoy the festivities!"

Masks... masks again.

It seemed like everything around Kitakami was connected to masks.

D thought back to the green Pokemon he encountered yesterday and the mask it held, feeling a stir in his heart. He then asked:

"By the way, village chief, are there any unique Pokemon here in Kitakami?"

"Pokemon in Kitakami?" The village chief seemed a bit surprised, but still replied, "Of course! The most famous ones are the three Loyal Three Pokemon who sacrificed themselves to protect the village."

Munkidori, Fezandipiti, and the rest.

But that green Pokemon looked nothing like a dog, monkey, or bird.

After a brief thought, D asked again, "Besides the three Loyal Three Pokemon, are there any others?"

"I saw an unknown Pokemon with a mask in the mountains before. Is it also a Kitakami Pokemon?"

D was curious; he wanted to ask if the village chief knew anything about that Pokemon.

But unexpectedly, the old man's face changed dramatically upon hearing his words.

"A masked creature wandering the mountains? Did you encounter a ghost?!"

D was stunned on the spot.

What did he mean?

Was that timid Pokemon actually the ghost from Kitakami legends who killed the three Loyal Three?

How could that be possible?

It was so timid that it shivered the moment anyone got close, and eventually ran away out of fear.

Could such a Pokemon really be a ghost?

D's first reaction was that it couldn't be; either the legend was wrong, or there was another story behind it.

"That was a brutal ghost!" the village chief expressed fearfully, "I advise you not to go near the Shadow Cave anymore. If you anger the ghost, you'll end up in a very dangerous situation!"

D trusted his eyes more than legends.

There was no way he could believe that Pokemon was the violent ghost.

He remained silent, while Koraidon next to him suddenly roared at the village chief:

"Graah!"

The village chief was startled and instinctively took a few steps back. What was wrong with this Pokemon? Why did it suddenly charge at him, roaring angrily?

Seeing Koraidon's reaction, D sensed something was off and quickly stopped Koraidon, soothing its emotions with his hand.

Only then did Koraidon calm down.

The village chief breathed a sigh of relief, then looked ahead toward the hill and said:

"Phew... Loyalty Plaza is just up ahead. That's where the townsfolk of Kitakami buried their remains. Would you like to visit it?"

---

---

---

---

# 325: Chapter 322: Second Encounter

"We've arrived. This is where the three Loyal Three Pokemon are buried."

The two walked along the path of Apple Hill, eventually reaching a wide, flat clearing.

The clearing was circular, surrounded by a wooden fence, with beautiful park benches placed along the edges where some townsfolk sat with children and Pokemon, resting.

At the innermost part of the plaza stood a small shrine, housing statues of three different Pokemon.

D looked at them. Clearly, these were the Loyal Three Pokemon the village chief mentioned.

"Thanks to them, the town was saved from the hands of ghosts, rescuing everyone here," said the village chief, his tone filled with gratitude.

D asked with interest, "Are their bodies buried underneath this?"

"Yes." The village chief nodded. He noticed a stain on the shrine and went up to clean it.

At that moment, a villager ran over from the back, shouting, "Administrator, something's wrong! The truck transporting apples has fallen into a ditch!"

"What?" The village chief was taken aback, quickly saying to D, "Let's call it a day for our exchange activities. You can feel free to explore the area; I'll go check out the situation."

"Do you need my help?" D inquired.

The village chief thanked him, then smiled and shook his head, "Thank you very much, but the town has staff specifically for handling such matters, so no need to trouble you."

Seeing this, D didn't insist. He watched the village chief leave in a hurry and then turned his gaze back to the shrine honoring the Loyal Three.

Koraidon was also examining those peculiar statues within the shrine.

"Koraidon, did you notice something just now?" D suddenly asked.

"Gash-ah?" Koraidon opened its mouth slightly, a bit troubled because it didn't know how to convey its thoughts to D.

D watched Koraidon, sensing something seemed off.

Under his gaze, Koraidon gradually lowered its head and then said, "Gash-ah!"

D couldn't help but fall into deep thought.

Judging by Koraidon's demeanor, it had evidently noticed something and thus had shown a temper to the village chief earlier.

D prided himself on understanding Koraidon well; his Koraidon would never arbitrarily lose its temper or show hostility to strangers without reason.

Seeing D didn't understand, Koraidon let out a sigh.

"Gash-ah..."

Just then, a familiar voice came from behind.

D turned his head and saw Helian excitedly running over from outside: "D? Are you here to see the graves of the Loyal Three too?"

"Huh?" D was a bit surprised, "I thought you had already left Kitakami and returned to the Sinnoh Region."

"No rush, my training is finished for this trip, so I'm planning to wander around here and enjoy the pastoral scenery," Helian said.

"I see."

D nodded, then looked at the shrine in front with Helian.

On the right side of the shrine stood a stone tablet, recording the heroic deeds of the Loyal Three.

"The 'Loyal Three Statue'—these were the brave Pokemon that defended Mossui Town a long time ago. To defeat the ghosts attacking the village, they fought fiercely and unfortunately perished.

Their remains were buried under the statues and are solemnly commemorated."

Helian recited it word for word.

D listened and watched silently.

Koraidon next to him grew increasingly uneasy as Helian read aloud, eventually furrowing its brow with a furious expression.

D keenly noticed Koraidon's emotions.

In that moment, a spark of insight hit D, and he asked Koraidon, "Did you get upset with the village chief because of the ghosts?"

"Gash-ah!" Koraidon nodded.

D was even more surprised. What's going on? Does Koraidon know ghosts?

But wasn't Koraidon supposed to be from the ancient past of Paldea? How could it possibly know the ghosts of Kitakami?

Even if these ghosts had survived since that far-off era, Koraidon couldn't possibly be acquainted with them.

D had many questions in his heart, but with Helian around, he didn't inquire on the spot.

Not until after visiting the Loyal Three Statue, when saying goodbye to Helian and returning on the road to Mossui Town, did he ask:

"Do you know the ghosts?"

"Gash-ah..." Koraidon's expression was conflicted, unsure how to express itself, but finally, it firmly nodded: "Gash-ah!"

D wondered if he would believe it...

"I knew it!" Seeing this, D immediately had his answer, but yesterday when observing that Pokemon, it seemed not to recognize Koraidon.

D's thoughts were a jumble, and he voiced his thoughts directly, "But it doesn't seem to recognize you."

Koraidon's mood appeared low: "Gash-ah..."

Witnessing this, D didn't know what to say.

He finally sighed, reached out to pet Koraidon's head, and said, "In any case, let's go investigate the place from yesterday."

"Gash-ah!"

Upon hearing this, Koraidon immediately walked to D's side, letting him ride on its back.

Once D was seated, Koraidon leapt up, its double wings suddenly spread out, flying towards the ghostly aura veiled Ghost Mountain.

---

A dozen minutes later, D was once again at the entrance of Oni's Maw.

Moving further ahead, passing through the narrow suspended mountain path, he would reach the cave where the mysterious Pokémon resided yesterday.

Recalling yesterday's events, D felt that the Pokémon probably fled in terror because it feared Koraidon.

After all, Koraidon, summoned from ancient times, indeed looked ferocious enough.

To modern Pokémon, its existence is akin to dinosaurs from the Jurassic era in comparison to present-day chickens.

"Wait for me here. I'll go verify alone." D settled Koraidon in the large bushy area nearby.

Koraidon obediently agreed and crouched honestly behind the bush, only revealing half of its head, pitifully watching D's back.

After confirming Koraidon wouldn't be discovered, D then proceeded towards the cave.

Upon approaching, he spotted the Pokémon from yesterday.

It seemed to hear some noise from outside, immediately turning towards him in distress.

In the next second, the gaze of the human and the Pokémon met in mid-air.

D tried hard to express his goodwill: "Hello, do you remember me? I came here yesterday."

However, no sooner had he finished speaking than the Pokémon's face showed a panicked expression, desperately pulling out its last Teal Mask from under its cloak to wear on its face.

With the mask on, all the Pokémon's emotions were hidden behind it.

D was quite taken aback by this scene because he found that mask surprisingly suited this Pokémon!

Based on past experiences, a guess quickly came to his mind—"Could this mask be like Dialga's Adamant Orb, a special item for this Pokémon?"

D locked eyes with the masked mysterious Pokémon. Thankfully, this time, it didn't run away upon seeing him as it did yesterday.

D was very pleased, thinking his guess was correct, this Pokémon feared Koraidon!

"Don't worry, I mean no harm, and I won't hurt you."

To avoid frightening the Pokémon, he lowered his voice while observing the mask.

For some unknown reason, the mask had several damages on it, looking quite aged, as if it had been made a long time ago.

As D observed the Pokémon, it was also secretly watching him.

However, its face was entirely covered by the mask, leaving its emotions unreadable.

D shifted his gaze to the stone table, noticing that some Berries had visibly been eaten, evidently by this Pokémon.

The Pokémon also noticed what he was looking at, and its actions became somewhat flustered and cautious.

D keenly noticed this, shaking his head and saying:

"No need to be nervous; those are gifts from me and Pawmot for you. Do you remember Pawmot? The pink Pokémon from yesterday."

The mysterious Pokémon hesitated for a while and then nodded softly.

"That's good." D showed a slight smile, his mind filled with thoughts.

Is this the Masked Ghost described in the town legends?

He didn't speak further, and the atmosphere in the air suddenly fell silent.

D suddenly remembered the stone bed and table in the cave and asked: "Have you always lived here alone?"

But unexpectedly, upon hearing these words, the Pokémon emitted a strong feeling of sadness.

D was slightly stunned.

In that moment, various thoughts emerged in his mind.

Could it be that someone else, or other Pokémon, once lived here with this Pokémon?

But this topic seemed to sadden the Pokémon, so D didn't delve deeper.

Having met the purpose of this visit, he suddenly stood up, and the Pokémon visibly tensed, watching him through the mask.

D was slightly taken aback, quickly explaining: "No need to be nervous, I'm just heading back and won't disturb you any longer."

With that, he took off his backpack, pulling out a bottle of special ranch milk and some Poffins to place on the stone table: "These are gifts for you, goodbye!"

After doing this, D turned and left the secluded cave.

He found Koraidon hidden behind the bush, patted its head, and said: "Let's go!"

Koraidon glanced at the direction behind D, then carried him away from Oni's Maw back to Mossui Town.

Unknowingly, it was already midday.

The more D thought about it, the more he felt something was amiss. The legends about Mossui Town seemed problematic, but he couldn't figure out what.

Without any other choice, he decided to have lunch first and deal with the specifics later.

Today, the lunch at the civic center was quite lavish, making D feel like he had experienced a country feast.

Both the food and the environment were full of rural character and charm.

After lunch, D planned to take a rest with his Pokémon, maybe a nap.

However, as soon as he returned to the room, released his Pokémon from their Poke Balls, and fed them Poffins, there was a knock on the door.

---

The door opens, and a grandmother with a head full of white hair stands outside, her expression complex.

Upon seeing the visitor, D is quite surprised: "Yukiko Grandma?"

Yukiko nods and straightforwardly asks: "You broke into Oni's Maw yesterday and saw a ghost there, didn't you?"

D pauses for a moment: "How did you know?"

Yukiko: "The entire town is talking about it! You all have such daring courage to go to that place!"

D: "..." The countryside is annoying in this way; nothing stays hidden.

D initially thought Yukiko, like the town mayor, had come to persuade him not to visit Oni's Maw again, but her next words caught him by surprise.

Yukiko looked complicated as she asked: "Did you see the ghost with your own eyes? How was it?"

D sensed something was off: "I saw it, but it doesn't quite match the ghost described in the legend..."

"Ah, just as expected." Yukiko lets out a sigh and then says: "Come with me! My husband wants to tell you something."

D is dumbfounded for a moment, but quickly reacts and decisively follows her.

He follows Yukiko, taking Koraidon and the others with him, leaving the community center and arriving at a house on the outskirts of the town.

Under the eaves, a hunched old man with a face full of wrinkles and a middle part hairstyle awaits.

"Let's talk inside the house!" the old man says.

D nods and then enters the living room, taking Koraidon and the others with him.

They sit on the couch, and the old man looks at him and Koraidon and the others for a while before speaking: "You saw the ghost at Oni's Maw, didn't you?"

D is somewhat puzzled: "If that green-masked Pokémon is a ghost."

"I knew it!" Upon hearing this, the old man understands: "It's hard to believe that the ghost willingly appeared in front of you; it seems that the time is right!"

D: "..."

In his heart, he thought it didn't actually appear willingly; if Koraidon hadn't alertly discovered it, he probably wouldn't have known anything!

"Have you heard the legend of the ghost circulating in the town?" the old man asks.

D nods: "I've heard of it."

"What do you think of the ghost legend?" the old man asks again.

D pauses, thinking about how he sees it…

"I suspect the masked Pokémon I encountered isn't the ghost."

Or perhaps the ghost has two personalities, one timid and fearful, the other fierce and aggressive, decisive and ruthless.

Like Long Kui in Chinese Paladin.

However, the old man shakes his head: "No, it is the ghost, and that event did indeed happen; it's just that what is recorded diverges from the true history, which is the opposite!"

"The opposite...? What do you mean?"

"Since you've encountered the ghost... no, you should respectfully call it Ogerpon, I'll tell you all about it! Let me recount the true history passed down through our family for generations!" the old man says solemnly, beginning slowly.

"This story was also told to me by my father, a true story that you absolutely must not tell anyone else in the village! If it wasn't willing to reveal itself to you, I...forget it, you absolutely cannot spread this!"

Seeing the old man's stern face, D nods in agreement: "I promise."

Cyclizar, Pawmot, and the others nod repeatedly as well.

Sunshine streams in through the window slits, leaving mottled shadows on the floor in the living room.

Dust floats slowly in the beams of light, the old man's voice heavy and ancient, as if an old scroll slowly unfolding before D.

"Long, long ago, a man from a distant land and a ghost arrived at Kitakami due to losing their way."

"The people in the village feared their foreign appearances and thus didn't allow the man and ghost to approach the village."

"The man and ghost felt saddened by the villagers' lack of welcome, but as long as they were together, they were happy."

"Thus they left the village, living modestly in a cave in the back mountain."

"Only the mask artisan in the village felt sorry for them and created several masks for the man and ghost."

"The masks were decorated with gems the man and ghost brought from abroad, radiant and incredibly beautiful."

"Wearing the masks allowed them to hide their faces and interact amicably with the villagers; the man and the ghost were very happy and grateful to the mask artisan's kindness."

"From then on, the man and ghost would wear masks and secretly attend village celebrations."

"However, those exquisitely crafted masks soon gained high popularity, and word spread to distant countries."

"Perhaps hearing of these rare radiant masks, some greedy Pokémon came to Kitakami..."

"They stealthily sneaked into the man and ghost's dwelling, attempting to take the carefully guarded masks; the man happened to be there, barely managing to keep one mask."

"But he was outnumbered and couldn't protect the remaining three masks, which were stolen by those Pokémon, and he was fatally injured, losing his life."

"Hours later, when the ghost returned to the cave and saw everything..."

"In the end, it donned the remaining green mask, descended the mountain to the village, and took out the Pokémon cheerfully flaunting the shiny masks."

"The oblivious villagers were baffled by what had occurred, fearing only the ghost's wrathful appearance."

"The villagers believed it was the three Pokémon that saved the village from the ghost's hands and affectionately called them the Loyal Three, giving them a respectable burial."

"The injured ghost, drowning in grief, returned alone to the cave in the back mountain."

"Our ancestor, the mask artisan, also struggled to let the truth be known, but nobody paid attention, and he was even persecuted as a heretic."

D grows increasingly silent, and at the end, he can't help but speak: "How could such a thing happen..."

Beside him, Koraidon and Cyclizar hang their heads sadly.

Pawmot holds tightly to a small hammer in its hand, thinking of the Pokémon it saw yesterday, its face full of anger.

Even Flutter Mane, floating in mid-air, reveals its small fangs, growling angrily.

The old man watches their reactions and then speaks:

"Since Ogerpon is willing to appear before you, it means it has a fondness for you, so I hope you can take Ogerpon with you, leaving this place to live elsewhere."

"Rather than remaining in Kitakami, enduring the hatred and dislike of the people here for no reason."

---

---

---

---

# 326: Chapter 323: Longing

The story ended, and for a moment, the air was filled with silence, no one spoke first.

D recalled the scene inside the fearsome cave he had seen before, where polished stone beds, tables, and stools were placed in that narrow space.

He could almost imagine the scene back then in his mind.

A man from a foreign land arrived with Ogerpon and found the cave, intending to settle there.

Together, they found a rough large stone on the mountain and brought it back to the entrance of the cave, with anticipation for a new life, they carefully polished the surface of the stone smooth.

The bed for sleeping, the table for eating, the stool for resting...

Until the arrival of those three greedy Pokemon disrupted this peaceful life.

The man lost his life in the incident, leaving Ogerpon alone in that cave filled with memories.

At that moment, D finally understood why Ogerpon was so sad when he asked if it had always lived here alone.

"Sigh."

The old man in front let out a long sigh: "The real history is completely opposite to the legends known by the villagers, if they knew that the three Loyal Three they buried with honor were the bad Pokemon, I can't imagine what would happen."

No one could guarantee they wouldn't be treated as heretics and suffer persecution again.

D nodded: "I understand."

The old man glanced over at the Pokemon beside him and was about to say something when the door was suddenly pushed open.

"Bang!"

A little boy of about four or five years old and a slightly older girl barged in directly.

Their faces were full of anger: "It's too much! How can the villagers treat Ghost like this!"

Seeing the two, the old man and Yukiko were shocked, quickly pulling them in, they checked outside to ensure no one else heard, then sighed in relief and closed the door properly.

Yukiko covered the girl's mouth, warning: "Keep your voice down, you must not let this out!"

The girl was upset: "But...but!"

The boy was silent, but his expression showed that he was definitely angry too.

The old man looked helpless: "Eavesdropping on adult conversations is not what good kids do!"

Feeling guilty, the siblings lowered their heads.

The old man sighed: "Sigh, I was planning to tell you when you were older, but since you've already heard, keep this truth in your heart and never tell it outside!"

Yukiko looked at D: "Sorry for letting you see this, these two kids are my granddaughter Carmine and grandson Kieran."

D nodded at the unfamiliar pair in front of him, kind of greeting them.

After calming down the grandchildren's emotions, the old man looked at D and said:

"Judging by your appearance, you're a strong Trainer, right? In that case, go capture Ogerpon in the Trainer's way and take it away from here!"

At this, D thought to himself that it would be easy if Ogerpon were a wild Pokemon, but clearly, Ogerpon wasn't a wild Pokemon.

He was willing to take Ogerpon away to start a new life, but whether Ogerpon was willing to leave this place full of memories was uncertain.

"I'll give it a try," D said.

"I'll count on you then," the old man nodded: "If you need any help, feel free to come to us, it's also a way to make up for the villagers' transgressions against Ogerpon!"

D nodded and then stood up to bid farewell to the old man, turning to leave, ready to see Ogerpon again.

However, before he could walk out the door, the two children named Carmine and Kieran chased after him.

"Uncle!" Carmine shouted.

D's face darkened, I'm not that old yet?!

Carmine turned to her brother hiding behind her, looking both disappointed and frustrated, then roared: "Kieran you coward, hurry up and say it!"

Kieran hesitated but then gathered courage to ask: "Well, are you a Trainer?"

D looked at him and nodded: "Sort of!"

Main job Rancher, side job Trainer, it's fine.

Kieran sighed in relief and then whispered: "Since I was little, I really liked Ogerpon, after hearing everything grandpa said, I hope it can have a better life, so if it wants to leave here, please take good care of it!"

D was slightly taken aback, then smiled and nodded: "I will."

Saying goodbye to the siblings, D rode Koraidon to Ghost Mountain again, finding that cave once more.

Unexpectedly, Ogerpon wasn't there.

D looked around but didn't find Ogerpon anywhere, it seemed it had gone out.

With nothing else to do, he returned to Mossui Town.

*

Late night, Ghost Mountain.

Ogerpon sat on the stone stool hugging its mask as usual, but compared to before, there was a pile of Berries on the stone table today.

Ogerpon dazedly recalled the events of the past two days.

That day, it met the strange human on Ghost Mountain.

In its memory, it had been a very, very long time since anyone dared to come here.

---

So it hid away, quietly observing the other's actions.

As a result, the other person was very perceptive and immediately discovered it, then came over to visit its home.

Initially, Ogerpon was very frightened and scared, thinking that the human would chase it away and attack it.

But instead of doing so, the human greeted it kindly and even gave it a few Berries.

Ogerpon was extremely surprised, but it was too scared and had never encountered such a situation before, so it fled in a panic.

When it returned to the cave, it saw a small mountain of Berries piled on the stone table.

Ogerpon initially thought that after the person left, they would never return, but the next day, the person came again. They spoke kindly to it and gave some food when they left.

But there were so many Berries, it simply couldn't finish them!

Would they come again tomorrow? A hint of anticipation rose in Ogerpon's heart.

Ogerpon looked at the table full of Berries in front of it and suddenly thought of events from long ago.

When it was in its childhood, a tall human adopted it.

The man brought it here and cared for it like a child.

He would also bring Berries from outside and place them on the table, leaving them for it to eat.

In Ogerpon's heart, the man was like a father to it.

However... the man left.

That day, in anger, it went to the town to avenge the man and defeated those four greedy Pokemon.

But the person who had adopted it could never return.

"Plop!"

A teardrop fell onto the damaged Teal Mask, followed by another.

Thoughts of longing went silently, as Ogerpon buried its face in the mask and sobbed quietly amid the sudden heavy rain outside.

*

Early the next morning.

A sudden heavy rain blanketed the entire Mossui Town.

The agricultural exchange meeting was forced to halt, so D could only stay in the community center listening to the rain.

A few Pokemon stayed listlessly in the room.

D watched for a while and then stood up, recalling them into the Poke Balls. He put on a raincoat and rode Koraidon to Ghost Mountain.

It wasn't until he saw Ogerpon holding the mask in the cave that D felt relieved.

He brought Koraidon into the cave.

After learning about Ogerpon's story, D viewed Ogerpon differently now.

Because this Pokemon was truly pitiful.

Despised by the people of Mossui Town, it could only stay alone, clinging to that mask in this cave filled with memories.

Swept up in sympathy, D walked in with a backpack full of food.

Not knowing how to comfort Ogerpon, he simply dumped the food onto the stone table for it, completely ignoring the fact that Ogerpon hadn't finished the Berries from yesterday.

Ogerpon stared blankly at the busy human in front of it.

It had thought the human wouldn't come today...

"Here!" D grinned, then handed over a bottle of warm goat milk that had been heated at the community center. To keep it from cooling, he had kept it warm in his pocket: "Rainy days are quite cold, have some warm milk to warm yourself up."

He placed the goat milk in Ogerpon's hands and then turned to take out a towel from the backpack to wipe the cold rainwater off Koraidon to prevent it from catching a cold.

Seeing Ogerpon looking over, D comforted, "Don't be afraid of it, this is Koraidon. Though it looks fierce, it's actually quite gentle!"

"Ah gasi!" Koraidon shook the rainwater off vigorously, splashing D.

After wiping Koraidon dry, D opened the backpack again and took out a blanket handed to him by the Indeedee Sibling and spread it on the stone bed.

Then, he released Slugma from its Poke Ball.

With the warm help of Slugma, the cold cave soon became warm.

The rain poured outside, yet inside it was beautifully cozy.

Even the dull gray walls were illuminated to a warm orange hue by Slugma's flames.

In just a few minutes, the cave underwent a drastic transformation.

So quickly, Ogerpon hardly even had time to react.

"Boni..."

D took out a frying pan from his backpack, set it atop Slugma, and began preparing breakfast for Ogerpon.

"Thank you, Slugma," D said, feeding two Berries to Slugma. After Slugma ate them all, its body temperature surged dramatically.

The oil in the frying pan quickly heated up, sizzling noisily.

D quickly cracked open an egg, added sliced tomatoes, and when the process was complete, he toasted two slices of fragrant bread using Slugma.

Finally, he placed the cooked egg and other ingredients into the soft bread, completing a warm sandwich.

D handed the sandwich to Ogerpon, "Go on, eat!"

Ogerpon's face was filled with bewilderment.

D realized his actions might seem too abrupt to Ogerpon.

So he thought for a moment and explained to Ogerpon, "Don't worry, Ogerpon."

Upon hearing the human call its name, Ogerpon's face showed a clearly shocked expression.

"Boni...?"

"A villager from town has already told me everything about you."

---

"Boni..."

"That person is a descendant of the craftsman who once made your mask, and he feels very sorry for everything you've gone through."

"Boni..."

"I'm so saddened, however, from the first time I saw you, I knew you weren't the vicious demon described in the rumors."

Upon hearing this, Ogerpon looked up, its eyes resembling a bright star.

D grinned, "So, trust me, we mean no harm and won't hurt you, nor will we dislike you, because you have never done anything wrong."

Ogerpon was a bit dazed, it looked at the person in front of it, and then a happy expression appeared on its face.

"Boni Boni～"

"Eat up!"

Ogerpon finally began to eat the sandwich, yet as it ate, large tears suddenly fell from its eyes.

Years of loneliness, being ostracized, the grievance of having its mask taken away, the pain of its father being hurt by villains...

All these emotions intertwined into a mess, making Ogerpon so sad it couldn't control its big and sobbing cries.

D was a bit bewildered, why did it suddenly start crying?

D didn't know what to do, he hurriedly stood up, but unexpectedly, a few Poke Balls in his pocket accidentally fell out.

The Poke Balls on the ground automatically opened upon impact, and the silhouettes of two Pokemon appeared from the white light.

One was Flutter Mane, and the other was Cyclizar.

Cyclizar, seeing that Pokemon crying so sorrowfully, couldn't help but be puzzled.

While Flutter Mane's gaze turned to the Pokemon beside it.

Being a Ghost Type Pokemon, it keenly sensed a strong obsession there.

It saw a tall, stately figure standing beside Ogerpon looking at it.

As if realizing it had been discovered, that ghost turned its head and looked at Flutter Mane.

Once Ogerpon stopped crying, D helped it wipe its tears, then asked, "Want to leave here with us?"

Ogerpon was stunned.

D sincerely said, "I'm a Trainer from another place, and in a few days, I'm going to leave here."

"So, I want to take you away from this place to my hometown, where no one will fear you, nor ostracize you, they are all good people who love Pokemon."

"There are also many Pokemon living in my home, and there you can meet many new friends; do you want to come with us?"

As he spoke, D extended his hand toward Ogerpon.

Ogerpon listened dumbly to these words.

It looked down at the mask in its hand, then at the human in front of it, thinking that once they left, they'd never meet again...

Thinking of this, Ogerpon couldn't help but feel very sad.

However...

Looking at the hand inviting it, Ogerpon glanced around and looked at this warm cave.

It suddenly felt very afraid, not wanting to return to that cold and lonely atmosphere again.

Thus, Ogerpon mustered up the courage, and cautiously extended its own hand, placing it in the hand of the human in front of it.

D felt relieved, then turned his hand to hold Ogerpon's somewhat cold little hand.

"Then, come home with me!" D said softly.

"Boni!"

The rain outside had unknowingly stopped, D took several Pokemon and walked out of the fear cave.

The rain had cleared, and above was a blue sky with not a cloud in sight.

Just looking up at the sky would make people feel good.

D did not immediately leave, he turned around and looked at the cave where Ogerpon once lived, silently promising in his heart:

"Rest assured, I will definitely take good care of Ogerpon, and will never let it fall into such a situation again!"

Meanwhile, Flutter Mane also looked back toward the cave entrance.

It saw that ghost walking out, standing at the cave entrance looking at them, and waved goodbye to them.

Flutter Mane sensed that strong obsession was dissipating, eventually, that ghost also vanished from this world.

"Flutter Mane?"

At this moment, D's voice came from behind.

Flutter Mane turned around.

D took out a Poke Ball and said to it, "We should head back!"

"Dream."

Flutter Mane responded, turned around, and entered the Poke Ball.

---

---

---

---

# 327: Chapter 324: New Memories, Sudden Upheaval

D and Ogerpon rode down the mountain on Koraidon, and as they were about to reach Mossui Town, fear suddenly appeared on Ogerpon's face.

"Pony..."

"Ah gah ss?"

Koraidon glanced back and then landed outside the town, turning with D to look at the frightened Ogerpon.

"What's wrong?" D asked, puzzled, but quickly realized that Ogerpon might be afraid of going into town due to previous exclusion by the townspeople.

"Do you not want to go in?" D asked.

"Pony..." Ogerpon nodded gently.

Just at that moment, a villager carrying a hoe passed by.

Ogerpon hurriedly put on a mask and hid behind D.

The villager glanced over, recognizing D as a farmer from outside, visiting Kitakami.

He waved with a smile and greeted them: "Good afternoon! Have you had lunch?"

"Not yet," D replied politely. "I toured around outside, and now I'm about to head back."

The villager nodded with a smile and left carrying his hoe.

Koraidon lowered its head helplessly: "Ah gah ss..."

"Don't be afraid," D turned to Ogerpon and thought carefully before asking, "It was the descendant of the village's mask artisan who told me about your situation. His ancestor wanted to reveal the truth to other villagers but was persecuted to death by them."

"Pony..." Ogerpon showed hesitation, touching the mask on its face, recalling the human artisan who made those masks for it.

"That artisan's descendant has been very concerned about you and feels deeply sorry. Would you like to meet him with me?" D asked.

Fearing Ogerpon might be scared, D comforted it further: "Don't worry, no one will harm you this time. Both I and Koraidon will stand by your side."

"Ah gah ss!" Koraidon agreed loudly.

Ogerpon looked up at them and couldn't help but show emotion.

After hesitating, it finally mustered the courage to nod gently.

D reached out to pat Ogerpon's head, then turned to the town, noticing there was still some distance to Kieran's home, and they might encounter several villagers along the way.

Ogerpon could be very scared then.

Thinking briefly, D took out an empty Poke Ball: "If you're scared, you can come inside here first!"

Ogerpon almost immediately ran into the Poke Ball.

The red and white Poke Ball gently shook twice before settling down completely.

This signified success in capture.

However, unlike the feelings when capturing Dragonite and the others before, even knowing this Pokemon might be a Legendary Pokemon, D couldn't feel happy at all.

Because Ogerpon was simply too pitiable.

It's the most pitiful Pokemon apart from Calyrex, and in some sense, even more tragic than Calyrex.

"Once back to the ranch, I'll have Alcremie and the others take good care of Ogerpon and play with it," D thought silently.

He sighed quietly, put away the Poke Ball, and then entered the town with Koraidon, indeed encountering many villagers who greeted him along the way.

D responded to each, and soon, he arrived at Kieran's home.

Kieran's grandfather Yukito was feeding a Poliwag by the Magikarp Fountain when he saw D coming and quickly stood up to ask, "Have you seen Ogerpon?"

D nodded, then took out Ogerpon's Poke Ball and released it.

At the moment of seeing Ogerpon, Yukito widened his eyes and hastily closed the courtyard gate, pushing D and the two Pokemon inside.

Preparing lunch, Yukiko saw this scene and showed a shocked expression, dropping the iron basin in her hand onto the floor with a clang.

On the sofa watching TV, Kieran and Carmine quickly stood up.

Carmine, shocked, covered her mouth with her hand, while Kieran stared blankly at Ogerpon.

Although he hadn't seen it personally, the masked figure seemed so familiar!

Carmine asked in disbelief, "That Pokemon is..."

D glanced at Ogerpon hiding behind him and introduced, "This is Ogerpon."

Kieran felt incredibly excited at that moment.

He always liked Ogerpon because it was so strong, defeating the Loyal Three by itself, which was really cool!

And after learning about Ogerpon's truth from his grandfather, he liked it even more.

Beside him, Yukito, having confirmed the doors and windows were shut tight, was finally moved to tears, looking at Ogerpon and murmuring, "That's great... That's great..."

"Pony?" Ogerpon remained timid as it cautiously looked at the unfamiliar humans in front of it.

D introduced, "This is the descendant of the artisan who made masks for you. These two are his grandson and granddaughter."

In continuation, D looked at Yukito and said, "Ogerpon is willing to leave with me. I plan to take it away to my hometown once the exchange event ends in a few days."

"Good, good, I'll entrust everything to you then!" Yukito felt a huge stone had fallen from his heart.

Sitting relieved on the sofa, he quickly bounced up as if sitting on a spring: "Oh right, there's one more thing!"

---

"What?" D was slightly taken aback.

Yukito explained, "The mask, Ogerpon's mask!"

"My ancestors once made four masks for the ghost, but now it seems like only one remains by its side... Huh? This mask seems to be broken?" Yukito observed carefully.

Hearing this, Ogerpon also removed the mask and looked sadly at the Teal Mask in its hand.

D had initially wanted to ask about the whereabouts of the other three masks, but upon hearing this, he changed his question, "Can that mask be repaired?"

This thing is so important to Ogerpon, carrying its past memories. If it can be repaired, it should be.

"Although my skills aren't as good as my ancestors', don't worry, fixing this mask won't be a problem!" Yukito said after examining the mask carefully.

"That's good." D breathed a sigh of relief.

Yukito: "I have all the other materials needed for the mask's repair here, but there's one crucial material that requires your help."

"What is it?"

"A crystal shard submerged at the bottom of the Crystal Light Pool," Yukito said. "However, the Crystal Light Pool is located at the peak of Ghost Mountain, and on the way, many dangerous Pokemon reside. Reaching there is not easy."

Ogerpon, upon hearing this, couldn't help but feel very worried.

D ignored the latter part because to Koraidon and Pawmo, no matter how strong the wild Pokemon were, it wouldn't be an issue: "The peak of Ghost Mountain? Got it, I'm heading there now."

D took out the Poke Ball, recalled Ogerpon, and then turned around without hesitation, mounting Koraidon and heading straight for the peak of Ghost Mountain.

However, halfway there, Ogerpon suddenly came out of the Poke Ball on its own, looking worried: "Boni..."

D immediately realized Ogerpon's feelings. He reached out to stroke its head, comforting it, "It's okay, don't worry."

But Ogerpon still couldn't be at ease, and after brief hesitation, it decided to stay outside.

Although it wasn't very good at battling, it could still help if D needed it.

Thus, D and Ogerpon rode Koraidon, flying towards the summit of Ghost Mountain.

Meanwhile, at Yukito's house.

Yukito looked at the Teal Mask in his hand and said to the curious Carmine and Kieran, "There are four masks in total, besides the Teal Mask, there are three others."

"What are they?" Carmine hurriedly asked.

"They are the Blue Wellspring Mask, the Red Hearthflame Mask, and the Gray Cornerstone Mask," Yukito sighed. "But these three masks are now kept in the shrine at the Kitakami Center, and the villagers regard them as relics of the Loyal Three Pokemon..."

"Damn it! How could this happen!!!" Carmine gritted her teeth in anger.

Kieran didn't speak but clenched his fists.

"Sigh, forget it. What's most important now is helping Ogerpon fix this Teal Mask first." Yukito let out a sigh, leaving his meal untouched as he focused on repairing the Teal Mask.

After finishing lunch, Carmine and Kieran became increasingly agitated.

They went into town, with Carmine trembling with anger, "This is too much! Those things belong to Ogerpon!"

Kieran looked towards the direction of the Kitakami Center, tugged on his sister's sleeve, and summoned the courage to say, "Sister, let's go get the masks back for the ghost!"

Upon hearing this, Carmine seemed suddenly inspired and excitedly said, "That's right! I didn't think you'd be so smart, Kieran!"

Carmine looked around, "At this time, everyone's having lunch, so the Kitakami Center should be empty. Let's go! We'll snatch the masks back for Ogerpon!"

The siblings quickly put together a plan and quietly sneaked into the Kitakami Center halfway up the mountain.

*

Ghost Mountain Peak.

With Koraidon's guidance, D avoided the trouble of climbing and arrived directly at the top.

But when he saw the situation at the summit clearly, he was stunned.

A beautiful lake lay quietly at the mountain top, and beneath the calm surface were blocks of crystals emitting dazzling light!

These were clearly crystals only found at the bottom of the Paldea Giant Pit!

Professor Sada mentioned that these crystals contained immense Tera Energy.

At this moment, D suddenly realized how the Slugma earlier had become Terastalized.

But... why would there be Tera from the Paldea Region all the way here in Kitakami on the other side of the planet?

Ogerpon didn't understand these things. It looked at the shining stones below the lake's surface, and its mood suddenly became cheerful: "Boni!"

It could feel that its mask once held this kind of power!

Seeing Ogerpon happy, D thought it was because it enjoyed the beauty of the Crystal Light Pool.

Koraidon landed slowly, and as D dismounted from it, he asked, "Ogerpon, have you been here before?"

"Boni~"

Ogerpon shook its head and then cheerfully ran to the lakeside, gazing brightly at the beautiful stones at the bottom of the lake.

There were no other humans here, and Ogerpon was very relaxed.

D and Koraidon followed with smiles.

He observed the situation at the lake bottom, pondering how to obtain the crystal shards below.

Looking around, the whole surface surrounding the Crystal Light Pool showed a peculiar white, with some scattered bits of rocks on it.

---

"It seems like Ghost Mountain might have once been a volcano," D speculated in his heart.

On an unknown date in an unknown year, after the volcano erupted, the Crystal Light Pool gradually formed in the crater.

The abundant volcanic ash scattered across the area made the land incredibly fertile, attracting people to settle and establish Kitakami.

"Could it be that the Tera comes from underground?" D couldn't help but guess.

"Boni?"

At this moment, Ogerpon wore a troubled expression because the stones were all under the water and it didn't know how to retrieve them.

Seeing this, D smiled and began to undress, "Leave it to me!"

Under the sunlight, he gracefully leaped and plunged into the lake, picking up several crystal shards from the bottom.

A moment later, D resurfaced, smiling at Ogerpon, "Successfully got them!"

"Boni!" Ogerpon let out a happy growl.

D put on his clothes and didn't linger long, "Next, let's head back to town to repair the mask!"

He mounted Koraidon, reached out to pull Ogerpon up, and returned to Mossui Town together.

As they approached, Ogerpon was still very afraid and voluntarily returned to the Poke Ball.

Seeing this, D sighed helplessly.

With things like this, he didn't feel like staying in Kitakami any longer because Ogerpon seemed to dislike it here.

"Well, maybe we can end the exchange activity early and go back to Paldea!"

Koraidon landed in Yukito's yard, and D handed the collected Tera Shards to Yukito.

Yukito was greatly shocked at how fast he was, "So quick?!"

"That's because of the help from my Flying Pokemon!" D laughed.

Koraidon proudly let out a cry, "Aga hiss!" Yes, it was indeed the one!

"I knew I was right!" Yukito took a deep breath, "Now, it's time for the final step!"

He placed the Tera Shards onto the mask and then picked up tools to continue working.

Taking this opportunity, D glanced around but didn't see Kieran and Carmine.

Huh? Where did those two go?

Just as D was wondering, Yukito suddenly exclaimed from inside the house, "It's finished!"

D immediately walked over, only to see Yukito holding a brand-new Teal Mask.

Set in the mask was a crystal radiating a beautiful glow, with a mysterious energy slowly flowing within.

With just one look, D could feel something unusual about this mask.

It was definitely an exclusive item for Ogerpon, just like Dialga's Adamant Orb!

"Alright, release Ogerpon now!" Yukito said with anticipation.

D nodded, and with a flash of white light, Ogerpon appeared in the room.

D reached out to receive the mask from Yukito and then handed it to Ogerpon.

Ogerpon stared blankly at the brand-new Teal Mask, and at this moment, time seemed to roll back.

Back then, a mask craftsman once handed it a mask just like this.

As past memories surfaced, tears glistened in Ogerpon's eyes.

It looked up at D, then wiped away its tears and put on the mask that carried new memories.

At the very moment Ogerpon donned the mask, atop Apple Hill at Loyalty Plaza.

As if resonating with the mask's presence, a pillar of purple light shot up from the shrine dedicated to the Loyal Three.

Three Pokemon exuding an ominous aura crossed their arms and strode out slowly from within the pillar of light.

Seeing the familiar world, they had evil grins on their faces.

Meanwhile, as D was asking Ogerpon how it felt wearing the new mask, a frantic voice came from outside.

Immediately following, a girl rushed in hurriedly and exclaimed:

"Grandpa Yukito, it's bad! Kieran and Carmine got caught trying to steal masks at the Kitakami center!"

---

---

---

---

# 328: Chapter 325: Gibber Gabber, Zombie Village

Kitakami Square.

When D and Yukito arrived there, Kieran and Carmine were angrily confronting the Kitakami administrator and several village guards.

The administrator frowned, looking serious. In this remote rural town, stealing was a very serious offense.

"Kieran, Carmine, why did you steal those things? Those are the relics left behind by the Loyal Three Pokemon who once protected the village!"

Hearing this, the siblings held the masks tightly behind them.

Kieran hid behind his sister, while Carmine angrily glared at the group of self-righteous people in front of them and shouted in frustration:

"These masks are not those bad guys' things! They originally belonged to the ghost! It was those three villains who snatched the masks from the ghost!"

The administrator widened his eyes and became a bit angry: "What nonsense are you talking about? Who told you this? Clearly, the Loyal Three protected the village from the ghost!"

Carmine stomped angrily in place and looked around at the growing number of villagers, feeling extremely flustered.

Just then, D and Yukito pushed through the crowd to come in, shielding Kieran and Carmine behind them.

Grandma Yukiko took the siblings into her arms, and Yukito glared at them angrily.

These two kids had been too impulsive now!

He shouldn't have told them those things in advance!

"Yukito, what on earth is going on?!" The administrator asked, frowning.

Yukito sighed, showing a helpless expression: "At this point, some things can't continue to be mistaken, administrator, the legend about the ghost and the Loyal Three is actually the opposite in reality!"

"It was those three Pokemon who killed the ghost's family in order to seize the masks, and the ghost killed them only to avenge its family!"

"It never harmed the villagers from start to finish!"

"What's going on?"

"What really happened?"

"Is the legend false?"

The surrounding townspeople murmured among themselves.

Upon hearing this, Ogerpon, hiding behind D, once again showed a frightened expression and fearfully moved closer to D.

"It's okay, don't be afraid." D softly comforted it.

"How could such a thing be?" The administrator's expression became more serious, "Everyone saw at the time! The Loyal Three died tragically at the hands of the ghost, Yukito, you're causing chaos!"

Moreover, so many years have passed since that event, and the truth is no longer important. Compared to that irrelevant truth, clearly, the development of Kitakami is the most important thing.

"Let this matter end here! No one is allowed to mention it again, nor is it to be leaked!"

The administrator warned with a glance to everyone around.

At that moment, a commotion suddenly arose in the distance.

Accompanied by sharp growls, everyone quickly turned their heads.

Then, they saw three Pokemon speeding toward them.

One shaped like a dog, strong-bodied, one like a monkey, short and cunning-looking, and one like a chicken, gracefully flapping its wings and charging forward.

Seeing this scene, the villagers all had shocked expressions on their faces.

"Are those the Loyal Three Pokemon?"

"Weren't they already sacrificed? How are they resurrected?"

"Oh my god! What are they going to do!"

Ogerpon, hiding behind D, also saw that scene. After many years, seeing those three Pokemon that killed its father again, Ogerpon still could not control its emotions.

It let out an angry and repressed growl: "Boni...!"

D squinted his eyes at those three Loyal Three Pokemon. Although he didn't know what exactly happened or how they were resurrected, he quickly came to himself and asked softly: "Is it them?"

Ogerpon angrily nodded: "Boni!"

It clenched its fist, and in a state of anger, a terrifying green energy rapidly condensed in its hand, transforming into an enormous thorny club.

Is this Ogerpon's ability? D was a bit surprised, but he still reached out to stop Ogerpon's action.

In Ogerpon's puzzled gaze, Koraidon stepped forward and then slightly turned its head: "Aga-si!"

D smiled and said, "Let us do it! This time you're not alone."

"Boni..." Ogerpon was stunned for a moment, then nodded vigorously: "Boni!"

Meanwhile, the administrator was delighted to see the resurrection of the Loyal Three: "It's the Loyal Three Pokemon!"

However, before he finished speaking, the red Pokemon beside D suddenly burst forth with a terrifying aura.

Then, its crimson body rocketed forward, with scarlet energy silently pulsating, and under the bright sun in the high sky, a violent ancient explosion swept across.

The strongest dog-like one at the forefront was instantly sent flying and lay on the ground with its tongue out, life or death unknown.

Seeing this scene, the villagers let out a gasp of shock.

Kieran and Carmine excitedly clenched their fists: "Koraidon! Well done!"

The administrator widened his eyes in disbelief: "What are you doing?!"

D: "Standing up for my Pokemon."

"Pokemon?" The administrator was momentarily dazed. He wondered what standing up for what? When did the Loyal Three ever bully this outsider's Pokemon?

But in the next second, he noticed the unknown creature dressed in a green cloak, wearing a verdant mask on its face, standing beside this outsider.

---

At first, the villagers thought it was a person wearing a mask, but now...

Combining this with what Kieran and Yukito just mentioned about the identity of this unknown creature, an answer instantly emerged in everyone's mind.

A ghost!

Someone subconsciously took a step back.

D didn't pay too much attention, considering that technically, the people of Kitakami hadn't persecuted Ogerpon, they were just affected by the misunderstanding, and Ogerpon didn't hate these people.

He directed his gaze towards Koraidon.

At this moment, after Munkidori was knocked down, only Fezandipiti remained.

However, that Fezandipiti was very smart, seeing the situation wasn't right and immediately abandoned snatching the mask, turning to flee.

"It's running away!" Carmine shouted urgently.

D took a glance, then chased after it with Ogerpon and Koraidon.

Carmine and Kieran quickly followed as well because Fezandipiti was flying in the direction of Mossui Town!

The remaining administrators and some townsfolk looked at Munkidori and Fezandipiti lying on the ground, their faces peaceful, unsure of what to do for a moment.

"Administrator, what should we do?" A village guard asked.

Without hesitation, the administrator said, "The Loyal Three are crucial to the tourism development of Mossui Town, we rely on them to attract tourists. We must ensure they don't die under any circumstances! Quickly, go to town and get healing medicine from Miss Joy!"

"Yes, yes!"

*

Fezandipiti didn't fly far; it landed in Mossui Town.

When D arrived with Ogerpon, Fezandipiti had already reached the Taoye Store in town.

In the shocked gaze of the store owner, Fezandipiti grabbed a dusty, purple doll from the stall with its wings, shaking it continuously.

The manner was as if it was worshipping or praying.

"Gaga hiss?"

Koraidon looked at Fezandipiti with a trace of confusion.

"What is that?" Carmine, panting as she caught up, asked.

"I don't know." D shook his head.

And just then, the purple peach doll suddenly moved eerily.

Next, the peach split in half, revealing a strange creature inside.

Fezandipiti showed an obvious expression of joy.

Seeing this, D was slightly stunned.

Could that thing also be a Pokemon?

"Bonnie!"

Ogerpon, nearby, also saw this scene and showed an angry expression.

D immediately understood that this thing must have bullied Ogerpon!

"Koraidon, attack it!" D directly ordered.

"Gaga hiss!"

However, at this moment, a sudden change occurred.

The purple peach Pokemon suddenly emitted a layer of ghostly purple light.

Before Koraidon could charge in, it swiftly hid behind the store owner.

Koraidon was forced to stop.

"Grandma Rinko, please move!" Carmine shouted anxiously.

However, the old woman stood still.

Something's wrong! D immediately sensed something unusual in the air.

The next second, he saw the old woman's mouth move, slowly uttering two words:

"Ki be..."

Meanwhile, at Kitakami Center.

The group waiting to treat Munkidori and Fezandipiti suddenly froze.

Then, in the perplexed gaze of surrounding villagers, they slowly straightened up, eyes vacant, like zombies.

"Ki be..."

The remaining villagers witnessing this eerie scene stepped back.

"Mayor?" Someone tried calling out.

But the mayor couldn't respond, their mouths repeating ki be, while uniformly heading down the mountain.

At this moment, not only at Kitakami Center but even in Mossui Town, many people had become like zombies.

Some had been eating, some going to the bathroom, some sleeping.

But now, they all stopped what they were doing, walking out from their homes with vacant stares, stiff postures, gathering in front of Taoye Store, surrounding D's group.

Witnessing this eerie scene, Carmine puzzledly asked, "Everyone? What on earth is happening?"

"They're being controlled by that Pokemon!" D immediately identified.

Trying to manipulate these villagers as a shield to prevent itself from harm?

Truly villainous!

Seeing the surrounding villagers increase, some even starting to grab Kieran's legs, pulling him constantly.

Kieran fearfully screamed, "Sister!"

Carmine plunged in to pull her brother out.

Seeing this, D frowned, turned to take out the Poke Balls of Dragonite and Flutter Mane.

"Dragonite, get them out of here!"

"Roar!"

Dragonite surveyed the surroundings and quickly understood the situation, flying forward, knocking aside the crowd, using its claws to lift Kieran and Carmine into the air.

Seeing them escape, D and Ogerpon also mounted Koraidon to ascend to the sky.

At this moment, Fezandipiti arrogantly rushed toward Koraidon.

Koraidon, in ride form, wasn't suited for defense; seeing Fezandipiti about to collide with them, Flutter Mane, having used Phantom Force earlier, suddenly darted out.

Its powerful telekinesis materialized instantly, like a tsunami crashing toward Fezandipiti.

Fezandipiti's attack was instantly interrupted.

"Power Gem!" D said in a deep voice.

---

Flutter Mane didn't respond; it silently vanished from its original spot, then flickered behind Fezandipiti, unleashing a beam as dazzling as a gem, engulfing Fezandipiti.

Dragonite followed up with a Dragon Rage from a distance.

Fezandipiti let out a shrill cry, then fell to the ground like a kite with a broken string.

D quickly scanned the area below but found that the strange Pokemon had completely disappeared.

D frowned; yet another Pokemon he had never seen before.

This feeling of knowing nothing was really frustrating.

Dragonite and Flutter Mane came over to Koraidon's side.

Kieran and Carmine watched the townspeople below, who were like walking corpses, in panic.

"Why has everyone become like this?"

"Sister, what should we do?"

"Grandpa and Grandma are still at Kitakami Center, who knows what happened to them..."

The two kids could only look to D for help.

D thought for a moment and asked, "Have you seen that purple Pokemon earlier?"

The siblings shook their heads.

"How about Ogerpon?" D asked again.

"Boni!" Ogerpon looked furious.

D immediately knew the answer: "Is it in cahoots with those three Loyal Three?"

"Boni!" Ogerpon nodded; it was wearing a mask, so D couldn't see its expression, but he could feel Ogerpon's overwhelming anger.

Below, the villagers stretched out their arms, trying to grab them like wild beasts.

Watching this scene, D's mind inexplicably conjured up images of zombies.

At this moment, another group of controlled villagers arrived.

D turned his head and saw Yukito, Yukiko, and several village leaders among the crowd.

Kieran and Carmine also saw the scene and shouted anxiously, "Grandpa!"

"Sister, what should we do?" Kieran asked.

Even the usually cheerful Carmine was at a loss now.

D said, "We can only try to find that purple Pokemon; I suspect it's using some ability to control the townspeople."

But for now, they had to leave this place.

D looked around and rode Koraidon to circle Mossui Town, finally choosing to land on the roof of the public hall.

Gradually, the sky turned darker.

A mysterious purple mist began to permeate the entire Mossui Town.

The controlled villagers wandered the streets like zombies, occasionally uttering low growls.

Watching this scene, D's expression turned strange.

Holy shit, how did this place suddenly turn from Pokemon World into a horror movie?

Kind of scary, too.

Kitakami is not fun at all; once he got back, he would never come again.

The only consolation was that not everyone had been controlled by that Pokemon.

Because D saw a few normal villagers cautiously avoiding the others and sneaking back home, locking their doors and windows tightly.

Since not everyone was controlled, it meant that the Pokemon needed certain conditions to control humans.

At this time, Carmine and Kieran suddenly remembered the masks they were holding.

They hurriedly placed the three masks in front of Ogerpon: "Right! Masks! Ogerpon, these are the masks we retrieved for you!"

Ogerpon stared blankly at the two siblings: "Boni..."

"Take them! With these, all the masks are back, and you can safely return to Mr. D's homeland!" Carmine said.

D, being called "Mr." made him feel, "..."

Ogerpon remembered how these two kids stood up for it in the town during the day and felt touched.

Kieran also happily looked at the ghost, "Don't be afraid, we're all your good friends!"

Ogerpon was very happy: "Boni～"

The past two days had been its happiest since its father left, because it met D, and the two people who wanted to befriend it helped it retrieve the masks.

"By the way... Mr. D." Kieran said hesitantly.

D: "What's up?"

Kieran said timidly, "Can I go see the ghost at your place when I grow up?"

D was slightly stunned and then smiled, saying, "Of course you can. When I leave here, I'll tell you where I live, and you can visit Ogerpon anytime."

"Boni～" Ogerpon's little face also showed a smile.

After a brief moment of happiness, Carmine returned to her panicked state.

"Mr. D, what should we do now? That Pokemon is too evil! Using the townspeople as shields to protect itself!"

D: "First of all, don't call me 'Mr.'; I'm only fourteen years older than you, not that old yet."

Seeing D's relaxed demeanor, Kieran couldn't help but feel envious.

He truly was an adult! Not scared at all when faced with such things!

D, having many cards to play, pondered which one to use: "As for solutions..."

First, they could rely on their own power to search gradually, like sending out the Ghost Type Flutter Mane to track it.

Flutter Mane, coming from ancient times, is a born hunter, plus it's a Ghost Type Pokemon, capable of using Phantom Force to hide its presence without being noticed.

Second method, call over Indeedee and ask it to use Lunar Dance to purify the controlled villagers.

The third option, the simplest, was to directly summon Calyrex to engage in large-scale purging, launch a holy war in Kitakami to solve the problem, while also seizing faith here.

---

---

---

---

# 329: Chapter 326: The Holy War Begins! Calyrex Descends upon Kitakami!

Thinking of this, D immediately began preaching to Carmine and Kieran:

"I wonder if you've heard the legend of the King of Bountiful Harvests."

The siblings were momentarily stunned by the sudden topic change.

Carmine: "The King of Bountiful Harvests?"

D nodded, immediately becoming the Great Preacher nearby:

"His name is Calyrex, a Pokemon beloved and trusted by the people of Paldea. Next, I will tell you a story about Calyrex."

From wandering the land riding his loyal steed, to being revered as a king by humans, then to being forgotten by the world, endlessly roaming the icy tundra in loneliness.

Until a millennium later, when the power of Calyrex was awakened, he re-emerged to save a mountain. In gratitude, modern humans built a shrine for him on that mountain, where incense continues to burn.

"Wow, what an incredible Pokemon!" After hearing the story, Carmine was amazed: "Even more powerful than those three Loyal Three! No, those troublemakers don't compare to Calyrex!"

Beside her, Kieran was also captivated by a great Pokemon that had once been a king, ruling a kingdom.

But the question is, what does such a Pokemon have to do with Mossui Town?

After a brief shock, Carmine quickly regained her composure, slightly puzzled:

"Uncle D, aren't you digressing? Isn't the most important thing right now finding a way to save everyone in the town?"

D continued: "Fortunately, I am friends with Calyrex, and I can ask him to come over to Porto Marinada Town to help."

What?!

Growing up in the countryside and having little exposure to the world, the siblings were immediately astounded.

Kieran: "D-san actually knows such a Pokemon!!!"

Carmine was shocked, covering her mouth: "Uncle, you're amazing!"

D: "..."

Forget it, let the little girl call me whatever she wants!

Getting Calyrex to come over is a very easy thing to do.

D glanced down the mountain, asking: "Is there a Pokemon Center in town?"

"Yes, it's on the street behind the Taoye Store!" Carmine quickly replied.

D nodded, then sent the two kids onto Dragonite, while he rode Koraidon, bypassing the people wandering the streets, passing through the purple mist lingering in the air, and landed at the Pokemon Center.

The lights were off inside the Pokemon Center, seemingly hearing the sounds from outside, a faint sound of footsteps came from within the Pokemon Center.

Soon, a crack was opened in the curtain.

Miss Joy peeked through it, saw a Trainer and two children outside, quickly opened the door, and quietly called:

"Come in quickly!"

The group hurriedly took refuge in the Pokemon Center.

Miss Joy observed the situation outside through the window, looking worried:

"Everyone in the town suddenly became very strange…"

While speaking, she closed the curtains and looked at the two children in front of her, softly comforting them:

"Don't worry, I have just reported this to the Sinnoh League next door by phone, someone will come to help us soon."

Carmine and Kieran were not afraid at all, they looked at D with anticipation, eager to meet the great King of Bountiful Harvests.

Miss Joy was taken aback by this situation: "Eh?"

D was observing the environment inside the Pokemon Center and quickly noticed a row of video phones in a corner of the lobby.

D turned to Miss Joy, asking: "Miss Joy, can I use the video phone here?"

Miss Joy hadn't figured out what was happening, but she nodded: "Sure, of course!"

D immediately went to dial the ranch's number.

Soon, the call was connected, and his sister Indeedee's face appeared on the screen.

Hearing D's voice, Alcremie and Glaceon also quickly gathered around.

A group of Pokemon squeezed their heads together, happily watching the screen.

D smiled and greeted everyone, then looked at his sister Indeedee, saying:

"Sister, something happened on my end, and I need to ask Calyrex to come over."

Hearing this, his sister Indeedee quickly turned to look toward the living room behind her: "Awwee." Calyrex was still watching TV there!

Hearing that D needed his help, Calyrex floated over, asking: "Crown moo gee gee?"

D: "..." Almost forgot telepathy can't be used over the internet.

After a brief thought, he succinctly explained what was happening to Calyrex.

"Something happened, there's a strange Pokemon in this town, using unknown abilities to control all the townspeople, and I can't track it…"

Calyrex understood, nodding slightly, indicating that he would come over immediately.

Seeing this, D said to his sister Indeedee: "Sister, take Calyrex to the Pokemon Center in town, and use the Pokeball Transmission Machine there to send Calyrex over."

"Awwee!" Hearing the urgency in D's voice, his sister Indeedee's expression turned serious.

The call ended, and everyone in the ranch sprang into action.

Calyrex's figure flashed, disappearing from its original spot, then appeared in front of the stable.

Calyrex was quite surprised by D's sudden request for support.

Because D rarely asks for his help, and he can also sense that the strength of that Koraidon is extremely formidable.

---

At times like this, when asking for His help, could it be that D has encountered a crisis that even Koraidon has no way of handling?

Thinking of this, Calyrex instantly became serious. He called out towards the stable, and the Spectrier with purple ghostly flames at its feet emerged.

Calyrex mounted it, holding the reins and becoming one with his beloved horse, transforming into a majestic mounted form with full power unleashed in an instant.

*

"Is that Calyrex?"

As D was making the phone call, Carmine and Kieran were also watching from behind.

When they saw the Pokemon named Calyrex appear, the siblings instantly widened their eyes.

Carmine was surprised, "His head is so big!"

D: "…That's quite rude." Though indeed, it was quite large.

Carmine immediately covered her mouth with her hand.

Beside her, Kieran cautiously asked, "Would Calyrex be angry?"

D thought carefully, then shook his head and said, "Not really, because Calyrex is a Pokemon with a loving heart, but we shouldn't say such things about Him as it's very impolite."

"We understand!" The siblings nodded in unison.

Miss Joy, standing not far away, still hadn't quite grasped the situation.

D didn't offer much of an explanation either, but the speed from the ranch was astonishingly fast, and within just ten minutes, a Poke Ball from the Paldea Region was transmitted through the Pokeball Transmission Machine.

D took out the Poke Ball, and amid a flash of white light, Calyrex riding the Spectrier appeared in the hall.

Seeing Calyrex in this form, D was taken aback.

Why did Calyrex even ride in on the Spectrier?

The other three present couldn't help but widen their eyes as well.

"Amazing!"

"He's riding! Is this Horse-type Pokemon the King's steed?"

Calyrex's expression was full of majesty as He glanced around the environment, then focused His gaze on D.

"Where is that Pokemon?"

D then explained in detail to Calyrex what had happened in the town.

When He heard that the villainous Pokemon had killed Ogerpon's father to steal masks and then robbed all its masks, Calyrex immediately felt a surge of anger.

His gaze towards Ogerpon was now filled with compassion and pity.

"I never imagined that such evil Pokemon still existed in this world! They deserve to be punished!" Calyrex said with a tone of authority.

Having never witnessed such a scene, Carmine and Kieran were immediately impressed by Calyrex's regal presence.

"Exactly! Those bad Pokemon deserve a good lesson!" Carmine said angrily, gritting her teeth.

"Calyrex, please, we're counting on you! I hope you can save everyone in the town!" Kieran was still worried about his grandparents.

Calyrex nodded slightly, then rode His beloved horse to the outside of the Pokemon Center.

The townsfolk who had been wandering the streets gathered upon hearing the commotion from that direction.

Soon, a crowd of townspeople surrounded the Pokemon Center.

Calyrex's gaze calmly swept over the people.

He didn't speak, but with a slight mental movement, a tsunami-like, grand and awe-inspiring wave of telekinesis surged out, instantly engulfing the entire town.

Suddenly, the dazzling light turned the night as bright as day.

The villagers, who were about to attack, abruptly paused in unison, their consciousness gradually clearing up under the strong stimulation of telekinesis.

Realizing their situation and the surrounding circumstances, the faces of the villagers immediately showed expressions of confusion, shock, or embarrassment.

But quickly, these emotions passed, and they all focused their gaze on the large-headed Pokemon, mounted on a pitch-black fine horse before them.

"What… is that?"

"What in the world happened?"

Kieran and Carmine saw their grandparents in the crowd and rushed forward, throwing themselves into the two elders' arms: "Grandpa! Grandma!"

Yukito and Yukiko hurriedly embraced them, while looking ahead with faces full of astonishment.

That Pokemon… where did it come from?

Seemingly sensing the feelings of the two elders, Kieran and Carmine quickly explained:

"Don't worry! That Pokemon is called Calyrex, and Uncle D brought Him here to help everyone!"

Meanwhile, Calyrex, who had been investigating with closed eyes, suddenly opened His eyes.

Found it!

He let out a cold snort, and a powerful psychic force instantly swept outward.

The hiding Pecharunt only saw a flash of white light before completely disappearing from his original spot, transported to another location.

When Pecharunt surveyed his surroundings, he immediately panicked.

What happened? How did those people break free of his control?

What is with that Pokemon exuding such terrifying power?

Realizing the dire situation, Pecharunt turned to flee.

Seeing this scene, Ogerpon let out an angry growl.

But before it could act, countless tiny ethereal bodies suddenly appeared around Calyrex.

These ethereal bodies flickered softly in mid-air, like stars in the night sky.

However, the next second, the stars shattered, and the countless tiny purple ethereal bodies flew toward Pecharunt.

"Buzz!"

A strange buzzing sound arose, and Pecharunt, yet to escape, was immediately engulfed by these ethereal bodies.

With a loud explosion, Pecharunt lost consciousness, and his purple body fell to the ground, completely losing its ability to battle.

---

The villagers stared at the scene, dumbfounded.

After a long while, the village chief snapped back to reality and exclaimed loudly, "What kind of Pokemon is that!"

Carmine and Kieran saw the culprit controlling everyone being defeated and instantly shouted excitedly.

"Calyrex is amazing! Awesome! It should teach that villain a harsh lesson!"

Calyrex?

Upon hearing this, the village chief stood frozen in place.

Why did he feel that name was so familiar?

The village chief looked over at D not far away, and suddenly, a memory surged in his mind of the legend of The King of Bountiful Harvests that D had told him about while visiting the rice fields a few days ago!

If Calyrex truly exists, then that legend... is real!

The village chief was astonished.

Calyrex, however, paid little attention to the villagers.

It rode forward on its beloved steed, arriving at the fallen Pokemon, a slight thought flickered in its eyes as it saw this Pokemon's past and future.

After a while, it withdrew its gaze and sighed:

"Thou hast always been loved, yet remain unaware; instead, thou used thy power to capture those who truly loved thee, and in the end, thou wrought this tragedy out of greed, taking away what others loved."

"Thou can neither take away thy life nor seek forgiveness from those thou hast harmed."

Calyrex initially planned to imprison the Pokemon named Pecharunt here until it learned the meaning of love.

But upon reconsideration, it realized it lacked the authority.

Thus, Calyrex stepped aside, ignoring this sad and pathetic Pokemon.

Ogerpon looked at the Pokemon that had once led the Loyal Three to take its mask and loved ones, its eyes burning with fierce anger.

The surrounding villagers were still present, their expressions complex as they looked at the Pokemon that had controlled them, then glanced at the so-called "ghost."

Under the villagers' gaze, Ogerpon tried to suppress its emotions.

Upon seeing this scene, D sighed and stood beside Ogerpon.

Ogerpon was stunned for a moment, then blankly raised its head.

D looked down, meeting its gaze, and spoke: "Go ahead! Do what you want, and I'll bear all the consequences."

Whether it continued to be seen as a ferocious ghost by the villagers or feared by the world, no matter the outcome, it didn't matter.

Because from now on, Ogerpon would no longer belong to Kitakami; it would join him in the distant Paldea Region to start a new life there.

There, it would no longer be a ghost, free to play with everyone in the town, and during the celebration hosted by Calyrex in the mountains, it could take off its mask and celebrate with everyone.

At this moment, not only D, but Yukito, Yukiko, Carmine, and Kieran were also watching Ogerpon.

The atmosphere briefly fell silent, no one spoke, as if awaiting Ogerpon.

"Boni..."

Ogerpon's eyes reflected D's face, watching his hand on its head, and its eyes gradually grew wet.

Ogerpon removed the Teal Mask from its face, revealing its true appearance.

A rich Grass Type Energy quickly condensed from its hand, eventually transforming into a massive thorn stick.

Ogerpon grasped the thorn stick tightly, then walked toward the barely breathing Pecharunt under the watchful eyes of everyone and every Pokemon.

One step, two steps, three steps...

A drop of tear fell onto the stone below with a "plop."

Just like one day long ago, when Ogerpon charged down the mountain in anger to avenge the man in town.

"..."

The townspeople slightly opened their mouths, looking dazed at the "ghost."

Gradually, Ogerpon finally reached Pecharunt, slowly and steadily raised the thorn stick in its hand.

Then, struck down with force.

"Boom!"

*

The journey to Kitakami finally came to an end.
