Before leaving, the village chief tearfully shoved a purple peach doll containing the power of Calyrex's Imprison into D's hands.

The previous events had all been recounted to them by the non-controlled villagers upon their return.

Now everyone in Kitakami knew it was the villainous peach Pokemon controlling the townsfolk, not only the people but even the Loyal Three were its followers, attempting to seize Ogerpon's mask.

The village chief grew fearful of these four Pokemon, afraid Calyrex's Imprison might weaken, and one day these villains would emerge again, turning the villagers into their toys.

"Mr. D, please take these Pokemon that plagued the town away with you!"

D: "..."

What a joke!

Although Ogerpon hadn't killed Pecharunt that day, merely created a large crater in the ground beside it.

But now even looking at this doll made him uncomfortable, let alone having it with him while Ogerpon was around.

If he accepted it, Ogerpon would likely recall those unpleasant memories every time it saw the doll.

Therefore, D pushed the peach doll back: "Calyrex's power is mighty; before this Pokemon understands what true love is, it absolutely cannot come out."

"If you truly dislike it, just dig a pit on Apple Hill and bury them there!"

This thing was better left here to "nourish" the people of Kitakami!

The village chief was on the verge of tears.

"Let's leave it at that! The exchange meeting is over, I should head home," D said.

Not only did he capture Ogerpon, but Calyrex also harvested a wave of faith from Kitakami, further strengthening its power.

Thinking of this, D turned to leave.

Yet the village chief called out to him again: "Wait! Mr. D!"

"What is it?" D stopped and looked back.

The village chief was full of hesitation and finally stammered:

"Could you refrain from revealing the truth about the legend circulating in Kitakami... please!"

Because they still needed to rely on the reputation of the Loyal Three to develop tourism, attract visitors, and prosper the town!

"..."

D was speechless.

Not to mention, apart from Yukito's family, the people of this town in some way were quite similar to those four Pokemon.

Pecharunt and the Loyal Three wanted to seize Ogerpon's mask, while the villagers wanted to use the fame of the Loyal Three to attract tourists and develop their hometown; they all had bright futures.

As for the merits of the Loyal Three, they didn't care at all.

It's like that saying: whether black or white, if it brings money to Kitakami, it's a good cat.

D, quite speechless: "In that case, you might as well promote Ogerpon instead; after all, they're all Legendary Pokemon, there's not much difference."

"This... this..." The village chief hesitated, quite troubled.

"I'm going!"

D said no more, mounted Koraidon, and flew into the sky.

Kitakami gradually disappeared behind him, eventually vanishing from sight, and D took out Ogerpon's Poke Ball from his waist, softly speaking to it:

"Ogerpon, please take care from now on."

The Poke Ball shook slightly, as if Ogerpon inside was responding.

D smiled faintly and then stowed the Poke Ball away.

The wind tousled his hair as D gazed at the endless blue sky above, speaking to Koraidon:

"Huff—time to go home!"

"Ah gah si~"

---

---

---

---

# 330: Chapter 327: Natural Energy and the Gigantamax Arboliva

League Year 199, March 8, D successfully arrived in the Paldea Region.

Shortly after reaching home, Mayor Thomas came to visit the ranch.

D hadn't had time to greet all the Pokemon yet, and hurriedly went to welcome Mayor Thomas into the living room.

The elderly man, holding a cane, sat down on the sofa and asked kindly:

"Did the exchange go smoothly this time? I heard that tourism is very developed in Kitakami, attracting many tourists every year!"

On hearing this, D was at a loss for words.

He shook his head and couldn't help but sigh, "Porto Marinada Town is still the best!"

"Hmm?" Mayor Thomas was quite surprised, "Did something happen there?"

D nodded and then briefly told Mayor Thomas what had happened to him in Kitakami.

Including the story of Ogerpon and the Loyal Three, and the mayor of Kitakami wanting to exploit the legend of the Loyal Three to boost tourism.

After listening to everything, Mayor Thomas looked astonished and murmured:

"How could it be like this..."

"But fortunately, I brought Ogerpon back, so it won't have to suffer anymore." As he spoke, D took out Ogerpon's Poke Ball.

With a flash of white light, Ogerpon appeared in the living room.

Seeing the unfamiliar surroundings and unknown humans, Ogerpon seemed a bit flustered and quickly covered its face with its small hands.

"Don't be afraid, this is Mayor Thomas, and he won't dislike you," D said.

"Bonnie...?" Only then did Ogerpon cautiously lift its head to look at the kind old man in front.

Mayor Thomas smiled and nodded at it, comforting:

"Relax, child, no one here will harm you, and you can come play in town anytime in the future. I'm sure everyone will love you."

"Bonnie?" Ogerpon had never encountered such a thing and never been welcomed before.

Thus, upon hearing these words, its first reaction was disbelief, really?

D nodded beside it: "Of course it's true, just like in Kitakami, Porto Marinada Town will also hold lively festivals, and we'll go have fun then!"

"Bonnie!" Ogerpon looked forward eagerly.

Seeing this Pokemon's look, Mayor Thomas couldn't help but sigh, "Poor child."

D took out Ogerpon's four masks and asked: "Ogerpon, do you want to continue wearing these masks?"

He knew that Ogerpon wore masks to conceal its true appearance so as to blend in with the villagers of Kitakami.

But everyone in Porto Marinada Town loves Pokemon, and Ogerpon is very cute; no one should dislike it.

Under these circumstances, it doesn't need to wear masks to hide its face at all.

Thinking this, D shared his thoughts with Ogerpon.

After listening, Ogerpon looked down at the masks, then at Mayor Thomas, and happily jumped twice: "Bonnie!"

D understood: "Then I'll help you put these four masks away."

"Bonnie～"

Mayor Thomas had other town matters to attend to.

So, after visiting D, Mayor Thomas left the ranch.

Only then did D have the chance to release all the Pokemon.

He then called Alcremie and Glaceon to introduce them to Ogerpon.

Actually, Alcremie and Glaceon already knew.

Because they had just been hiding behind the sofa eavesdropping.

To this poor Pokemon, Alcremie couldn't help empathizing, almost crying.

Glaceon beside it was also angry for Ogerpon.

Ogerpon stared blankly at the few Pokemon in front, then softly said, "Bonnie～"

D smiled, reaching out to pat Glaceon and Alcremie's heads:

"Then, I'll leave Ogerpon in your care! I have a little matter to handle, need to go to town."

Alcremie immediately nodded and agreed, then took Ogerpon to tour the ranch.

The few Pokemon left.

Calyrex also sent Spectrier back to the stable, then teleported to the living room, gracefully sitting on the sofa and continuing to watch TV.

Dragonite ran out to water the fields. In its absence, those working Dragonite surely didn't help water.

When it thought of this, Dragonite felt very proud; it is the most reliable and capable Dragonite, and D's favorite Dragonite!

Flutter Mane also drifted out, not sure what it was going to do.

Only Koraidon and Pawmot stayed in the living room, looking down at the Slugma slowly crawling on the floor.

"Come here, Slugma!" D waved to Slugma from the fireplace, holding a Berry to lure it.

Slugma's eyes fixed on the Berry, then slowly crawled over, into the fireplace.

D fed it the Berry, seeing Slugma's silly look, couldn't help but smile.

"You can stay here for now! I'll head to town later to find someone to make a nest for you."

"Pawpaw!"

Pawmot squatted at the fireplace, happily watching Slugma inside.

This way, they now have a warm Slugma-Powered Home!

"Play with Slugma for a while, I'm heading out." D said to Pawmot.

---

"Pawmot!" Pawmot responded, not turning its head, as it chatted away with Slugma, though who knows what they were talking about.

D didn't pay it much mind. After cautioning his sister Indeedee, he turned and left the house, riding Koraidon to Keri's forest cabin.

Keri was at the doorstep, sawing wood. When she saw D approaching, she appeared quite surprised, "A rare visitor!"

D: "..."

"What are you planning to buy today?" Keri stopped her work.

D got straight to the point, asking, "Do you have any pipes for Slugma to reside in?"

"Heating pipes?" Keri immediately understood his meaning.

D nodded repeatedly, "Exactly!"

"Looks like you've caught a Slugma-type Pokemon."

Keri put down the axe, removed her gloves, and walked into the house:

"I've got some. I happen to have the blueprints from when we built your house before. So... are you planning to construct a whole heating system exclusively for Slugma in the living room, or cover the entire house?"

"The whole house!" D replied without hesitation.

This way, the area where Slugma could roam would be larger.

Hearing this, Keri looked at the house blueprint and said, "In that case, some areas will have to be rebuilt, and it will be more expensive. Oh right, you only have a fireplace in the living room, correct?"

"Yes." D thought that price wasn't an issue because the farm's profits are very stable now, with Life Sheep Milk selling at a good price.

"Very well." Keri took out a pen and made some modifications on the blueprint, finally concluding, "The price will depend on the materials used and the construction progress. I'm helping Elif Cherry Garden dig a new wine cellar today, so I might only be able to come over tomorrow."

D: "It's okay, I'm not in a hurry."

Keri smiled slightly, "Alright, let's leave it at that for now, I'll come over once I'm finished."

"Okay."

After bidding farewell to Keri, D headed to Porto Marinada Market to buy a batch of fresh ingredients, before heading back to the farm.

But as he returned to the farm, he found a group of Pokemon surrounding a very large, tree-sized, unfamiliar Pokemon.

Seeing this scene, D paused slightly.

The group of Pokemon turned their heads upon hearing the commotion from behind them.

Soon, the tree-like Pokemon also raised its head to look this way.

It was a tall and slender Pokemon, covered in an outfit made of green leaves, wearing a brown long skirt below.

The collar made of green leaves was high, covering its neck, but from its familiar eyes, D immediately recognized the Pokemon.

It was Dolliv!

No, wait! It should be called Arboliva now!

D happily set his things down and walked forward, looking up at the tall Arboliva.

Arboliva gazed gently as it met his eyes.

Having evolved, Arboliva appeared more mature, shedding the childish innocence from its Dolliv days, now a trustworthy Pokemon.

"Evolved?" D asked.

Arboliva gave a slight nod.

D took a deep breath and nodded with joy, "That's great, congratulations, Arboliva."

Their shared experience over such a long period speaks for itself, words of rapport unwanton.

Meanwhile, Alcremie looked up at the towering Arboliva.

Being a good companion that came to the farm together, now seeing Arboliva looking so impressive, Alcremie couldn't help but feel happy for Arboliva, yet a bit envious.

"Ma-ma..." If it could evolve too...

As if sensing Alcremie's feelings, Arboliva extended its arm and picked up Alcremie, placing it on its body.

"Ma-ma!"

Alcremie exclaimed in surprise.

Seeing this, D couldn't help feeling deeply moved.

He remembered how Arboliva first appeared at the farm.

Back then, it was just a tiny Smoliv.

It was so shy and introverted, liking the quiet as it nestled in a pot on the windowsill, basking in the sun.

After evolving into Dolliv, its temperament hadn't changed much, remaining shy and reserved, though compared to its Smoliv days, Dolliv occasionally joined Alcremie's games now.

But most of the time, it stayed near Gogoat's Grassy Terrain, and the World Tree.

Only... what D didn't understand was, why did it become so big after evolving into Arboliva?

Its size was reaching nearly four meters, like a big tree!

Normal Arboliva sizes seemed to be around one meter and five, right?

D thoughtfully looked at Gogoat and the World Tree, guessing:

"Could it be due to absorbing a large amount of Natural Energy?"

It's well known that the concentration of Natural Energy near the World Tree far exceeds other places.

Hence, the Gogoats and Dollivs often lie around there.

Natural Energy is a valuable resource!

If the amount is large enough, it could even lead to the legendary battle between Earth God Groudon and Sea God Kyogre.

---

"So, it must be those natural energies that caused Arboliva's size to change," D concluded in his heart.

If placed in the game "Arceus," the current Arboliva has clearly transformed into a big "boss Pokemon."

Anyhow, it's a good thing.

Watching Arboliva coaxing Alcremie and looking happily at everyone's Ogerpon, a smile appeared on D's face.

"It seems Ogerpon is getting along very well with everyone in the ranch," D thought.

The only pity is that, aside from the Grass Type Pokemon, the other Pokemon in the ranch don't seem to be attracted by the natural energy.

Pawmi evolved into Pawmot, Smoliv evolved into Arboliva, Caterpie evolved into Butterfree, Diglett evolved into Dugtrio...

Of the Pokemon that first came to the ranch, it seems that only the Naclstack haven't evolved yet.

Thinking of this, D turned to look at the five Naclstack surrounding the giant Arboliva, showing a puzzled expression.

Logically, he has always been diligently nurturing the Naclstack, with daily nutritious meals and workouts not stopping, they should have evolved long ago!

But why?

D bid farewell to a few Pokemon and turned back to the living room to prepare lunch with his sister Indeedee.

As he handled the ingredients, he began to ponder.

As everyone knows, there are three reasons why Pokemon don't evolve.

The first, their bodies haven't met the conditions for evolution.

The second, the Pokemon wants to maintain its current form, thus resisting evolution, using Bide to suppress the feeling of evolving.

The third, the Pokemon is wearing an Everstone.

So, which situation is it for the Naclstack?

D didn't know and decided to investigate properly after lunch.

To welcome Ogerpon and Slugma's arrival, today's lunch at D's home was prepared very abundantly.

Various meat dishes favored by carnivorous Pokemon, vegetable dishes favored by Grass Type Pokemon, and various sandwiches and desserts.

Once everything was prepared, D went out to call the group of Pokemon back to eat.

But just as he reached the door, he saw Zapdos with a shoulder bag hanging around its neck descending from the sky.

D: "..."

Just as he had anticipated, Zapdos really had become the Heroic Expedition Team's delivery squad.

Teddiursa and Mew have sent postcards back again.

D hurriedly went forward and, under the indication of Zapdos, reached into its shoulder bag.

Soon, D pulled out a photo and a Poke Ball.

Seeing these two items, D understood.

Oh, Teddiursa and Mew have made new friends outside again!

"I wonder which Pokemon it will be this time."

With curiosity, just as D prepared to examine the photo and Poke Ball closely, he suddenly heard a slightly excited exclamation from behind him.

"That Pokemon... Could it be the legendary Zapdos? Wow! This is just like Eelektross jumping over the dragon gate, it's so surprising! No wonder even the chief trainer recognizes it!"

D turned around and saw a girl wearing a Magnemite hair accessory, with long hair of blue and pink standing in the distance, her eyes fervently looking at Zapdos with the red shoulder bag.

D was slightly taken aback.

Is that... Lono?

---

---

---

---

# 331: Chapter 328: Lono's Shock, the Flying Pawmot!

After the initial surprise, D quickly regained his composure.

After all, Geeta had previously mentioned to him that there would be Gym Leaders coming over for trial challenges at the ranch to refine their battle skills and enhance their strength.

D originally thought it would be Kofu, the Gym Leader of Cascarrafa Gym, who was closest to Porto Marinada Town, coming over first.

Unexpectedly, it was Lono, who was eight hundred miles away, who came running over first...

Bear in mind, Levincia City and Porto Marinada Town are separated by the entire Paldea Region!

In D's mind, Lono was labeled as a person of action.

At this moment, Lono shifted her gaze from Zapdos to him and waved her hand.

"Hey~ Hello hello~! I'm Lono from Levincia Gym, the head said there are trial challenges here, so I came over!" The girl was full of energy.

D nodded slightly and replied with a smile, "Hello, the head has already informed me. Indeed, there are a few trials here  at the ranch, but we haven't had lunch yet, and the Pokemon are all still hungry, so we can only start the trials after lunch."

"Ah... Got it, got it!" As soon as she finished speaking, a rumbling sound came from Lono's stomach.

She had taken a morning flight from Levincia City to Porto Marinada Town, and rushed over immediately upon landing without having lunch.

Lono suddenly felt a bit embarrassed.

D glanced at Lono, understanding her situation.

He thought that since these Gym Leaders were all here to help Pawmot and the others train their battle abilities, they might buy some local specialties like sheep milk, cow milk, or berries during their visit, just like Leon did before.

"It seems you haven't had lunch yet either. If you don't mind, why not join us for a meal?" D suggested.

"Ah... Can I?" Lono's eyes lit up; this way, she wouldn't have to make another trip.

Seeing D nod, Lono immediately expressed her gratitude, "Then I will impose!"

Hearing the commotion, a group of Pokemon also gathered around, curiously observing Lono from a distance.

Meanwhile, D shifted his gaze back to Zapdos and inquired, "Are you eating lunch here?"

"Chirp!" Zapdos called out proudly, rejecting D's invitation because Mew and Teddiursa were still waiting for its return.

"Alright then, I'll pack some food for you!"

While rummaging through his satchel, D noticed that the medicines inside were almost depleted.

With Zapdos returning this time, he could restock them.

Saying this, D went into the house and found some healing potions and food to put into Zapdos's satchel.

He didn't stop until the satchel was stuffed full.

"Off you go! Give my regards to them!" D reached out and petted the soft, dense feathers on Zapdos's neck.

"Chirp!"

Zapdos didn't react too much. It took two steps back, flapped its wings, and flew into the sky.

D stood in place, watching it go off into the distance.

Lono walked over at some unknown moment, gazing in amazement at the scene.

"Mr. D... That Pokemon is Zapdos, right?"

D nodded, surprised: "Have you seen other Zapdos?"

Lono shook her head, her eyes glowing with excitement. "That's the legendary Electric Type Pokemon! Every Electric Type Trainer dreams of having it!"

D: "..."

Almost forgot, Lono's gym specializes in Electric Types.

"It's just so-so!" D replied, since this Zapdos was merely a friend of Mew and Teddiursa, and wasn't closely associated with him.

"No wonder the head called you an exemplary trainer!" Lono said with genuine admiration.

Even Zapdos, such a rare Pokemon, you managed to capture...

And by the looks of Zapdos flying off, it was clearly a messenger for the ranch, just like the Dragonites!

She knew only Dragonites were used for deliveries here, but had no idea that even this rare Pokemon Zapdos served as a postman!

"Wow!"

This is way too extravagant!

D smiled politely; he didn't reveal what Zapdos was up to, nor did he mention the existence of the Heroic Expedition Team.

Seeing them not returning for a long time, his sister Indeedee, who had already set the lunch on the table, had no choice but to come out and call them in.

"Let's go inside!"

"Thanks for having me!"

The two went into the room, and as Lono saw the spread of food on the table, she couldn't help but gulp: "Wow, so lavish!"

"Today, two new Pokemon joined the ranch, so we're celebrating their arrival!"

D held a berry dessert and placed it on the floor near the fireplace.

The Slugma inside slowly crawled out happily to eat the food.

"So many Pokemon!" Lono exclaimed in surprise, eyes wide.

Her gaze swept across the living room.

Cyclizar, Indeedee, some unfamiliar Pokemon (Koraidon), some unfamiliar Pokemon (Ogerpon, Alcremie, Dragonite, some unfamiliar Pokemon (Calyrex), Sinistea and Polteageist, many Dragonairs.

There was even a shiny Dragonair inside!

Pearly pink, so cute! Lono's attention was instantly grabbed by the shiny Dragonair.

"Pawmo~"

At this moment, Pawmot flew in from outside.

The weather was great today, it had just let the hens out of the Chicken House to sunbathe, and from afar, it smelled the aroma of food, and hurriedly flew in.

---

Hearing the growl, Lono instinctively glanced at the Pawmot.

But as soon as she saw what the Pokemon looked like, her mouth opened in astonishment.

That pink Pokemon with a red cape tied around its back and a small silver hammer in its hand, could it be...!

"Pawmo?" Seeing a stranger at home, Pawmot looked over, a bit puzzled.

D introduced, "This is Lono, a trainer skilled in Electric Type Pokemon, here at the ranch to participate in a trial."

Pawmot instantly understood.

Clutching the little hammer in its hand, it looked at Lono with determination: "Pawmo!"

Lono felt like her heart was melting from the cuteness, what an adorable Pawmot!

She lunged forward and hugged the fluffy Pawmot: "So cute!"

Pawmot was startled, quickly pushing this person away with its paws, then hiding behind D.

Lono laughed awkwardly, "Haha, sorry, I've never seen a Pawmot with this color variation before, it's just too adorable this way!"

As she spoke, Lono suddenly realized something.

No, wait! How is it that Pawmot can levitate?!

D noticed her gaze and explained, "Pawmot is a very special Pawmot, it's excellent at controlling electric energy and can use electromagnetic levitation to fly."

"Amazing!" Lono was shocked, "Even as a Gym Leader specializing in Electric Type Pokemon, I can't do that!"

D chuckled and said, "There's a Thunder Trial here at the ranch, the trial is to battle against Pawmot. If you're interested, you can choose this trial later."

"That's so cool! I'll choose to challenge the Thunder Trial then!" Lono added, "Hehe, usually it's trainers who come to challenge me, now it's my turn to be challenged, it's quite refreshing!"

D smiled gently, pulled out a chair, and let a few Pokemon sit on it.

Lono sat opposite, looking expectantly at the table full of food: "I'm digging in!"

D: "..."

No wonder she's a girl from the anime world!

He doesn't have this habit at home and never says "I'm digging in."

Hearing someone say it for the first time, D found it quite novel.

Lono didn't eat in an elegant and quiet manner, instead, she seemed very lively.

D observed Lono quietly while eating.

He found that Lono now seemed a bit different from the Lono in the game.

Because she didn't speak in the quirky "catch your eye with an Electroweb," "good morning, afternoon, evening, hello" Lono manner.

However, what really caught D's attention was the Magnemite hair clips on Lono's head.

Because he noticed, those Magnemite hair clips actually moved!

"Hmm? Mr. D, are you looking at the Magnemite?" Lono lifted her head following D's gaze.

D blinked and nodded, "Are they real Magnemite?"

"Of course!" Lono grinned, "They've been my companions ever since I started my journey!"

As if to support Lono, the two Magnemite jumped on the spot.

D was very shocked.

He always thought those were just Magnemite hair clips, but they turned out to be real Magnemite!

If he remembers correctly, a single Magnemite weighs around 6kg, so two would be 12kg.

Oh my! In that case, Lono is quite the super rookie too!

With two Magnemites on her head, wouldn't her neck get tired?

But remembering that Ash had once lifted Nebby, weighing 999kg, with one hand, D was at ease.

You can't view people in this world by common standards.

So he withdrew his gaze and quietly continued eating.

Soon, lunchtime was over, and Lono, satisfied, casually praised, "Mr. D's cooking is really good!"

D chuckled, "Just practice makes perfect."

"Haha, Mr. D is so modest!" Lono laughed.

She found D a bit different from what she had imagined.

A few weeks ago, the chairman gathered the eight Gym Leaders of the Paldea Region and assigned them a task to go train at a place called D Ranch in Porto Marinada Town.

At that time, all the Gym Leaders were surprised.

The chairman didn't explain much, just said they must go.

Before coming, Lono imagined what the owner of the ranch would be like.

Would he be an old farmer, wearing a straw hat, with a full white beard, looking very aged.

However, when she saw this person at first sight, she immediately recognized him.

OMG, isn't this the protagonist from the Man and Dragon show that was aired on TV back then? Even appeared on TV!

Those delivery Dragonites left a deep impression on her back then, and she still remembers them vividly today.

No farmer's straw hat, no white beard, tall and big, with a layer of stubbly black beard on his chin, looking quite imposing.

But upon actual interaction, Lono found him surprisingly easy to talk to, polite, and his cooking skills were excellent, plus he was an uncle who really liked Pokemon.

"Uncle D!" Lono, off-guard, blurted out what she had in mind, full of energy: "I'm ready! We can start the trial now!"

---

Just back at the farm, exhausted from the journey, D, who hadn't even had time to shower or shave, almost lost his breath, asking with wide eyes, "What did you call me?"

Lono realized her impolite address just now and quickly replied obediently, "Mr. D."

D: "..."

He sighed and handed some Pokémon food to Lono, asking her to feed her Pokémon.

With so many Pokémon earlier, there wasn't enough space at the table, and Lono hadn't let her Pokémon out. D noticed this issue.

Lono: "Thank you very much, Mr. D!"

"Just wait for a moment!" D turned around and headed into the room, entering the bathroom.

Looking at himself in the mirror, he finally understood why from Lono to Carmine they all called him an uncle…

Because he got a bit tanned working on the farm in the summer, his hair hadn't been cut for a long time, and he was busy with Ogerpon matters during those days of agricultural exchange in Kitakami, so he hadn't thought of shaving.

In addition, during this short year, he consumed a lot of rich food like fish, meat, and Life Sheep Milk, becoming much stronger, almost turning into a burly man like Bruno, the Fighting Elite from the Kanto Region.

D shaved, tidied up his appearance, and once everything was done, he looked much younger.

When he stepped out of the room, Lono was feeding her Pokémon on the grass.

D glanced over, and names of those Pokémon floated in his mind: "Kilowattrel, Bellibolt, Luxray, Electrode, Mismagius."

Plus the two decorative Magnemite on her head, there were seven Pokémon in total.

Hearing the noise from behind, Lono turned around, her mouth agape in surprise: "Mr. D?"

D was quite satisfied with this form of address, and modestly nodded: "Mm."

"Wow!" Lono was really surprised: "May I boldly ask your age?"

"Twenty-one."

Lono was even more surprised; she hadn't expected Mr. D to be so young, just four years older than her!

D displayed a graceful yet respectful smile: "If young people from the city worked on the farm for a few years like I do, I'm sure they'd turn out like this too."

Lono instantly understood.

D didn't say more and switched to the main topic: "Are you ready? Sure you want to start the Thunder Trial challenge?"

"Hey everyone~! Are you all ready~?" Lono looked at the Pokémon in front of her.

"Watt!"

"Dream!"

"..."

Bellibolt, Mismagius, and the others responded one after another.

"Hehe, seems like everyone's ready!" Lono cast her gaze at D, her voice cheerful and lively: "Then~ Gym test, start!"

D: "…Pfft!"

Oops, she mixed it up!

Lono laughed awkwardly: "Haha, I'm used to welcoming challengers, so those words just slipped out~ Suddenly switching roles, I haven't adapted yet."

D showed understanding, and he led Pawmot, draped in a red cape, to the other side of the battle area.

Koraidon, Cyclizar, Flutter Mane, Alcremie, Naclstack, and Ogerpon all gathered around, watching the battle from the side.

With a whistle, her brother Indeedee walked to the center of the battle area.

As the whistle blew, Lono energetically pointed forward:

"Electrode, it's time! Let's pass this trial like Eelektross leaping over the dragon gate!"

---

---

---

---

# 332: Chapter 329: Concept for a Legendary Beast Chat Group

The Electrode wasn't Lono's strongest Pokemon. She fielded it in the first match to gauge her opponent's strength.

On the battlefield.

With a round figure resembling a giant red and white ball, the Electrode faced off against the Pawmot wearing a red cloak and wielding a little hammer.

"Electrode, use Discharge!" Lono energetically issued the order.

In mid-air, Electrode suddenly erupted with a burst of intense electricity, enveloping it in a bright flash before the electricity shot out in all directions like striking lightning.

D calmly watched the scene, silently observing Pawmot's performance in battle.

He didn't give any commands because his Pawmot's ability is Charge.

Charge: When hit by an Electric Type move, it doesn't take damage but instead recovers.

As an Electric Type Pokemon himself, D could sense that the electricity on Pawmot was somewhat different from what Electrode was releasing.

For instance, Pawmot's current wasn't a pure yellow; it gleamed with a hint of lightning-like blue.

Faced with Electrode's attack, Pawmot didn't retreat; instead, it confidently charged forward into the electricity.

It swung its little hammer, its body bathed in Electrode's current, and then let out a loud shout.

"Pawmot!"

The next moment, the surrounding currents, as if drawn in, surged madly into Pawmot's body, and into the silver hammer.

In an instant, Pawmot's eyes turned blue, and the hammer in its hand was enveloped in a powerful current, as if the Thunder God had descended to the world.

Seeing this, Lono's eyes were filled with shock: "That ability... could it be Charge?"

No! It's not just Charge!

That Pawmot is using Electrode's electricity to power up!

Utilizing the opponent's current to charge itself, what kind of Pokemon is this!

Lono immediately felt the challenge was thorny; this trial was too restricting for her as an Electric Type specialist.

Seeing Pawmot about to launch an attack, Lono quickly became alert:

"Electrode! Be careful!"

In this state, Pawmot was extremely dangerous, and she couldn't allow Electrode to engage it head-on!

In this situation, the best strategy was to avoid Pawmot's attack and wait for its move, the charge state to disappear!

On the field, Electrode, hearing its Trainer's voice, also became vigilant.

However, the charged-up Pawmot looked spiritedly at Electrode.

The next second, its red cloak fluttered behind it, and then it shot straight into the sky.

Lono, who was about to instruct Electrode to dodge, was slightly taken aback.

However, the next moment, a yellow lightning bolt descended from the sky, and Pawmot tightened its grip on the hammer, crashing down with a mighty current.

Lono was dumbfounded.

What kind of move is that?!

When the dust from the explosion settled.

A large pit appeared on the ground, and Electrode lay at the bottom, covered in wounds.

Yet, it hadn't lost its ability to battle, meaning the duel wasn't over.

Electrode immediately extricated itself, creating distance from Pawmot.

"Chase it!" D wanted to end the battle here, "Use Quick Attack!"

Pawmot moved incredibly fast; just as Electrode made some distance, its body transformed into a white light and caught up, slamming Electrode out.

Lono nervously called out, "Electrode!"

Electrode fell to the ground.

Just when D thought it had lost its ability to fight, the resilient Electrode managed to force itself up and floated again.

D showed surprise; this Electrode has incredible perseverance!

Seeing Electrode still standing, Lono breathed a sigh of relief.

She calmly analyzed and quickly thought.

Got it!

Facing the restraining Pawmot, Lono swiftly altered the strategy:

"Electrode, use Screech!"

With the command issued, Electrode's body trembled, and a strong current constantly collided within, creating a sharp screeching sound through friction.

Pawmot furrowed its brows, experiencing a headache and instinctively wanting to cover its ears with its pads.

"Don't show any openings!" D frowned, reminding, "The hammer!"

Pawmot quickly reacted, covering its ears with one hand and hurling the Mjolnir toward Electrode with the other.

"Quick, dodge it!" Lono used a Divine Technique.

Electrode flashed upwards, narrowly dodging the hammer below.

The screeching reached its peak, and Pawmot's mind hummed from the pain, quickly covering its other ear with the other paw.

At this moment, Pawmot shut its eyes, covered its ears with both hands, helplessly floating in the air, appearing wide open with vulnerabilities.

Seeing the opponent exposed, Lono felt joy in her heart, chuckling, "Hehe, Mr. D, I'm getting serious now! Electrode, use Foul Play!"

As Screech stopped, a dark aura enveloped Electrode, its spherical body hurtling toward the pained Pawmot clutching its ears.

Foul Play: A Dark Type move that uses the opponent's strength to attack. The stronger the opponent, the more damage this move inflicts.

---

Just when Lono thought she and Electrode were about to secure victory, her gaze fell on D across from her.

He showed no trace of panic on his face; instead, his mouth curved into a smile, as if he was anticipating something.

Something's wrong!

Lono's heart tightened.

And at that moment, a sound cut through the air beside her ear.

Lono turned her head and saw the hammer thrown by Pawmot was now seemingly attracted, speeding towards Pawmot.

And Electrode happened to be right in its path!

Lono: !!!

How is this possible?

"Quick, dodge!" Lono exclaimed.

She knew too well that her Electrode was already on its last legs, standing by willpower alone, and if it took one more hit, it would likely lose its ability to battle.

But it was too late.

As Lono's words were still hanging in the air, the hammer went "thud" as it hit the back of Electrode's head.

Foul Play failed, and Electrode, along with the hammer, plummeted to the ground, its eyes turning into swirls, completely losing its ability to battle.

"Phew——!"

Brother Indeedee blew the whistle to announce the end of this trial.

With her mouth agape in shock, Lono then said with a face full of resignation:

"Mr. D is just too amazing!?"

D stepped forward, massaging Pawmot's temples, and asked with a smile, "Electrode isn't your strongest Pokémon, is it?"

"Hehe, you saw right through me!" Lono grinned, looking at the other Pokémon in the ranch, saying, "It seems Mr. D is the same!"

Lono paused, not feeling down about losing the battle, still her lively self:

"Oh dear, losing the battle does make me feel a bit unwilling, but... now I understand why the chief sent us here!"

D turned his head to look at the other Pokémon and asked:

"Want to continue? Besides the Thunder Trial, there are other trials in the ranch."

Lono waved her hands repeatedly, "No, no! I need a break first. I have a feeling Mr. D, you might defeat all of my Pokémon at once!"

D: "..."

He indeed was capable of such a thing.

At this moment, Sister Indeedee brought a cup of Life Sheep Milk to feed Electrode.

In the blink of an eye, Electrode's injuries were completely healed.

Witnessing this incredulous scene, Lono was amazed, "What's that potion? Its effect is incredible!"

"That's not a potion," D replied with a smile, "That's a specialty of my ranch, Life Sheep Milk, which helps Pokémon quickly recover their stamina."

Lono suddenly thought of something, looking at the Dragonite lounging in the sun on the grass not far away, she asked:

"The Dragonites deliver this specialty to the town every day?"

At this point, Lono's eyes suddenly lit up, "Can Levincia City also order items from the ranch?"

D nodded, then shook his head, "Sorry, the ranch currently doesn't offer cross-city delivery services."

"Ah? What a pity..." Lono felt very disappointed.

Seeing Lono's expression, D seemed deep in thought, then asked, "By the way, have you heard of Phone Rotom?"

"Phone Rotom? What's that? Like Rotom Refrigerator or Rotom Washing Machine?" Lono suddenly became spirited.

"Sort of!" D said casually, "I heard the Rotom Company is developing such a thing."

Rotom Technology Company is the company founded by Nemona's father, mainly responsible for developing communication devices and Rotom technology.

Like Rotom Refrigerators, Rotom Washing Machines and such, which are then sold worldwide.

"Never heard of it!" Lono shook her head.

D didn't continue the topic.

Since Phone Rotom hasn't been invented yet, and the internet isn't widespread, Lono probably hasn't been exposed to live streaming and video creation yet.

Discussing these things with her is meaningless.

Fortunately, Lono didn't mind, soon being attracted by the two horses belonging to Calyrex.

"Wow! Mr. D, you have Horse-type Pokémon here too!" Lono exclaimed excitedly, "I've visited ranches with stables before and experienced horseback riding across vast grasslands!"

D: "..."

"I'm sorry, there's no stable in the ranch, and these two Horse-type Pokémon have very proud natures; they don't like being ridden as they're the king's beloved horses."

Just kidding, Glastrier and Spectrier are Legendary Pokémon, and they once ruled over the entire Crown Tundra, only becoming Calyrex's exclusive steeds after being tamed.

At this moment, Glastrier snorted, arrogantly glancing at Lono with a domineering and cool posture.

"The king's beloved horses?" Lono exclaimed.

"Calyrex." D started explaining to Lono, "The Legendary King of Bountiful Harvests, it once rode Glastrier and Spectrier across the land."

"Wait, wait, wait!" Lono asked in disbelief, "Calyrex? The one that was on TV before?"

Lono was referring to the Legendary Pokémon Calyrex, which made an entire mountain flourish in an instant.

"Yes!" D smiled slightly, "There's even a shrine on the mountain built by people to express their gratitude to Calyrex!"

---

"No, no, no!" Lono shook his head repeatedly and then explained, "I meant, is Calyrex also in D's ranch?"

Lono turned to look at Koraidon, then at Ogerpon, and then at the Flutter Mane floating in the sky above the ranch.

Just who... is Calyrex?

"Calyrex is watching TV in the living room!" D explained.

Huh? Lono immediately widened his eyes.

D gave Lono a detailed introduction about Calyrex.

And then...

+1 follower of Calyrex

*

At four in the afternoon, Lono left. She said she only had one day off and had to get back to work tomorrow to entertain Trainers challenging Levincia Gym.

Before leaving, Lono spent a lot of money to buy ten bottles of goat milk from the ranch, stating she would return again and she hoped D could establish an intercity Dragonite delivery service.

However, D had no way to make this happen.

He wasn't just worried about bad people but also that the Dragonites might get lost.

So, all of this would have to wait until the birth of the Rotom Phone.

Thinking of this, D glanced at his little treasure chest at home.

At that moment, a good idea suddenly popped into his head.

Perhaps... he could invest the money he earned and provide Nemona's dad's company with a research direction for smart phones to accelerate the birth of the Phone Rotom?

This was indeed a good plan; however, unfortunately, his status was just that of a countryside farmer, with no opportunity to meet big tycoons like Nemona's dad.

D pondered the functions of the Rotom Phone as seen in animations.

His keen eyes caught sight of Calyrex watching TV on the couch, and he suddenly remembered the "Crown moo chirp chirp" sound Calyrex made when he called from Mossui Town.

Telepathy couldn't be transmitted through phone lines...

For the longest time, D thought the Rotom Phone could only provide navigation and positioning services for the Dragonites.

But now it seemed the Rotom Phone could offer many, many more services.

As everyone knew, Rotom was also a Pokemon; even if it merged with appliances, they could still communicate with each other.

In that case, if each Pokemon were equipped with a Phone Rotom, wouldn't that mean everyone could call each other?

The Pokemon speaks on one end, and the Rotom inside the phone automatically transcodes and translates it, delivering it to the other side.

Then Calyrex could directly watch TV and surf the internet through the phone.

Not just Calyrex.

There was also Giratina who always idled on Flower Sky Island, lazily sunbathing like a salted fish in the Reversed World.

Thinking of this, D suddenly became interested.

There were quite a few Legendary Pokemon on the ranch now.

Like Calyrex, Koraidon, Ogerpon, Mew, and Giratina next door.

Maybe he could help create a Legendary Beast chat group to pull in these Pokemon, which originally had no connection, and see what kind of magical reactions they might have with each other.

But given Giratina's irritable nature...

D worried it might become an internet troll, the kind that would crawl along the internet cables to beat someone up at the slightest hint of disagreement.

"Just don't know how much the investment would cost..." D murmured.

He planned to first gather some information, then decide based on the actual situation.

As night was falling, D was about to go outside to play with the Pokemon for a while, but then he suddenly remembered the Poke Ball and photo brought back by Zapdos.

D smacked his forehead, hurriedly went to the Poke Ball rack to pick up the photo and Poke Ball, and began to examine the good friends Mew and Teddiursa had made this time.

The back of the photo still imprinted with Mew and Teddiursa's little paw prints, but compared to before, there was an additional mark.

At first, D didn't recognize it, but after a while, he realized, could this be the mark left by Zapdos?

Turning the photo over, the background showed a dense, deep forest with a winding river flowing through the woods.

By the river stood a few figures with smiling faces.

The smiling Teddiursa and Mew.

The tall Zapdos with a red bag slung across stood behind them, looking proudly at the camera.

And in between Mew and Teddiursa stood a resolute, sharp-eyed Pokemon with a "sword" in its hand, the Yellow-beaked Duck Pokemon.

---

---

---

---

# 333: Chapter 330: Farfetch'd Who Dreams of Becoming a Swordmaster

Accompanied by a flash of white light, a yellow-beaked Pokemon covered in brown-gray feathers appeared in front of D.

It held a long leek in its hand, with its chest and abdomen covered by a layer of milky-white duck down, and had yellow feet.

Three strands of hair stood upright on its head, and a black "V"-shaped marking on its forehead made its expression look exceptionally resolute.

This is... Farfetch'd?!

D widened his eyes in disbelief.

His mind immediately recalled the Farfetch'd that gave a bundle of leeks to Mew and Teddiursa last year.

Could it be that one?

The Farfetch'd in front hugged the leek, lifted its head, and looked at him.

D: "Hi? Hello?"

The Farfetch'd solemnly nodded its head: "Quack!"

D was amazed.

Whoa! This Farfetch'd has style!

"Are you friends with Teddiursa and Mew?" D asked. "Were you also invited to live at the ranch by them?"

"Quack?"

The Farfetch'd was taken aback, shook its head, and then vigorously waved the leek in its hand.

D didn't understand and could only cast a pleading look at Calyrex on the sofa.

He invited the Farfetch'd to sit on the sofa first.

With Calyrex's help in translation, D finally understood the intent of this Farfetch'd.

Turns out, it wasn't the Farfetch'd that sent a bundle of Big Roots to the ranch but another Farfetch'd that Teddiursa and Mew encountered during their journey.

The Farfetch'd said it had always lived in a dense forest, diligently training beside a waterfall, continuously practicing the technique of Cut, attempting to sever the waterfall, and pursuing the ultimate path of the leek.

Unfortunately, its training had reached a bottleneck, unable to progress further.

Five days ago, it met the Heroic Expedition Team.

The Heroic Expedition Team discovered it sitting on the waterfall with a troubled expression.

With kindness, Mew and Teddiursa asked why it was so unhappy.

The Farfetch'd shared its troubles with them.

"Quack." (My training has reached a bottleneck; no matter what I do, I can't take the next step. I don't know how to continue.)

After hearing this, Mew and Teddiursa asked the Farfetch'd some more questions and told it the story of the Heroic Expedition Team.

The Farfetch'd was extremely impressed by their deeds of accumulating virtue and helping countless weak ones.

The Pokemon celebrated over drinks (Berries), and amidst the festivities, Mew and Teddiursa pointed out a path for it.

That was to go to a place called D Ranch, where someone would help it hone its skills.

So, the Farfetch'd came to refine the techniques of the leek to the extreme, so it could cut through the waterfall.

After hearing the Farfetch'd's story, D exchanged glances with Calyrex.

"Cut through the waterfall?" he asked in shock.

Just relying on that leek?

Seeing the human in front of it doubt its abilities, the gaze of the Farfetch'd grew sharp.

It placed its right wing on the leek's stem and, with lightning speed, made its move.

D only saw a flash of white light, and a glass cup on the coffee table was split in half from the middle.

The cut was finely smooth, a testament to the skill of the one who executed the move.

D was taken aback.

What move was that? Cut or Iaido Slash? So impressive!

The Farfetch'd calmly sheathed the leek back: "Quack." (It's just that I still can't achieve that feat yet.)

D came to his senses: "So, you're here at the ranch to train?"

The Farfetch'd nodded: "Quack!"

D, a bit confused, scratched his head: "But you already seem very skilled! I've never been involved in this sort of thing, so I don't know how to help you train in sword techniques…"

If it were a Farfetch'd that knew nothing, it'd be easier to say...

The Farfetch'd immediately showed a look of disappointment.

Seeing this, not wanting to betray the trust of Mew and Teddiursa, D thought for a moment and then said:

"However, I can give it a try. Could you show me all the moves you currently know?"

The Farfetch'd nodded.

A human and a Pokemon came outside.

Then, the Farfetch'd began demonstrating the sword techniques it had mastered.

Cut, Iaido Slash, Aerial Ace, Fury Cutter.

When the Farfetch'd stopped, D was stunned on the spot: "Just these? No more?"

Farfetch'd: "Quack!"

D was even more baffled: "Do you know Sand Attack?"

The Farfetch'd shook its head.

"Leer?"

The Farfetch'd continued to shake its head.

"Peck?"

The Farfetch'd continued to shake its head.

D was shocked: "Then you're quite unbalanced in your skills!"

The Farfetch'd, however, felt that these were enough. Using Sand Attack or trying to Peck an opponent with its beak during battles wasn't in keeping with a swordsman's demeanor.

The Farfetch'd stood there gazing at D, waiting for his response.

If this place couldn't help it further enhance its abilities, then it would have no choice but to leave and train alone in the wild.

D also observed the Farfetch'd.

A Farfetch'd that only knew four moves… It seemed the Farfetch'd was eager to train in Slash and swordsmanship moves; other moves were clearly not within its consideration.

When it came to slash moves and swordsmanship moves, an array of techniques floated through D's mind:

The Air Blade, said to be able to even cleave the sky,

The Night Slash, which could easily hit vital points,

The Bitter Blade imbued with the user's longing for the world.

Above those would be some exclusive moves of Legendary Pokemon.

---

Moves like Dialga's Spacial Rend, the Swords of Justice's Sacred Sword, Keldeo's Secret Sword, Zacian's Behemoth Blade, and Groudon's Precipice Blades.

But a single Farfetch'd can't learn these moves!

D stared at Farfetch'd, and suddenly a dish he once made came to mind: the Berry Platter.

It's just that it's a double-edged sword, allowing Pokemon to learn new moves but also making them forget moves they already knew.

Seeing D not responding for a while, Farfetch'd sighed.

It said nothing more, gripping its leek and turned to leave.

Seeing this, D hastily called out, "Wait a minute!"

Farfetch'd stopped and turned to look back.

D: "Have you ever heard of a sword technique that can cut through space?"

"Gah?!" Farfetch'd was shocked. Cut through space? It didn't understand, but it sounded amazing!

"That move is Spacial Rend." Then, D elaborated on Precipice Blades, Sacred Sword, Behemoth Blade, Bitter Blade, and other moves to this Farfetch'd.

At the end, he looked at the Farfetch'd whose eyes were filled with excitement and said, "I have a way to let you learn these moves."

"Gah!" Farfetch'd's eyes were filled with determination; no matter how hard it is, it won't give up!

"No, it has nothing to do with effort," D shook his head.

"Gah?" Farfetch'd was confused. Then how to learn?

"It mainly relies on your luck." D detailed the effect of the Berry Platter: "This magical cuisine I created has two effects."

"One lets you randomly grasp a move from a pool, and those divine sword techniques I mentioned earlier are in there, but the chance is extremely low."

"The other effect makes you randomly forget the moves you've already learned, but the only good part is that with time and effort, you can relearn them."

"I won't force you to use this method; the choice is entirely up to you." said D.

Farfetch'd fell into deep thought.

This wasn't quite what it expected.

But thinking about those powerful sword skills this human just mentioned, not to mention waterfalls, those are legendary sword techniques that can even cut through the sky and space...

Thinking of this, Farfetch'd's heart couldn't help but be moved.

But... besides these moves, it might also forget the sword techniques it has already mastered.

Farfetch'd was very worried, a thrilling feeling of fear and desire spread throughout its body.

In its heart, a black and a white Farfetch'd appeared.

The "Angel" Farfetch'd, wearing a halo and dressed in a white robe, worriedly said: "Better not try, find another way to train!"

The "Demon" Farfetch'd with sharp teeth spoke temptingly:

"You've been training for a long time, stuck at a bottleneck, unable to advance your abilities. If you miss this opportunity, there might never be another! These are sword skills that only Legendary Pokemon can master!"

Farfetch'd's little heart pounded wildly.

Should it try? Maybe its luck is good, and it can learn Spacial Rend on the first try?

As long as it learns one divine sword technique, it will leave! Absolutely won't greed for other skills!

Thinking of this, the angel Farfetch'd was knocked away by a leek from the demon Farfetch'd.

Farfetch'd's heart had already made a decision: "Gah!"

D raised an eyebrow: "Want to give it a try?"

Farfetch'd nodded.

D didn't say much more. He turned back to the living room, found a basket of berries, picked up a knife, cut the berries into pieces, and arranged them on a plate.

In just a few minutes, a Berry Platter was prepared.

D looked down, and the information about the Berry Platter surfaced in his mind.

[Berry Platter (B): A delicacy made from various berries, blending the flavors of different berries, favored by some Pokemon.

Cooking Effect: Skill Refinement, after consumption, a move on the Pokemon will randomly change, with a certain chance to comprehend a powerful rare move.

Evaluation: Basic cuisine, easy to make, a nice choice for home and travel.]

D carried the Berry Platter outside.

The Farfetch'd, with its leek in hand, was waiting under the eaves, casting a sharp gaze at the other Pokemon in the pasture.

"It's ready! Want to give it a try?" D placed the Berry Platter in front of Farfetch'd.

Farfetch'd looked down, staring at the simple, cut-up berries in front of it, hardly believing it.

Can these things really help it comprehend such powerful sword skills?

To verify its thoughts, Farfetch'd started to eat the Berry Platter.

When it consumed the last piece, its gaze suddenly turned sharp.

Here it comes!

D held his breath, his eyes unwaveringly fixed on Farfetch'd.

Suddenly, Farfetch'd yawned and then lay on the grass and began to snore.

D: "?"

Is this... the Psychic Type move, Sleep?

He stepped forward and shook Farfetch'd; it was in a deep sleep.

After a while, it finally woke up, looking very refreshed.

Farfetch'd looked at D.

D: "Congratulations, good start, you successfully comprehended Sleep on your first try."

However, this wasn't the answer Farfetch'd wanted.

With determination, it looked at D: "Gah!" (Again!)

---

D felt that this Farfetch'd would not give up, so this time he used up a whole basket of Berries in one go, directly making eight Berry Platters.

In the ranch, on the grass.

Eight Berry Platters were laid out in a line in front of Farfetch'd.

Farfetch'd looked serious and began to eat them vigorously one by one.

From the first platter, Farfetch'd forgot Cut.

Feeling the sensation of forgetting a move within its body, Farfetch'd widened its eyes in shock.

From the second platter, Farfetch'd learned Sand Attack.

Farfetch'd nearly choked in surprise.

Still unconvinced, it went straight to the next Berry Platter.

From the third platter, Farfetch'd forgot Iaido Slash.

The determined Farfetch'd: "Quack!" (Again!)

It just wouldn't believe it!

Yet when you're unlucky, even drinking cold water can cause a toothache.

From the fourth platter, Farfetch'd forgot Sleep.

From the fifth platter, Farfetch'd forgot Sand Attack.

From the sixth platter, Farfetch'd forgot Aerial Ace.

From the seventh platter, Farfetch'd forgot Fury Cutter.

By this point, Farfetch'd had forgotten all its skills, and a stifling black aura surrounded it.

But Farfetch'd did not give up.

With a serious face and a belly as round as a ball, it staggered to the last Berry Platter.

Now, it had nothing left to lose!

"Quack!"

Farfetch'd let out a fearless shout and quickly pecked at the last Berry Platter.

When the last piece of Berry was swallowed.

Farfetch'd's aura suddenly surged, its eyes became incredibly sharp, and then it brandished its leek, leaping into an intense battle dance.

That move was... Sword Dance!

After finishing the dance, Farfetch'd felt full of power throughout its body.

But its stretched belly made it feel incredibly uncomfortable.

In the end, the overly full Farfetch'd flopped onto the grass, its mind blank, staring weakly at the darkening sky, it murmured:

"Quack..."

At this moment, its view was suddenly blocked by a few heads.

Pawmot, Glaceon, Ogerpon, and other Pokemon had somehow gathered around, leaning over to look at this Farfetch'd with the immense appetite who ate nine Berry Platters in one sitting.

Alcremie: "Murmur!" (It can eat so much! It ate so many Berry Platters in one go, even more than Dragonite!)

Glaceon: "Eevee..." (It looks like it's about to die...)

Upon hearing this, Ogerpon started to worry, reaching out its small hand to check Farfetch'd's nostrils, finding it still breathing, it let out a sigh of relief, joyfully saying: "Bonnie!" (It's not dead yet!)

Farfetch'd felt lifeless and its eyes had already become vacant.

D smiled as he approached, looking at this worked-up Farfetch'd, and asked, "Do you feel alright?"

Farfetch'd did not respond.

Only then did D begin to worry, he touched Farfetch'd's now hard belly, wondering if it would be okay.

In this world, there have also been quite a few cases of Pokemon getting sick from overeating...

D was very concerned, and there was no way to solve the problem of Pokemon eating too much here at the ranch.

He hurriedly took out Farfetch'd's Poke Ball and recalled it, then rode Koraidon to the town's Pokemon Center.

Upon hearing Farfetch'd had eaten nearly forty Berries in one sitting, Miss Joy was shocked.

"How did this happen?"

She looked worried, quickly having her assistant Pokemon, Indeedee, place Farfetch'd onto the ambulance, pushing it into the emergency room.

D held Farfetch'd's leek, feeling quite complicated.

This Farfetch'd... how could it be so silly?

He waited nearly an hour in the Pokemon Center, and then the emergency room door finally reopened.

Miss Joy came out, letting out a sigh of relief:

"It's alright now, I just induced vomiting in Farfetch'd, and it has brought up the Berries it ate."

D immediately felt relieved.

Miss Joy asked, "Mr. D, is Farfetch'd a Pokemon you just caught?"

D shook his head: "No, it came to the ranch from elsewhere."

"I see, no wonder there's no such Pokemon as Farfetch'd in the Paldea Region," Miss Joy realized: "Oh right, Farfetch'd has woken up, you can go in and see it now!"

D nodded and walked into the ward with Koraidon.

Farfetch'd was lying on the bed looking dazed, and when it heard the sound, it turned its head.

"Feeling better?" D asked very apologetically: "Sorry, it's my fault, I shouldn't have let you eat so many Berries at once."

At that moment, his focus was entirely on the moves that Farfetch'd was about to learn, and he hadn't noticed how much it was consuming.

Farfetch'd's gaze cleared up, remembering how this human had brought it for treatment with a genuine concern, it was slightly moved internally, and then gently shook its head:

"Quack." (It's not your fault.) It was its own decision to eat.

D handed over the leek: "Your weapon, take it!"

Farfetch'd took the leek, instantly feeling a sense of security.

Having vomited for a long time earlier, Farfetch'd was now in a very weakened state.

D wasn't in a hurry to leave, so he simply stayed there with Farfetch'd.

He poured a cup of warm water for Farfetch'd and helped it drink:

"Don't worry, let's head back together later, I promise, I'll find a way to help you relearn the moves you've forgotten."

---

---

---

---

# 334: Chapter 331: Ranch Expansion Plan (Double Chapter)

Just do it.

To help Farfetch'd practice better, D immediately went to Keri's cottage and ordered nine wooden stump dummies from her after leaving the Pokemon Center.

Keri nodded, understanding that trainers had previously ordered wooden stump dummies from her to help with Pokemon special training.

"I'll bring them along when I visit you tomorrow!"

Tomorrow she was going to D Ranch to install a full house heating system for the room where Slugma lived.

"Alright."

D nodded, then rode Koraidon back to the ranch.

In the evening, days of traveling had left D feeling exhausted all over; he rested on the sofa, leaving dinner preparation to his sister Indeedee.

It was late, and the tired birds returned to their nests.

The ranch was shrouded in night, and the entire world fell into a peaceful silence, occasionally interrupted by the calls of Bird-type Pokemon from the wild.

The living room was lively, and thanks to Slugma's presence, the room was snugly warm.

Fortunately, the temperature wasn't too high yet; otherwise, the house might have turned into a sauna.

D felt completely relaxed, turning his gaze to his large family of Pokemon.

Slugma was munching happily on a plate of Poffin in the fireplace, a contented smile on its face, occasionally letting out joyful cries.

Glaceon and Cyclizar lay in front of the fireplace, curiously watching Slugma eat.

Pawmot and Alcremie, worn out from playing during the day, lay on either side of Calyrex, who was watching TV, sprawled on the sofa resting.

On the coffee table, Sinistea and Polteageist were dozing off.

Flutter Mane floated on the ceiling, its schedule adjusted by D so it would start dozing once it got dark.

Koraidon lay behind the sofa, resting with closed eyes.

Just looking at these diverse Pokemon, D felt deeply satisfied.

Ogerpon sat obediently next to him, its star-like eyes gazing around.

"Poni..."

Everything in front of it seemed like a dreamy scene.

This place is wonderful!

The Glaceon that took it to pick flowers, the Alcremie that shared sweet cream with it, the Pawmot that showed it around the Chicken House, the Cyclizar that secretly brought it Berries, and the Indeedee Sibling who helped prepare food for it...

Everyone was so kind, no one disliked it or feared it. Here, it didn't have to wear a mask to hide its true face.

Not only that, the town mayor had even invited it to play in the town.

This was something Ogerpon never would have dared to imagine back in Mossui Town!

Thinking of all this, Ogerpon felt the urge to cry, its eyes gradually welling up with tears.

But soon, D noticed this.

When he spoke, the other Pokemon immediately gathered around, worriedly asking why it was crying, if someone had bullied it, ready to help it teach that bad Pokemon a lesson!

Hearing this, Ogerpon couldn't help but burst into laughter, gently shaking its head, moved: "Poni~"

Watching this scene, D couldn't help but smile, feeling proud of them from the bottom of his heart.

These kind-hearted Pokemon were all like his children!

After dinner, D went to bed early to rest.

Ogerpon's room was arranged on the second floor by D, making it neighbors with the Indeedee Sibling.

It lay on the bed, listening to the occasional sounds of Pokemon cries from downstairs.

Gradually, the sounds grew weaker, as if everyone had drifted into the land of dreams.

Ogerpon wasn't sleepy; it got up from the soft bed and walked over to the bookshelf with four masks displayed.

Ogerpon picked up the Teal Mask, embedded with a Tera Shard that D had fished out from the Crystal Light Pool for it.

"Poni~"

*

The next morning at nine, Keri arrived at the ranch in her large truck.

The Machamps cooperatively unloaded the square pipes and wooden stump dummies from the truck.

Keri directed them from the side, and once all the construction materials were ready, she said to D:

"Take a look, where do you want to place the wooden stump dummies?"

"Hmm, let me see." D turned around and looked around; after searching for a while, he couldn't find a suitable spot.

He realized the remaining space at the ranch seemed to be running out...

The Chicken House, the Miltank House, the former Salt House, the stable, the Berry Garden, the World Tree, the flower field, the Gogoat pen, the Glass Greenhouse, the Pokemon Tavern, and the large plot of farmland.

Unknowingly, the ranch had gained so many things?!

D couldn't make up his mind for a moment.

Keri hadn't been to the ranch in a long time; she looked around, taking in everything, and smiled, saying:

"It seems like your place is getting more and more prosperous!"

D's gaze landed on the vacant lot next to the stable, pointing there he said: "Put them there!"

That was the last remaining empty space; other places couldn't be used for building, and there was still an open pasture reserved for the Spectrier, Glastrier, and Miltanks to run and roll around.

If that spot was taken, they wouldn't have a place to run freely.

For Horse-type Pokemon, this was very important.

If they didn't run for extended periods, they would become emotionally depressed and dissatisfied.

"Alright!"

Keri led the Machamps to the vacant lot.

Next, after knocking and striking those wooden dummy posts a few times, once everything was completed, a practice area specifically for Farfetch'd was set up.

There were a total of nine wooden dummy posts, arranged in three rows both horizontally and vertically, looking very orderly.

D had agreed with Farfetch'd last night that to avoid letting the Berry Platter affect its body, whether by being overfull or becoming a fat duck with thick fat, it would only be provided with six portions of Berry Platter each day.

Twice each in the morning, noon, and evening.

But Farfetch'd did not agree, as it patted the fluff on its chest, assuring that it could eat more!

So D had to change six times to nine times.

Even so, Farfetch'd still couldn't stop thinking about the Berry Platter. Having endured so much yesterday, it swore to master that move of Divine Sword Techniques, or else all the suffering would be in vain!

D called the Farfetch'd back here from its daze over a Berry and said to it:

"Practice those moves right here!"

Farfetch'd held its leek, with a serious expression and determined eyes.

After a night's rest, it once again regained its full swordsman demeanor.

Hearing D's words, it suddenly raised its leek and vertically slashed at one of the wooden dummy posts.

Feeling the impact and heaviness in its hand, Farfetch'd was very satisfied with this place; it was even more perfect than the waterfall it used to train its sword techniques before.

"Quack!" Farfetch'd nodded at D, then its agile figure quickly shuttled among the dummy array, continuously striking the wooden dummies with the leek in its hand.

Seeing this scene, D hesitated as if wanting to say something.

Actually, he wanted to tell Farfetch'd that it could pause the training first, and then start retraining the forgotten moves after gaining usable skills through the Berry Platter.

After all, it only knows Sword Dance now; even if it forgets, it would be a loss...

But seeing Farfetch'd's serious and hardworking appearance, D swallowed back these words.

Forget it, just let Farfetch'd continue practicing, constantly increasing its move proficiency is also great for it.

When it practices these moves to an exceptional level, even if forgotten, it can quickly relearn them.

Keri watched this scene with great interest from the side: "What a hardworking Pokemon it is!"

D smiled, thinking indeed, this Farfetch'd worked so hard to master its moves yesterday that it almost ate forty Berries, nearly bursting itself.

The two turned and walked towards the house.

Next, they'd help Slugma build a residence.

On the road, Keri looked into the distance, her gaze landed on the other side of the river, and suddenly said to D:

"Haven't you thought about expanding the ranch? That way, the Pokemon would have more space to move about, and you could raise more Pokemon."

D was slightly taken aback: "Expand the ranch?" How?

Following Keri's gaze, he saw that open area across the river.

D instantly understood, surprised: "Can those lands be bought?"

"Of course." Keri nodded with a smile, "However, it would require a significant amount of money, but there's an advantage. Once you buy it, that land is yours, perfectly suited for a rancher planning to expand their operations."

D was deeply intrigued.

If he bought that land, he could raise more Pokemon!

He'd always wanted to raise some Wooloo and Tropius, as well as other Pokemon.

But due to the limited space of the ranch, these ideas had never been realized.

And now...

D was somewhat excited but soon calmed down because there were still some details he didn't understand.

"What about the property rights?" D asked.

"Property rights?" Keri was a bit puzzled by this question.

D explained, "If I buy that land, would there be a time limit, like after seventy years the land no longer belongs to me?"

"How could that be?" Keri, a bit confused but still explained, "Once you buy that land, it's yours! The League will issue you a land deed and register the information online."

"Even when you get old, you can pass it down to your descendants, it'll always be yours. If anyone encroaches on your land, you can report it to the League, and they'll send someone to deal with it."

"Perpetual land?" D felt as if his breath nearly stopped.

"Unless you sell it to someone else." Keri laughed.

"Where can I go to make the purchase then?" D asked.

"It's simple... Uh, you can ask Mayor Thomas. He's the mayor and can help you resolve this issue," Keri said.

"Alright, I understand." D decided to find Mayor Thomas soon and bring that piece of land across the river into his ranch.

By then, he'd hire several more Diglett workers, then plant a few plots, grow fruit trees, crops, and flowers, creating an area similar to the Apple Garden in Kitakami.

Besides planting, he could also raise more productive Pokemon.

Like Tropius.

Tropius grows fruit on its neck, with two harvests a year. Although the fruit from one Tropius might not yet exceed what it usually eats, it's a Pokemon! Whether it makes money doesn't matter, as long as it's kept at home he's happy.

---

This must be the greatest joy for collectors!

Watching various kinds of Pokémon live on their farm, helping with work, or engaging in production.

The bustling, vibrant, and flourishing scene is delightful just to imagine.

Besides, he can plant a Scallion Garden for Farfetch'd, raise a fluffy herd of Wooloo, and collect all types of Eevee to create an Eevee Paradise, where he can build slides, swings, and seesaws for the Eevee to play...

And he will feel very happy just sitting nearby watching the smiles on the Eevee.

Keri brought the Machamp into the room to install pipelines.

This task requires smashing through walls to install the pipes inside, so the house's power supply was completely cut off to prevent accidents.

Calyrex can't watch TV, so it can only sit in the garden with Sinistea and Polteageist, enjoying tea.

Other Pokémon in the pasture are playing on the grass.

D is afraid Calyrex will be bored, so he purposely comes over to chat with it.

"In two more years, I'll buy a computer and a mobile phone for you, and then you can watch whatever TV you want!" D said.

Calyrex was slightly stunned and used Telepathy to say, "A phone? What is that?"

"Um..." D, who was excited, calmed down.

He thought carefully for a while, then explained, "It's a kind of machine, like a combination of a mini TV and a video phone, developed by humans."

"As long as you connect to the internet, you can use the phone to contact people worldwide. Besides, you can search for cartoons on the internet with your phone and watch whatever you want without watching ads."

In this world, video software probably doesn't require memberships, right?

Of course, it doesn't matter if it does, I can get Calyrex a century-long permanent premium membership.

Hearing this, Calyrex's eyes lit up instantly, "There truly exists such a thing in this world!"

"That's right!" D nodded, giving Calyrex an example, "If Zacian and Zamazenta also had phones, you could video call with them in the Paldea Region and see how they're doing, very convenient, isn't it?"

Calyrex nodded slightly and looked at D with a bit of embarrassment, "I... I want a phone."

Seeing Calyrex eager to have one immediately, D then realized, and couldn't help but say:

"Ahem, this kind of phone hasn't been developed yet, probably in two more years."

Calyrex's eyes showed a hint of disappointment, but soon, it perked up again.

"I have lived in this world for thousands of years, mere two years, I can wait!"

D nodded solemnly, promising, "Yes, at that time I will buy one first thing and give it to you!"

His little follower is so nice! Calyrex thought, moved.

And D fell into contemplation again.

So next, he had much more to do.

Buy land to expand the farm, raise more Pokémon.

Check the situation of Nemona's father's company, choose the right timing to invest, and provide them with research directions.

Besides that...

Just then, the ground beneath D's feet suddenly stirred.

Immediately after, Dugtrio poked its head out and said to D, "Cheep cheep!"

"Hmm?" D looked at Dugtrio and asked, "What's wrong?"

Dugtrio: "Cheep cheep!"

Calyrex helped translate beside him, "They say they found something strange in the underground cave."

---

---

# 335: Chapter 332: The Five Garganacl

Something strange?

D stood up, followed the Diglett into the Salt House, and went down the underground staircase until he reached the Underground Cave where the Naclstack lived.

"Drip! Drip!"

At this time, the Naclstack were all playing on the ground, leaving the cave empty and particularly silent, so silent that the sound of dripping water could be heard clearly.

D crossed the cave and, led by the Dugtrio, came to a rock wall.

He suddenly noticed that a section of the rock wall had a color that was so out of place with its surroundings.

"Squeak squeak squeak!" The Dugtrio shook its body, indicating that the strange stone it had found was right there.

D took a closer look and saw marks on the stone as if something had been gnawing at it. To get a better view, he asked the Dugtrio to help dig out this stone.

The Dugtrio squeaked its agreement.

Then, D watched as it used Dig to burrow into the soil, creating a small mound on the ground.

Then, the Dugtrio underground began to move, tunneling into the rock wall.

Soon, with a rumbling sound, the soil around the stone was dug out by the Dugtrio.

Without support, the stone clattered to the ground.

"Huh?"

D squatted down to observe closely, and suddenly, he felt the stone looked very familiar.

The color and texture were somewhat like the Everstone that Teddiursa picked up and gave to him...

Not sure, better take another look.

D returned to the living room, took out an Everstone from inside, and compared it with the big stone.

Quickly, he reached a conclusion.

The big stone in front of him was clearly an unpolished raw Everstone vein!

Looking at the gnawing marks on the large Everstone, an unbelievable thought suddenly popped into D's mind.

Could it be that his Naclstack had gnawed on this thing?!

D quickly found the Naclstack and brought them back underground, pointing to the stone and asking, "Have you eaten this thing?"

The Naclstack gathered around the Everstone and looked at it, then happily nodded, "Stack～"

Indeed! They had gnawed on it when they were bored, but the stone was not tasty at all; they only took one bite and never ate it again!

At that time, one Naclstack discovered this stone.

It knocked off a small piece with its head, then started eating it, only to find the taste terrible.

The other Naclstack saw this scene and crowded around to watch, asking if it was tasty.

The first Naclstack mischievously lied to them, saying it was tasty.

Then the second stone-gnawing Naclstack appeared.

Soon after, the third, fourth, and fifth Naclstack all got tricked into taking a bite.

Finally, upon discovering the truth, the Naclstack "angrily" chased and played around in the cave, and when they got tired, they huddled together for a nap, without anyone paying attention to the stone they just ate.

When Calyrex translated the truth for D, he couldn't help but facepalm.

Well, now he knew why the Naclstack hadn't evolved into Garganacl!

"You guys! So, who was the first to discover this stone?" D asked, feigning anger with a stern face.

The Naclstack, seeing D angry, suddenly became flustered, all looking a bit at a loss, their heads drooping like a row of children who knew they had done wrong.

The Naclstack who had tricked its companions into eating the bad-tasting stone weakly spoke up: "Stack..." admitting it was its fault.

"You little rascal!" D poked its forehead with his finger.

The Naclstack's eyes darted around, unsure where to look.

D sighed helplessly, "Alright, I'm not angry, it's no big deal."

The Naclstack were stunned for a moment, then immediately became happy.

However, D's next words once again threw them into a panic.
