"If you eat this stone, you won't be able to evolve into Garganacl."

"Stack?!" The Naclstack asked in shock.

D turned around and began to explain the stone to them:

"This is an Everstone, it has the effect of preventing Pokemon from evolving. As long as a Pokemon carries it, it can no longer evolve further."

Generally, when an Everstone vein like this is found in the wild, it is mined by professionals and transported to factories for processing into round, small Everstones that are easy to carry.

These Everstones are sent to major towns to be sold to Pokemon and Trainers who need them.

The Naclstack were very anxious, as they were supposed to evolve into Garganacl to protect D and Alcremie!

They had all made a promise to D that once they evolved into Garganacl, they would let D sit on them and carry him around on the grass for fun.

And now, in this situation... does this mean they can no longer evolve?

The Naclstack looked hurriedly at the stone, then at each other, and finally cast their eyes on D who knew everything.

"Stack?" trembled one Naclstack, asking with a shaky voice.

Even without Calyrex translating, D could understand its meaning.

It was asking: Can they still evolve?

D thought carefully for a moment, knowing that Garganacl is a Rock Type Pokemon.

This kind of Pokemon has a very special physique; although they usually like eating cured meats, they occasionally eat stones and ores too.

Like most Pokemon that eat stones and ores, their bodies can perfectly digest these stones and metal ores, breaking them down into energy and nutrients to supply their bodies.

---

I wonder if the Everstone falls within this category...

But looking at the Naclstack, it's clear that the Everstone in their bodies hasn't been digested yet, otherwise, they would have evolved into Garganacl.

However, D wasn't sure. This knowledge fell outside his expertise.

After a moment's thought, D decided to take the Garganacl to the Pokemon Center for a checkup.

He remembered there were machines there similar to X-rays that could examine a Pokemon's body.

D went back inside to find the Poke Balls for the Naclstack and then recalled them into the balls.

He mentioned his plan to Keri and left everything on the ranch to be managed by Indeedee. After arranging everything, D called over the nearest Cyclizar and rode to Porto Marinada Town.

At this hour, the Pokemon Center was empty except for a few Trainers who had stayed overnight.

When D arrived, Miss Joy was idly behind the counter.

Seeing D, she was quite surprised: "Mr. D! What brings you here? Did the Pokemon at the ranch fall ill again?"

D nodded and explained his purpose: "My Naclstack accidentally ate an Everstone and couldn't evolve. I came to get them checked."

"Understood!" Miss Joy didn't say much more. She took D to the examination room and instructed him to release the Naclstack one by one for an X-ray examination.

Accompanied by the machine scan, D soon saw on the screen a black shadow, an undigested piece inside the Naclstack's bodies.

Miss Joy said: "That should be the Everstone. Its mineral properties are quite unique; even Rock Type Pokemon can't digest it."

"Some Pokemon Trainers even deliberately let their Pokemon swallow the Everstone to prevent it from affecting their combat abilities."

At this, Miss Joy looked helpless: "I've encountered several of these Trainers before, where Pokemon that ingested the Everstone had a stomach ache, and they could only be treated by inducing vomiting to expel the stone."

D: "..." These Trainers are just too lazy!

Next up, Miss Joy checked the other four Naclstack.

Sure enough, inside each Naclstack, there was an Everstone of varying sizes.

"In this case, inducing vomiting is the only solution," Miss Joy said.

Upon hearing that the stones they swallowed could be removed, the Naclstack immediately cheered up.

Seeing this, D felt helpless: "You mustn't eat random things in the future!"

"Stack!" The Naclstack nodded in unison.

D sighed with relief and said to Miss Joy: "I'll leave it to you then, Miss Joy."

Miss Joy smiled slightly: "Providing treatment for Pokemon is the duty of every professional. Mr. D, please wait outside."

D nodded and instructed the five Naclstack to listen to Miss Joy before heading to the lobby with Cyclizar to wait.

After a while, Miss Joy returned from the treatment room with five pale-looking Naclstack.

Her Pokemon assistant carried a tray with the Everstones that the Naclstack had accidentally eaten.

"All done, they're fine now," Miss Joy said with a tight-lipped smile.

D paid the examination fee, took out the Poke Ball to let the Naclstack rest: "Thank you, Miss Joy."

Miss Joy wrapped the Everstones in a napkin and handed them to D.

These are not worthless items; Everstone sells for 3000 League coins each in the market!

D reached out to take them, deciding to have them framed back home as a black mark in the history of the Naclstack.

Then, he bid farewell to Miss Joy and rode the Cyclizar back to the ranch.

Seeing their safe return, Alcremie sighed with relief, running back and forth among the Naclstack, observing each one's condition with concern.

Only after confirming that all the Naclstack were unscathed, Alcremie finally relaxed.

Seeing this, D couldn't help but laugh and ask: "Why are you so worried? Were you afraid of the Naclstack being bullied by bad guys?"

"Cream!" Alcremie stood proudly with hands on its hips, indicating it can now deal with bad guys and protect the Naclstack.

Yesterday, it even used Metronome to pull off a move with seemingly great power!

D chuckled.

The Naclstack were also delighted.

Just then, they suddenly began to shine with a bright light.

Within the white glow, the Naclstack's bodies began to rapidly transform.

They gradually stood upright, eventually becoming five enormous, imposing giant Pokemon, resembling mighty guardians.

Their bodies exuded purity, with a faint salty aroma emanating from them.

The golden sunlight poured upon the Garganacl, making them appear sacred and pristine.

After brief astonishment, D quickly regained his composure.

He smiled at the five Garganacl before him, "Congratulations to all of you." It seems they had already met the conditions for evolution long ago!

"Boom～!"

The Garganacl emitted a series of Growl, their voices sounding akin to rocks colliding, heavy yet textured, filled with overwhelming joy.

---

One of the Garganacl stepped forward with heavy, powerful strides, crouched slightly, extended its arm, and invited D.

D rarely felt a bit embarrassed.

After evolving, the Garganacl's height noticeably exceeded two and a half meters. Although it was much taller than him, letting a 1.8-meter-tall man like him sit up there was quite awkward.

However, the Garganacl didn't think much about it.

Seeing D not getting up, it scratched its head in confusion, then extended another arm, lifting D up like a chick, and placed him on its shoulder.

D: "..."

"Boom～" The Garganacl let out a happy rumbling sound.

The surrounding Pokemon saw this scene and gathered around.

D turned his head to see that Alcremie had already climbed onto another Garganacl, standing on top of its head like a captain commanding a warship, issuing an order for full attack.

"Mama!"

Alcremie raised its finger with fighting spirit.

Immediately, a purple light blade formed in its hand and flew forward.

Faintly, the space around the purple light blade was torn apart by its powerful energy.

"Boom!"

A loud crash, the light blade made a big pit in the ground.

"Mama!" Alcremie became panicked and flustered.

D: "...Spacial Rend isn't used like that!"

D glanced over and found Farfetch'd hadn't come over. It was still diligently practicing moves by the wooden dummy.

Thankfully, Farfetch'd didn't see this move Spacial Rend, or else he wouldn't know how to explain.

After playing around, D jumped off the Garganacl, ready to check Keri's progress.

However, other Garganacl surrounded him again, they also wanted D to sit on them.

"..."

D helplessly rode on each one in turn.

Finally, every Garganacl was happy.

"Alright, you all go play! I have things to do." D said.

Nearing noon, the heating system renovation inside the house was almost complete.

When D entered the house, Keri was working on the final task — the kitchen renovation.

They emptied a cabinet and rebuilt it into a stove, connecting its interior to the heating system.

"Alright, it's done!" Keri stood up and dusted off her hands.

D walked up and looked around, sure enough, he found heating pipes installed everywhere in the house.

The pipes connected all parts of the house, from the living room's fireplace to the kitchen, bedroom, and every room on the second floor, covering almost all areas.

Almost identical to the animation named "The Slugma-Powered Home."

Keri smiled and asked, "Are you satisfied?"

D nodded with satisfaction, so now, Slugma could move freely inside the house.

"I didn't dismantle the stove over there because if I did, and Slugma got sick, you wouldn't be able to cook dinner."

Keri pointed to the stove below and said, "Here I installed special heat-conducting materials that can efficiently convey Slugma's temperature upwards. As long as Slugma is underneath, you can safely start cooking."

"What about when summer comes?" D asked.

In summer, if Slugma were still in the heating system, they'd burn up...

Keri replied with a smile, "The materials of the heating system can effectively insulate temperature. In summer, you can close the vent, preventing warm air from entering the room, hence keeping the temperature down."

"That's a relief." D sighed.

Keri: "If there's nothing else, shall I leave?"

"Alright, thank you for your hard work!" D responded, paid Keri for the project, and saw them off the ranch.

Returning to the house, he discovered the Slugma that was originally in the fireplace was now autonomously exploring the pipes.

Slugma crawled with curiosity, its movements slow, like magma, its body emitted a fiery glow, warming the room through the heating vents.

Soon, the house was cozy and warm.

D folded his arms, smiling as he watched the scene.

He saw Slugma climb to the second floor, entered his bedroom, then returned to the fireplace, gradually familiarizing itself with each place.

When it crawled over to the kitchen, D squatted down, tapping the stove to call Slugma: "Slugma, come over here!"

"Goo?"

Hearing the sound, Slugma made a turn and slowly crawled over.

D fed two Berries to Slugma, laughed, and said: "Eat, eat! You can live here comfortably from now on!"

"Woo～"

Slugma ate the Berries happily, its body emitting a "boom boom" sound.

A puff of white smoke instantly ventured out through the chimney.

Outside, Pawmot heard the sound, saw the white smoke, and knew Slugma was happy now.

It immediately flew back to the living room, came to D's side, took out its cherished Berry and Poffin, and handed them to Slugma in front.

"Bamor!"

Slugma slowly began to eat.

Seeing this, Pawmot was extremely happy.

Now it too had its own Slugma-Powered Home!

---

---

---

---

# 336: Chapter 333: A Bigger Ranch, A Bigger Home

At noon, after an entire morning of diligently practicing Scallion Techniques, Farfetch'd, drenched in sweat, found D, eagerly anticipating what kind of moves it would comprehend today.

Farfetch'd had a vague premonition that today was going to be a lucky day, perhaps it might suddenly grasp the move it had been desiring.

"It really is a huge gambling duck," D couldn't help but silently comment.

The berries at home were all eaten up by Farfetch'd yesterday, so he didn't make a Berry Platter for it this morning.

Just moments ago, he had ordered the Moto-Duck to the Berry Garden to pick a basket of berries.

D placed the berry basket on the dining table, took out a few berries, and swiftly chopped them into small pieces with a kitchen knife to make three portions of Berry Platter.

"How was this morning's practice? Have you relearned all the forgotten moves?" D asked.

Farfetch'd nodded: "Quack!"

It had practiced the Cut technique countless times before, and the usage method and skills for this move were already deeply etched into its mind, allowing it to execute with great ease.

Thus, it managed to relearn it in just one morning and even gained a new understanding of this move.

D was a bit surprised; it seemed that this Farfetch'd had a high level of proficiency in Cut!

At the same time, he was somewhat curious. If Farfetch'd could train Cut to a state of perfection and firmly embed it into its mind and memory.

Then, the question arises, would the Berry Platter still make it forget this move?

Thinking about this, D carried a few Berry Platters to the grassy area outside.

Just as Farfetch'd was about to start eating, D slapped his forehead and suddenly shouted at it.

Farfetch'd turned its head in confusion: "Quack?"

"Wait a moment!" D turned back to the kitchen and took out the Lucky Pudding he had previously made for Teddiursa from the fridge.

Looking down, the information about this Lucky Pudding surfaced in his mind.

[Lucky Pudding (A): A dessert made with a feeling of happiness, crafted using Light Cream, MooMoo Fresh Milk, and Honey.

Cooking Effect: Good Fortune, temporarily increases luck slightly after consumption, raising the chance for Pokemon to find rare items during travel.

Evaluation: A delicious dessert that brings happiness and easily leads to good luck after consumption.]

"If you eat the Lucky Pudding first, and then start eating the Berry Platter, it might increase the probability of comprehending the moves," D contemplated the linkage between the two different dishes.

He handed the Lucky Pudding to Farfetch'd, saying, "Eat this first; it can boost your luck, making you more fortunate."

Luck is quite a mysterious thing.

Besides having good things happen in daily life, it can also play a role in battles.

For instance, luckily evading an opponent's move or hitting a critical spot when using a move, causing a critical hit and the like.

Farfetch'd ate the Lucky Pudding, then focused on the three Berry Platters before it with bated breath.

From the first portion, Farfetch'd comprehended Double Team.

With the second portion, Farfetch'd forgot Cut.

The third portion…

As soon as it swallowed the last Berry Platter, a pained expression suddenly appeared on Farfetch'd's face.

Its body began to glow with a white light, and a terrifying power gathered furiously.

D's eyelid twitched, and he couldn't shake off a sense of impending doom.

He instinctively moved back to dodge.

In the next second, a violent explosion erupted from Farfetch'd, the terrifying impact blowing away the surrounding grass.

D was knocked to the ground by the blast's impact, covering his head, and only when the sound subsided did he stand up in shock to inspect.

Seeing Farfetch'd lying at the epicenter of the explosion with its eyes spinning, D immediately understood what had happened.

That move was Explosion!

Explosion: A Normal Type move that causes a massive explosion, attacking all Pokemon around, with a power of 250, but leaves the user in a fainted state.

"What terrifying power..."

It was D's first time seeing a Pokemon use Explosion in real life, and judging by Farfetch'd's condition, this move must be extremely painful for the user.

Lono's Electrode might know Explosion, but D thought that even if it did, Lono would never let it use it, as this move is quite damaging to the body.

He exhaled a breath, calling over Koraidon to feed some Life Sheep Milk to Farfetch'd.

Soon, Farfetch'd recovered.

However, it didn't look happy. Although it had grasped two new moves, neither was the one it wanted.

Farfetch'd was not content, gripping its scallion tightly as it stood up again.

No way! Try again!

But its stomach was already round and full from three Berry Platters and a Lucky Pudding.

D was quite speechless: "Forget it, you'd better rest for a bit first, and digest the food in your stomach before we talk!"

"Quack..."

Farfetch'd felt its belly and realized it had no choice.

But it didn't give up and instead dashed into the dummy area, cutting swiftly at those wooden dummy stakes with its scallion.

The techniques for using Cut were already second nature to Farfetch'd, whose posture was agile, the scallion in its hand constantly sweeping and slashing, weaving among the dummies.

After who knows how long, a layer of milky white light gradually enveloped the battle-scarred scallion.

---

A flash of white light formed a gleaming "Cut" that hit the dummy.

Farfetch'd looked somewhat puzzled at the leek in its hand.

Why did it feel like... it had mastered Cut even more proficiently than before?

*

In the afternoon, D was contemplating the ranch expansion. So, after lunch, he took Cyclizar to visit Mayor Thomas in town.

D let Cyclizar go and talk to its parents. Cyclizar happily nodded, went up to its parents, nuzzled their necks, and told them about its recent experiences, saying it really liked its current life.

D and Mayor Thomas sat down on the sofa, and Mayor Thomas's wife served them two cups of tea.

"Thank you."

D politely thanked her and then inquired with Mayor Thomas about acquiring land.

Upon hearing his intention, Mayor Thomas appeared very surprised: "You want to expand the ranch?"

D nodded: "Yes, more and more Pokémon have come to live at the ranch, and it's getting a bit too crowded."

"I see!" Mayor Thomas seemed very pleased and sighed: "You're much more capable than your grandfather! He would be very happy to see this day."

After all, the ranch was the old man's life's work, and Mayor Thomas believed he wouldn't want to see it abandoned.

D pursed his lips and smiled lightly, without saying anything.

"Purchasing land? Of course, no problem!" Mayor Thomas asked with a cheerful smile: "Where do you plan to buy land?"

D: "The piece of land across the river, it's a flat and open wasteland, very close to the ranch. Just building a bridge over the river would merge the two areas together."

Upon hearing this, Mayor Thomas immediately knew which piece of land he was referring to.

He nodded and then explained the details of land purchase to D.

"Undeveloped land usually costs more than 1.2 million League coins per acre, but the Paldea League offers subsidies for agriculture and ranching, so ranchers can enjoy agricultural subsidies. It will be much cheaper, around 1.1 million."

D nodded, 1.1 million League coins per acre isn't too expensive.

Over the past year, he had saved quite a bit and could purchase several acres.

"Will there be taxes afterwards?" D asked again.

Mayor Thomas shook his head: "Non-city land doesn't require tax payment because such land belongs to undeveloped areas, the Paldea League won't help you develop it, nor send people to maintain it."

"After purchase, the land will entirely belong to you, including the maintenance of the land and wild Pokémon disturbances, which you will have to handle yourself."

For instance, wild Pokémon might destroy farmland, knock down fruit trees, or steal crops.

Speaking of which, Mayor Thomas cheerfully added: "However, with the strength of your Pokémon, these issues shouldn't be a problem."

D nodded with a smile, thinking that was right. No matter what Pokémon came to the ranch, they would never return!

There's an old saying—Orges are never slaves, unless the food and lodging are covered!

"If you want to purchase the land, I can accompany you to the town's land management bureau to submit an application, and someone will go over to measure the land, then price it for you," Mayor Thomas said.

"There's no need," D didn't want to trouble the elder: "I can go myself, and here are some fresh milk produced by Miltank at the ranch, I hope you like them."

Saying this, D placed a package of MooMoo Milk on the table.

Although the milk quality couldn't surpass Wooloo milk, it was still excellent.

Over the past two years, Mayor Thomas had helped him a lot, even gifting him a Cyclizar as an assistant, it was only appropriate to give some presents.

Mayor Thomas displayed a kind smile: "You kid, you came over and even brought gifts..."

D got up and called out: "Cyclizar!"

"Gao!"

Cyclizar immediately responded, then said goodbye to its parents and ran back to D's side.

D patted its head and then bid farewell to Mayor Thomas: "We won't disturb you anymore, see you later, Mayor Thomas!"

Mayor Thomas smiled and nodded: "Go ahead, if there's anything, come find me!"

D replied and turned to leave with Cyclizar.

Mayor Thomas watched his back and couldn't help but feel very emotional.

This kid has truly grown up!

Taking a closer look, D was already into his twenties. Next, if he got married and started a family, believe his parents and grandfather could rest assured in another world.

*

The land management bureau was very efficient, and upon hearing D's intent, they immediately dispatched personnel to accompany him for a land measurement at the ranch.

After careful measurement, the bureau's staff explained the scope and price of the land to D:

"There are a total of 7 acres of wasteland, with farmer subsidies, totaling 7.7 million League coins."

7 acres... nearly double the size of the previous ranch, he could afford that price.

Thinking briefly, D nodded: "Then I'll take this one."

Joking aside, a permanent piece of land at this price, he would regret not taking it for a lifetime!

"Alright, then please follow me back to the land management bureau for registration," the staff grinned: "We will record your information and identity credentials online, and afterwards, the land will be entirely yours."

D didn't say much, and immediately took his bank card and went to town for it.

Upon returning to the ranch, his bank card's balance was almost emptied.

But even so, D was still very excited.

Because the piece of land across the river was officially incorporated into the ranch.

He had Koraidon carry him across the river, to where the Psyducks lived.

Standing on this vast grassy land made D feel unprecedented excitement.

This place, would be his territory in the future!

D didn't ride Koraidon, he joyfully dashed through the grass.

Unable to understand why D was so obsessed with land, Koraidon sat bewildered at its original spot.

Unable to comprehend, so it decided not to think, it let out a joyful call and happily followed D, running across the grass.

With such a large area, what should he use it for?

Create a Wooloo Ranch, establish a Tropius orchard, plant more apple trees, cultivate more farmland!

But there was one more issue...

D's pace slowed down gradually, with a larger ranch area, relying solely on footwork, he would require an enormous effort to reach here.

Although he could ride Koraidon, but if he planted apple trees here, how would he transport the harvested apples to the shipping crates?

He couldn't use Koraidon like a horse or cow to pull a cart, could he?

D fell into deep thought.

Then he thought about the farmers' tractors.

But he'd never driven a tractor...

Just at that moment, a more suitable Pokémon for such tasks emerged in D's mind.

That would be the Steel + Poison Type Revavroom!

If he recalled correctly, in the game, Team Star Village once modified Revavroom, transforming it into their unique combat vehicle.

Then the question arises, could this type of Pokémon be modified into a tractor for hauling Berries?

Furthermore, there was another problem.

This technology was developed by Ortega in Team Star, and it utilized Armarouge and Ceruledge as power sources for the combat vehicle.

Three Pokémon at once, Revavroom, Armarouge, Ceruledge, the future seemed too extravagant...

It felt like asking Groudon as the Earth God to farm.

"Forget it, no rush on this matter." D shook his head, putting aside these thoughts.

He just bought the land, hadn't even seen a shadow of apple trees yet, thinking about tractors was too soon.

Take your time, there's no hurry, as long as everyone's here, there will be bread, there will be milk.

Right now, the most important thing is to contact Keri to order wooden fences, and fence off the entire land, also to build a bridge over the river to connect the two pieces of land.

---

---

---

---

# 337: Chapter 334: Visitor

The newly purchased land is divided into two types: one is mountainous, the other is flat.

The flat ground is covered with lush grass, which seems perfect for grazing.

Then, D climbed up the small hill and gazed into the distance from the top. From there, he could see the back of the mountain in the distance, and even a corner of the Abundant Shrine faintly appeared.

D was thinking about planting a bunch of apple trees on this little hill in the future, just like Apple Hill in Kitakami.

Some Pokemon also inhabit this land, like the light and airy Hoppip family.

This kind of Pokemon has a small, round, pink body with two long green leaves sprouting from its head.

Their small yellow eyes looked at the world with innocence and liveliness, their tiny arms and legs waving gently in the air, and a little pink tail swaying like a cat's.

When the wind blows, these Hoppip and Jumpluff are swept into the sky, drifting gently with the wind, and then flying away.

"Indeed, just like recorded in the Pokédex, the bodies of Hoppip are very light," D murmured as he observed the scene.

They move around with the wind. It's said that there were no Hoppip in the Paldea Region initially until one day some Hoppip were blown across the sea, reaching the Paldea Region.

From then on, the Paldea Region also had Hoppip.

Besides Hoppip, Floette and Flabebe also inhabit this field.

The place where Floette resides is a wildflower cluster.

They are very small, not even as big as a palm, holding a flower in their arms and flying around the blossoms.

For some reason, seeing these Pokemon reminded D of the Thumbelina from Andersen's fairy tales.

His presence disturbed the group of Floette and Flabebe.

These little guys immediately hid behind the flowers, cautiously watching him.

When D made eye contact with them, Flabebe and Floette would immediately tuck their heads in, quite like covering their ears while stealing a bell.

D couldn't help but chuckle. He didn't disturb the group of Flabebe and Floette. After inspecting his domain, he turned and returned to the ranch.

Early the next day, D found Keri and ordered a wooden fence from her, also asking her to help build a bridge on the ranch.

Keri happened to be free, so she simply pulled up the building materials and drove the big truck, carrying D to the ranch.

Machamp and Conkeldurr were sitting in the back compartment alongside a pile of wood, stones, and cement.

D sat in the passenger seat and curiously asked, "Do you need a driver's license to drive this kind of vehicle?"

"Of course," Keri nodded. "You need a driver's license no matter what kind of vehicle it is. Why? Are you thinking of buying a car?"

"Yes, the ranch has expanded, so I'm thinking of buying a vehicle for transporting goods," D replied honestly.

Keri said, "In that case... you don't need to buy a big truck like mine, a pickup should be enough. It's just right for carrying berries, corn, carrots, and such crops, and it's not expensive either."

"Of course, if you want to go out and don't feel like riding Koraidon, you could drive a pickup and take your Pokemon on a trip outside."

Hearing this, D imagined himself driving a pickup truck loaded with Pokemon down the highway.

And indeed, it was quite an appealing thought.

Capturing a Revavroom wasn't feasible at the moment, and he didn't know the modification method either. So for now, it was more convenient to just buy a pickup.

But first, he'd have to get a driver's license.

He just wasn't sure if this world had driving schools or similar places...

With a slight thought, D voiced his question aloud.

Keri answered one by one, "You don't have to go all the way to the Pokemon League Headquarters; getting a driver's license isn't like getting a Breeder certification. There's a vehicle management office here in Porto Marinada Town. You can take the test there, and if you pass, they'll issue your certificate directly."

"Hmm... the exam content is simple, mostly basic tasks like driving around obstacles, which is very easy compared to becoming a Breeder."

"That's good," D breathed a sigh of relief. This way, he wouldn't have to go to a driving school or similar place.

He had taken driving school exams in his previous life and could drive. Now he just needed to take an exam to get his driver's license directly.

Keri looked at the road ahead, full of sentiment, "Life is full of uncertainties!"

"Hmm?" D raised an eyebrow.

Keri smiled and said, "I was reminded of some past things. Back when I learned to drive, I just wanted to drive and take my Pokemon to travel around the world. But who would've thought I'd never go anywhere, always staying in this place as a carpenter."

"Road trip, huh?" D imagined it and nodded, "Sounds great. Do you plan to go out later then?"

Keri sighed helplessly, "I'd love to! But I can't leave these Pokemon behind. Not just Machamp and the others but everyone in that forest as well."

"If I'm not here and they get hurt, there'd be no one to treat them. So, as they say, plans can't keep up with changes!"

D pondered carefully and realized it made sense.

If he had to leave the Pokemon on the ranch to travel, he couldn't do it either.

"What about you? Do you plan to go out again?" Keri turned to look at D and asked.

She remembered D was only in his early twenties, still very young, and becoming a Trainer seemed to have more prospects than being a farmer in the countryside.

---

D shook his head, thinking about the Pokémon on the ranch, feeling that he might never leave and would spend his life farming.

Keri noticed his thoughts and said with a smile, "There aren't many young people like you these days!"

"Kids nowadays all dream of becoming a formidable Pokémon Master, leaving their hometowns to travel, and the land is all left barren. When people like Mr. Elif age, maybe no one would want to stay and farm."

D listened thoughtfully.

At this moment, he somewhat understood why the Paldea League is so supportive of agriculture and animal husbandry.

But how to put it?

Becoming a powerful Trainer, setting off on journeys, and encountering Pokémon is a dream of everyone living in the Pokémon World.

They are influenced by Pokémon culture from a young age, and under this influence, many people like Ash, Misty, and Brock are born.

Thinking of this, D smiled, "That's not necessarily true. These kids will grow up eventually, and maybe once they retire, they will bring their Pokémon back to their hometowns to inherit the family farm."

Keri was stunned for a moment, then laughed with relief, "You're right!"

At this moment, they arrived at the ranch.

Keri stopped the car and directed Machamp and Conkeldurr to unload the materials.

She first went to survey the small river running through the ranch.

This river flows down from the back mountain, possibly from Casseroya Lake and Glaseado Mountain, passing through the ranch and finally emptying into the sea.

After a while, Keri chose a suitable spot to build the bridge.

Machamp and Conkeldurr immediately started the construction, building piers, pouring concrete, reinforcing with steel bars…

Macho and Conkeldurr were very familiar with the work.

As they were busy, Keri followed D across the river to the land where Psyducks resided.

Looking at this uncultivated land, Keri couldn't help but say, "Clearing such a large wasteland will take a lot of effort!"

"Not all the land will be turned into farmland," D shook his head. "Only a small part will be used to plant crops and fruit trees, while I'll scatter pasture seeds on the rest for Pokémon grazing."

Like Wooloo, Tropius, and Lechonk…

"Are you fencing it all in?" Keri asked.

D nodded, "That way I can see the boundaries of the ranch and feel more at ease."

Although he could make wooden fences himself, the ranch didn't have enough wood. Cutting trees from the back mountain would violate the forest protection laws, and if caught by the Forest Rangers, he would face fines.

Moreover, those trees on the back mountain are homes for Pokémon.

D didn't want and wasn't willing to cut down those trees.

In this situation, the simplest solution was to pay someone to install them.

"Alright!" Keri smiled, roughly calculating in her head and said to D, "Such a large area, the workshop's fences may not be enough, I'll have to reorder some wood and with the bridge, it will take about half a month to complete."

D had no objections, "It's fine, like before?"

"Okay."

With everything settled, all that was left was to wait.

D wasn't in a hurry to immediately buy Wooloo, Tropius, and apple saplings, because after purchasing that land, his savings were depleted.

He needed to rest and recover before he could start anew.

Building a bridge takes a lot of time, and with installing fences, a few days weren't enough.

Therefore, Keri would come to the ranch daily to work, returning home at dusk, constantly repeating the cycle.

Meanwhile, D stayed peacefully at home.

In his free time, he'd watch TV with Calyrex, make berry platters for Farfetch'd, play with the Pokémon, and welcome some newcomer Trainers from Paldea Academy for a challenge.

He would watch these determined Trainers command their Eevee, Sprigatito, Pawmi, and Caterpie to participate in the Grass Trial, Thunder Trial, and Storm Trial.

The results were brutally overwhelming.

These Pokémon were all inevitably defeated in one hit, leaving the rookie Trainers immensely shocked, exclaiming, "What amazing Pokémon!"

During this period, in order to let Calyrex play on a phone sooner, D started to gather information on some big companies in the Paldea Region.

For information on Nemona's family, D even specially ordered the daily newspaper from town.

In these relaxed and leisurely days, time unknowingly moved to the end of March in the League year 199.

On this morning, Allen the mailman came to the ranch to deliver the newspaper.

D took the newspaper from him, smiling, "Thank you, Allen!"

Allen looked at him, hesitating to speak.

D was a bit surprised, "What's up? Something wrong?"

Allen reminded him, "The racing tournament is coming up! You promised me!"

D: "..."

He just remembered this. Allen had previously helped him find a housekeeper Pokémon, and he promised to help Allen win back the money he lost in last year's horse racing tournament.

"Don't worry!" D grinned.

Seeing that he remembered, Allen was relieved and asked, "Which Horse-type Pokemon did you catch? Rapidash or Mudsdale?"

---

D smiled mysteriously: "Neither."

He took Allen to the stables where Glastrier and Spectrier were kept.

Glastrier and Spectrier lifted their eyelids and glanced at the human.

Allen was taken aback because he suddenly felt an inexplicable chill and pressure!

These two Pokemon were clearly extraordinary!

Allen opened his eyes wide in shock: "What...what...what kind of Pokemon are they?"

"I told you, don't be surprised!" D said.

Allen nodded: "Go ahead, tell me!"

D: "The one on the left is Glastrier, and the one on the right is Spectrier. They are both legendary Pokemon and the exclusive steeds of Calyrex."

"W-What?!" Allen was so shocked that he gaped in awe, stammering: "C-Calyrex's steeds?"

Allen had seen Calyrex on the ranch, but to avoid disturbing its life, neither he nor anyone in town had disclosed this information.

Because everyone greatly respects this magnificent King of Bountiful Harvests.

D said speechlessly: "Calm down."

Allen wanted to say how could he possibly stay calm, but then he thought, since even Calyrex came to D Ranch, it was no surprise that these two horses would come too.

At that thought, he calmed down a bit and excitedly asked: "Are you planning to ride them in the race tournament?"

"There shouldn't be any problem with that, right?"

"Of course not!" Allen excitedly grabbed D's hand and exclaimed: "I'm entrusting my fortunes and life to you, Brother D! I'm counting on you!"

D: "..."

Allen excitedly hopped away from the ranch.

D then returned to the living room, sat on the sofa, and took out the morning paper to read.

From what he understood recently, Nemona's family's industry was vast in the Paldea region.

Rotom Technology Company was the main company, primarily responsible for developing Rotom Technology, such as Fridge Rotom with refrigerator functions and Wash Rotom with washing machine functions.

It's said that this technology of integrating Rotom into appliances originated from the ancient Hisuin region.

In modern times, humans developed and modified this technology, producing more Rotom Technology.

Nemona's family's tech products are not only sold in the Paldea region but also in distant places like the Galar Region, Unova Region, and Kalos Region.

It's almost the leading enterprise in the entire Paldea region.

Looking at it this way, Nemona's family is truly a wealthy and prestigious family!

Nemona herself is undoubtedly a wealthy heiress.

Besides Rotom Technology, Rotom Technology Company also covers various communication industries.

The video phones used by the Paldea League are part of Nemona's family's industry.

Such a large company, he, a mere farmer in the countryside, probably wouldn't have a chance to engage with... and perhaps doesn't need his investment.

D found himself in a dilemma; he didn't want to start a smartphone company to get a share because it would require a lot of manpower and resources, leaving him no energy to run the ranch.

That kind of life wasn't what he wanted.

He's a lazy person, only wanting to play with phones, not make them.

D read in the newspaper that this world's chip technology and networks had developed to a sufficient level, fully supporting smartphone production.

However, the technology had taken an unusual path, and no one had noticed the potential direction of Phone Rotom, just missing that final step.

So the question was, how could he contact Rotom Technology Company?

Should he ask Geeta to introduce him? Seek help from Professor Sada and Professor Turo?

Just when D was very conflicted, an unexpected visitor arrived at the ranch.

Hearing his brother Indeedee's notification, D put down the newspaper and went outside, seeing a rugged figure standing at the ranch gate.

It was a man, a dark-skinned, tall man with a well-defined face, a pair of thick eyebrows, and fiery red hair.

Alder had arrived.

---

---

---

---

# 338: Chapter 335: Alder's Gratitude

Alder looked at the young man opposite, suppressing his inner hope and excitement, and politely asked,

"Excuse me, is this D Ranch?"

D snapped back to reality, thinking Leon was quite fast, he nodded and said, "Hello, Mr. Alder."

"You know me?" Alder was slightly surprised.

D didn't deny it, "Champion of the Unova Region, you're well-known even in other places."

Alder blushed deeply, he had a keen sense of self-awareness… Although he was a champion, his fame was limited to the Unova Region, how could it reach all the way to Paldea?

Moreover, during that time he had left the Pokemon League and stayed away from worldly affairs...

Is it possible that this Trainer named D, as mentioned by Leon, is a fan of his?

Otherwise, how could he be so clear about his situation and try to contact him through Leon for help.

Alder could think of no other reason.

D, unaware of what Alder was thinking, pondered about the injured Volcarona, and then said to Alder,

"Come in first, let's talk!"

"Okay!"

Alder nodded, gripping the strap of the backpack behind him nervously, both looking forward to and fearing what was to come.

He hoped this Rancher could really heal Volcarona's hidden injuries, but feared his hopes would be dashed and Volcarona would continue to lose life.

Following behind the other, Alder recalled the events from not long ago.

At that time, while wandering around the Unova Region, he suddenly received a call from the Unova League.

The staff from Unova League told him over the phone that the Champion Leon from the Galar Region had something to discuss with him.

Then, Alder was very puzzled, he had never met Leon, only heard each other's names, what could he want?

In the midst of confusion, Alder dialed the number Leon had left.

During that phone call, Leon told him about a way to save Volcarona and asked him to go to the Paldea Region, to a place called D Ranch.

Alder was utterly shocked, with many questions in his mind, but to save his partner, he didn't question further and immediately set off for the Paldea Region after hanging up the phone.

The two entered the living room, Alder glanced at Calyrex, then averted his eyes, took off his backpack and handed over the diagnostic report for Volcarona.

"These are diagnoses from the Pokemon Center about Volcarona's condition. It says the injuries are very severe, and current medical technology cannot cure them."

D reached out to take it and glanced at it, only to realize he couldn't understand it.

However, from the writing on the report, he roughly guessed that Alder's Volcarona should have damage to its very essence, with multiple hidden injuries.

After a brief thought, D sent his sister Indeedee to get Life Sheep Milk, then said to Alder, "Could you let me see Volcarona?"

"Oh, oh!" Alder quickly took out a red-and-white ball from his bag.

With a flash of white light, a large moth-like Pokemon, over 1.6 meters tall with a pair of blue eyes, appeared in the living room.

Though a moth-like Pokemon, Volcarona looked very handsome, with a pair of red antennae on its cheeks, white fur wrapping its upper body, and three pairs of orange-red wings on its back.

The three pairs of wings should be as bright and scorching as blazing flames, but now they appeared dim and dull, even the blue eyes had lost their color.

Volcarona was weak, lying on the floor, without enough strength to fly.

Watching this scene, Alder's eyes turned sour.

Volcarona was the first Pokemon with whom he truly connected, they fought side by side, sharing wonderful moments.

Alder always thought these days would continue forever, but endings always come so abruptly...

Alder sniffled weakly, holding back tears, squatted down gently stroking his old partner.

His tone could not conceal sadness: "Can Volcarona… fly again?"

D shook his head, unsure himself: "Let's give it a try!"

As he spoke, D handed a bottle of Life Sheep Milk to Alder, "Give it to your Volcarona to drink."

Looking at the bottle that emitted a sweet fragrance and creamy liquid, Alder instantly knew what it was.

This is… a bottle of milk?

Alder was stunned, but his hands didn't stop, he fed the fresh milk to Volcarona.

When Volcarona drank the last drop of sheep milk, the next second, something miraculous happened.

A burst of creamy white light suddenly erupted from Volcarona's body, enveloping its form.

In Alder's dazed gaze, Volcarona's eyes gradually regained their spark.

The surrounding space grew hotter, and the six wings on Volcarona's back unfurled like flames, radiating a fiery light.

For a moment, it was as though the Fire God had descended.

Watching this, Alder was utterly shocked, overwhelmed with joy.

Volcarona was recovering!

When the white light faded, Volcarona landed back on the ground.

Though its spirit remained somewhat weak and the body looked frail, its state compared to just moments ago was immeasurably improved!

---

Alder looked at the scene in wonder, uncertainly asking, "Is it… okay now?"

D didn't know either, because although the Life Sheep Milk seemed effective, it was still far from the true Ho-oh Sacred Ash.

After a brief thought, he turned to look at Calyrex behind him.

Calyrex was also drawn to the scene.

D asked, "Calyrex, can you help me check this Pokémon's condition?"

Calyrex nodded slightly.

Under Alder's stunned gaze, Calyrex slowly levitated over and then used telekinesis to carefully examine the condition of this Volcarona.

Calyrex could feel the man's emotions towards Volcarona, which touched its heart. In this situation, it was happy to help them.

Therefore, facing the Volcarona lying on the ground, Calyrex gently raised its hand, scattering soft blue lights.

The blue lights eventually gathered into a glistening blue droplet that slowly dripped onto Volcarona from mid-air.

"Is that Life Dew?" D was seeing this move for the first time.

Life Dew healed Volcarona's hidden injuries, and Volcarona finally fully recovered to a healthy state.

It energetically fluttered its wings, levitating into the air.

Tears already glistened in Alder's eyes.

The tall and rugged man didn't say a word, just stepped forward and tightly hugged his Pokémon partner.

Volcarona's eyes curved into crescents, happily grasping its Trainer with its small feet and adjusting its body temperature to suit humans, acting as a large fluffy and warm pillow for Alder to hug.

Seeing this, D and Calyrex exchanged smiles before turning back to sit on the sofa.

After a long while, Alder sniffed heavily and adjusted his state before sitting back on the sofa, his dark face all red.

"Sorry for making a fool of myself!"

D smiled, realizing Alder had this side: "Just a little help, glad Volcarona is okay."

For him, it was just a bottle of sheep milk, but for Alder, it was of deep significance.

Seeing his expression, Alder was exceedingly serious:

"Regardless, I, Alder, owe you a favor."

Alder furrowed his brow: "If it weren't for you, I might eventually lose Volcarona forever. Having spent years with it, I can feel its life nearing its end…"

Truthfully, Alder was prepared for that day—facing Volcarona's passing, he would lay it to rest at the Celestial Tower in the Unova Region.

D blinked, looking at Alder in front of him, implying something: "I feel like you've changed a lot."

"Changed?" Alder was slightly startled before relaxing: "Perhaps!"

Alder spoke slowly, like having an insight:

"Trainers strive daily to climb to the Champion's throne, representing ultimate strength, but being Champion is just one inevitable path in life, not the end."

"Volcarona's situation made me realize the greatness and unpredictability of life… Nothing remains powerful forever."

Alder chuckled bitterly: "Knowing Volcarona's injuries couldn't be cured with current medical standards made me sad… Because of this, I discovered the joy of being with Pokémon is so profound."

"Indeed!" D agreed.

Whether becoming a Trainer or doing anything else, the most important thing has never been battles or getting stronger, but Pokémon itself.

Then, D chatted a lot with Alder.

He learned from Alder that the Unova League matters are now handled by the Elite Four, and the Unova League is currently collaborating with the Paldea League to fully build Blueberry Academy.

Alder said some gym leaders in the Unova Region are preparing to send their grandchildren to Blueberry Academy for study.

During casual talking, Alder's state and mood slowly recovered.

In a normal state, Alder is very approachable, carefree, optimistic, and humble, completely lacking any Champion's airs.

D found it quite interesting.

However, for some reason, Alder seemed to mistake him for a fan and even wanted to give him a bunch of signed Poke Balls…

"Hahaha!"

Alder felt D's attitude towards Pokémon resonated well with him, so he asked D for a pen, then took out Poke Balls from his backpack, starting to sign them.

"These small gifts are for you as decorations!" Alder said.

D: "?"

He quickly realized his actions seeking Alder's help through Leon were mistaken by Alder for fandom!

Yes! Who would do such a thing if not a fan!

In Alder's eyes, he might be seen as a fan who greatly admires him.

As a fan, he constantly follows Alder, learns that Alder's main Pokémon Volcarona was gravely injured, and immediately seeks ways to find Geeta through Leon to help Alder.

"…"

D, for a moment, didn't know what to say.

He wasn't a fan of Alder!

Alder was deeply touched and placed the pile of Poke Balls on the coffee table, eagerly patting D's shoulder, saying to D:

---

"If you ever need help in the future, don't hesitate to ask! I'll be there as soon as I can!"

D: "..."

He silently swallowed his words because, beyond this reason, D didn't know how to explain to Alder how he knew him or that he knew about Volcarona's severe injuries.

However, Alder still felt unsatisfied: "Is there anything you want? If I can do it, I'll certainly help you get it!"

"No." D shook his head.

Speaking of the Unova Region... What concerned him more was that Hydreigon.

Zekrom, Reshiram, Kyurem.

But even though Alder is the Champion, he probably doesn't know about these three Legendary Pokémon.

At this moment, Alder suddenly noticed the newspaper that D placed on the table.

He asked somewhat unexpectedly, "Are you very interested in this Rotom Technology Company?"

"Not really!" D was stunned for a moment, taking quite a while before realizing what Alder was talking about.

He honestly said, "I just have an idea about a communication device I'd like to discuss with them."

"Oh? A communication device? Like a video phone?" Although Alder wasn't interested, since D was, he had to ask more—perhaps he could help in some way?

Seeing Alder's curiosity, D thought for a moment and felt this wasn't something he couldn't talk about.

So, he took the pen Alder used for signing and sketched a smartphone on a piece of paper.

"I'm thinking, could we merge video phones and computer networks together? Create a small smart communication device like this, equipped with the Pokedex function inside, which would be very portable. By then, everyone could carry one and contact others anytime."

Alder pondered that this was basically the Unova Region's Pokedex, right?

Some trainers in the Unova Region receive a Pokedex from the local professor when they set out, and that Pokedex includes communication functions, allowing them to contact the professor anytime.

Alder didn't quite understand, but he could help with this.

"I don't understand these things, but I have a way for you to meet the owner of Rotom Technology Company!" Alder said.

"Huh?"

Alder grinned: "There will soon be a banquet held in the Unova Region, inviting famous billionaires, entrepreneurs, and powerful trainers from all over the world. Following past practices, the owner of Rotom Technology Company will also be present."

"I know him, I suppose!" Alder recalled and said, "A few years ago, I visited, and his little daughter really loved Pokémon battles."

"Every time she wore a cute dress at the banquet and challenged the trainers, Gym Leaders, and Elites present—I also battled that little girl!"

Hearing this, D was momentarily stunned.

This little girl, could it be referring to Nemona?

Alder looked at him: "Just in time, I will invite you in my name to that banquet and introduce you to that guy."

Though Alder didn't know D before, the young man in front of him not only knew Leon but also had many powerful Pokémon at his side.

The Champion's intuition told Alder that the scarlet muscular Pokémon in the living room and the one that just used Life Dew to heal Volcarona must be extremely powerful.

He is certainly qualified to attend that banquet.

---

---

---

---

# 339: Chapter 336: Rookie Trainers and the Grass Trial

Honestly, when it came to attending the wealthy banquet, D's heart was somewhat anxious.

In his past life, he was just a mere office worker, having never been to such gatherings full of big-shot elites.

But in order to experience smartphones soon, D decided to go and see what kind of banquet the upper class of the Pokemon World held.

D's mind was a whirl of thoughts, yet his face remained calm.

Alder continued happily, "Come to think of it, I haven't been to one in a while either, but don't worry, we'll go together when the time comes!"

"Thank you, Alder," D said politely.

Alder cheerfully patted his shoulder, "No need to be so polite with me, hehe. Those kinds of events have lots of food you can't find outside; the big bosses are always talking business, while trainers like me take Volcarona around to eat."

D: "..."

After thinking for a moment, he asked, "What about the time?"

Alder carefully recalled, "Early April! Either the first or the second, today is March 23rd, we have eight days left."

D nodded, that was fine, he could comfortably attend the horse race.

Alder: "I'll be staying in the Paldea Region during this time, and then we'll fly to Unova together!"

"Great!" D invited, "Why not stay at the ranch? The town is planning a horse race, Mr. Alder can relax there."

"Alright, it's settled then!"

With Volcarona safe and sound, the weight was finally lifted from Alder's heart, allowing him to enjoy a tour of his benefactor's ranch.

Seeing his interest, D took Alder outside for a stroll around the ranch.

The ranch wasn't very big, but it felt very fulfilling.

Neatly arranged furrows had crops growing robustly.

Several Gogoat leisurely sunbathing on the grass.

Lots of Pokemon not found in the Unova Region were chasing each other around playfully, their mouths filled with merry laughter.

Gazing at the well-kept ranch and the Pokemon, Alder remarked:

"So happy!"

Humans and Pokemon living happily together, this was truly the life he dreamed of.

D raised an eyebrow: "Oh? Mr. Alder wants to be a rancher too?"

To be honest, Alder was tempted.

Nothing in this world lasts forever; Pokemon lives will eventually come to an end.

Maybe they won't accompany each other to the very end, but the time and memories shared with Alder are eternal happiness.

Because of this, they must live in the present and cherish every moment.

However, unfortunately, these thoughts were just dreams for Alder.

Because the Unova Region was unstable...

Thinking of this, Alder sighed and shook his head, "Though I envy it, as Champion of the Unova League, the champion's duty is to hope for and guard the happiness of humans and Pokemon."

So, once he returned to Unova, he planned to continue his travels.

Meeting more trainers, teaching them to maintain relationships with Pokemon, building bonds between humans and Pokemon.

D knew this would be the answer; thinking of the Plasma Team in the Unova Region, D encouraged Alder, "Good luck, Mr. Alder!"

"Mm!" Alder nodded with a smile, he certainly wouldn't let his fans down!

At this moment, several shadows suddenly flew across the sky.

Alder looked up and saw several Dragonite carrying satchels landing in the ranch.

After finishing a day's deliveries, they happily found their buddy Indeedee.

They received their Poffin from Indeedee, and ran to sit on the grass, enjoying the delicious Poffin while sunbathing.

Gazing at the Dragonite feasting away, Alder's eyes widened.

Just then, five Dragonair flew from somewhere, approaching the Dragonite, wanting to taste the Poffin too.

"!!!"

Alder's head filled with a big exclamation mark: "So, so many Dragonite!"

One, two, three...

Counting the one he just saw, there were actually four Dragonite and five Dragonair on this ranch!

Alder was greatly shocked: "Those, those Dragonite are your Pokemon?"

Goodness, so many Dragonite!

As far as he knew, even Lance, the Dragon Elite from the Kanto Region, didn't have that many Dragonite!

"Well, not all of them," D explained, "They're Pokemon I hired; currently working on the ranch, helping deliver orders from customers to the town."

Though these gluttonous Dragonite certainly had thoughts of becoming his Pokemon...

However, hearing this, Alder's disbelief only intensified, "Deliveries?"

"Yes." D nodded.

Alder was momentarily speechless.

Thinking of the Pokemon he just saw, D clearly wasn't just an ordinary farmer...

---

Alder seemed to have thought of something amusing and said to D:

"I really wonder what Lance's expression would be when he sees this scene."

D: "..."

Since Lance is originally from the Dragon Taming Clan, having Dragonite help deliver things probably isn't surprising to him, right?

But as a trainer from the Paldea Region, D was far from the Kanto Region, and Lance's name couldn't reach here.

Therefore, D pretended not to know Lance.

As a result, seeing this expression, Alder grew more interested and began telling him about Lance's exploits.

"Lance is the Champion of the Kanto Region, born into the Dragon Taming Clan in Blackthorn City, and he's very skilled at using Dragon Type Pokemon."

"Among them, Dragonite is the representative Pokemon of the Dragon Taming Clan, though Dragonite is incredibly rare; there aren't that many even within the clan."

"It's said that initially, Lance used Pokemon like Gyarados, Charizard, and Aerodactyl, which have 'dragon' in their names."

"If the elders of the Dragon Taming Clan knew that your ranch was home to so many Dragonite, they'd certainly be amazed. In comparison, your place is the true Dragon's Holy Sanctuary!" Alder exclaimed.

D: "..."

"Mr. Alder, do you know Lance?" D asked.

Alder nodded: "He attended a banquet before, and I talked to him for a bit."

D suddenly understood: "I see!"

Alder looked at the group of Dragonite and other Pokemon in the ranch and suddenly changed the topic: "But speaking of which, you're a Champion, aren't you?"

No, D shook his head: "The Champion of the Paldea Region has always been Top Champion Geeta."

"Ah?" Alder was a bit surprised; did he see it wrong?

But then, Alder remembered that the Paldea League was different from other regions.

Aside from the regional Champion, there were many Champion-level trainers here.

And D before him, was likely one of them once, who eventually grew tired of the Champion's lifestyle and chose to retire, bringing his Pokemon to this place to run the ranch.

Alder instinctively felt he had discovered the truth, and he felt more and more that he was kindred spirits with D.

In attitude and views towards Pokemon.

Not focusing on Pokemon battles, but on the happy times living together with Pokemon.

Just then, a new trainer holding a Pichu came to the ranch.

"Excuse me! Is this D's ranch?" The girl asked expectantly.

D was a little startled, then glanced at her Naranja Academy uniform and nodded: "I'm D."

The girl's eyes lit up, and then she said with determination: "Great! Mr. D, Pichu and I are here to participate in the Grass Trial at the ranch!"

For such scenarios, D was not surprised, as students passing through would challenge, and the ranch's trials gradually became known.

Therefore, he asked: "How many badges have you collected?"

The girl was a bit embarrassed: "Well... Pichu and I just entered school this year and haven't started Gym challenges yet, but don't worry Mr. D! We've gone through hellish training at the academy, so there won't be any problem!"

D kept a straight face: "No, you do not qualify to participate in the trial."

The girl instantly wilted like a frost-beaten eggplant.

As early as last year, D Ranch's Grass Trial started gaining fame within the academy and rumors began circulating.

It was said that there was a place called D Ranch in a remote countryside town.

The rancher there was a very strong Champion-level trainer, who set up extremely difficult trials at the ranch.

It was said the difficulty of these trials was even higher than the Champion League challenge of the Paldea League.

But unlike those, the purpose of the trials at D Ranch was not to win but to train, to help Pokemon improve strength and hone battle skills.

Moreover, those who could pass the trials were said to receive a mysterious gift from the trial's owner.

Many students came over to participate in the trials, but they were so hard that none had succeeded.

Later, Mr. D even added conditions to the trials, stating that a certain number of badges must be collected to participate.

For this reason, everyone speculated whether Mr. D was a mysterious Champion-level trainer.

To battle against a Champion-level trainer, many trainers came, and being able to participate in the trial became a point of pride for some students.

Like "I participated in the Grass Trial! Sustained for thirty seconds against that Gogoat!"

"Hmph, just a minute! I lasted a full minute!"

"Me too, I joined the Thunder Trial and successfully dodged several attacks from a Champion-level Pokemon, impressive right?"

Yet even so, some who came to the ranch to challenge were rejected by Mr. D.

Because their skills didn't meet the requirements.

Initially, these trainers were quite disheartened, but they worked hard to collect Gym Badges.

---

But eventually, a few Trainers discovered a way to challenge the Grass Trial, which was to act pitifully in front of Mr. D and express their desire to battle powerful Pokemon, telling him they wanted to experience the legendary Grass Trial.

In such cases, Mr. D, faced with the enthusiastic challengers, would relent and allow them to try.

Thinking of this, the girl pleaded, "Please Mr. D, let us try! Let us witness the strength of Champion level Pokemon, Pichu and I will work hard to reach that level!"

D felt a headache coming on, here we go again.

He softened his stance, "It's not that I don't want you to challenge, it's just that if the disparity between Pokemon's strength is too great, there's a risk of serious injury."

He had Life Sheep Milk here, so there wouldn't be any problem, but if these Trainers went out into the wild with such a mindset to challenge stronger Pokemon, that would be dangerous.

The girl, full of fighting spirit, said, "Don't worry! I've prepared all kinds of healing potions!"

The little Pichu in her arms also jumped onto the grass, clenching its tiny paws with determination, weak currents of electricity coursing through its cheeks, "Pi—chu!"

D: "..."

He sighed helplessly and said, "Alright, I'll let you try, but remember to stay within your limits, understood?"

"Yay!" The girl jumped up with joy, then high-fived Pichu.

D turned and went to the battle arena, calling out a Gogoat, and said to it, "Play with this little Pichu for a bit!"

Gogoat looked at the tiny Pichu, then nodded, "Baa~"

The traveling girl came to challenge!

The girl sent out Pichu!

Gogoat used Grassy Terrain, and Pichu was overwhelmed by the grass, leaving Pichu dumbfounded.

The bewildered Pichu used Thunder Shock, but it didn't hit Gogoat.

Gogoat used Vine Whip, entangling Pichu and sending it flying.

Seeing Pichu about to hit the ground, Gogoat caught it again with Vine Whip, repeating the cycle.

This battle, D did not provide any commands.

He observed from the side, and Alder watched the scene with interest.

Based on the previous conversation, he roughly guessed D's intentions, so he spoke up:

"You're helping these novice Trainers strengthen their skills."

D showed an awkward but polite smile, thinking that wasn't really it, he just wanted the challengers to help his Pokemon hone their battle techniques and enhance their strength.

Unexpectedly, most of these challengers turned out to be rookie Trainers…

If you put it in a game, those Pichu and Caterpie probably wouldn't exceed level 8.

A level 8 Pichu vs. a level 60 Gogoat.

It's like challenging a final boss just after leaving the beginner's village.

At this moment, the battle was coming to an end.

Gogoat's Vine Whip was like a huge trampoline, and soon, Pichu was knocked unconscious.

The battle ended with Pichu losing its ability to fight.

"Pichu!" The girl quickly ran up to check Pichu's condition and found that it wasn't injured, just passed out.

D stepped forward, finding it strange that several Trainers used the same words to challenge the Grass Trial recently.

To uncover the truth, he directly asked the girl in front of him.

"Secrète Marseille!"

The girl, somewhat embarrassed, bowed before explaining:

"That… that's because all the first-year students think battling a Champion level Pokemon is an impressive feat! Since we rarely see Champion level Trainers in our daily lives, everyone wants to see a Champion's Pokemon… and those who successfully challenge the Grass Trial would be admired by everyone..."

D immediately understood what was going on.

He said with a face full of helplessness, "I am very welcoming for you to visit Gogoat and Pawmo, but using battles with a Champion Pokemon as a way to show off is not right."

The girl blushed, not because she was shy, but because she felt a bit ashamed.

Beside him, Alder nodded in agreement and said, "That's right, if you want to battle a Champion's Pokemon, then work hard together with your Pokemon, one day, you too will become new Champions!"

The girl looked up dumbfoundedly at the man in front of her, then nodded heavily, "Yes!"

D handed a few bottles of MooMoo Fresh Milk emblazoned with the D Ranch logo to the girl, "This is a special fresh milk from the ranch."

"Eh?" The girl was a little overwhelmed.

D smiled and said, "I hope when you go back to school, you can help me tell everyone, before collecting six badges, it's better not to come challenge the Trial yet."

"Because if there's too big a difference in strength between Pokemon, it might cause irreversible damage during battle, and worse, some Pokemon might lose their lives and never be able to battle again."

The girl listened dumbfounded.

D gently asked, "Do you really like Pokemon?"

The girl finally came to her senses, looked at the Pichu in her arms, and seriously nodded, "Yes! I love Pokemon and want to live with Pichu always!"

D said with a smile, "Then, move forward with your feet on the ground! Take it and explore every corner of this world! Once you have enough strength, I will have a serious battle with you, until then, work hard!"

The girl looked serious, holding the bottles of milk, and nodded, "Yes, I understand, thank you, Mr. D!"

---

---

---

---

# 340: Chapter 337: The Clash Between Glastrier and Spectrier

March 24, 199 in the League calendar, the day before the horse racing event, Keri's construction concluded.

The pasture had expanded several times over, surrounded by a neat and brand new wooden fence, officially marking this piece of land as part of the ranch.

A river cut through the ranch, splitting the pasture into two.

An sturdy stone bridge stood over the river, connecting these two distinct zones.

That group of Psyduck, usually living on the opposite side of the river, were attracted to the stone bridge, and bounced their way over, holding their heads with their hands, wearing a dazed expression.

One Psyduck stepped onto the bridge and walked across.

To its surprise, it discovered that it could get to the other side of the river this way!

"Quack Quack!"

This Psyduck let out an excited cry, dashed across the bridge, jumped into the river, and made its way back across the water, then continued to dash back onto the bridge.

The other Psyducks saw this and started imitating their companion, as if they'd found a new toy.

D, who was overseeing the area with Alder, couldn't help but twitch his mouth at this sight.

"Hahaha!" Alder laughed heartily, "These Psyducks are truly adorable!"

D sighed helplessly and rubbed his forehead.

The two continued surveying the area, arriving at a slope, where Alder gazed around and felt how beautiful the pastoral landscape was.

Above them stretched an endless sky of blue and white clouds, beneath them was vibrant greenery, with the wind casting the huge pasture into a rhythm, like a wave of green.

D observed the surroundings, deciding to reserve the slope for grazing Wooloo.

Wooloo, a type of Pokemon, loves to roll, and having such a spot means they can roll down from this slope for fun.

The ranch in Porto Marinada Town had Wooloo for sale, and D planned to purchase a batch.

However, Tropius, another type of Pokemon, was no longer for sale in Porto Marinada Town.

Because raising Tropius yields less profit than it costs, it's a loss trade for the rancher.

After all, Tropius's neck-grown bananas yield twice a year, but the growth cycle is too slow.

This meant, if he wanted to keep Tropius, he would have to go into the wild and capture them himself.

However, all this needed to wait until after the horse racing event, once he returned from the Unova Region.

In the afternoon, in preparation for tomorrow's horse racing event, D began special horse training.

Upon hearing his plan, Calyrex willingly lent out his beloved horse.

Taking someone's hand means taking it with grace, so both Glastrier and Spectrier understandingly snorted before D, allowing him to choose.

Neither showed any resistance, not only because D provided them carrots every day, but also because his strength had gained their respect.

After all, even Calyrex recognized this human; not to mention the constantly wandering Koraidon—what more was there to say?

Faced with the obedient horse Pokémon, D reached out and patted their heads, asking:

"Which one of you wants to join me in the horse racing event? Compete against other horse Pokémon, become the strongest horse Pokémon in the world?"

Spectrier remained eerily silent, expressing little reaction.

Whereas Glastrier bulged its eyes, snorting aggressively.

The strongest horse Pokémon? Isn't that already it? Does this even need competition?

Moreover, when they galloped through the Crown Tundra, those fellows were probably not even born yet!

"Ice—Hiss!" Glastrier let out a long whistle.

D: "I know you're amazing, but others don't know yet! If you participate in the horse racing event, many will witness your prowess; if you defeat the other horse Pokémon and become the top, people will see you as the strongest horse Pokémon in the world!"

Glastrier imagined that scenario.

—It stood on the podium, surrounded by humans with adoring eyes, excitedly shouting: "The most formidable Glastrier King in the world! You're incredible! We'll build a shrine for you!

Upon hearing this, Glastrier couldn't stand it any longer.

It proclaimed loudly: "Ice—Hiss!"

It was going to participate!

D chuckled: "Then we'll count on you, Glastrier?"

Glastrier nodded vigorously.

But just then, Spectrier beside it wasn't happy.

It discontentedly glared at Glastrier: "Dark Howl—Hiss!"

Glastrier gave Spectrier a sharp look, what's up?

Spectrier glowed dimly all over, its aura suddenly escalating—this opportunity should belong to it!

Glastrier, quick to ignite, equally released its power with defiance.

Icy air spread, Glastrier's side of the stable was swiftly coated in frost visibly.

Alder, witnessing this scene, raised his brow and murmured: "Your two horse Pokémon are about to fight!"

D was immediately struck with a headache.

Seeing the two horses about to clash, he urgently intervened: "Stop it now!"

Glastrier and Spectrier ignored him, maintaining their intimidating standoff.

D: "If this continues, we'll have no carrots for the next week!"

Upon hearing this, the two horses instantly shifted their gazes, though the matter had not ended, as their eyes settled on D.

---

It's quite evident from his look that he's wondering whom to choose for the horse racing festival.

D: "..."

Wait, isn't the horse racing festival supposed to allow only one horse?

D lightly coughed and, after a brief moment of thought, said, "In that case, let's decide by drawing lots! Even if you don't get chosen, it's fine. There will be another horse racing festival next year, and you can go then."

Drawing lots...

Glastrier and Spectrier reluctantly accepted.

Seeing this, D returned to his room and prepared two white bowls.

He placed a Pecha Berry in one bowl and an Oran Berry in the other, then placed the bowls upside down in front of the two horses.

"Make your choice! Whoever picks the Pecha Berry will qualify for the horse racing festival. The one who picks the Oran Berry can participate next year!" D said.

Upon hearing this, Spectrier and Glastrier widened their eyes and stared intently at the two identical bowls.

They both displayed clear hesitation on their faces, neither wanting to make the first decision, afraid of picking the wrong one.

After a while, Spectrier made its decision.

It gently lifted its hoof and placed it on the bowl on the right.

Seeing this scene, Glastrier immediately snorted with dissatisfaction and nudged Spectrier aside; it also wanted to choose that bowl!

D was at a loss for words.

"No snatching. Everything should be done in order. Spectrier made the choice first, so the bowl on the right is theirs!"

Thus, Glastrier could only let go of its spot angrily.

D opened the upside-down bowl, revealing a pink Pecha Berry inside.

Then D opened the bowl on the left, which contained a blue Oran Berry.

With that, the answer was clear.

The Pokemon that qualified for the horse racing festival was—Spectrier!

"Congratulations, Spectrier! And Glastrier, don't be discouraged; it'll be your turn next year," D said.

"Neigh~oo!" Spectrier couldn't hide its excitement.

Glastrier was very upset, snorting and turning its head away from D, refusing to play with him.

D: "..."

Alder, struggling to contain his laughter, said, "Your Pokemon truly has a unique personality!"

D was momentarily at a loss for words.

As he returned the bowls to the kitchen, Calyrex, who was watching TV, casually asked, "Whom did you choose?"

"Spectrier," D responded.

Calyrex nodded slightly, "Spectrier's speed does surpass Glastrier's; with it, you can rest assured."

After taking a few steps, D suddenly felt curious and asked:

"By the way, Calyrex, when you were racing with Spectrier and Glastrier in the Galar Land, would one of them get upset if you rode the other?"

This question surprised Calyrex a bit.

After a moment of reflection, they shook their head and said, "I never encountered such a situation. When I was riding one, the other would stay at my abode, awaiting my return."

Is that so? D pondered and then said, "Spectrier drew the lot for the horse racing festival, and Glastrier got upset."

Calyrex couldn't help but chuckle, "I've never encountered such a situation before; it seems my beloved horse cares quite a bit about you!"

D: "..."

Calyrex's face showed a reminiscing expression, "My beloved horses are not the same."

"Spectrier likes calmness, is secluded and cold, and rarely interacts with other Pokemon."

"Glastrier has always had a direct and aggressive nature; when it encounters something it wants, it'll snatch it by force. But now it's only a bit angry, which truly surprises me!"

D wondered if it might be because of the presence of Koraidon?

But thinking of Glastrier's angry look just now, he still felt a bit guilty.

So he went to Glaceon's ice cellar to gather a basket of Iceroot Carrots to put in Glastrier's trough for an extra treat.

Glastrier was still angry; it was doing fine originally, but as soon as it saw him approach, it turned around immediately, showing its rear to him, ignoring him.

D: "..."

Forget it!

He led Spectrier out of the stable to an open field across the river and, holding Calyrex's Reins of Unity, mounted it.

Riding felt somewhat different than riding Koraidon and Cyclizar.

Spectrier's form was large, and if it were to run at full speed, an inexperienced rider could easily fall off.

Though humans in the Pokemon World have unique constitutions and aren't easily killed from falls, things like cuts and bleeding are still common.

D hadn't known how to ride a horse before, having been just a corporate worker in his past life, living in a concrete jungle, with no chance to engage in horseback riding or experience the great plains.

All his riding skills were learned from Calyrex.

Right when the two horses first arrived at the ranch.

By tightening his legs on the horse's belly and pulling the reins, Spectrier automatically began to trot, running across the grassland.

Horse racing festivals in the Pokemon World are quite unique, as Pokemon are allowed to use moves during the race, D pondered while explaining the rules to Spectrier.

"In that race, many Horse-type Pokemon will participate, and during running, they're allowed to use moves."
