"So be cautious, while progressing along the racecourse, you'll need to avoid opponents' attacks."

---

With a neigh from Spectrier's mouth, it indicated it understood.

At this point, the moves mastered by Spectrier appeared in D's mind.

Double Kick, Hex, Stomp, Confuse Ray, Haze, Shadow Ball, Agility, Double-Edge, Disable, Bulldoze, Night Shade, Poltergeist...

"Double Kick can fend off approaching opponents, Confuse Ray can disrupt them, Bulldoze can lower nearby Pokemon's speed, Haze can clear buffs on Pokemon." D contemplated.

Coupled with Spectrier's running speed, he felt his chance of winning was quite high.

Time ticked by; D and Spectrier trained together for an afternoon. By the end, he led Spectrier back to the stable.

As he was about to leave, D found the basket of icy carrots had been eaten entirely by Glastrier.

Still upset, Glastrier kept snorting, causing a ruckus in the stable.

So D brought another basket of icy carrots to appease Glastrier.

"This guy really is..."

D momentarily didn't know what to say.

He sighed helplessly and turned back to the living room.

After dinner, D finished making a berry platter for Farfetch'd and sat with Alder on the sofa watching TV.

Speaking of Farfetch'd, it was quite unlucky; a long time had passed, and it constantly tried to understand moves, only to forget them again.

The unluckiest time, it even lost all its moves, becoming a "blank slate" Farfetch'd.

Yet the harsher this became, the more Farfetch'd refused to give up.

Every day after finishing its berry platter, it carried its leek over to the wooden training dummy and began practicing, hitting the dummy now and again.

Today, it went there to train again.

D didn't know when this path for Farfetch'd would finally end.

Just as he worried about Farfetch'd, Ogerpon excitedly ran over with bright eyes.

Upon hearing D was going to town tomorrow to participate in an event, Ogerpon nervously yet eagerly looked at him: "Bonnie?"

D came back to his senses, naturally understanding Ogerpon's thoughts, and smiled while nodding: "Of course, you can go too."

"Bonnie!" Ogerpon was extremely happy.

D: "I'm going to participate in the tournament with Spectrier, so you stick close to Koraidon. Mr. Alder, please do take care of them at that time."

Alder patted his chest assuredly: "Don't worry! By the way, is Ogerpon also a Paldea Region Pokemon?"

"No." D shook his head: "I encountered Ogerpon in Kitakami. It wasn't happy living there, so I brought it back."

"There really are many kinds of Pokemon living in this world!" Alder exclaimed.

After speaking, Alder took Volcarona and the others to follow the Indeedee Sibling into the Pokemon Tavern to drink.

Meanwhile, D returned to his room, sat at the desk, and began writing plans for smartphones in his notebook.

In his past life, he worked at a gaming company and had some exposure to this type of content.

So in order to play with smartphones soon, D drew his impression of Phone Rotom on paper.

He marked the locations for touchscreens, cameras, and other structures, as well as the prospects of smartphones and the features they should have.

Accompanied by the development of the times, those old-fashioned Pokedexes are destined to face elimination.

Moreover, compared to the Pokedex, the all-purpose Rotom Phone is more suited for trainers on their travels.

Whether it's the Pokedex function included, the camera feature, the online forum function, navigation function, or communication function, all are things the traditional Pokedex can't accomplish.

Ten years later, the internet will connect the entire Pokemon World, allowing trainers from the Paldea Region to communicate with trainers far in the Sinnoh Region.

---

---

---

---

# 341: Chapter 338: Horse Racing!

League Calendar Year 199, March 25th, the horse racing festival officially began.

Early in the morning, D brought a few Pokemon and Alder to the venue.

Though it was still early, by the time D arrived, quite a few people and Pokemon had already gathered on the field.

Trainers passing by like Ash, students wearing Paldea Academy uniforms, townsfolk here to watch, and so on.

The field was brightly decorated and bustling with excitement.

A banner hung at the entrance read "Porto Marinada Town Spring Horse Racing Festival," with stalls selling race coupons not far away, and behind them, a variety of small prizes and items, all exchangeable with the coupons.

Seeing this scene, Alder couldn't help but exclaim, "Wow! It's really lively here!"

D nodded with a smile, "Yeah! The horse racing festival here in Porto Marinada Town is quite famous, attracting many trainers to participate every year around this time."

This event is akin to the bug-catching contests held in small towns.

"It's a pity, if only I had a horse-type Pokemon by my side, I'd want to sign up and give it a try!" Alder lamented with a look of regret.

D recalled the Pokemon Alder had.

Accelgor, Bouffalant, Druddigon, Vanilluxe, Escavalier, Volcarona.

It's a shame there's no cow racing, or Alder could participate riding his Bouffalant.

"Bonny..."

Following beside D, Ogerpon looked timidly at the people around.

D patted its head, soothingly said, "Don't be afraid, no one here dislikes you."

No sooner had he finished speaking than several young trainers, intrigued by the never-before-seen Pokemon Koraidon and Ogerpon, approached them.

"Wow! A never-seen-before Pokemon!"

One of the trainers took out a Pokedex to scan it, only to receive a response indicating no related data in the Pokedex.

Another trainer scanned the Pawmo with a red cape floating in mid-air, and the Pokedex provided an introduction:

"Pawmot, the Hand Press Pokemon. Although usually slow in movement, when compelled to face battle, it defeats enemies with lightning speed. Its fluffy fur serves as a battery, capable of storing electricity equivalent to an electric vehicle."

"Pawmot?!" the trainer exclaimed in surprise.

He looked wide-eyed with astonishment at the Pokemon before him, so different from his previous impression.

Its pink fur, silver metal hammers, and bright red cape bestowed a dashing presence...

Could this truly be the Pawmot he knew?!

More importantly, it was actually flying... how is that possible?!

Noticing the astonished and puzzled gaze from the other side, Pawmot greeted them warmly:

"Bamo~"

Then it clenched its fists and soared into the sky like a superhero.

"So impressive!!!"

The two trainers, who hadn't witnessed such a scene, exclaimed in excitement.

"Sir, are you the trainer of these Pokemon?" the young man on the left asked admiringly.

D nodded slightly, "That's right, this one is Koraidon, and this one is Ogerpon, a Grass-type Pokemon."

"Koraidon?"

"Ogerpon?"

The youth murmured the names of the two Pokemon.

They looked at the tall Pokemon with a crimson body and a strong muscular physique.

Koraidon stared back fiercely.

The aura of the fierce beast suddenly spread, startling the two young men.

"What a frightening Pokemon!"

D was somewhat puzzled, glanced at Koraidon, and reached out to it.

Koraidon immediately approached obediently, like a big dog.

"Agah sis~"

"Not scary at all!" D remarked.

The youngsters swallowed nervously, thinking that the Pokemon's gaze at them just now was different...

Alder, standing aside, tried hard to stifle his laughter but eventually couldn't help it and burst out laughing.

"Hahahahaha!"

Koraidon is such an amusing Pokemon!

The young trainers, too scared to look at Koraidon, turned their attention to Ogerpon.

Detecting their gaze, Ogerpon hid behind D.

D asked, "Do you find this Pokemon scary?"

The two youths shook their heads vigorously, "Not scary at all."

"You're not afraid of it?" D asked again.

The young trainers hesitated, sensing the man was confirming something with them.

One of them asked curiously, "Why should we be afraid?"

With a smile, D said, "Nothing, Ogerpon, did you hear? No one is afraid of you or dislikes you."

"Bonny..." Ogerpon looked around at the lively crowd, feeling a warm surge within, and happily bounced twice, "Bonny!"

The two young trainers felt a stirring within, "Sir, could you have a Pokemon battle with us?"

D glanced at the people registering there. As he arrived early and there was still a while before the festival began, he decided to entertain these trainers first.

When D asked which Pokemon they wished to battle against, both chose Koraidon.

For no other reason than it looked powerful, and battling it would surely be thrilling!

---

So, D directed Koraidon to play with the two youths.

They seemed to have been traveling for some time, as the Fuecoco and Sprigatito by their side had both evolved to their second stage.

But even so, they were no match in front of Koraidon.

D wanted to entertain them, so he didn't rush to end the battle, turning real-time into a turn-based style you-go-I-go play.

Once both of their Pokemon were exhausted, D stopped and bid them farewell, preparing to head to the registration booth.

Alder remained on the spot, patting his chest as he promised, "Go ahead! I'll take care of the Pawmots!"

D smiled and nodded, then turned and walked toward the registration booth.

On the way, he encountered Allen, who was looking for him everywhere.

As soon as he saw him, Allen excitedly asked, "Have you registered?"

D: "Just about to!"

Allen patted his shoulder: "Good buddy, it's all on you!"

D: "… How many horse race tickets did you buy?"

Allen chuckled: "Not much, just ten thousand League coins' worth!"

D replied speechlessly, "Aren't you afraid of losing it all?"

Allen suddenly became serious: "That's because I believe in you!"

Then he said pitifully, "You wouldn't treat me that way, would you?"

D: "…"

He pushed Allen's face away: "Stop!"

Allen stepped back and laughed: "So it's settled. I'll wait for you in the stands, do your best!"

After saying this, Allen hopped away.

D sighed helplessly.

This guy…

He turned and walked into the registration booth.

"Name." The white-bearded old man in charge of registration looked up.

"D."

"Which horse-type Pokemon are you participating with?"

"Spectrier."

Upon hearing the name, the old man's hand paused slightly, and he looked up in surprise, "Spectrier? What kind of horse is that?"

"Uh…" D wasn't sure how to explain, so he thought for a moment and replied, "It's a Ghost-type horse Pokemon from the Galar Region."

"Oh, so it's a Pokemon from another region!" the old man realized. "Can I have a look at it?"

"Of course." D nodded and took out Spectrier's Poke Ball to release it.

With a flash of white light, a horse-type Pokemon with an entirely black body, blue mane, and light blue tail, with a misty vapor-like aura around its limbs, appeared in front of the registration booth.

Seeing this, the old man's eyes widened, and he rushed forward, looking at Spectrier with excitement and murmuring:

"Such a strong body, such a graceful stance, such jet-black fur, what a perfect horse! In all my years of raising horses, I've never seen such a perfect horse-type Pokemon!"

D thought to himself that of course, this one was once the ruler of the Crown Tundra, though later tamed by Calyrex. But Spectrier is incomparable to ordinary horse-type Pokemon.

"It's called Spectrier?", the old man asked.

"Yes."

The old man nodded: "Got it, I'll remember its name. There's still an hour before the event starts; you're free to go! I hope you can ride it to a perfect performance in the event!"

"The holding area for participants is in the back; collect your attire and track number there and wait."

"Alright, thank you." D politely expressed his gratitude and continued forward with Spectrier.

After passing through a tiered viewing stand, they arrived at an open field.

At this moment, many contestants had already gathered there.

They had also released their horse-type Pokemon, feeding them cubed sugar while gently stroking their heads.

D first collected his number card and participant attire.

Both were number 06.

This meant they would be competing on track number six later.

D led Spectrier to rest on a quiet patch of grass, casting his gaze toward the other contestants not far away.

Including himself, there were a total of eight contestants in this horse race event.

Each had a different horse-type Pokemon.

There was a fiery and majestic Rapidash with flames dancing across its body, a Zebstrika with black and white stripes, a tall and sturdy Mudsdale, and a sandy Hippopotas...

To D's surprise, there was even a relatively immature Ponyta among them.

The owner of the Ponyta was a young boy, and the little Ponyta seemed quite unimpressive compared to the tall and mighty horse-type Pokemon around it.

But compared to that sandy Hippopotas, a Ponyta was still considered a normal horse-type Pokemon.

But there was nothing to be done—horse-type Pokemon were incredibly scarce in the Pokemon World.

It was already impressive that these trainers didn't bring a Horsea...

Time ticked away, second by second.

Gradually, all the seats in the stands were filled, and the sound of fireworks exploded, sweeping in like a tidal wave of cheers from the crowd.

Witnessing this scene, D felt a tinge of surprise.

Because the horse racing event appeared to be much more popular this year than the previous year!

He clearly remembered that the stands were not full last year...

Times have changed, and it seemed that Porto Marinada Town was flourishing more and more.

Among the audience, there would surely be many who had come upon hearing the legend of Calyrex.

Just like how Kitakami used the ghost legend as a gimmick to attract tourists and develop tourism.

At the sight of this, even Spectrier, whose usual eyes were as serene as a still well, expressed a hint of excitement.

So many humans… so many Pokemon…

Soon, it would be crowned the King of Horses before the eyes of all these people and Pokemon!

"Are you ready?" D asked, donned in protective gear as he turned around.

Spectrier let out a screeching whinny.

"Then, let's go!"

D mounted up and, along with the other participants, entered the field one by one on horseback.

"Welcome everyone to this year's horse racing event in Porto Marinada Town! I'm Jinhei, this year's event commentator!"

"Next, I'll be your guide through this exciting race!"

"Now, everyone can see, our participants are all in position! First entering the field is our number one contestant!"

"The number one contestant's partner is a Rapidash, oh wow, look at that Rapidash, how handsome and elegant! Those blazing flames, just like the enthusiasm of all you spectators!"

Once the number one contestant reached their position, the number two contestant followed closely.

"Next is our number two contestant; it looks like our number two contestant is quite unique! Their partner is actually a slow-moving Hippopotas! But since Hippopotas does indeed have 'horse' in its name, let's look forward to what this contestant and their partner will showcase!"

The stands erupted into laughter, clearly not optimistic about the slow Hippopotas.

Then, the third contestant, fourth contestant, and fifth contestant entered the field one after another.

Soon, it was D's turn to enter the field with Spectrier.

"Now… entering the field is our number six contestant, huh? What kind of Pokemon is that?"

The commentator was momentarily stunned, unable to recognize the Pokemon's name.

Hearing this, the spectators in the stands turned their gaze below.

A majestic horse as black as night slowly emerged from the passage.

It had blue mane, with limbs shrouded in purple, mist-like fog.

Even more striking were its closed eyes.

Though its eyes were closed, it seemed to be able to sense everything around it, walking precisely to its destination.

Witnessing this, the audience was amazed.

"What's going on? What kind of Pokemon is that?"

"This is incredible! Is that horse blind?"

"Its eyes are clearly shut; how does it see the way?"

"But that horse is so cool! I love its feet! They're amazing!"

The commentator found the contestant information after a brief search and finally located the contestant's registration details.

"This is so surprising! The number six contestant's partner turns out to be a horse-type Pokemon that's never been seen before!"

"Huh? Never been seen before?!" The audience widened their eyes, even more curious about that Pokemon.

Commentator: "That's right! According to the information provided by the number six contestant… Spectrier, that Pokemon is called Spectrier!"

---

---

---

---

# 342: Chapter 339: King of Horses

Spectrier?

"Is that a Pokemon from the Paldea Region?"

"I don't know, I've never heard of that name in the Paldea Region!"

"So it's a Pokemon from another region! Wow, I never thought there would be such a Pokemon! Amazing!"

"I wonder what kind of performance contestant number six and this Spectrier will deliver. Let's wait and see! But there are still contestants who haven't appeared. Next, let's welcome contestant number seven!"

D rode the Spectrier to his starting position.

He lowered his head and spoke to Spectrier: "Remember to use Agility first thing, then rush out at the fastest speed to distance yourself from the others."

Spectrier let out a neigh, indicating it understood.

Seeing this, D didn't say much more. The specific strategy would depend on the actual situation once they started running.

Because once they were in the lead, they would immediately become the target, and the other seven would try everything to attack him and Spectrier and pull them down.

After waiting for about three minutes, all eight contestants were present.

The staff holding the starting gun also came to one side of the starting line. Seeing everyone ready, this staff raised the starting gun.

With a bang, Spectrier stepped on the cloud and rushed out straight.

Its eyes flashed with blue light, its body became lighter, and its running speed increased immediately.

Watching this scene, the commentator exclaimed, "Wow! Our number six contestant has bolted ahead! Spectrier's speed is so fast! It used Agility!"

"The other contestants aren't giving up either. Contestant number one's Rapidash also used Agility!"

"Oh? The ground is shaking! Contestant number seven's Mudsdale used Bulldoze! The speed of the other Horse-type Pokemon has slowed down!"

Despite this, Spectrier remained in the lead.

D quickly glanced back at the situation behind him. The closest to him now was that Rapidash.

After a brief thought, he quickly ordered Spectrier, "Haze!"

Spectrier did not respond while running, but soon, a black mist exuding a gloomy aura spread across the field, obscuring the view ahead.

Two contestants, inexperienced in the face of sudden changes, failed to react and plunged into the black mist.

Their vision was obscured by the mist, and the Horse-type Pokemon they were riding immediately fell into a panic.

Panic-stricken, they rushed out of the racetrack and got disqualified.

"Damn, that horse's speed is too fast! It can't go on like this!" Contestant number one gritted his teeth and ordered Rapidash, "Fire Blast!"

Not only him, but those left behind quickly reacted too.

The man riding Zebstrika simultaneously ordered, "Zebstrika, use Thunder Wave!"

For a moment, several different moves simultaneously attacked D and Spectrier ahead.

Seeing this scene, the commentator exclaimed, "Oh my, contestant number six has attracted hostility, and the other contestants chose to team up! Contestant number six is in danger!"

D also noticed the situation behind him and after brief contemplation, he quickly said, "Phantom Force!"

In the next moment, Spectrier flickered and vanished along with D from their original spot.

Their attacks directly missed.

Immediately after, Spectrier, with D, appeared further ahead.

"Dodged!" The commentator exclaimed, "Contestant number six's Spectrier used Phantom Force to pull back and also dodged those attacks!"

"Oh, it looks like the other contestants haven't given up! Oh no, the second wave of attacks is coming!"

"Just as I imagined, they've teamed up."

D wasn't too panicked inside. He watched those attacks and at the right moment had Spectrier use Phantom Force again.

After Phantom Force ended, the speed-driven Spectrier distanced itself even further from those people.

But it seemed they hadn't given up.

Seeing this scene, D couldn't help but sigh.

Well, he didn't intend to, just wanted to rely on Spectrier's proud speed to take first place.

But it was clear now that that wasn't going to work.

So once Phantom Force was over, D solemnly said, "Bulldoze!"

Spectrier let out a neigh, and a mighty force quickly gathered at its hooves, then stomped heavily.

Then a boom was heard, as if the vast racetrack trembled along.

A terrifying ground shockwave spread quickly in all directions.

Rapidash running at the front was the first to be attacked.

Under that strong impact, Rapidash staggered, neighed urgently, and fell directly to the ground.

Next, the second... the third...

When Bulldoze ended, the entire racetrack fell into a bizarre silence.

Because aside from that Spectrier, all Horse-type Pokemon had fallen to the ground due to the shockwave!

"That power... so strong!" The commentator's voice was full of shock, "It's amazing! Absolutely amazing! Contestant number six's Spectrier knocked down all contestants with one Bulldoze!"

"No one can stop it now! Ah! Spectrier has reached the finish line! It seems the champion of this year's Horse Race Tournament is about to be crowned!"

In the stands, Alder watched the Spectrier below, a smile appearing on his face.

He was right; that Spectrier is a Legendary Level Pokemon!

Calyrex, Koraidon, Spectrier, and Glastrier... and those nine Dragonites.

A ranch has so many legendary and rare Pokemon at the same time.

"Really, the younger generation is truly awe-inspiring!" Alder exclaimed.

He couldn't help but wonder if he, as the Unova Region Champion, would be a match for D.

Alder felt uncertain.

The only words he could think of were invincible in the world.

During these days at the ranch, D introduced him to Calyrex.

Even as the champion, although Calyrex didn't show its prowess, Alder could still perceive this Pokemon's extraordinariness.

Even if D didn't use Koraidon, just with Calyrex alone, he could probably sweep through their Unova League.

A Pokemon with faith as energy, Alder had never heard of before.

Now it's only a Paldea Region, and that Calyrex already possesses such tremendous power.

If someday the whole world were to know about Calyrex, what kind of heights would its strength reach? Alder didn't even dare to imagine.

The Horse Race Tournament officially came to a close.

Amidst the cheers of the audience, D stepped onto the champion award stage with Spectrier.

Basking in the adoring gazes of the crowd, Spectrier had apparently already become the world's greatest Horse-type Pokemon.

It glanced at the golden "Crown" in D's hand, wondering why the crown of this era is different from before?

But Spectrier didn't think much about it. It was very happy now because it had already become the "King of Horses."

Next was the coronation ceremony!

Just like thousands of years ago when humans revered Calyrex as a king.

Thinking of this, Spectrier let out a neigh toward D.

D turned to look and found Spectrier staring at the trophy in his hand, somewhat surprised: "You want this?"

Spectrier nodded and then lowered its head, indicating for D to put the "Crown" on.

D: "?!"

He paused for a moment and asked, "You want me to put this on your head?"

Spectrier lowered its proud head, exuding an aura of regality all over.

D was silent.

Looking at Spectrier's eager expression, he eventually chose to comply with its wishes and inverted the golden trophy onto its head.

Spectrier mimicked Calyrex's posture, raising its head with dignity.

D: "..."

At this moment, Mayor Thomas approached with a camera. Seeing Spectrier's appearance, he was a bit surprised but didn't comment.

He waved to D, indicating for them to stand properly for a champion commemorative photo.

D stood straight immediately.

Beside him, Spectrier raised its head proudly, with a golden trophy inverted on its head.

With a click, the picture was fixed.

---

---

---

---

# 343: Chapter 340: Recipe Unlocked: Iceroot Carrot Pokeblock

The coronation ceremony is over, and now it's time to return to one's throne.

With a proud expression, Spectrier held its head high, ready to return to the stable in the pasture to eat a few Shaderoot Carrots.

Seeing that Spectrier was about to leave, D didn't even care about talking to the excited Allen. He said a few words casually to bid farewell and then got up to catch up with Spectrier.

"Want to go back?" D asked.

Spectrier nodded slightly.

"Alright." D nodded, leading Spectrier to the exit of the horse race meeting, where they met Alder and a group of Pokemon waiting there.

Alder and the Pokemon stared at the golden trophy atop Spectrier's head, suddenly dumbfounded.

"What... what's going on?" Alder exclaimed.

D: "That's the crown."

Koraidon twitched the corners of its mouth: "Ugh..." It was clearly a trophy.

Alder: "Pfft—"

With a proud expression, Spectrier glanced at Alder in front of him, then continued onward with its head held high.

Hm, foolish human!

Alder was momentarily at a loss, wondering why he felt disdained by this Pokemon.

D looked at Ogerpon, smiling and asking, "Did you have fun today?"

"Boney!"

Ogerpon nodded its little head repeatedly; it was very happy today, as everyone wasn't afraid of it, and it loved this place!

"That's good then." D smiled slightly: "Next time, I'll take you to the Harvest Festival behind the mountain."

"Boney~" Ogerpon looked forward to it.

Now, it's time to go home.

On the way back to the pasture, Spectrier never entered the Poke Ball; it wore the trophy like a king surveying its territory.

D and Alder walked behind.

The two could tell what this Pokemon was thinking—it was showing off.

"Could Spectrier have mistaken that trophy for a crown?" Alder posed as if he wanted to laugh but dared not let it out.

D: "...It seems so."

Alder suppressed his laughter: "It suits quite well."

D thought of Glastrier at home and wondered what reaction Glastrier would have upon seeing Spectrier.

Soon, D got the answer to this question.

When they arrived at the pasture, Spectrier headed straight to its stable.

Glastrier, who had been sulking in the stable, raised its head and instantly saw the golden crown atop Spectrier's head.

!!!

Glastrier widened its eyes in astonishment.

Spectrier glanced sideways at it and then walked into its stable, bowing to munch on the Shaderoot Carrots inside.

Glastrier's gaze locked on the trophy, watching it as it moved.

That thing... is a crown!

Judging by Spectrier's demeanor, it was evident it had won the horse racing meeting, becoming the mightiest Horse-type Pokemon in the world.

And that crown is proof of its ascension as the king among horses!

Glastrier's heart was filled with envy and jealousy.

Its breath gradually became heavy, with a cold icy breath constantly spraying from its nostrils.

That crown rightfully should have belonged to it!

Spectrier ignored the increasingly agitated Glastrier.

Just as D thought the two Horse-type Pokemon might fight over the "crown," Glastrier suddenly turned its head and gazed at him.

Glastrier had a resentful expression, then grumpily turned, showing him its rear.

D: "..."

He knew it!

Alder, standing by, could hardly hold his laughter. He found D's household far too entertaining.

Almost every day, he saw various Pokemon—happy, joyful, jealous, lazy, or having tantrums...

These are the troubles of capturing too many Pokemon!

But for a Trainer, it's indeed a blissful trouble.

"What do you plan to do?" Alder asked with a smile.

D let out a helpless sigh; based on past experience, he should go and comfort Glastrier now.

But knowing Glastrier's temperament, it probably wouldn't be easily coaxed.

D found himself in deep thought.

In his mind, he pictured Glastrier's favorite food, Iceroot Carrot, and recalled the cubed sugar other trainers fed the Horse-type Pokemon during the horse racing meeting.

Cubed sugar—a high-end sugar product made from refined granulated sugar, shaped into semi-square blocks.

Got it!

D's mind instantly came up with a plan to appease Glastrier.

He would merge Iceroot Carrot with cubed sugar, crafting them into Iceroot Carrot Pokeblock for Glastrier!

Thinking of this, D went to the Iceroot Carrot field and picked a basket of Iceroot Carrots.

Now, both the Iceroot Carrot and Shaderoot Carrot fields were under the care of Calyrex.

The growth of these special carrots took a long time, so to ensure his two beloved horses could eat their favorite foods daily, Calyrex would routinely accelerate the ripening of the carrot seeds in these fields.

D then harvested them and delivered them to the two horses' stables.

He brought the basket of Iceroot Carrots into the kitchen and began preparations.

Iceroot Carrot is a particularly unique ingredient, growing only in icy, snowy lands with very demanding environmental requirements.

Due to its distinct growth environment, this carrot naturally contains Ice Type Energy.

The Ice Type Energy stored inside the carrot affects it, transforming the ordinary carrot seed into something mutated, with all colors turning a snowy pure white.

---

It feels cold to the touch, and you can vaguely see the chill emanating from it.

D cuts the Iceroot Carrot into pieces and then blends it into a snowy white radish puree.

Next, he puts it into a pot and starts to boil it, adding white sugar during the process and constantly stirring.

When it seems about right, D takes out the Iceroot Carrot puree and then places it into square molds.

After waiting for a while, blocks of pure, white, shiny Iceroot Carrot Pokeblock, exuding a cold aura, are completed.

D originally thought the candy would feel like jelly candy, but instead, he found it hard, like hard candy.

"Could it be influenced by the Ice Type energy inherent in the Iceroot Carrot?"

D thought while looking down.

The next second, information about the candy emerged in his mind.

[Iceroot Carrot Pokeblock (B): Candy made from pure Iceroot Carrot and white sugar, possessing a unique flavor absent in ordinary radish candy, favored by certain Pokemon.

Cooking Effect: Cold, cold, cold. After being consumed by Pokemon, there's a certain chance they will learn Ice Type moves, restoring the Ice Type energy in their body. This effect is only effective for Ice Type Pokemon.

Additional Effect: Improves the favorability of Ice Type Pokemon.

Evaluation: Slight.]

"Huh? This effect is similar to cream ice cream?" D was somewhat surprised.

But the effect of this Iceroot Carrot Pokeblock is distinctly better than cream ice cream, making it a superior dessert.

Moreover, its production method is very simple, allowing for large quantities to be made at once. Whether it's Glaceon or Glastrier, they can eat it as a snack anytime.

So here comes the question, if the candy is made with Shaderoot Carrot, will it be a Ghost Type exclusive enhancement?

Thinking of this, D immediately took out two Shaderoot Carrots to experiment.

Soon, neat rows of pure purple pokeblocks were arranged in front of him.

[Shaderoot Carrot Pokeblock (B): Candy made from pitch-black Shaderoot Carrot and white sugar, possessing a unique flavor absent in ordinary radish candy, favored by certain Pokemon.

Cooking Effect: Dark, dark, dark. After being consumed by Pokemon, there's a certain chance they will learn Ghost Type moves, restoring the Ghost Type energy in their body. This effect is only effective for Ghost Type Pokemon.

Evaluation: Slight.]

"Just as expected!" D thought.

There aren't many Ghost Type Pokemon in the ranch, counting the Gimmighoul, there are three.

If you add the friendly neighbor Giratina, that's four.

At this time, the observer Alder couldn't help but ask: "Are those the energy cubes that Pokemon eat?"

D was slightly taken aback, then shook his head: "No, these are treats I made called pokeblocks for Glastrier and Spectrier."

"I thought they were energy cubes!" Alder was a little surprised, he looked at those pokeblocks and continued: "Their texture looks very much like top-grade energy cubes, all very pure."

D's eyes lit up: "Mr. Alder, do you know how to make energy cubes?"

Alder shook his head with a smile: "I don't know how to make those things!"

Seeing D's expression, Alder suddenly realized: "You want to learn how to make energy cubes?"

D nodded: "Because I heard that energy cubes contain nutrients very beneficial to Pokemon."

"You're not wrong." Alder recalled carefully, explaining: "However, each type of energy cube has a different formula, and the effectiveness of these energy cubes varies with the formula."

"For some top breeders, they develop special energy cubes based on the different conditions of Pokemon, and the exclusive formula for those is quite expensive!"

"However, generally, those breeders won't sell their formulas."

Upon hearing this, D became interested: "Formula?"

"That's right!" Alder nodded: "There are many Berries in this world, some of which only grow in specific regions, and each Berry can combine with others to create a type of energy cube. If really counted, there are probably several thousand formulas!"

D understood.

Seeing his curiosity about energy cubes, Alder promptly said:

"When we get to the Unova Region, I'll arrange for a breeder to teach you!"

D felt a bit moved but hesitated: "Wouldn't that be troublesome..."

He's never really pulled strings before!

"What are you thinking!" Alder laughed: "Of course it's in my personal capacity. The person is a friend I met when I was Champion; she's a top Pokemon breeder!"

Seeing this, D didn't decline: "I'll leave it to you then, Mr. Alder."

"Aren't you going to give those candies to Glastrier? Go on! Then we can discuss when to set off for the Unova Region." Alder said.

D nodded, putting away the Shaderoot Carrot Pokeblock, planning to give it to Spectrier tomorrow.

If he gives them together, Glastrier might get more upset.

With the Iceroot Carrot Pokeblock in hand, he approached the stable.

Seeing him coming, Glastrier immediately turned his back.

Nearby, Spectrier seemed to enter a sage mode, with a trophy balanced on its head.

---

As a great king, all the trivial matters around it ceased to concern it.

Not listening, not watching, not caring.

After all, it is now the most powerful Horse-type Pokemon in the world, already crowned as the Horse King.

D: "..."

He had a feeling that Calyrex's two beloved horses were about to malfunction.

He coughed lightly, then walked up to Glastrier. Just as Glastrier was about to turn away, its eyes caught sight of the plate of pure white treats in D's hand.

It paused slightly in its movements.

D seized the opportunity, picked up a cube of sugar, and slipped it into Glastrier's mouth, then leaned close to its ear and whispered:

"Go on, eat it! This is a special treat I prepared just for you. Spectrier doesn't have any!"

Spectrier glanced over slyly, its exceptionally keen senses picking up the whispered conversation between the human and horse.

But now, as a Horse King wearing a crown, it was above caring about such things.

Its tongue rolled around the cube of sugar that had been shoved into its mouth, and Glastrier exhaled two heavy snorts, wanting to refuse but finding its mouth uncooperative.

With the sugar cube down its gullet, Glastrier's ice-blue eyes lit up instantly.

This thing... why the heck does it taste so good!

Saliva started to flow in its mouth, and its eyes and brain were completely captivated by these sugar cubes, dissipating much of its inner irritation.

This human...

Actually prepared such delicious food for it, is he trying to apologize?

Seeing his sincere gesture, Glastrier decided to forgive him!

Glastrier snorted a couple of times and turned to start gobbling down the treats.

Seeing this scene, D knew he had successfully placated the ill-tempered horse.

Compared to Spectrier, Glastrier was clearly not as clever.

But being temperamental had its advantages; Pokemon with such personalities often had straightforward ways of thinking, essentially a clear distinction between love and hate.

After feeding Glastrier, D found Glaceon and Alcremie in the Berry Garden, playing hide and seek.

He shared the remaining candies with these Pokemon and then called Flutter Mane to the kitchen, feeding it a few pieces of Shaderoot Carrot Pokeblock.

"How does it taste?" D couldn't help but ask as he watched Flutter Mane eating.

Flutter Mane, with its little fangs showing, frowned, clearly not too fond of these carrot candies.

"Not a fan, eh..." D pondered.

Flutter Mane, coming from ancient times, was evidently a carnivorous Pokemon.

Compared to carrots, vegetables, and berries, it preferred chunks of meat and bones.

If that's the case, he'll just make a modified dish of Shaderoot Carrot Stew next time.

By the way, it seems that Porto Marinada Town doesn't sell vermicelli... he wanted to make a pork and vermicelli stew last winter but couldn't.

Aside from regular meat, there's lobster and fish from the sea.

The fish brought by those working Dragonites were all swallowed up by Giratina before he could even have a bite.

When he went to Giratina about it, Giratina boldly claimed that anything that fell into its world belonged to it.

He had been busy with Kitakami and Ogerpon affairs before, and now with some free time, D craved some lobster.

With that thought, D stepped out, wary of making his own Dragonite jealous, so he avoided those working Dragonites and found his own Dragonite, telling it that he wanted to eat lobster and crab.

Seeing that D came to it to catch fish instead of those wild Dragonites, Dragonite was overjoyed.

It immediately stood up from the grass and flew out to the sea to catch some fish for D.

---

---

---

---

# 344: Chapter 341: Guardian of the Sea

Dragonite's efficiency in getting things done was quick; it returned with the fish it caught before the sky darkened.

Its two arms were holding two lobsters, its mouth was biting a large squid, and a withered seaweed was wrapped around the little horn on its head.

Despite looking so disheveled, Dragonite's heart was very happy.

Seeing D who was welcoming it in the pasture, Dragonite cried out in joy, tossed the big lobsters and the squid from its mouth onto the grass, and hugged D with its seawater-scented chubby arm.

"Roar!"

"Silly." D helped Dragonite remove the seaweed from its horn, then picked up the lobsters and squid from the grass and took them back to the kitchen.

Dragonite sat on the grass to rest.

D turned back and advised, "Don't run around, I'll give you a bath later!"

"Roar~" Dragonite agreed happily.

The sky was getting dark, and the dragonites that worked were joyfully carrying their Poffin Boxes, heading off from the pasture.

Dragonite sat on the grass, bathed in the sunset, and looking at those dragonites in the sky, it waved its paw in excitement.

"Roar!"

Alder watched this scene, marveling: "This Dragonite really likes you!"

D smiled, putting the lobsters and squid into a basin.

Those lobsters and squid were still alive, not dead, it seems Dragonite's speed was really quick, probably found them and used Extreme Speed to rush back to the pasture.

Thinking of this, D told Alder about the amusing incident when Dragonite once left home due to jealousy.

"At that time, the other dragonites brought me many big fish as gifts, Dragonite was unwilling to lose to them, and then silently left home, flying elsewhere, wanting to catch a bigger fish to outshine the other dragonites."

"Hahaha!" Alder thought of that scene and laughed heartily, quickly asking, "What happened afterward?"

"Later on..." D recalled, continued: "Later, Dragonite encountered a mysterious large Fish-type Pokemon in the sea, but that Pokemon was not only huge but also very strong."

"Dragonite was no match for it and lost that battle, returning to the pasture crestfallen."

"It must have felt very upset." Alder said.

"Indeed!" D was filled with emotion: "But the fact that Dragonite had such intentions was enough, for a Trainer, as long as a Pokemon can always be by their side, that is the best gift."

Upon hearing those words, Alder was reminded of his Volcarona, his tone was equally emotional:

"Indeed! That call from Leon was the most precious gift I received in my life."

Listening to the duo's conversation, Cyclizar and Koraidon both looked over.

D usually wouldn't express his feelings towards Pokemon so plainly.

Even though it wasn't the first time hearing such words, Koraidon still felt a surge of warmth inside, as if struck by something.

And Cyclizar looked over enviously and longingly.

"Gao..."

D patted Cyclizar's head, saying to it: "Not just Dragonite, but you too!"

Even though among the Pokemon, Cyclizar's presence wasn't that prominent.

But its assistance to D was no less than that of any other Pokemon.

Being the first Pokemon to come to the pasture, Cyclizar was very familiar with everything in the pasture.

It was precisely because of this that the later Pokemon could so swiftly integrate into the life at the pasture.

Recalling all those past memories, D's emotions surged, he suggested Cyclizar go play outside for a while, he was going to prepare a lobster feast for everyone.

"Gao~"

Cyclizar nodded happily, turning around and running away.

Koraidon watching this scene, was unsure of what it was thinking.

Tonight's dinner was settled, two big lobsters, D planned to steam one and make garlic the other, as for that squid, its amount wasn't enough to share with everyone, D simply tossed it aside.

He fired up the stove and placed the prepared lobster into the steamer, while waiting, D began thinking about Cyclizar.

Compared to the other Pokemon at the pasture, Cyclizar's strength was somewhat lacking.

Apart from increasing its species strength with a birthday cake and self-training previously, Cyclizar hadn't had other means to become stronger.

As a Rancher, D didn't have much pursuit for Pokemon battles.

He didn't force Pokemon battles, only wanted to support Pokemon to do what they liked.

Whether it's lounging around and leisurely enjoying life or diligently working and training.

After all, the world is not so cruel, most Pokemon living with humans can happily and peacefully live out their lives.

They use their strength to protect their Trainer, and correspondingly, Trainers also use human knowledge and power to ensure they safely live through their lives.

So, he will also protect Cyclizar well.

D glanced back at Cyclizar lying in front of the fireplace observing Slugma, smiling.

At this moment, the lobster in the pot was about done steaming.

D took it out, habitually taking a look.

Yet this glance revealed some surprising stuff.

[Steamed Lobster (A+): Special dish made from a large lobster dwelling in the deep sea, retaining its original fresh and savory flavor, a gourmet sought after by seafood lovers, infused with the intense emotions of its maker, unexpected effects might appear.]

---

Cooking Effect: Guardian of the Sea (exclusive to Cyclizar), the Pokemon that consumes this dish can receive the Guardian of the Sea effect once; when the Pokemon faces a life-threatening crisis, this effect is automatically triggered, completely blocking all damage from that attack.

Evaluation: When emotions and cuisine blend together, it gives birth to an incomparable culinary masterpiece.

"?"

D stared at the plate of steamed lobster in front of him, slightly puzzled.

Exclusive cuisine for Cyclizar?

How could it be... was it because he had been thinking about Cyclizar while cooking?

Guardian of the Sea, seems like an enhanced version of Protect, completely blocking one attack.

However, for a Cyclizar that lives on the ranch all the time, this function might not ever be needed.

Thinking of this, D looked at the garlic steamed lobster on the other side.

[Garlic Steamed Lobster (B+): Cooking effect, specialized for lobsters, the lobster Pokemon that eats this dish will have a slight increase in defense and attack, lasting for 30 minutes.]

D: "..."

How terrifying! Making lobsters eat lobster, that's too much like hell.

Lobster Pokemon refers to ones like Corphish, Crawdaunt, and Clawitzer.

However, D was a bit puzzled: does Corphish count as the same species as regular large lobsters?

There aren't any Corphish on the ranch, so this dish doesn't seem of any use.

D didn't think much more about it, got up and served today's dinner on the dining table, and called the Pokemon playing outside back home for dinner.

At the dinner table, D looked at Cyclizar enjoying his meal, then asked:

"Mr. Alder, is emotion also a form of power for Pokemon?"

"Of course!"

Alder nodded and explained to D: "Some Pokemon Academies specifically teach this kind of knowledge. Besides the usual attributes we know, emotions can also turn into energy and play a role."

"Like Retaliate and Frustration from Normal Type, Grudge from Ghost Type, but only some Pokemon can use these moves."

"For example, when novice Trainers raise Pokemon like Eevee, they try to build a good relationship with them, using this method to enhance the power of Retaliate to face stronger enemies."

Hearing this, D couldn't help but think of Frustration.

Frustration: A Pokemon attacks the opponent to vent its displeasure, and the lower the Trainer's intimacy, the greater the power.

Alder continued: "Frustration works the same way..."

D: "But this move would affect the relationship between Pokemon and Trainers, wouldn't it..."

How could there be Pokemon that hate their own Trainer while still fighting for him—it's like a masochist-type Pokemon?

Alder laughed and nodded: "Yes, it indeed affects the relationship between the two. So after being captured by humans, Pokemon find it hard to use this move again. Even if they do, its power is weak, and for opponents, it's nothing."

D nodded thoughtfully.

So it was because he wanted to protect Cyclizar, for it to live through life safely, his emotions intertwined with the cooking, that led to the steamed lobster having the "Guardian of the Sea" effect?

This effect can only be triggered once, and the conditions are so demanding; it only triggers during a life-threatening crisis.

If possible, D hopes Cyclizar never has to use this guardian.

*

After dinner, Alder comfortably rubbed his belly and leaned back on the sofa.

"Ah~ I'm so full! Your cooking skills are amazing! I feel like I've gained quite a bit of weight during my time on the ranch."

Sister Indeedee brought him a cup of tea.

Alder immediately sat up straight: "Ah, thank you!"

"Indeedee~" Sister Indeedee smiled slightly and then stepped aside.

Alder looked at D and asked, "The things here should be settled now, right? When do you plan to set off for the Unova Region?"

D nodded; after the race ends, there's not much going on here.

The crops in the ground need some time before they can be harvested, and the new plot across the river doesn't need urgent development; it can wait until he's back.

Today is March 25th, and Alder mentioned the banquet is on April 1st.

Plus, Alder said he would introduce a Pokemon Breeder to teach him how to make Pokeblocks, which might take some time too.

In that case, it's best to go early and return early!

Thinking of this, D said: "Tomorrow then!"

Alder agreed enthusiastically: "Alright, I have no problems! You helped me so much, once in Unova Region, I have to give you a good reception!"

Alder looked like he wanted to take him all over the Unova Region to see its beautiful scenery.

D thought it wasn't necessary.

But he still tactfully asked, "Mr. Alder, don't you need to handle the matters of the Unova League? Geeta seems to be busy nonstop every day."

Hearing this, Alder blushed.

After encountering that incident, where Volcarona was severely injured and couldn't be healed, he couldn't focus on dealing with Unova League's matters, wandering throughout the Unova Region, seeking a cure for Volcarona.

As for Unova League matters, he irresponsibly threw them to the Elite Four...

But could he say any of this?

Clearly not.

The person in front of him is his fan, and to maintain his image in the fan's eyes, Alder coughed lightly, his dark face slightly reddening:

"The Unova League matters are now managed by the Elite Four, and once I return, I'll resume my responsibilities as Champion."

Alder spoke earnestly, and D chose to see through but not say through.

Alder saw D looking at him and, feeling a little uncomfortable, changed the topic:

"The banquet this time will have many well-known figures and famous Trainers attending, Lance might also be there."

"The Dragon Master Lance..." D murmured, "I do quite want to meet him."

"Oh?" Alder grew cautious: "You really admire Lance?"

"...Not really." D organized his words in his mind: "I've just heard his name for a long time."

"I see!" Alder realized: "There are battle venues at the banquet, you can spar with him. Oh, and you can bring all your Dragonite too."

He quite wanted to see Lance's expression when seeing so many Dragonite.

D felt a bit tempted but thought it through and decided against it.

Because Dragonite need to stay behind for work; taking them away means they can't deliver goods to the townsfolk.

"Dragonite need to work." D said.

Alder showed an expression of regret.

"Then..." D thought for a moment and asked: "Will Steven, the Champion of the Hoenn Region, be there?"

"Steven?" Alder recalled and then shook his head: "Probably not, as he's never attended such events."

D thought it made sense, as Steven is either on the road searching for stones or searching for stones.

"However, although Steven won't attend, Mr. Stone from the Devon Company will be there, as well as Giovanni, a well-known entrepreneur from the Kanto Region." Alder added.

Hearing this name, D was slightly surprised.

"Giovanni?"

Is it the Giovanni he knows?

The Viridian Gym Leader, the head of the notorious Team Rocket, the mastermind behind Mewtwo's creation, self-proclaimed strongest man...

"Whoa? You've heard of Giovanni?" Alder saw D's expression and was surprised, as the distance between the Kanto and Paldea Regions is indeed quite far.

"Yes." D nodded and spoke briefly: "Before settling here, I traveled around other regions."

"I see!" Alder understood: "I'm not familiar with Giovanni, but he is a famous entrepreneur in the Kanto Region and also serves as a Gym Leader in one city, with very strong capabilities."

"..."

D didn't know what to say for a moment.

Famous entrepreneur, this term seems to fit Giovanni quite well.

Though Giovanni is a hidden big boss, fortunately, Paldea and the Kanto Region are far apart, Team Rocket's reach doesn't extend here, which is something D doesn't need to worry too much about.

---

---

---

---

# 345: Chapter 342: Tachi Hero Farfetch'd, Heading to Unova

After confirming the time to depart for the Unova Region, D began to get busy.

Before officially setting off, he still had some things to do.

The most important among them was preparing the Berry Platter for Farfetch'd for these days.

This journey to the Unova Region would take at least a week before coming back.

Farfetch'd eats three meals a day, three servings per meal, needing to eat nine servings of Berry Platter a day, which amounts to sixty-three servings for seven days.

With so many Berry Platters, the berries at home were somewhat insufficient, so D simply headed to the Berry Garden and picked a few baskets of berries to prepare the Berry Platter Farfetch'd needed.

In the living room, D stuffed the prepared Berry Platters into the fridge while instructing the Indeedee Sibling watching on the side:

"I'm going to leave the ranch for a few days, these Berry Platters are Farfetch'd's food for the coming days, remember to give it three servings at a time, once each morning, noon, and evening."

"Make sure not to give too much at once, or if Farfetch'd eats up all these Berry Platters in one go, it might die from overeating, becoming the world's first Farfetch'd to die from overeating."

"Indeedee~"

"Indeedee."

The siblings responded in unison, indicating that they remembered.

"Very good, then I'll leave everyone at the ranch in your care!" D nodded and said, "If there's anything you can't solve, consult with Calyrex, and if Mew and Teddiursa deliver items and Pokemon, just let them stay at the ranch, everything will be settled upon my return."

"Indeedee!"

After giving his instructions, D headed to the dummy zone to find Farfetch'd practicing moves and techniques there.

Farfetch'd held a leek, its expression serious, swinging the leek continuously, leaving the technique simple and unadorned, chopping the wooden dummy repeatedly.

D stood by with arms crossed, watching until Farfetch'd emerged from its concentration and noticed him.

D: "How's it going? Is practice going smoothly? Have you realized any impressive moves?"

"Quack!" Farfetch'd nodded, then shook its head.

The nod indicated smooth practice, while the shake indicated it hadn't comprehended any impressive moves yet.

Every time Farfetch'd thought about this, it felt incredibly unlucky.

Clearly learning a couple of impressive moves at noon, only to forget them entirely by night.

The only comfort to it was that throughout this constant forgetting and realizing, its mastery of the sword skills it originally possessed had become more proficient.

And with each practice, Farfetch'd's understanding of these moves was continuously improving.

Like Cut, Iaido Slash, Fury Cutter, Aerial Ace.

Just like now.

To showcase its realized skills to D, Farfetch'd turned around, facing the wooden dummies in front, focusing its breath.

It held the leek, assuming a Sheathing the Sword posture, eyes sharp.

In the next moment, a flurry of Double Team quickly flew out from Farfetch'd, flashing white, with dozens of shadow clones simultaneously unsheathing for an Iaido attack.

These milky white lights enveloped the blade, transforming into Sword Qi that struck uniformly on the wooden dummies.

With a crunch, the battered wooden dummies finally couldn't endure Farfetch'd's drills, completely falling apart.

Farfetch'd sheathed its sword, and the surrounding Double Team disappeared in unison.

D's eyes shone.

This set of moves is impressive!

Double Team + Iaido Slash?

Yet, Farfetch'd's moves made D inexplicably feel familiar.

Sheathing the Sword, Iaido.

The subsequent move should be... Dragon Ascending?

But Farfetch'd didn't have a follow-up move.

D couldn't help but say, "Seems like something's missing."

"Quack?" Hearing this, Farfetch'd looked puzzled.

D: "After Iaido, you can completely follow with another move."

Farfetch'd grew more puzzled, what move?

It looked at D, inquiring, "Quack?"

"After the strike, you can seize the moment when the opponent hasn't reacted and charge to its front, jump high stepping on them, then chop down." D concisely explained.

Hearing this, Farfetch'd couldn't help but fall into deep thought.

It gazed at the leek in hand, pondering the possibility of this move.

Its wings caressed the leek's stem, and after a long while, Farfetch'd began to move.

It held the leek, focusing its breath.

Sheathing—

Farfetch'd's gaze locked onto a wooden dummy, its eyes becoming sharp.

Iaido Slash—

A dense white glow enveloped the leek, with the leek acting as a sword blade performing a horizontal slash.

A gap instantly appeared on the wooden dummy, but the attack wasn't over.

In a swift motion, Farfetch'd retracted the leek, charged forward, jumped high onto the dummy, leveraging over the top of the wooden dummy.

Then, chop—

"Beautiful!" Alder, watching from behind, applauded admiringly: "I didn't expect you'd have such unique insight into swordsmanship!"

D: "..."

Could he say he learned this from a certain two-character weapon?

Unfortunately, Farfetch'd can't use Protect. If it used Protect in Sheathing the Sword state, maybe it could really see through incoming attacks like Tachi Hero's Iaijutsu and perfectly defend against them.

Farfetch'd put away the leek, reminiscing the feeling just now, then looked at D with an excited expression.

---

"Gah!"

This place, it really didn't come to the wrong spot!

D: "…Good luck!"

Farfetch'd nodded heavily.

Looking at those wooden dummy stakes that had been damaged, D silently made a note in his heart, planning to contact Keri to come over and repair these dummies later.

Oh, almost forgot, he hadn't yet discussed the main issue with Farfetch'd!

Thinking of this, D spoke up: "For the next few days, I have something to do and need to be away for a few days. But don't worry, I've already prepared a Berry Platter for you, and the Indeedees will feed you in my place."

"Gah?" Farfetch'd looked puzzled.

D: "Don't worry, I'll be back soon. During the time I'm away, make sure to practice well."

"Gah!"

Seeing this, D nodded.

After explaining everything, he turned around and went back to his room to pack the luggage for this trip.

Upon learning about D's distant trip, Ogerpon and the little friends it made at the ranch came running over together.

"Bonnie Bonnie!" Ogerpon's starry eyes were full of anticipation.

D looked at Ogerpon, Alcremie, and Glaceon who had run into the room, and asked, "What's up?"

Ogerpon pointed to D's backpack: "Bonnie!"

D was slightly taken aback, then understood: "You want to come with me too?"

Ogerpon nodded repeatedly: "Bonnie!" That's right!

"I see..." D thought for a moment, then happily agreed: "Of course, we can go together!"

Hearing this, Alcremie and Glaceon also looked hopeful.

"Maa maa..."

"Bui..."

D looked at these two Pokemon again.

He suddenly realized that these two poor little ones hadn't gone far from the ranch since they arrived.

D felt a twinge of softness in his heart, then patted Glaceon and Alcremie's heads: "Would you like to come with us too?"

"Maa maa!"

"Bui!"

Alcremie was extremely happy, and Glaceon's eyes brightened with happiness.

D smiled and continued packing: "This trip will take several days before we return. If you have anything you want to bring along, you can give it to me, and I'll pack it for you."

Things like toys — every Pokemon at the ranch has their own toys, gifts from D who bought them from the town previously.

Upon hearing this, the three Pokemon thought for a while, then turned and ran off.

Glaceon ran back to its Ice Cave, wanting to bring along its favorite Ice Stone.

But the stone was bigger than itself, and Glaceon couldn't move it, so it had to ask Cyclizar for help with carrying.

Alcremie ran to the fridge, asking Koraidon to help open it and took several small cakes from inside to stash in its little bundle.

Meanwhile, Ogerpon went to the room where the four masks were stored.

Ogerpon wasn't afraid of going out; it was because the masks contained its power, and only by wearing a mask could it unleash its true strength.

D actually didn't know, but Ogerpon thought to itself that it was a very powerful Pokemon.

And venturing outside, who knows, they might encounter some bad guys.

Then it would don a mask and fight for D!

Soon enough, the three Pokemon found D with their cherished items.

Looking at the Ice Stone Cyclizar was holding, D felt a bit speechless.

"Are you sure you want to take this out?" D looked at Glaceon, his face full of unspoken words.

"Bui~" Glaceon nodded; it was its favorite item!

D: "But... it's too big, taking it along is so inconvenient!"

"Bui?" Glaceon blinked, looked at Alcremie and Ogerpon next to it, then at its Ice Stone.

"Bui..." It does seem quite large...

D persuaded: "We'll be back soon, no need to take it out. It's safe at the ranch, no Pokemon will come steal it."

Well then. Glaceon hesitated, but finally decided to leave the Ice Stone at the ranch.

D sighed in relief, and Cyclizar also relaxed, as the Ice Stone was really too cold.

It quickly returned the Ice Stone to the ice cellar, then ran to the fireplace to warm up in front of Slugma.

After storing Alcremie's small cakes safely, when D saw the four masks in Ogerpon's hands, he suddenly looked surprised.

"You want to take them with you?" D hesitated, then said: "People in the Unova Region won't dislike you, so you don't need to wear a mask outside, and in any case, I'm here."

Places like Kitakami are probably unique in that sense.

"Bonnie!"

Hearing this, Ogerpon knew D misunderstood; it shook its little head repeatedly, then put on a fiery red mask.

In the next second, in D's astonished gaze, Ogerpon's appearance began to change rapidly.

The burning aura constantly surged from the mask, and a strong Fire Type Energy spread over Ogerpon's entire body, gradually turning its originally green coat into a flame-like red.

In this form, Ogerpon looked furious, extremely aggressive.

---

D's eyes widened in shock.

Ogerpon's appearance had changed...

No! It seemed that not only its appearance but its attributes had changed as well!

Fire Type? Or Fire and Grass Dual Type?

In D's astonishment, Ogerpon cheerfully took off the Hearthflame Mask and then put on another blue Wellspring Mask.

This time, Ogerpon's outfit turned into a water-like blue.

The attribute changed again... this time it seemed to have become Water Type.

So what about that mask that looks like rock?

When Ogerpon put it on, D immediately had an answer in his heart.

Rock Type.

What on earth was happening? Was it because of the Tera Shards embedded in the masks?

D immediately thought of the Tera Shards he had salvaged from Crystal Lake before.

"Boni~"

Ogerpon looked very happy, it removed its mask and handed all four to D for him to store in his bag.

D finally came to his senses and reached out to take them.

All along, he thought these masks were just masks, but he never expected them to have such power inside.

Masks that could enhance Ogerpon's abilities and change its attributes...

It seemed now that this Pokemon, Ogerpon, is indeed extraordinary!

D took a deep breath, stuffed the masks into the backpack, and then went to the Poke Ball stand in the living room to find Alcremie, Glaceon, and Ogerpon's Poke Balls, preparing to take them with him tomorrow.

This time going out, in addition to these three Pokemon, he also plans to take Koraidon, Cyclizar, Flutter Mane, Pawmot, and Dragonite.

Alder said that Lance might attend that banquet as well.

If given the chance, D wanted to have a battle with Dragonite to see how far his Dragonite's strength has grown and how far it was from Champion Level.

After all, there were no opponents or battles on the ranch, so up until now, D also hasn't figured out Dragonite's true strength.

The next morning, D followed behind Alder to bid farewell to everyone at the ranch and embarked on the road to the Unova Region.

The two took a Squawkabilly Flying Taxi to Levincia City.

On the way, Alder said to D, "The banquet will be held in Driftveil City, and I checked before, there's a luxury cruise that goes directly from the Paldea Region to Driftveil City, so we can just take the cruise."

"Okay."

Cruise... D had no objections at all.

In fact, if not in a hurry, he personally preferred taking a ship over a plane.

Because that way, Koraidon wouldn't have to be stuffed into the luggage compartment for shipping.

Alder laughed, "But there's still a few days before the banquet, so during this time, I'll take you to meet a Pokemon Breeder, who will teach you how to make Pokeblocks."

D nodded and said that when it comes to the Unova Region, one cannot miss mentioning the Unova Trio Dragons.

The handsome and cool Zekrom, the graceful and sleeker Reshiram, and the cold child-devouring dragon Kyurem.

D's heart stirred slightly, recalling his experiences playing Black and White back then.

Besides furry Pokemon like Pawmot, D loved strong and cool Dragon Type Pokemon the most.

And among the Tao Trio, they were the best of the Dragon Type Pokemon.

Thinking of this, D began to inquire, "By the way, Mr. Alder, are there any powerful Legendary Pokemon in the Unova Region?"

Anyway, these three Pokemon would eventually be left for Plasma Team's crimes.

Rather than letting Ghetsis use these three Legendary Pokemon to commit crimes in the Unova Region, it would be better for him to take them home and settle them at the World Tree.

All things considered, it might as well help Alder and the Unova Region solve a big problem.

---

---

---

---

# 346: Chapter 343: Ursaluna Crossing the Sea?

"Legendary Pokémon of the Unova Region?"

Upon hearing this question, Alder paused slightly and then began to recall carefully.

"Of course there are!" He flashed a smile: "The most famous of them are the Dual Dragon Pokémon representing truth and ideals."

"Dual Dragon... Pokémon?" D asked.

Alder nodded: "They are the Electric-type Zekrom and the Fire-type Reshiram."

"Zekrom appears in myths as a legendary Pokémon. It can use its tail to generate lightning, hiding its entire body within thunderclouds, flying across the skies of the Unova Region. It's said that traveling Trainers have once seen its soaring silhouette in the sky."

"Reshiram's tail, on the other hand, ignites flames. It's said that when Reshiram's tail burns, it generates heat energy causing atmospheric movement, altering the world's weather."

"In the ancient myths of the Unova Region, if humans disregard truth and indulge in desires, it will use flames to burn their kingdoms to the ground."

D: "Sounds quite impressive."

"Indeed, these Pokémon have existed since before the founding of Unova!" Alder smiled.

At this, he seemed to think of something, and his expression became heavy: "The allure of legendary Pokémon is great, causing some individuals with ulterior motives to seek them out, attempting to harness the power of legends."

D, similarly preoccupied with Hydreigon: "..."

Compared to Ghetsis, he probably isn't too malicious, right?

Although Alder's words seemed fine, they actually revealed a lot of information.

For instance, Reshiram and Zekrom are likely no longer in their Dragon Stone forms, roaming the Unova Region like normal Pokémon, from time to time.

D remembers when Ash first traveled to the Unova Region, he encountered Zekrom in the anime.

At that time, Zekrom used Thunder on Pikachu, severely impacting Pikachu, preventing it from using Electric-type moves...

Alder did not continue the topic because the situation in the Unova Region is quite special.

Though it appears calm on the surface, there are actually undercurrents at play.

The mysterious organization advocating "liberating Pokémon" was already faintly heard by Alder months ago, as this group uses speeches to stir public opinion, heavily criticizing him for excessively draining Volcarona's stamina, leading to injuries that cannot heal.

This incident led him to self-doubt and was one of the reasons for leaving the Unova League.

But all of this has no relation to D, so Alder did not tell him, turning to another topic.

"Oh right, like in the Paldea Region, there's also an Abundant Shrine in Unova!"

Abundant Shrine? Is it Landorus? Information instantly popped into D's mind.

Alder: "However, the shrine there doesn't worship Calyrex, but a Pokémon called Landorus."

"According to legends, Landorus absorbs wind and thunder, converting them into energy to nourish the soil, enriching the earth, and providing nutrients for crops to grow vigorously."

"It is said that the lands visited by Landorus will have abundant harvests, thus this Pokémon is also known as the God of the Fields."

D raised an eyebrow: "Sounds quite similar to what Calyrex specializes in!"

Alder smiled and nodded: "Exactly, Calyrex is adept at making crops mature rapidly, while Landorus can supplement soil nutrients. If these two Pokémon worked together, crops could grow infinitely!"

D isn't too surprised upon hearing this.

Because Calyrex also possesses the ability to make soil fertile, a power that overlaps completely with Landorus's.

But compared to Landorus, he is more concerned with Reshiram and Zekrom.

Yet from Alder's recent words, he could tell that Alder also didn't know where these two Pokémon were.

The only clue seems to be Kyurem.

Because in the game, Kyurem's location has always been fixed, near the Giant Chasm in Lacunosa Town.

However, D can't be sure because this world is vast; the terrain and town distribution differ from those in the game, aligning more closely with the anime.

In such circumstances, no one can say for certain if Kyurem will definitely be in that location.

D plans to devise a detailed strategy once he reaches the Unova Region, hoping to gather some information from others.

If time permits, he intends to visit Lacunosa Town, to pay a visit to this legendary strongest dragon.

The Squawkabilly Flying Taxi is fast, and within a few hours, D and Alder reached their destination, Levincia City.

They didn't linger in the city, heading straight to the port, buying two direct tickets to Driftveil City in the Unova Region from the ticket booth.

Coincidentally, a ship was just preparing to set sail.

The two hurriedly checked their tickets and boarded. Shortly after, accompanied by the melancholy sound of the horn, the large luxurious cruise ship slowly left the port and sailed out to sea.

Gazing at the slowly receding Levincia City, D gently exhaled.

---

Luckily, just in time!

Alder looked relaxed: "This cruise won't reach Driftveil City until tomorrow. There's plenty of entertainment and food on the ship, so let's enjoy it for now!"

"Alright," D nodded with a smile.

There were many guests on the ship, and D glanced around, spotting many elegantly dressed ladies and gentlemen in refined suits with canes.

Among them were some young trainers with their Pokemon.

Soon, two people caught each other's eyes, held hands, and headed to the onboard battle area to have a Pokemon battle.

D saw this and shook his head with a smile.

It's just the Pokemon World after all!

Everything can be settled with a Pokemon battle.

Alder was very interested in these things. He said something to D and then ran off to watch the young trainers' Pokemon battles.

D, on the other hand, wasn't interested.

He stayed on the deck, thinking that this was the first time the Alcremie and Glaceon had gone out, and they might never have seen the ocean in their lives. So he took out a few Poke Balls and released his Pokemon.

With flashes of white light, Alcremie, Glaceon, Flutter Mane, Cyclizar, Pawmot, Dragonite, and Ogerpon appeared on the deck.

Sure enough, just as D expected, this group of Pokemon, after taking in their surroundings, immediately ran to the ship's railing and started exclaiming at the distant ocean.

At this time, the cruise ship had already left the waters of Levincia City and was out on the open sea.

Sunshine, blue skies, white clouds, and a vast ocean stretched out before them, with the glittering sea surface, white waves gently lapping against the ship with the sea breeze.

In the distance, several Wingull flew across the sky, their distant cries carried by the sea breeze.

Gazing at this beautiful scene, the Pokemon who had never seen the ocean were completely stunned.

"Maa maa..."

"Bui bui..."

Even the ancient Flutter Mane gazed at the shimmering sea in awe.

Dragonite scratched its head with a big paw, looking puzzled. It didn't quite understand what was so fascinating about the ocean.

Koraidon, next to him, said helplessly, "Ah gaa sss..." Not everyone has seen the ocean.

Dragonite suddenly looked enlightened.

D introduced with a smile, "This is the ocean. We are now sailing away from the land, out on the ocean."

Hearing this, Ogerpon nodded happily: "Bo ni!"

Seeing this, D was a bit surprised: "Ogerpon, have you seen the ocean before?"

Saying that, D casually took out a bag of Translation Nuts Cake from his bag and started eating it.

These were made from sunflowers he had previously asked Calyrex to help ripen. They're quite handy to have when traveling.

Ogerpon jumped up twice: "Bo ni bo ni!" (Seen it! My dad and I took a small boat, crossing the ocean to reach Kitakami!)

As expected, Ogerpon was a Pokemon from elsewhere heading to Kitakami! D thought.

At this moment, Ogerpon seemed to suddenly remember something and joyfully said, "Bo ni!" (At that time, I saw a big bear with a moon on its forehead swimming across the ocean!)

Hmm? D was a bit surprised: "A big bear? Is it also a Pokemon?"

Ogerpon nodded its small head: "Bo ni!" (That's right! Dad said it's also a Pokemon. Our small boat passed by it. When I asked where it was going, it said its hometown had terrifying Pokemon and it had to cross the ocean to go elsewhere.)

At this point, Ogerpon couldn't help but show a worried expression: "Bo ni..." (But our boat was too small; otherwise, we could have taken it along. I don't know if that big bear succeeded in crossing the ocean...)

Ogerpon was initially concerned about that Pokemon, but later, even its own safety was at risk, and it couldn't think about that Pokemon anymore.

A big bear Pokemon with a moon on its forehead... A Teddiursa? Or maybe an Ursaring?

No, Ursaring doesn't have a moon. Could it be the Ursaluna from the Hisuin Region?!

D doubted it because Ursaluna is a Normal+Ground type Pokemon, which is weak to water, and couldn't possibly swim across the ocean.

But Ogerpon wouldn't lie, and besides Ursaluna with a moon on its head, no other Pokemon looks like that.

Thinking of this, D was stunned.

No way? An Ursaluna that can swim across the ocean, that's too incredible!

Moreover, D was quite intrigued by the fact that the Ursaluna mentioned its hometown had terrifying Pokemon.

These terrifying Pokemon, could they refer to the time-space distortion events in the Hisuin Region?

The time-space distortion caused by Giratina, Palkia, and Dialga, according to Professor Laventon's research, suggests that the so-called time-space is where multiple universes overlap, saturating the power and causing distortion.

In the game, time-space distortion causes many powerful Pokemon not belonging to this era to appear.

Thinking of this, D couldn't help but imagine.

An Ursaluna witnessed the arrival of the time-space distortion and saw the red-eyed Pokemon that appeared with it.

Frightened, Ursaluna left the Hisuin Region, planning to cross the ocean to seek refuge elsewhere.

---

On the way, it met Ogerpon, who was traveling to Kitakami with a man by boat, and had a brief exchange with Ogerpon.

So what would the fate of that Ursaluna be?

Did it sink into the sea, or successfully reach its destination?

D did not know.

Unfortunately, Ursaluna no longer exists in this world because the Peat Block that evolves Ursaring has ceased to exist.

Speaking of the Pokemon Ursaring, D thought of his own Teddiursa.

Looking at Teddiursa, it seems that it did not intend to evolve into Ursaring.

To prevent itself from evolving, Teddiursa has been carrying an Everstone.

Just as D was deep in thought, a slightly excited female voice suddenly came from behind.

"Wow! What a beautiful and wild Pokemon!"

D and a group of Pokemon turned around to see an elegant woman, dressed in an expensive pencil dress and wearing a hat, walking over, enthusiastically looking at Flutter Mane.

"If I'm not mistaken, this should be a big Misdreavus, right?"

"It's called Flutter Mane." D asked, "Is there something you need?"

"Oh haha! Don't be so tense!" The woman laughed softly: "Little brother, I really like this Pokemon, would you like to exchange it with big sister me? I can trade a very rare Snivy for your Flutter Mane!"

D thought he heard wrong: "What?"
