The woman took out a Poke Ball, and accompanied by a flash of white light, a snake-like Pokemon appeared on the deck.

It has a green body, but its abdomen and feet are cream-colored, and a yellow stripe runs from its back to its tail.

"Trading Pokemon!" the woman said, "Didn't you know? Using that machine over there, you can swap Snivy and Flutter Mane into each other's Poke Ball!"

D understood and refused without hesitation: "No thanks, I'm not interested."

The woman glanced at the few Pokemon beside him and reluctantly said: "Alright then, if you change your mind, be sure to come find big sister me!"

With that, the woman turned and left.

Flutter Mane squinted, belatedly realizing her intention.

Flutter Mane was angry, how dare this human treat it like a Berry to be traded!

It ground its teeth, about to rush out and teach that person a hard lesson.

Seeing Flutter Mane about to run off, D quickly grabbed it: "Stop right there!"

"Grrrr!"

Flutter Mane fiercely showed its little fangs.

D: "..."

D struggled hard to hold Flutter Mane back, just as Alder returned and, surprised at the scene, asked: "What's happening?"

D, somewhat helpless: "Someone wanted to trade Pokemon with me, and Flutter Mane got angry."

Hearing this, Alder paused for a moment, and when he saw the Pokemon next to D, he immediately guessed D must have refused.

After calming Flutter Mane's emotions, D finally asked: "Alder, is exchanging Pokemon commonplace in this world?"

"No, but it happens sometimes." Alder looked rather helpless: "Because not every Trainer and Pokemon can become a good team."

"Sometimes, after capturing a Pokemon, a Trainer might find they are not very compatible, and a captured Pokemon, without forming deep bonds with the Trainer, might occasionally prefer another kinder, more capable Trainer."

"At times like this, they would exchange Pokemon to form a more suitable partnership."

D understood: "I see, to be honest, this is the first time I've encountered this."

Alder sighed, "Of course, not everyone is like that. There are still people in this world who treat Pokemon as tools or pets. To them, as long as there's a better Pokemon, the old one can be freely abandoned."

This is common among some nouveau riche.

Whenever this happens, Alder thinks there might be some merit in Plasma Team's advocacy for "liberating Pokemon."

---

---

---

---

# 347: Chapter 344: Arrival in Unova, Breeder’s Advanced Training! The Mysterious Pokeblock!

Year 199 of the League era, March 28th, after a day and night of sea travel, D and Alder's luxury cruise ship officially arrived in the Unova Region.

The massive ship docked at Driftveil City's port, under the azure sky, as tourists poured off the ship like a tide into the city.

In the distance, the port was bustling, with numerous cargo ships and fishing vessels nearby.

Machamp-led Pokémon and workers were loading batches of goods onto ships, transporting them elsewhere.

Fishermen returning from the sea were sorting their catch, preparing and sending the fish to the city restaurants.

Suddenly, a gust of wind blew in from afar, whisking away some ladies' hats and causing a commotion among the crowd.

D and Alder's hair fluttered upwards, and Alder grinned, introducing loudly amidst the strong wind:

"This is Driftveil City! The constant strong wind is Driftveil City's signature specialty!"

D asked, "Is the Gym Leader here Boss Clay?"

"That's right!" Alder nodded: "But being the Gym Leader is just one of his jobs, Boss Clay's true identity is the owner of the mining company. See that tall mountain to the north?"

Upon hearing this, D looked into the distance.

He saw the towering mountain shadow standing far to the north.

Alder: "The entire Twist Mountain is Boss Clay's property!"

"Wow!" D exclaimed in amazement.

The two disembarked the cruise, and Alder deftly guided D away from the port and into the city: "The coal mined from Twist Mountain supports the entire Unova Region, it's quite impressive, isn't it? The banquet in a few days is hosted by Boss Clay himself!"

These are things not found in the games.

D listened with great interest, occasionally nodding, especially when he heard Alder mention that Boss Clay has a daughter.

"A daughter?"

"Yes! I've heard she's named Lacey or something? I can't quite remember, haven't met her a few times." Alder said.

"I see..." D nodded and didn't inquire further.

"Let's go!" Alder looked at a pager-like communicator and said: "My friend is already waiting there, I'll take you to meet her!"

"Alright." D replied sweetly.

He followed Alder forward, crossing several streets, finally reaching a giant building.

Above it was the sign "Pokemon Breeder Association Driftveil Branch".

Alder strode inside, bypassing the lobby and directly taking D up the elevator.

With a "ding" sound, the elevator stopped on the 7th floor.

Alder walked out and arrived at a warmly decorated elegant room.

A woman in casual wear, with squared glasses, was gracefully standing by the cabinet, polishing a Gible's scales.

The Gible seemed to thoroughly enjoy the activity, its eyes comfortably squinting.

Under the woman's polishing, Gible's scales gradually became clean and tidy, even emitting a bright and beautiful luster.

"Hi, Yu Hui, long time no see!" Alder greeted with a hearty laugh.

Upon hearing the voice, the woman named Yu Hui turned around, her gaze sweeping over Alder and D.

Seeing Alder's relaxed smile with no trace of gloom, her face showed surprise.

"Your mood seems quite good!" Yu Hui remarked meaningfully.

"That's because the problem bothering me for so long has finally been solved." Alder grinned, evidently in high spirits.

"Oh?" Yu Hui was astonished: "Volcarona is okay now?"

Alder nodded: "All thanks to D, his Gogoat can produce a magical milk that heals all of Volcarona's hidden injuries."

Gogoat? Milk? Yu Hui was slightly stunned, looking incredulously at the young man beside Alder.

In fact, Alder had sought many people to heal Volcarona, and as a top breeder, she was one of them.

But even she couldn't heal Volcarona, yet this young man managed to...

"Your name is D?" Yu Hui asked curiously.

D nodded: "That's right."

Yu Hui: "Are you also a Pokemon Breeder?"

"No." D shook his head: "I'm just a Rancher."

Yu Hui: "...?"

Alder laughed: "D is indeed a Rancher, but he's also an excellent Pokemon Trainer. He's here to learn how to make Pokeblocks to care for his Pokémon."

Yu Hui snapped back to reality, knowing Alder had mentioned this in a previous message.

"Since it's your request, rest assured it'll be in good hands with me!" Yu Hui agreed with a smile, not asking further.

"Thanks a lot!" Alder said, then turned to D: "Yu Hui will teach you how to make Pokeblocks. Meanwhile, I'll visit Boss Clay to inquire about the banquet details and meet you later."

"Alright." Watching Alder take the elevator downstairs, D then turned his attention to the woman before him, politely saying: "Hello, Miss Yu Hui."

---

"Follow me!" Yu Hui said, "I'll take you to the baking room where we make Pokeblocks."

The two of them went downstairs, and as they passed the hall, D saw several young people there participating in some examination.

"That's the breeder's examination." Yu Hui paused and guided D closer to observe, asking, "Do you know what a Pokemon Breeder is?"

D was slightly taken aback and then replied, "Someone who takes care of and raises Pokemon?"

"Yes and no," Yu Hui smiled, "A Pokemon Breeder refers to someone among Pokemon Trainers who specializes in cultivating and managing the health of Pokemon."

"Helping to groom Pokemon to make them look vibrant, and crafting different Pokemon foods according to each Pokemon's needs, are all easy tasks for a breeder."

"In addition, the field of breeders also includes Pokemon Researchers and Pokemon Doctors. In short, it's a profession with a very broad scope."

At this point, the two arrived next to the examination area.

D saw a Pokemon standing in front of each of the young people.

They were walking around these Pokemon, as if observing something.

Yu Hui: "This is a skill that must be mastered to become a Pokemon Breeder, determining their condition through their appearance and expressions, and providing corresponding improvement plans."

D became interested immediately: "Can you really tell so much just from appearance and expressions?"

"Of course." Yu Hui smiled slightly, lifted her foot, and walked toward Koraidon, who was standing next to D, beginning to observe this unfamiliar Pokemon.

"A strong aura of Dragon Type Energy... This Pokemon is a Dragon Type Pokemon."

"Its body is very robust, those well-developed muscles and body prove the richness of the nutrition and calories in its usual diet."

"Its eyes are bright, this Pokemon is currently in a very good mood. It seems it is very happy by your side, its eyes glow when it looks at you. This Pokemon must really like you."

"It looks like you take good care of it!" Yu Hui smiled, "If you regularly polish the scales on this Pokemon, its condition would become even more perfect!"

"Clap clap clap!" D clapped his hands in admiration, "Miss Yu Hui is amazing!"

"May I know the name of this Pokemon?" Yu Hui asked.

D nodded, reached out to pat Koraidon's head as it approached, and introduced, "Its name is Koraidon, a Pokemon that has always been by my side."

"Koraidon..." Yu Hui murmured the name once and then said, "I'll remember it. Let's go! Next, I'll teach you how to make Pokeblocks!"

With Yu Hui leading, D arrived at a baking room.

Please maintain these HTML tags () in your final output.

It was filled with many specialized machines and refrigerators.

Yu Hui introduced, "To make Pokeblocks, you need a specialized berry processor. This machine mixes the berries put into it, and after a series of processes, it makes them into Pokeblocks that drop out from the exit."

The berry processor... it reminded D of the Berry Blender from the games.

He asked, "Does the machine make Pokeblocks automatically?" If it's all automated, then making Pokeblocks seems too simple?

Yu Hui shook her head, "Although the machine is fully automated, if you just put in berries randomly, it will produce Pokeblocks so unpleasant that they can make a Pokemon faint. Your ranch should also grow berries, right?"

D responded, "Yes, that's right."

Yu Hui: "Then you should also know that different berries have different flavors, nutrition, and uses. Some berries are spicy but very nutritious, while some are sweet but don't contain much nutrition."

"A qualified breeder will select berries with high compatibility and make use of all the berries' strengths. Only this way can you make high-quality Pokeblocks."

"Although hundreds of berries can be combined, sometimes just one different berry can dramatically change the effect of the Pokeblock."

D asked with interest, "So, Pokeblocks are made by randomly pairing those berries?"

Yu Hui nodded slightly, "You could say that. I have a basic Pokeblock recipe here, give it a try!"

With that, Yu Hui handed over a note.

D reached out and took it, seeing that it read:

[Pokeblock Recipe: Aspear Berry x1, Lapapa Berry x1, Pinap Berry x1, Effect: Pokemon consuming this Pokeblock over a long period will become stronger in body.]

"To make Pokeblocks, first wash the berries clean," Yu Hui instructed.

D nodded, went to the washing area, and began carefully cleaning the skins of the three berries.

When washing the Pinap Berry, D suddenly thought of a question, "Do these berries need peeling?"

Yu Hui: "Some require peeling, and some don't. For Pinap Berries, you need to remove the excess peel and only use the flesh. The same goes for Nanab Berries, whereas Pecha Berries don't need peeling. The specifics depend on the properties of each berry."

---

D roughly understood.

He carefully washed and peeled the Berries, then placed them into the Berry processor.

After waiting for nearly fifteen minutes, with a ding, a few dozen Pokeblocks, about the size of glass marbles, rolled out from the outlet at the bottom of the processor.

D picked up one.

In the next second, the information of this Pokeblock appeared in his mind.

[Sturdy Pokeblock (C): A Pokeblock made from Aspear Berry, Lapapa Berry, and Pinap Berry, with a sour taste.

Cooking Effect: Level 1 Strong Polishing, after eating, the Pokemon will polish its strength, the nutrients inside will make the body stronger, gradually building muscle.

Evaluation: The most basic Pokeblock, the first choice of Coordinator Trainers participating in the Glamorous Contest.]

"Looks like you succeeded on your first try!" Yu Hui laughed, "These Pokeblocks are almost impurity-free, meaning their quality is quite good."

"Humans can eat these Pokeblocks too, right?" D asked.

"Of course."

Upon hearing this, D tossed the Pokeblock in his hand into his mouth and chewed twice, then... frowned at the sourness.

Yu Hui chuckled, "Very sour, right? This is just the case for us humans. The tolerance of Pokemon is completely different from that of humans. Some foods that are hard to swallow for us are rare delicacies for Pokemon."

Upon hearing this, D fed a Pokeblock to Koraidon, who also stuck out its tongue at the sourness, starting to salivate excessively.

D: "..." Is there something wrong with his production process?

Yu Hui smiled, "It seems you've successfully learned how to make Pokeblocks, so next, try developing your own Pokeblock recipes!"

"I have other things to do. If you need anything, you can contact me via the phone over there. Feel free to use the Berries here."

After saying this, Yu Hui turned and left to attend to her own matters.

D wasn't worried. Now that he roughly knew how to make Pokeblocks, it was time to practice and become proficient.

So, after Yu Hui left, D stayed behind and continued to practice, trying to develop his own Pokeblock recipes.

This process was not smooth.

The first time, an excessive amount of Berries caused the processor to explode and emit smoke, halting operation.

The second time, the wrong mix of Berries resulted in Pokeblocks full of impurities and an extremely awful taste. Koraidon spat it out after one bite and almost fainted.

The third time, D learned from past mistakes and used several Berries he was more familiar with.

This time, he finally succeeded in making Pokeblocks again.

[Nutritious Pokeblock (D-): Effect, provides minimal nutrition needed for Pokemon's body, Evaluation: The low compatibility of the Berries led to very poor quality Pokeblocks.]

Looking at this hard-earned Pokeblock, D couldn't help but wipe the sweat from his forehead.

Indeed, making Pokeblocks without a recipe was incredibly difficult.

This was not something an average person could produce.

But D did not give up. He discarded these unpalatable Pokeblocks and continued exploring.

As time ticked by, the process of randomly combining Berries to make Pokeblocks felt like a lottery, truly addictive.

After countless failures, D finally succeeded in making a second kind of Pokeblock.

This one was a strangely colored Pokeblock, blue-pink with purple spots, resembling a poisonous mushroom and looking downright awful.

Moreover, this Pokeblock emitted an indefinably awful smell.

The moment Koraidon saw this Pokeblock, it immediately retreated several meters, pressing itself against the wall and looking over with a face full of fear.

D: "..."

It's just a Pokeblock, is it really that frightening?

While grumbling inwardly, D looked down, and the information of these Pokeblocks emerged in his mind.

[Poipole Delicacy (B+): A foul-tasting Pokeblock that can make a Pokemon faint upon consumption, but for Poipole, it's a rare delicacy.

Cooking Effect: Poipole Special Attack, Poipole's favorite flavor, placing it in the wild has a chance to attract Poipole, this effect is only effective for Poipole.

Evaluation: A Pokeblock exclusively for Poipole.]

# 348: Chapter 345: Banquet of Elites

Huh? Poipole?

Isn't that an Ultra Beast from the Alola Region?

How come there's a Pokeblock with this kind of effect?

Looking at the oddly colored Pokeblock in front of him, D was a bit surprised.

Just putting it out in the wild has a certain chance of attracting a Pokemon like Poipole...

This effect reminded D of the way players capture Pokemon in the Safari Zone in the game.

In the Safari Zone, Pokemon battles are not allowed, so the conventional methods of capture simply won't work there.

To catch a Pokemon, Trainers can only use Pokeblocks bought at the entrance to lure those wild Pokemon and then capture them.

However, Pokeblocks specific to certain Pokemon like this one are still very rare.

D pinched the Pokeblock in his hand and looked at the taster Koraidon, asking, "Wanna give it a try?"

Koraidon shook his head vigorously, "Aga-sii!"

It poked at the Poke Ball attached to D's waist, looking troubled, indicating that it really couldn't eat anymore, and suggested that D could let other Pokemon try instead.

"True." D nodded, then took off Cyclizar's Poke Ball from his waist.

Along with a flash of white light, Cyclizar's figure appeared in the room.

"Gao~" Cyclizar blinked, looking all innocent.

D picked up a Pokeblock and tentatively asked, "Would you like to help me taste this Pokeblock, Cyclizar?"

Smelling that Pokeblock, Cyclizar immediately showed a serious expression.

It instinctively wanted to refuse, but seeing D's expression seemed to really need its Help.

So, Cyclizar gathered its courage, held its breath, took a Pokeblock with its claw, and put it in its mouth.

After chewing it a few times, Cyclizar suddenly fell to the ground, fainted by the awful taste.

D: "..."

Koraidon had a look of relief on its face.

"Aga-sii..." Luckily, it didn't eat it.

D couldn't help but fall into deep thought; these Pokeblocks are all made from Berries, so theoretically speaking, they shouldn't be this awful?

After thinking for a moment, he pinched his nose and tried eating a Pokeblock himself like trying durian for the first time.

Bitter, sour, astringent—a taste indescribably rushed straight to his brain.

The next second, D felt as if his head started steaming and his stomach began churning wildly.

He turned around and buried his face in the trash bin—"Ugh!"

When Yu Hui came in after finishing her work, she immediately saw D hugging the trash bin, vomiting like crazy.

Seeing this scene, she immediately knew what was going on.

Yu Hui burst into laughter, "Looks like you've made some awful Pokeblocks!"

D, with a pale face, looked up, "Miss Yu Hui..."

"Don't worry, this is a journey every Pokemon Breeder must go through." Yu Hui said with a smile.

She stepped forward to check D's Pokeblock-making setup, then said," It looks like you've grasped the basic method of making Pokeblocks, so next, I should teach you more advanced content."

D and Cyclizar sat side by side, drinking a bottle of sugar water to ease the discomfort in their stomachs.

Hearing this, he was a bit surprised, "More advanced content?"

Yu Hui nodded, "Only beginner Breeders use machines to make Pokeblocks, and some top Breeders often handcraft Pokeblocks, taking their time to refine them."

"Compared to the former, the latter is more difficult, but correspondingly, Pokeblocks meticulously crafted by Breeders tend to have better quality. Here, I've written down the crafting process for you to take a look at."

Yu Hui handed over a piece of paper filled with words.

D reached out to take it, seeing the whole process of handcrafting Pokeblocks written on it.

Baking, melting, simmering, mixing, compressing, solidifying...

Each step was very complex and consumed several times the duration a machine would.

Yu Hui: "Machines can only make limited types of Pokeblocks because their capacity is fixed and can only mix a maximum amount equivalent to five Berries."

"However, with handcrafting, it's not necessarily like that. For example, the famous Gluttony Pokeblock, that special Pokeblock was made through handcrafting."

"By fusing the essence of dozens of Berries, it's finally compressed into a small Pokeblock, which can feed a gluttonous Pokemon like Snorlax with just one."

Hearing this, D immediately understood.

"So, certain specific Pokeblocks can only be made by hand?" he asked.

"That's right," Yu Hui nodded, "Machines are machines, after all, even large-scale Berry processors can't compare to humans, because cold machines can't create Pokeblocks with warmth and emotion."

D nodded thoughtfully.

Previously, he thought if the process of making Pokeblocks was so simple, he could just buy a machine and be done with it.

But now, it seemed that just that wasn't enough.

"Here you go!" Yu Hui handed over another recipe: "This is a basic Gluttony Pokeblock recipe. The Pokeblocks made from it can fill up the stomach of a Pokemon like Munchlax with just one. Why don't you try making it?"

D reached out to take it.

[Basic Gluttony Pokeblock Recipe: Pecha Berry x2, Oran Berry x1, Bluk Berry x1, Tamato Berry x2, 1/3 of a Nanab Berry, Effect: Provides basic nutrition to Pokemon and can fill up a Pokemon's stomach.]

---

Yu Hui: "There's a pot over there. You can give it a try first. I'll guide you from the side."

"Thank you, Miss Yu Hui."

D expressed his gratitude, then found the corresponding Berries recorded in the recipe, peeled, cored, and washed them clean.

Then, in order, he placed these Berries into the pot to stew and melt until they formed a crystal-clear jam.

Yu Hui observed from the side: "There are very few impurities inside, meeting the qualified standards. It seems your cleaning work is quite thorough. Next, add the flavoring Nanab Berry."

"This kind of Berry is soft and easily melts, so it should be added last. Its role is to add sweetness to the Pokeblock, improving the taste."

D watched the pot intently. Seeing it was almost done, he turned off the heat, then took out the jam, sifted out the residue, and poured it into a professional machine for compression.

When the compression ended, he proceeded to the final step, placing it into molds to solidify.

Soon, all the preparation work was completed, and five steaming hot Pokeblocks made from seven Berries were freshly produced.

D looked down, and the information about these Pokeblocks surfaced in his mind.

[Beginner Gluttony Pokeblock (C+): Cooking Effect, Gluttony, is a Pokeblock that can satisfy the hunger of Pokemon. Just one piece can fill up a Munchlax and Pokemon with similar appetites beneath it.]

"Not bad!" Yu Hui cast an approving glance: "The hand-making of Pokeblocks is similar. Breeders need to explore different recipes themselves. Besides this method, you can also purchase developed recipes from other Breeders."

"This is also a way for Breeders to earn money. If one day you discover a rare Pokeblock recipe, you can sell it to the Breeder Association and other Breeders."

Upon hearing this, D immediately thought of the [Poipole Delicacy] he had just researched, a Pokeblock that could be used to attract Pokemon like Poipole.

It's just that Poipole isn't in the Unova Region. Although the Ultra Wormhole appears randomly across the world, the probability of encountering it here is still too low.

"By the way, Miss Yu Hui, do you have a pen? I want to record the Pokeblock recipe I just made." D said, mainly worried he might forget it later.

"Of course." Yu Hui took a pen out of her pocket.

D wrote down the recipe for the Poipole Delicacy.

To solidify the method of making Pokeblocks, D stayed at the Breeder Association for practice over the next few days.

During this period, Alder only came once or twice. Upon learning that D was in training, he didn't disturb him and turned to continue with his own business.

Volcarona's body had already healed, and the position of Champion in the Unova League was still vacant. Until a new Champion appeared, Alder felt he had to take on the duties of a Champion, guiding more Trainers and Pokemon to meet.

The sudden return and change of Alder caught the other four Elites of the Unova League completely off guard.

Especially after learning that the once-nearly-dead Volcarona had returned to its peak, the shock was even greater.

While D was busy practicing Pokeblock making, the outside world was also undergoing huge changes.

Driftveil City was particularly bustling these days.

Luxury cruise ships, private planes, and special flights were arriving in Driftveil City in succession.

Famous entrepreneur Giovanni from the Kanto Region, the inheritor of the Dragon Taming Clan and Dragon Elite and Champion from the Kanto Region, Lance, and top figure in the Pokemon Research Realm, Professor Oak.

Mr. Stone, president of the Devon Company from the Hoenn Region.

Sinnoh Region's strongest Champion, Cynthia, and the Psychic Elite Caitlin and Ghost Elite Shauntal from the Unova Region.

Champion and big star Diantha from the Kalos Region.

And numerous prestigious families from the Paldea Region, the boss of Rotom Technology Company, and his daughter Nemona.

*

Four days later, D finally mastered the skill of making Pokeblocks proficiently.

That afternoon, Alder came to the Breeder Association to find D, who was immersed in mixing Berries to create Pokeblocks.

"How's it going?" Alder asked.

"Almost there!" D let out a sigh of relief: "Thanks to Miss Yu Hui, I can now make special Pokeblocks."

"If that's the case, then let's go!" Alder laughed.

D was slightly taken aback: "What?"

Alder: "The time has come, that banquet is about to start!"

D finally realized what Alder was talking about, astonished: "So soon?!"

Alder twitched his lips: "It's been four days since you arrived in the Unova Region. I just received the news that all the guests attending the banquet have arrived, we're the only ones left."

Upon hearing this, D immediately put down what he was doing, not forgetting the main purpose of his visit to the Unova Region—to deliver the Rotom Smartphone project plan to the boss of Rotom Technology Company.

The time had now reached four in the afternoon, and the banquet was about to officially start.

Before leaving, D originally wanted to find Yu Hui to express his thanks and say goodbye, but he learned from others that Yu Hui had left the Pokemon Association to travel to other cities.

---

D found himself at a loss for words.

Alder, on the other hand, offered some comfort: "Don't worry! She surely knows how you feel. I'll thank her for you later!"

D: "I'll leave it to you then." For now, this seemed like the only option.

So, D stepped out of the Breeder Association's door, setting off with Alder towards the location of the banquet.

Alder was driving a car through the streets of Driftveil City. After passing several streets, the car stopped in front of a luxurious mansion at the city's edge.

By evening, many people had already gathered there. The manor was brightly illuminated.

Gentlemen in suits, elegantly dressed high-society ladies, boys and girls in tuxedos and evening dresses, and some young people dressed as Trainers were all entering the manor.

Just looking at it felt very high-class, definitely the gathering place for the elite.

D, who had only ever seen such a scene in TV dramas, felt like a country bumpkin entering the city for the first time, inevitably feeling a bit nervous inside.

Everyone present was a high-society lady, while he was the only farmer from the countryside. It felt so strange...

Alder turned off the car: "We're here. This place is huge, right? It's all Boss Clay's property!"

D: "Oh!"

Alder turned his head and saw D looking a bit nervous.

He couldn't help but chuckle and said, "Are you very nervous?"

D wasn't afraid of Alder laughing at him: "I'm just a farmer, and I've never been to a place like this."

His identity here was indeed very out of place.

"Don't worry!" Alder reassured him: "Even as a farmer, you're the best farmer there is! Later, have a battle with them. If a Trainer can't even beat a farmer, they should be the nervous ones!"

D: "..."

Alder got out of the car, unconcerned about D, and started calling for Koraidon in the back: "Koraidon, get out of the car, I'll take you to have some delicious food!"

"Gaaaah!" Hunger having been appeased only with Pokeblocks for the past few days, Koraidon lit up at the prospect of other food. It jumped out of the car and followed Alder, looking back at D.

D followed with a helpless expression.

Thanks to Alder's reassurance, he felt less apprehensive.

After all, Alder had a point; stay calm, neither overbearing nor servile, and there would be no problem.

Thinking this, D felt much more at ease.

Though Alder appeared rough on the outside, he was actually someone who took good care of others.

He deliberately avoided others he knew and led D and Koraidon through the garden, quickly arriving at an area with a variety of gourmet food and drinks.

At this moment, many guests dressed as Trainers and Pokemon had already gathered there, enjoying the food alongside their Pokemon.

Alder also released his Volcarona, letting it and Koraidon feast on large drumsticks similar to those Luffy would eat.

He himself grabbed two glasses of red wine from a passing waiter, handing one to D: "Have a drink first! There's still some time before the banquet starts."

D reached out to take it and sipped it alongside Alder. Just as he was about to say something, he suddenly heard a voice from behind.

"Huh? Mr. Alder?"

Upon hearing the voice, Alder turned around, not surprised at all to see who it was, and instead laughed heartily: "It's Shauntal and Caitlin, huh? Long time no see, how have you been lately?"

The blond girl named Caitlin showed a slightly exasperated expression: "You're still the same as ever!"

As the three conversed, D turned his gaze to the blonde woman accompanying Shauntal and Caitlin, who hadn't spoken.

Their eyes met in mid-air. Cynthia politely nodded slightly, then looked toward Alder: "Long time no see, Alder."

"You're here for this banquet too, Miss Cynthia? I thought I wouldn't see you this year!" Alder said with a laugh.

Cynthia's eyes glimmered with amusement.

Meanwhile, Shauntal and Caitlin sneaked glances at the young man standing beside Alder.

Alder: "Oh, I almost forgot, I haven't introduced you yet. This is my friend, Champion Trainer from Paldea Region, D."

"D, these three are the Ghost Elite Shauntal and Psychic Elite Caitlin from the Unova Region. And this lady over here, she has quite the reputation, she is the Champion of the Sinnoh Region, Cynthia."

---

---

---

---

# 349: Chapter 346: The Young Lady Nemona Comes to Challenge!

"Hello, everyone." D greeted the three people in front of him with poise.

Holding a book in her arms and wearing black-framed glasses, Shauntal smiled gently in greeting.

Born into a wealthy family, Caitlin was naturally proud. Although she didn't mean to make friends, she couldn't help but glance at him when she heard he was a friend of Alder's.

Beside them, Cynthia nodded politely, wearing a gentle smile that didn't quite match her cold exterior: "Hello there."

Seeing this, D couldn't help but think: Cynthia doesn't seem as aloof as she looks!

But then again, he remembered that Cynthia's personality in the anime was just like this.

Though she appeared graceful and serene, like an ice queen, she was actually gentle and unassuming, without any airs of a strong person.

"Alright, alright, we won't disturb you anymore!" Alder grinned and said, "See you later!"

The three nodded, turned around, and left. Shauntal pulled Caitlin, preparing to find a quiet place to discuss novel details with Cynthia.

Before leaving, Cynthia looked somewhat surprised at the unfamiliar red Pokemon enjoying the feast.

Once the three left, Alder said, "That Shauntal is not only an Elite of the Unova League but also a very accomplished novelist. She used to visit Cynthia with Caitlin to discuss novel details."

D curiously asked, "Isn't the League's salary enough? I've noticed many Champion Elites take on side jobs while holding their positions."

Like that big star from the Kalos Region...

"No." Alder shook his head. "It's probably out of interest, right? Life is so long, you can't be a trainer forever; you have to find other things to do."

D: "Understood."

As the sky gradually darkened, guests arriving for the banquet came one after another.

These people were divided into two groups: one composed of wealthy entrepreneurs from various regions, who gathered to chat and discuss industry-related topics.

The other group consisted of trainers from around the world. Alder said these people were talented trainers from other places, all quite skilled.

The host of the banquet prepared a large plastic battlefield in the yard for these trainers to interact.

The banquet hadn't officially started, and those trainers were already friendly sparring around the battlefield.

D and Alder, along with Koraidon and Volcarona, became spectators, enjoying the banquet's food while watching the battles from afar.

Initially, D felt a bit reserved, wondering if it was inappropriate to be a glutton at such a banquet, making him seem like he was just there for free food.

Then D realized his worries were completely unnecessary.

Many trainers brought their Pokemon to indulge freely, even Alder joined in, gnawing on a steak with no regard for image.

D finally felt at ease.

The two hadn't eaten dinner, so they took the opportunity to fill their stomachs.

As soon as they finished eating, they saw the wealthy guests chatting in the yard begin to move to the banquet hall inside the manor.

In the banquet hall, the host Boss Clay stood on stage, smiling as he addressed everyone:

"Welcome to Boss Clay's banquet. We've prepared rich food and wine for everyone and your Pokemon. The time has come; please, enjoy the banquet!"

D and Alder stayed in an inconspicuous corner, watching the scene.

The real-life Boss Clay had little difference from his image in the game and was easy to recognize. D remembered his appearance, so he immediately recognized him.

However, D was surprised by the girl standing next to Boss Clay.

She was a girl with pink hair, wearing a pink knit sweater.

D: "Is that girl Boss Clay's daughter?"

Alder nodded: "Yes, her name is Lacey!"

"Lacey..." D murmured the name.

Then, he looked around, trying to find other familiar faces in the crowd.

Soon, D spotted several targets.

A handsome young man with a cape, a proper face, and a righteous aura was standing nearby, arms crossed, looking ahead.

"Lance." D's mind popped up with the man's name.

A man in a white lab coat, smiling while chatting with others—Professor Oak.

An exquisitely dressed person in a white evening dress, shining like a star on stage—Diantha.

And a person in a black suit, with meticulously combed hair, an eagle-like sharp, cool gaze, accepting a toast with a faint smile—Giovanni.

Whoa! Giovanni's really here?

Even though he had expected it, when D saw Giovanni's strong presence, he couldn't help but show a surprised expression.

Just then, Giovanni, who was chatting with the wealthy individuals, seemed to sense something, slowly turned his head.

The next second, their eyes met in mid-air.

Giovanni still wore that faint smile, as if he had casually glanced over.

---

D could feel a gaze lingering on him for a moment, then it turned away.

"What's the matter? Did you see someone you know?" A tycoon across the table teased.

"It's nothing." Giovanni's tone was indifferent: "Just an insignificant person, come, let's continue."

The two resumed their discussion about their collaboration.

D recalled the scene in his mind, and couldn't help but exclaim in his heart: "So sharp! As expected of Boss Giovanni from Team Rocket!"

Meanwhile, Alder also spotted the CEO of Rotom Technology Company among the group of tycoons, and he motioned:

"That's the person you're looking for. Didn't you write a proposal before? Do you want me to pass it on to him?"

D nodded: "Sure."

But just then, a crisp voice suddenly came from the front.

"Boss Clay, I want to challenge you!"

D looked up and saw Nemona, dressed in a little skirt, standing spiritedly in front of Boss Clay.

She held a Poke Ball in her hand, clearly intending to battle Boss Clay in a Pokemon match.

Boss Clay laughed heartily at her words: "As expected of Miss Nemona! Alright, you're full of spirit. In that case, let me have a great match with you!"

After speaking, Boss Clay turned to the others: "You guys carry on, don't mind us!"

The other tycoons jokingly teased little Nemona a bit.

Nemona's father, a middle-aged man wearing glasses and a suit, looked helpless, seemingly quite troubled by his daughter's behavior.

He had two daughters. The eldest was born early and learned all sorts of manners from a young age, and unsurprisingly grew up to become an all-around wealthy lady.

So, he and his wife adopted a laissez-faire approach when raising their second daughter, Nemona.

This resulted in Nemona's carefree personality, that free-spirited, fiery demeanor, which was hardly like that of a graceful young lady from a wealthy family.

But what could they do?

She was their precious daughter, and as long as she was happy, that was enough.

Thinking this, the man followed his daughter and Boss Clay to the battle arena in the courtyard.

Nemona was the first to send out her Pokemon.

It was a young dragon baby on all fours, with scales of varying sizes covering it, the most notable being the heart-shaped scale on top of its head.

"A pseudo-legendary Pokemon from the Alola Region, Jangmo-o." D silently recited the Pokemon's name in his mind.

Jangmo-o, which would eventually evolve into the powerful Kommo-o.

No wonder, being the daughter of a big boss... Nemona hadn't even turned ten to start her journey, yet she already had a highly talented pseudo-legendary Pokemon.

This kind of start is simply not something those random Insect-Catching Youths on the roadside can compare to!

In response to Nemona's Jangmo-o, Boss Clay sent out an Excadrill to battle.

Jangmo-o versus Excadrill.

One was a novice Pokemon just starting out, while the other was a Gym Leader level Excadrill that had undergone professional training and plenty of honing.

The outcome of this battle was decided even before it began.

But Boss Clay seemed to have his own plans.

With one look, Excadrill understood his intention. It cooperated willingly with its trainer, putting on an act, deliberately being hit by Jangmo-o's moves, and dramatically took a fall under everyone's gaze.

Excadrill couldn't get back up.

The attendant, acting as referee, stepped forward to examine, then declared: "Excadrill is unable to continue battling. This match is won by Miss Nemona!"

Boss Clay retrieved the "fainted" Excadrill with a Poke Ball, and with a "troubled" expression, said: "Oh dear! We lost! You're getting better and better, little girl!"

Though Nemona was carefree, she wasn't stupid. She instantly saw through Boss Clay's playfulness.

"You did it on purpose!"

Boss Clay, wearing an innocent face, shrugged: "No, it's because you've improved!"

"Ahhh!" Nemona was very angry, but unable to find a retort, she finally pouted and shouted: "I'm not talking to you anymore!"

"Hahaha!"

The tycoons present were amused by Nemona's antics.

Nemona glanced around. She was about to turn and run off to find another strong Trainer to battle, but just then, she suddenly spotted a familiar figure in the crowd.

Nemona's eyes lit up, and she enthusiastically waved: "Mr. D! You came to the party too!"

As Nemona's words fell, everyone present turned their gaze toward him in unison.

D: "..."

Don't come here, little Nemona!

But Nemona, being her usual self, ran over: "Ah! Koraidon is here too!"

Koraidon: "Ah gahss~"

Seeing Koraidon still remembered her, Nemona was even more delighted: "Mr. D, I challenge you!"

At this moment, Boss Clay and the CEO of Rotom Technology Company also walked over.

Upon seeing Alder, the two immediately spotted the young man standing beside him and astonishingly asked: "Who might this be?"

Alder: "He's a friend of mine, the Champion Trainer from the Paldea Region, D! It's been a while, Boss Clay, Nikar."

Boss Clay, mindful that Alder's Volcarona had recovered, linked it to their previous conversation and instantly deduced the relationship between this young man named D and Alder.

---

It was he who saved Alder's Volcarona.

But how did he do it? With injuries of that severity, even modern medical technology can't completely heal them…

Boss Clay looked over curiously.

Nikar also looked a bit surprised at this young champion from the Paldea Region.

How did Nemona get to know someone like this?

Although he is a big boss, compared to that, the League's champion-level trainers command more respect.

Yet Nemona couldn't care less about all these twists and turns; she happily looked at D: "Mr. D, please have a Pokemon battle with me!"

D looked helpless.

At this time, Alder laughed heartily: "In that case, D, why don't you have some fun with Nemona! I'll have a chat with these old friends!"

"Alright."

Seeing this, D could only nod and agree.

He was pulled to the battlefield by Nemona, and amid the watching trainers, D said to Nemona, "No Koraidon."

"Huh?" Nemona looked disappointed, but quickly cheered up: "Then which Pokemon did you bring this time, Mr. D?"

Hearing this, D immediately thought of which Pokemon to battle Nemona with.

That would be Alcremie.

In terms of strength, Alcremie is the weakest among the Pokemon he carries with him.

Moreover, Alcremie hasn't gone through any special training and spends every day playing around, with little growth in strength.

It should be able to have a back-and-forth match with Nemona's Pokemon.

"I've already decided which Pokemon to use," D said.

"Then let's start!" Nemona excitedly ran to the other side of the battlefield.

Seeing this scene, D felt somewhat helpless but still took out Alcremie's Poke Ball.

Some trainers remained around to watch.

Because they just heard Alder's words and knew that this young man battling the little girl was a champion-level trainer from another region.

Champion-level trainers are rare, so these people wanted to see a champion-level trainer's battle and the Pokemon he raised.

D didn't hold back, and under the expectant eyes of everyone, threw the Poke Ball in his hand.

Accompanied by a flash of white light, a small-sized, adorable-looking Alcremie appeared on the battlefield, exuding a sweet aroma.

Seeing this scene, some of the watching trainers showed a stunned expression.

"Is that Pokemon… Alcremie?"

"Alcremie looks so soft! I feel like I could swallow it in one bite…"

"Is the champion really going to use this Alcremie to battle?"

"Seems fine, after all, his opponent is a little girl not even ten years old."

"So true."

At this moment, Nemona also saw the Alcremie across from her.

Her eyes lit up, "Is it Alcremie? I've never battled with a Pokemon like Alcremie before! Well then, let's begin, Mr. D!"

As she finished speaking, Nemona threw a red-and-white ball.

A flash of white light, and a Goomy appeared opposite Alcremie.

Goomy, a pseudo-legendary Pokemon from the Kalos Region.

Another pseudo-legendary! D said, "You go first!"

"No need to hold back!" Nemona refused, "Mr. D, let's do this together!"

D was slightly startled, then chuckled and said, "Alright."

"Go, Goomy!" Nemona shifted into battle mode.

Both gave their commands at the same time.

"Goomy, use Bubble Attack!"

"Alcremie, Metronome."

---

---

---

---

# 350: Chapter 347: Ultra-Extrovert Nemona

As the command was issued, both Pokemon reacted simultaneously.

The small, slime-like Goomy concentrated the Water Type energy within its body, blowing countless bubbles towards Alcremie.

Alcremie, on the other hand, gently wiggled its fingers.

A few observing Trainers immediately recognized this move.

"It's the luck-based Metronome!"

"I wonder which move that Alcremie will come up with."

Metronome is a unique move, as it has no inherent power and doesn't provide any additional boost.

However, it can randomly execute any move from all possible types.

Many scholars who have studied Pokemon moves have researched this move, but they never discovered its mechanism.

In the end, these scholars could only attribute it to divine favor.

The seemingly harmless bubbles filled the air, surging towards Alcremie.

Alcremie stood still, filled with determination, and continued to wiggle its fingers.

Suddenly, the finger-wiggling stopped.

A sound like a silver bell, "Mā mā," came from Alcremie's mouth.

Immediately, countless Razor Leaves shot out from its side, heading towards the bubbles.

The two different types of moves collided in midair; the fragile bubbles were completely destroyed by the sharp Razor Leaves.

The remaining, intact leaves then swooshed towards Goomy.

The Bubble Attack was breached! Seeing this, Nemona immediately shouted, "Protect!"

Goomy instantly curled up, fiercely resisting the impact of the Razor Leaves.

"Great, successfully protected!" Nemona's eyes brightened: "What an exciting battle, I'm getting fired up! Again, Mr. D! Prepare for our next attack, Goomy, Water Gun!"

D didn't know what to say for a moment.

Because that Goomy seemed too fragile, it felt like any one of Alcremie's Fairy Type moves could knock it out instantly.

As for the other Pokemon...

Flutter Mane, Pawmot, Koraidon... in comparison, Alcremie was the only Pokemon that Goomy might stand a chance against.

Given this, everything depended on Nemona's luck.

After all, Alcremie's chance of drawing a powerful move was increased, but it wasn't guaranteed to draw those strong moves.

Therefore, D continued: "Metronome again."

"Mā mā!"

Metronome activated, and when faced with the incoming Water Gun, Metronome drew Protect.

Alcremie successfully protected itself.

"Ahhhh! We need to get serious!" Nemona was getting more and more fired up; after a miss, she immediately called out the next attack: "Goomy, get close and use Iron Tail!"

"Mā mā!"

Goomy immediately started crawling quickly across the battlefield, closing in on Alcremie.

In this situation, D still ordered: "Continue with Metronome!"

"Mā mā!" Alcremie answered, then continued to wiggle its fingers.

In the next moment, an enormous Water Energy emanated from Alcremie.

Almost instantly, countless beams of blue-white light interweaved and shot out from around Alcremie, overwhelming Goomy in brilliant radiance.

This scene immediately drew some Trainers' attention.

They stared intently at Alcremie, muttering, "What is that move?"

"Hyper Beam?"

"No! Doesn't seem like it! I've never seen any Pokemon use that move before!"

Nemona's mouth formed an O shape.

The referee, serving as the judge, stepped forward to check Goomy's condition and then announced: "Goomy is unable to battle! This match is won by Mr. D!"

"Amazing!" Nemona wasn't at all disheartened by the loss.

D had a slightly embarrassed but polite smile: "One day, you'll reach this level too."

"What was that move Alcremie used earlier?" Nemona eagerly came over to ask.

D shook his head, not revealing the Origin Pulse: "I'm not really sure; the moves summoned by Metronome are entirely random, and before today, I had never encountered it."

The surrounding Trainers who heard this weren't surprised at all, as Metronome sometimes summons moves that even Trainers haven't seen before.

They were just a bit in awe of Alcremie's luck.

Seeing no one noticing, D quietly sighed in relief.

However, does this mean he's kind of bullying a kid?

Thinking about it, D smiled and asked, "Nemona, care for another battle?"

As a battle enthusiast, Nemona agreed without hesitation: "Sure, sure!"

So Nemona sent out Duskull.

And D gave Koraidon a look.

Koraidon immediately understood his intention and took the stage to play along with D.

A few rounds later, due to its slow speed, Koraidon was hit critically by Duskull's attack and lay motionless on the ground, losing its fighting ability.

Upon seeing this, Nemona immediately understood what had happened.

She widened her eyes, with a disbelieving expression: "Mr. D, even you!"

D, with a calm demeanor, replied: "Nemona is just too strong." This way, it wouldn't be considered bullying a child, right?

---

Seeing Nemona looking upset, D quickly stepped forward and took a bottle of sheep milk from his backpack, changing the subject: "Come on, let's restore your Goomy's strength first!"

The surrounding Trainers gradually dispersed, as other strong Trainers started their own battles.

The crowd here slowly dispersed, and Nemona took out the Poke Ball of her Goomy and released it.

At this time, Goomy had already opened its eyes, but it looked very weak.

D handed the Life Sheep Milk to Nemona, letting her feed it to Goomy.

With a gulp of sheep milk, the injuries on Goomy's body healed visibly fast.

Seeing this, Nemona widened her eyes in shock: "Amazing, so amazing!"

D grinned and said, "Amazing, right? This is a special product from my ranch! No matter how badly a Pokemon is injured, just one bottle restores them immediately."

"Wow!" Little Nemona was immediately captivated by the magical effect of the sheep milk.

At this moment, Caitlin's voice suddenly came from behind:

"Did Alder's Volcarona recover from its injuries by drinking this sheep milk?"

D turned around and saw Caitlin and her group standing not far away, watching them.

Caitlin, Shauntal, and Cynthia looked curious.

Shauntal gave a soft smile in their direction, feeling a little embarrassed: "Sorry, we didn't mean to eavesdrop. We were just at the back of this garden and accidentally overheard."

"It's okay." D smiled and seized the opportunity to pitch to these Elite Champions: "If you ever need any, you can order from my ranch anytime."

Alder is a living advertisement; the effect of the sheep milk is self-explanatory.

"Very interesting." Caitlin showed an intrigued look: "I've never seen such sheep milk before."

D smiled and said, "This is the only place in the world to get it!"

After all, gathering Grassy Seed, Berry Milk, and a Tree of Beginning Leaf is extremely challenging for others, nearly impossible.

"Are you a Rancher?" Caitlin shifted her attention from the sheep milk to him.

D nodded: "That's right."

Shauntal, beside him, was very curious: "Then why did Alder say you are a Champion?"

Whenever this topic came up, D was quite helpless.

He accepted being called a Trainer because he had been training his own Pokemon recently. He barely considered himself a semi-amateur.

As for being a regional Champion... he couldn't accept that. It would be embarrassing if he got found out unless he participated in the Champion exam and passed, but that seemed pointless.

D had no clue what Alder had imagined.

After a brief thought, he explained, "Maybe there's something that made Mr. Alder misunderstand!"

Upon hearing this, Shauntal and Caitlin nodded and said no more.

But Cynthia seemed intrigued and asked, "Where is your ranch located, and what's its name?"

That was something he could answer! Cynthia could become a potential customer too!

D immediately perked up: "Paldea Region, Porto Marinada Town, D Ranch!"

"I'll remember that." Cynthia smiled slightly.

The three-member group didn't linger for long and left after exchanging farewells.

The garden was suddenly left with only D and Nemona.

D wanted to say something to Nemona, but as he turned his head, he noticed Nemona seemed a bit unhappy.

D was slightly stunned and asked, "What's wrong?"

Nemona looked up at the adults not far away, who were chatting and smiling, and grumbled, "I don't like this kind of place."

D was a bit surprised, and the nearby Pokemon also looked at Nemona.

"Is it because the adults are drinking?" D asked.

"No." Nemona became somewhat dejected: "Everyone humors me in Pokemon battles because of my status, always intentionally losing to me."

"But Nemona is still Nemona. I don't want to be labeled as a little miss or a prodigy, nor treated differently because of my status."

"I want to be like everyone else... I was really happy when Mr. D battled me seriously earlier, but then you became just like everyone else."

D: "..."

He coughed lightly and explained, "Actually, I just worried others would think I'm bullying a kid, I really didn't mean it that way."

Though Nemona's identity was that of a little miss, her carefree and unrestrained nature made it hard for others to see her as a wealthy girl's daughter.

Upon hearing this, Nemona immediately turned around: "Really?"

"Of course it's true!" D nodded: "And whether it's me or others at this banquet, we are all much older than you."

"When you reach our age, you'll definitely become a great Trainer. By then, we might not be able to match your full strength."

Nemona listened intently to these words.

Moments later, her eyes lit up again, filled with determination: "Yes! I want to become a great Trainer!"

D smiled and nodded: "Good luck! Future Paldea Champion."

Nemona reverted to her lively self: "Mr. D, let's battle!"

D suddenly felt very troubled.

---

Are you kidding! Koraidon, Pawmo, Dragonite, Cyclizar, Ogerpon, Glaceon—any one of them could take down Nemona's Pokemon in a single move.

Thinking of this, D tactfully said, "The strength gap between my Pokemon and yours is too big; it's not suitable for us to battle right now. Let's wait until you grow up a bit more!"

*

Meanwhile, on the other side of the banquet.

Alder, Boss Clay, and Nemona's father Nikar were talking about parenting tips.

Seeing the moment was about right, Alder handed over a project proposal.

Nikar seemed surprised upon seeing it, "What's this?"

Alder: "A friend asked me to pass this on to you, please take a look."

A friend? Nikar was somewhat surprised but didn't decline; he opened the proposal promptly in front of Alder and Boss Clay.

At first glance, he saw an electronic device resembling Rotom drawn in pencil on it.

Nikar read out the name slowly: "Rotom Smartphone?"

"Rotom Smartphone?" Boss Clay, standing by, also showed a surprised expression, "What's that?"

He's only heard of the Rotom refrigerator, Rotom washing machine—those kinds of household items.

"I'm not too sure, it's written here," Nikar focused and continued reading.

A smartphone with full smart touchscreen capabilities, featuring an app with built-in Pokedex functions, enabling trainers to communicate with distant friends over the network, connecting trainers globally, achieving video calls, food delivery, navigation, online shopping, and other functions...

It can also house Rotom in such a smartphone to act as a smart AI, using voice commands to assist trainers traveling abroad with navigation, positioning, map viewing, and Pokedex inquiries...

Nikar became more and more serious as he read.

As the boss of Rotom Technology Company, coupled with their company already developing similar communication devices such as video phones, Nikar understood the content of the project proposal at a glance.

If this technology could indeed be achieved, it would be incredibly promising!

When the information age comes... such a Rotom Smartphone would hold an immeasurable future and position!

Nikar's heart suddenly became excited, and he eagerly asked, "Who wrote this proposal?!"

"Huh?" Seeing Nikar like this, Alder was somewhat astonished, not expecting D's thing to catch the big boss's attention instantly.

Nikar felt like the future of the world was in his hands, so excited he could barely breathe, "I want to meet him! Please, Alder!"

"Ha?" Alder scratched his head and said honestly, "You've already met him!"

Met him? Nikar was slightly stunned, but shortly after, that young figure who'd been following Alder appeared in his mind.

Meanwhile, D was quietly observing Lance with Nemona.

They hid by the garden in the yard, observing Lance from afar.

Lance was standing with his arms crossed in front of a bush neatly trimmed, his face resolute and his expression solemn, seemingly pondering something.

Draped behind him was a cape with a very novel style, half of his body shrouded in shadow, looking both handsome and cool.

Lance hadn't attended such a banquet much before, Nemona hadn't seen him, so she curiously asked:

"Mr. D, do you know that person?"

D nodded, "He's Lance, Dragon Elite of the Kanto Region, and also seems to have held the title of Champion in Kanto; he's very skilled with Dragon Type Pokemon."

"Wow!" Nemona exclaimed, looking between D and Lance, then asked, "Mr. D, do you want to talk to him?"

D pondered thoughtfully, then shook his head, "Not really, but I heard his ace Pokemon is a Dragonite too. I want to battle him to see how my Dragonite's strength measures up to the Champion."

"Battle?" Nemona was immediately drawn in, eagerly asking, "Mr. D, will you be giving it your all?"

"Probably!" D added, "But I'm not sure how to approach him to talk... If I challenge him and get rejected, that'd be too embarrassing."

Because in Lance's eyes, he's just an unknown trainer.

Upon hearing this, Nemona transformed into her lively self, enthusiastically saying, "I'll help you then!"

Before D could respond, Nemona charged out, loudly shouting:

"Hey! Big brother over there, come have a Pokemon battle with us!"

D: "!!!"

Yame–

But it was too late; Nemona's voice was loud enough to draw the attention of surrounding celebrities and trainers.

D buried his face in agony, feeling like he was about to suffocate.

Little Nemona is way too outgoing...

But at this point, there was no turning back.

He took a deep breath and slowly followed Nemona's steps into the spotlight of the cool Lance's slightly surprised gaze.
